# P2 — Domaine — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Replace the booking domain with the tickets & événements model. That means the new schema (migrations 0010–0012), the atomic write mechanism, the complete engine for spec E1–E13 without UI, the auth options (e-mail codes, takeover purge, DB rate limit), DB-backed concurrency tests T1–T15, a full seed v2 and the content bootstrap. Every task keeps `tsc`, the tests and `next build` green, with the old domain screens neutralised behind placeholders.

**Architecture:** The engine lives in `src/lib/booking/*` and `src/lib/db/atomic.ts`. It has no `server-only`, takes `db` and `now` as parameters, runs one `db.batch` per write path (locks first, guards plus `skedul_assert`, aid-marked rows), and returns `EngineResult` with `Effect` descriptors instead of sending e-mails or calling Stripe or Google. Pure rules live in `src/lib/*.ts` (recurrence, expiry, windows, wallet rules, lanes, guest links, throttle). Work happens on the Neon branches `tickets-dev` and `tickets-test`; development, preview and prod are never touched.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.2.9, React 19, drizzle-orm 0.45.2 (neon-http) + drizzle-kit, @neondatabase/serverless 1.1.0, Postgres 17 (btree_gist), better-auth 1.6.19 (emailOTP, admin), zod 4, @date-fns/tz, node:test via tsx.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-tickets-evenements-design.md` (binding; includes the P1 spike verdict in § 10).
**Contract appendix:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-p2-domaine-contract.md`. It holds the full schema, the module contracts and the engine API table that the tasks below implement. **Where a task differs from the contract, the task wins.** Each group starts with its « Contract deviations », and the appendix opens with an erratum.

- **Worktree:** `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets`, branch `feat/tickets-events` (contains `main` 7feb70b). `node_modules` is a junction to the main checkout.
- **Never** run `pnpm install` or `pnpm <script>` in the worktree. Always call `./node_modules/.bin/{tsx,tsc,eslint,next,drizzle-kit}` directly.
- **Typecheck:** `./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit`
- **Lint (changed files only):** `./node_modules/.bin/eslint <every file created or modified by the task>`. Expected: no error and no warning.
- **Pure tests:** `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test 'src/**/!(*.db).test.ts'` (single quotes in Git Bash and PowerShell).
- **DB tests, all:** `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --env-file-if-exists=.env.test --test --test-concurrency=1 'src/**/*.db.test.ts'`
- **DB tests, one file:** `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --env-file-if-exists=.env.test --test --test-concurrency=1 src/lib/booking/claims.db.test.ts`
- **Build:** `./node_modules/.bin/next build`. It needs the worktree's `.env.local`, created in Task 2.
- **Every task ends green:** tsc, eslint on its changed files, all pure tests, all DB tests (once Task 5 is done), and `next build`.
- **Migrations:** generate with `./node_modules/.bin/drizzle-kit generate --name=<name>`, or `--custom --name=tickets_manual` for 0012.
- **Applying migrations:** `./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/apply-migration.ts drizzle/<file>.sql`. It reads `.env.local`, which points at **tickets-dev**. dotenv 17 prints its « injected env » line first: the `→ <host>` line that follows must equal the tickets-dev host saved in Task 2, and the process environment must not already hold a `DATABASE_URL` (dotenv does not override it). The G1 steps check both. If either check fails, stop.
- **Never** run `db:migrate` or `db:push`. Only P2 migrations 0010–0012 touch tickets-dev; tickets-test is re-created from it.
- **Neon project** `nameless-meadow-98376303`:
  - `development` = `br-bold-art-af8tpfm5`: only the parent of tickets-dev; never write to it.
  - `main` (prod) = `br-misty-thunder-af8c8bd9`: **never touch**.
  - `preview` = `br-autumn-tooth-afnthws9`: never touch in P2.
- **Neon MCP:** always pass `project_id` **and** `branch_id`. These tools fall back to PROD when `branch_id` is omitted. Never print a connection string.
- **Working branches:**
  - `tickets-dev` = child of `development` (Task 2). `.env.local` points at it.
  - `tickets-test` = child of `tickets-dev`, created after 0012 (Task 5). `.env.test` points at it.
- **Package is CommonJS:** scripts use `async function main()` plus `main().catch(...)`; no top-level await.
- **Files:** Windows, CRLF working copies (autocrlf). Write whole files with the Write tool and partial changes with Edit. Never use bash heredocs for files that contain an apostrophe (known parse trap).
- **Engine location:** `src/lib/booking/*` and `src/lib/db/atomic.ts`. They never import `server-only`, `@/lib/db` (index), `@/lib/env`, `next/*` or `@/lib/email`.
  - `db: Db` is the first parameter.
  - `now: Date = new Date()` is the last positional parameter (before an optional test-only `opts`).
  - `now` reaches SQL as a parameter (`ts(now)`), **never** as SQL `now()`. Every inserted or updated `created_at`, `updated_at`, `status_changed_at`, `ended_at`, `paid_at`, `confirmed_at` and `cancelled_at` comes from `now`.
- **Every multi-row write is one `tryAtomic(db, plan, opts)`** (§5 atomic.ts):
  - `plan()` does the reads, generates the ids (nanoid) and returns `(aid) => Query[]`.
  - Lock first, in the global order: professional → event → occurrence (id asc) → customer → booking → purchase → ticket.
  - Every guarded UPDATE is followed by `G(pred, code)`.
  - Guards target rows known by JS id or marked by `aid` (`booking.ended_action_id`, `ticket_purchase.fulfilled_action_id`, `event.last_action_id`, `ticket_movement.action_id`). Never "rows already in the target state".
- **SQL binding rules (from the spike):**
  - Dates: `ts(d)` (`::timestamptz`).
  - Id lists: `textArray(ids)` (`= any(${sql.param(ids)}::text[])`); never a bare JS array.
  - `tsArray`, `dateArray`; ints used in arithmetic: `${n}::int`.
  - Enum literals compared or inserted from parameters: `${v}::booking_mode` etc.
  - `count(*)::int`.
- **Raw `db.execute` rows return timestamps and dates as strings, and `count(*)` without a cast as a string.** Convert with `new Date()` / `Number()`. Prefer builder selects (Date-mapped) for plan reads and builder inserts for single-row inserts. Use raw SQL for CTEs, guards and set-based updates.
- **Engine results** are `EngineResult<T> = { ok: true; effects: Effect[] } & T | { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode; message?: string }`.
  - The engine never throws for business outcomes. It rethrows unknown DB errors (programming errors).
  - It never sends e-mail and never calls Stripe or Google: it returns `Effect` descriptors (§5 types.ts).
- **Not in P2:**
  - UI beyond the `ComingSoon` placeholders, server actions for the new flows, `notify.ts`, Stripe calls, the Google sync rewrite.
  - Throttle and honeypot checks inside the engine. The P4 actions call `checkActionThrottle` before the engine.
- **Language:** code, comments and plan prose in English. User-facing strings in French, vouvoiement, **no em dash `—`** in UI strings (use « : » or a period).
- **Commit format:** `git add <files>` then `git commit -m "<type>(<scope>): <summary>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"`.
- **Test file naming:**
  - Pure tests: `*.test.ts`.
  - DB tests: `*.db.test.ts`.
  - Helpers in `src/test/` are never named `*.test.ts`, except the pure `src/test/db-guard.test.ts`.
- **No new dependencies.** zod v4 (`z.email()`, `z.iso.date()`), better-auth 1.6.19, drizzle-orm 0.45.2 and @date-fns/tz are already installed.
- **tsconfig target is ES2017:** no regex `s` flag and no named capture groups.
- **`src/lib/db/schema.ts` and `src/lib/tickets.ts` import only relative paths and npm packages** (drizzle-kit cannot resolve `@/`). `tickets.ts` imports only `zod`.
- **Do not modify** `scripts/spike/*` (they keep compiling; they only use the auth tables).
- **Next.js 16:** `params` and `searchParams` are async. Read `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` before touching a page. A page that redirects must `await params` first (static-redirect prerender trap).

## Review Focus

These are the five classes of input and failure most likely to bite. Reviewers check them first.

| # | Failure mode | Why it bites | Owning task(s) / proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Seat and ticket accounting under concurrency.** Over-selling, double spend, double return, a seat counter that drifts. | `SEAT` must run once per occurrence per action, after every booking end in that batch. `rel(o)` is evaluated inside SEAT. `RET` runs once per action. `releaseEndedSeats` must never be combined with SEAT on the same occurrence. Picks use SKIP LOCKED plus UNIQUE `booking_id`. A 0-row guarded UPDATE does not abort a batch unless G follows. | Task 14 (fragments), Task 23 (T1, T2), Task 25 (T3, T7), Task 15 (I1, I2, I4 after every DB test) |
| 2 | **Civil time and DST.** | Expander jumps (never/until), count walking from `start_date`. 31st skipped, nth and last weekday. `until` inclusive. Paris 2026-03-29 (02:30 → 01:30Z) and 2026-10-25 (02:30 → 00:30Z). Month-end clamping in expiry. "Local today" computed the same way in JS (`booking-window.ts`) and SQL (`at time zone p.timezone`). | Tasks 7, 8, 10, 18 (horizon boundaries) |
| 3 | **Series split against concurrent writers.** | Version guard. Reparented-row-count guard (catches rows `ensureHorizon` inserted after the plan read). Guards on delete and cancelEmpty (catch a booking taken after the plan read). `until_date = d − 1` vs CHECK. Phantom rows from `ensureHorizon`. Re-plan once on stale. | Task 9 (planner), Task 28 (T8 a and b via the `afterPlan` hook) |
| 4 | **Payment lifecycle.** | Duplicate delivery must be a no-op (`already_paid`). Paid from `expired` still gives tickets. A booking is never resurrected. Hold expiry followed by a late payment. `processing_at`. Hold quota. `attachCheckoutSession` on a non-pending purchase. | Task 20 (E1), Task 21 (T4, T14), Task 22 (T5) |
| 5 | **Identity and deletion.** | The pre-account takeover purge must keep the new session. The per-e-mail code cap must not burn the live code. `disabledPaths` derived from the plugin. R2 (every domain table cascades **directly** from `professional`) with NO ACTION FKs inside the domain. | Task 4 (FK review), Task 30 (T11), Task 32 (disabledPaths test), Task 33 (T12, T13, T15) |

## Contract deviations (G1)

1. **`src/test/db.ts` declares `Db` itself (Task 6).** The contract has it `import type { Db } from "../lib/db/atomic"`, but `atomic.ts` is only created in Task 14, so Task 6 would not compile. The harness instead declares `export type Db = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schemaModule>`. This is the same type as `atomic.ts`'s `Db`, so the two are interchangeable. Everything else in the file is the contract text verbatim. That includes the `// Task 15 inserts here:` block, so Task 15's edit still applies.
2. **New grep proof for Task 2.** The contract's grep (`eventType|classSession|…`) would never return "only schema.ts". It also matches legitimate identifiers that stay: `eventTypeId` in the kept dashboard components and `src/lib/availability.ts`, the demo code, `availabilityRuleInputSchema` in `tickets.ts`, and the stub signatures in `pro.ts`. Task 2 uses a grep on table usage instead (`from(…)`, `insert(…)`, `table.column`, `schema.table`, and imports of deleted modules). I ran it on a scratch copy: its expected output is one comment line in `src/lib/availability.ts`. Task 3's `tsc` is the final proof, because the tables are removed from `schema.ts` there.
3. **Redirect pages use `redirect(`/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}`)`** instead of `redirect(`/${slug}`)`. Next decodes dynamic params, so a `%2F…` segment could produce `//host` (an open redirect). For real slugs (`[a-z0-9-]`) the output is the same.
4. **Task 2 adds one test to `src/lib/admin-params.test.ts`**, so the parser change is TDD. The existing tests are unchanged.
5. **Migrations are applied by one chained command (Tasks 3, 4, 5).** The contract's "the first output line `→ <host>` must equal tickets-dev, otherwise stop" cannot work. That line is the second one (dotenv 17 prints its `injected env` line first). `apply-migration.ts` prints it and commits in the same run, so it is only a record. And `scripts/load-env.ts` (dotenv, no `override`) lets a `DATABASE_URL` already in the process environment win over `.env.local`. Each apply is therefore one `&&` chain run from the worktree: the env host check (which also stops when an environment `DATABASE_URL` points at another host), then `apply-migration.ts` with `DATABASE_URL` read from the checked `.env.local` and set in the same command. Nothing is applied unless the check prints `OK: tickets-dev`. Task 4 counts the `✓` lines with `awk` instead of `tee /dev/stderr | grep -c`: in the agent Bash tool, stdout and stderr are one file and `tee /dev/stderr` reopens it from the start, which erased the earlier lines (the check output) and overwrote the last one (verified).
6. **Task 1 cannot run `next build`.** The build needs `.env.local`, which Task 2 creates. `tickets.ts` is imported by no app code in Task 1. Builds start with the Task 2 baseline.
7. **`saveAvailability` (Task 4)** keeps only rows that pass `availabilityRuleInputSchema`, which has the same bounds as the DB CHECKs. It replaces the global rules in one `db.batch`, so a failed insert can never lose the old hours. The contract only said "delete the global rules, insert valid ones".
8. **`harness.db.test.ts` tests a populated pro for R2** (with deep NO ACTION references: a paid `ticket_purchase` referenced by the booking, the ticket and the movement, and a `booking_answer`), not only an empty one. The empty case is already covered by every `dbTest` teardown. Hand-made RDV rows are deleted at the end of their test, so the I1–I6 check that Task 15 adds after each `dbTest` stays green.

**Working directory (every task).** Every path in this plan is relative to the worktree root `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets`. A subagent's Bash cwd resets to the main checkout `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul`, which is on branch `preview` and holds the development `.env.local`. So every Bash call in this plan starts in the worktree: a single command starts with `cd /c/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets && `, and a block of several commands starts with the line `cd /c/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets || exit 1`. Run each block as one Bash call, because the `cd` does not carry over to the next call. With Read, Edit and Write, always use the absolute worktree path `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets/<path>`, never `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/<path>`.

### Task 1: Vocabulary: `tickets.ts`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/tickets.ts`
- Test: `src/lib/tickets.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `zod` v4 (`z.iso.date()`, `z.email()`).
- Produces (all exported from `src/lib/tickets.ts`, contract §5.1):
  - `BOOKING_MODES`, `RECURRENCE_FREQS`, `MONTHLY_BY`, `RECURRENCE_ENDS`, `VALIDITY_UNITS`, `OCCURRENCE_STATUSES`, `BOOKING_STATUSES`, `BOOKING_COVERAGES`, `ACTORS`, `PURCHASE_STATUSES`, `TICKET_STATUSES`, `TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS`, `MOVEMENT_KINDS`, `QUESTION_TYPES` (readonly tuples), with the types `BookingMode`, `RecurrenceFreq`, `MonthlyBy`, `RecurrenceEnd`, `ValidityUnit`, `OccurrenceStatus`, `BookingStatus`, `BookingCoverage`, `Actor`, `PurchaseStatus`, `TicketStatus`, `TicketCloseReason`, `MovementKind`, `QuestionType`.
  - `BOOKING_MODE_LABELS`, `BOOKING_STATUS_LABELS`, `COVERAGE_LABELS`, `PURCHASE_STATUS_LABELS`, `OCCURRENCE_STATUS_LABELS`, `TICKET_STATUS_LABELS`, `CLOSE_REASON_LABELS`, `MOVEMENT_KIND_LABELS`, `ACTOR_LABELS`: `Record<…, string>`.
  - `BOUNDS`, `MATERIALIZE_DAYS = 400`, `HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS = 393`, `MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS = 450`, `HOLD_MINUTES = 45`, `CHECKOUT_MINUTES = 31`, `SHOP_CHECKOUT_HOURS = 24`, `STALE_PURCHASE_GRACE_MINUTES = 60`, `MAX_PENDING_HOLDS = 2`, `THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC`, `THROTTLE_ACTIONS`, `type ThrottleAction`, `THROTTLE_LIMITS`, `OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT`, `THROTTLE_PURGE_DAYS`, `DEFAULT_COLOR`.
  - `ENGINE_ERROR_CODES`, `type EngineErrorCode`, `isEngineErrorCode(value: string): value is EngineErrorCode`, `ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<EngineErrorCode, string>`.
  - `normalizeEmail(email: string): string`, `offerLabel(offer: { label: string | null; quantity: number }): string`, `validityLabel(count: number | null, unit: ValidityUnit | null): string | null`.
  - zod: `dateKeySchema`, `hexColorSchema`, `idSchema`, `customerInputSchema` (`CustomerInput`), `answerInputSchema`, `answersInputSchema` (`AnswerInput`), `bookingSettingsSchema` (`BookingSettings`), `windowOverridesSchema` (`WindowOverrides`), `questionInputSchema` (`QuestionInput`), `ticketTypeInputSchema` (`TicketTypeInput`), `offerInputSchema` (`OfferInput`), `recurrenceRuleSchema` (`RecurrenceRuleInput`), `eventInputSchema` (`EventInput`, `EventInputParsed`), `availabilityRuleInputSchema` (`AvailabilityRuleInput`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `src/lib/tickets.test.ts`:

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";

import {
  ACTORS,
  ACTOR_LABELS,
  BOOKING_COVERAGES,
  BOOKING_MODES,
  BOOKING_MODE_LABELS,
  BOOKING_STATUSES,
  BOOKING_STATUS_LABELS,
  BOUNDS,
  CHECKOUT_MINUTES,
  CLOSE_REASON_LABELS,
  COVERAGE_LABELS,
  ENGINE_ERROR_CODES,
  ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES,
  HOLD_MINUTES,
  HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS,
  MATERIALIZE_DAYS,
  MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS,
  MAX_PENDING_HOLDS,
  MONTHLY_BY,
  MOVEMENT_KINDS,
  MOVEMENT_KIND_LABELS,
  OCCURRENCE_STATUSES,
  OCCURRENCE_STATUS_LABELS,
  OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT,
  PURCHASE_STATUSES,
  PURCHASE_STATUS_LABELS,
  QUESTION_TYPES,
  RECURRENCE_ENDS,
  RECURRENCE_FREQS,
  THROTTLE_ACTIONS,
  THROTTLE_LIMITS,
  TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS,
  TICKET_STATUSES,
  TICKET_STATUS_LABELS,
  VALIDITY_UNITS,
  availabilityRuleInputSchema,
  customerInputSchema,
  dateKeySchema,
  eventInputSchema,
  isEngineErrorCode,
  normalizeEmail,
  offerInputSchema,
  offerLabel,
  questionInputSchema,
  recurrenceRuleSchema,
  ticketTypeInputSchema,
  validityLabel,
  type RecurrenceRuleInput,
} from "./tickets";

test("enum values are exactly the ones of spec § 6.2", () => {
  assert.deepEqual([...BOOKING_MODES], ["tickets", "free", "none"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...RECURRENCE_FREQS], ["once", "daily", "weekly", "monthly"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...MONTHLY_BY], ["day_of_month", "weekday"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...RECURRENCE_ENDS], ["never", "until", "count"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...VALIDITY_UNITS], ["day", "month"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...OCCURRENCE_STATUSES], ["scheduled", "cancelled"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...BOOKING_STATUSES], ["pending", "confirmed", "cancelled", "expired"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...BOOKING_COVERAGES], ["ticket", "free", "guest"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...ACTORS], ["client", "pro", "system"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...PURCHASE_STATUSES], ["pending", "paid", "expired"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...TICKET_STATUSES], ["available", "booked", "used", "expired", "closed"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS], ["rembourse", "compense", "retire", "autre"]);
  assert.deepEqual([...MOVEMENT_KINDS], [
    "purchase",
    "offer",
    "spend",
    "return",
    "pointage",
    "unpoint",
    "convert",
    "expire",
    "close",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual([...QUESTION_TYPES], ["text", "choice", "boolean"]);
});

test("every enum value has a French label", () => {
  const pairs: [readonly string[], Record<string, string>][] = [
    [BOOKING_MODES, BOOKING_MODE_LABELS],
    [BOOKING_STATUSES, BOOKING_STATUS_LABELS],
    [BOOKING_COVERAGES, COVERAGE_LABELS],
    [PURCHASE_STATUSES, PURCHASE_STATUS_LABELS],
    [OCCURRENCE_STATUSES, OCCURRENCE_STATUS_LABELS],
    [TICKET_STATUSES, TICKET_STATUS_LABELS],
    [TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS, CLOSE_REASON_LABELS],
    [MOVEMENT_KINDS, MOVEMENT_KIND_LABELS],
    [ACTORS, ACTOR_LABELS],
    [ENGINE_ERROR_CODES, ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES],
  ];
  for (const [values, labels] of pairs) {
    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(labels).sort(), [...values].sort());
    for (const v of values) assert.ok(labels[v].trim().length > 0, v);
  }
});

test("horizon and hold constants keep their margins", () => {
  // the longest booking horizon (365) plus a month of slack stays materialised
  assert.ok(365 + 30 < MATERIALIZE_DAYS);
  assert.ok(HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS < MATERIALIZE_DAYS);
  assert.ok(BOUNDS.bookingHorizonDays.max + 30 < MATERIALIZE_DAYS);
  // one ensureHorizon call can fill a whole daily series window
  assert.ok(MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS >= MATERIALIZE_DAYS);
  // the seat outlives the Stripe session, which Stripe caps at 30 min minimum
  assert.ok(CHECKOUT_MINUTES >= 30);
  assert.ok(HOLD_MINUTES > CHECKOUT_MINUTES);
  assert.equal(MAX_PENDING_HOLDS, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT, { limit: 5, windowSec: 3600 });
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(THROTTLE_LIMITS).sort(), [...THROTTLE_ACTIONS].sort());
});

test("the source contains no em dash", () => {
  const source = readFileSync(join(__dirname, "tickets.ts"), "utf8");
  assert.equal(source.includes("\u2014"), false);
});

test("isEngineErrorCode accepts only engine codes", () => {
  assert.equal(isEngineErrorCode("full"), true);
  assert.equal(isEngineErrorCode("already_paid"), false);
  assert.equal(isEngineErrorCode(""), false);
});

test("normalizeEmail trims and lower-cases", () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeEmail("  Lea.Martin@Example.COM "), "lea.martin@example.com");
});

test("offerLabel uses the typed label, else a generated one", () => {
  assert.equal(offerLabel({ label: null, quantity: 1 }), "À l'unité");
  assert.equal(offerLabel({ label: null, quantity: 10 }), "Carnet de 10");
  assert.equal(offerLabel({ label: "   ", quantity: 4 }), "Carnet de 4");
  assert.equal(offerLabel({ label: "  Rentrée  ", quantity: 4 }), "Rentrée");
});

test("validityLabel", () => {
  assert.equal(validityLabel(3, "month"), "3 mois");
  assert.equal(validityLabel(30, "day"), "30 jours");
  assert.equal(validityLabel(1, "day"), "1 jour");
  assert.equal(validityLabel(null, null), null);
  assert.equal(validityLabel(3, null), null);
});

test("dateKeySchema accepts real calendar dates only", () => {
  assert.equal(dateKeySchema.safeParse("2026-10-12").success, true);
  assert.equal(dateKeySchema.safeParse("2028-02-29").success, true);
  assert.equal(dateKeySchema.safeParse("2026-02-30").success, false);
  assert.equal(dateKeySchema.safeParse("12/10/2026").success, false);
});

test("customerInputSchema normalises the e-mail and requires a name", () => {
  const ok = customerInputSchema.parse({ name: " Léa ", email: "Lea@Example.COM" });
  assert.deepEqual(ok, { name: "Léa", email: "lea@example.com" });
  assert.equal(customerInputSchema.safeParse({ name: " ", email: "lea@example.com" }).success, false);
  assert.equal(customerInputSchema.safeParse({ name: "Léa", email: "not-an-email" }).success, false);
});

test("offerInputSchema: validity needs both fields, price is positive", () => {
  const base = { ticketTypeId: "tt1", quantity: 10, priceCents: 19_000 };
  const ok = offerInputSchema.parse(base);
  assert.equal(ok.validityCount, null);
  assert.equal(ok.validityUnit, null);
  assert.equal(ok.isActive, true);
  assert.equal(offerInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, validityCount: 6, validityUnit: "month" }).success, true);
  assert.equal(offerInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, validityCount: 6 }).success, false);
  assert.equal(offerInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, validityUnit: "month" }).success, false);
  assert.equal(offerInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, priceCents: 0 }).success, false);
  assert.equal(offerInputSchema.safeParse({ ...base, quantity: 101 }).success, false);
});

test("ticketTypeInputSchema: RDV needs a duration, free RDV needs RDV", () => {
  const plain = ticketTypeInputSchema.parse({ name: "Cours collectif" });
  assert.equal(plain.rdvEnabled, false);
  assert.equal(plain.rdvDurationMin, null);
  assert.equal(plain.color, "#6366f1");
  assert.deepEqual(plain.rdvOverrides, {
    bookingHorizonDays: null,
    minNoticeMinutes: null,
    cancelDeadlineHours: null,
  });
  assert.equal(ticketTypeInputSchema.safeParse({ name: "x", rdvEnabled: true }).success, false);
  assert.equal(ticketTypeInputSchema.safeParse({ name: "x", rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 }).success, true);
  assert.equal(ticketTypeInputSchema.safeParse({ name: "x", rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 62 }).success, false);
  assert.equal(ticketTypeInputSchema.safeParse({ name: "x", rdvFree: true }).success, false);
});

test("questionInputSchema: a choice question needs two options", () => {
  assert.equal(questionInputSchema.safeParse({ label: "Niveau ?", type: "choice", options: ["Débutant"] }).success, false);
  assert.equal(
    questionInputSchema.safeParse({ label: "Niveau ?", type: "choice", options: ["Débutant", "Confirmé"] }).success,
    true,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(questionInputSchema.parse({ label: "Objectif ?", type: "text" }), {
    label: "Objectif ?",
    type: "text",
    options: [],
    required: false,
  });
});

const weekly: RecurrenceRuleInput = {
  startDate: "2026-10-12",
  startMinute: 1140,
  freq: "weekly",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays: [1, 3],
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "never",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: null,
};

test("recurrenceRuleSchema accepts the shapes of spec § 8", () => {
  assert.equal(recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(weekly).success, true);
  const once = { ...weekly, freq: "once", weekdays: null, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1 };
  assert.equal(recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(once).success, true);
  const firstSunday = {
    ...weekly,
    startDate: "2026-11-01",
    freq: "monthly",
    weekdays: null,
    monthlyBy: "weekday",
    monthWeek: 1,
  };
  assert.equal(recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(firstSunday).success, true);
  const fiveYears = { ...weekly, endMode: "until", untilDate: "2031-10-12" };
  assert.equal(recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(fiveYears).success, true);
});

test("recurrenceRuleSchema rejects what the DB CHECKs reject", () => {
  const bad: Record<string, unknown>[] = [
    { ...weekly, weekdays: [3, 1] },
    { ...weekly, weekdays: [1, 1] },
    { ...weekly, interval: 53 },
    { ...weekly, freq: "daily" },
    { ...weekly, freq: "once", weekdays: null, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1, interval: 2 },
    { ...weekly, freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: null },
    { ...weekly, freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: null },
    { ...weekly, endMode: "until", untilDate: null },
    { ...weekly, endMode: "until", untilDate: "2026-10-11" },
    { ...weekly, endMode: "until", untilDate: "2031-10-13" },
    { ...weekly, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: null },
    { ...weekly, endMode: "never", occurrenceCount: 3 },
    { ...weekly, startMinute: 1440 },
  ];
  for (const rule of bad) {
    assert.equal(recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(rule).success, false, JSON.stringify(rule));
  }
});

test("eventInputSchema fills the defaults", () => {
  const parsed = eventInputSchema.parse({ title: "Altinha débutants", rule: weekly });
  assert.equal(parsed.bookingMode, "tickets");
  assert.equal(parsed.isPrivate, false);
  assert.equal(parsed.capacity, 12);
  assert.equal(parsed.durationMin, 60);
  assert.deepEqual(parsed.ticketTypeIds, []);
  assert.deepEqual(parsed.questions, []);
  assert.equal(eventInputSchema.safeParse({ title: "x", rule: weekly, durationMin: 62 }).success, false);
  assert.equal(eventInputSchema.safeParse({ title: "x", rule: weekly, capacity: 501 }).success, false);
});

test("availabilityRuleInputSchema needs start < end within the day", () => {
  assert.equal(availabilityRuleInputSchema.safeParse({ dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 600, endMinute: 780 }).success, true);
  assert.equal(availabilityRuleInputSchema.safeParse({ dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 0, endMinute: 1440 }).success, true);
  assert.equal(availabilityRuleInputSchema.safeParse({ dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 780, endMinute: 600 }).success, false);
  assert.equal(availabilityRuleInputSchema.safeParse({ dayOfWeek: 7, startMinute: 600, endMinute: 780 }).success, false);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

Create `src/lib/tickets.ts` (the contract's §5.1 text, verbatim):

```ts
/**
 * Tickets & événements: the vocabulary shared by the schema (pgEnums), the
 * booking engine, the server actions and the UI. Enum values, bounds, French
 * labels and messages, engine error codes and the zod input schemas.
 * No drizzle and no Node APIs, so client components may import it.
 */
import { z } from "zod";

/* ───────────────────────── Enum values (pgEnum sources) ───────────────────────── */

export const BOOKING_MODES = ["tickets", "free", "none"] as const;
export type BookingMode = (typeof BOOKING_MODES)[number];

export const RECURRENCE_FREQS = ["once", "daily", "weekly", "monthly"] as const;
export type RecurrenceFreq = (typeof RECURRENCE_FREQS)[number];

export const MONTHLY_BY = ["day_of_month", "weekday"] as const;
export type MonthlyBy = (typeof MONTHLY_BY)[number];

export const RECURRENCE_ENDS = ["never", "until", "count"] as const;
export type RecurrenceEnd = (typeof RECURRENCE_ENDS)[number];

export const VALIDITY_UNITS = ["day", "month"] as const;
export type ValidityUnit = (typeof VALIDITY_UNITS)[number];

export const OCCURRENCE_STATUSES = ["scheduled", "cancelled"] as const;
export type OccurrenceStatus = (typeof OCCURRENCE_STATUSES)[number];

export const BOOKING_STATUSES = ["pending", "confirmed", "cancelled", "expired"] as const;
export type BookingStatus = (typeof BOOKING_STATUSES)[number];

export const BOOKING_COVERAGES = ["ticket", "free", "guest"] as const;
export type BookingCoverage = (typeof BOOKING_COVERAGES)[number];

export const ACTORS = ["client", "pro", "system"] as const;
export type Actor = (typeof ACTORS)[number];

export const PURCHASE_STATUSES = ["pending", "paid", "expired"] as const;
export type PurchaseStatus = (typeof PURCHASE_STATUSES)[number];

export const TICKET_STATUSES = ["available", "booked", "used", "expired", "closed"] as const;
export type TicketStatus = (typeof TICKET_STATUSES)[number];

export const TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS = ["rembourse", "compense", "retire", "autre"] as const;
export type TicketCloseReason = (typeof TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS)[number];

export const MOVEMENT_KINDS = [
  "purchase",
  "offer",
  "spend",
  "return",
  "pointage",
  "unpoint",
  "convert",
  "expire",
  "close",
] as const;
export type MovementKind = (typeof MOVEMENT_KINDS)[number];

export const QUESTION_TYPES = ["text", "choice", "boolean"] as const;
export type QuestionType = (typeof QUESTION_TYPES)[number];

/* ───────────────────────────── Labels (FR) ───────────────────────────── */

export const BOOKING_MODE_LABELS: Record<BookingMode, string> = {
  tickets: "Sur tickets",
  free: "Inscription libre",
  none: "Sans réservation en ligne",
};

export const BOOKING_STATUS_LABELS: Record<BookingStatus, string> = {
  pending: "En attente",
  confirmed: "Confirmée",
  cancelled: "Annulée",
  expired: "Expirée",
};

export const COVERAGE_LABELS: Record<BookingCoverage, string> = {
  ticket: "Ticket",
  free: "Inscription libre",
  guest: "Invité",
};

export const PURCHASE_STATUS_LABELS: Record<PurchaseStatus, string> = {
  pending: "En attente",
  paid: "Payé",
  expired: "Expiré",
};

export const OCCURRENCE_STATUS_LABELS: Record<OccurrenceStatus, string> = {
  scheduled: "Planifiée",
  cancelled: "Annulée",
};

export const TICKET_STATUS_LABELS: Record<TicketStatus, string> = {
  available: "Disponible",
  booked: "Réservé",
  used: "Utilisé",
  expired: "Expiré",
  closed: "Clos",
};

export const CLOSE_REASON_LABELS: Record<TicketCloseReason, string> = {
  rembourse: "Remboursé hors Skedul",
  compense: "Compensé",
  retire: "Retiré",
  autre: "Autre",
};

export const MOVEMENT_KIND_LABELS: Record<MovementKind, string> = {
  purchase: "Acheté",
  offer: "Offert",
  spend: "Utilisé pour une inscription",
  return: "Rendu",
  pointage: "Pointé",
  unpoint: "Pointage annulé",
  convert: "Converti",
  expire: "Expiré",
  close: "Clos",
};

export const ACTOR_LABELS: Record<Actor, string> = {
  client: "Client",
  pro: "Vous",
  system: "Skedul",
};

/* ───────────────────────────── Bounds ───────────────────────────── */

export const BOUNDS = {
  bookingHorizonDays: { min: 1, max: 365, default: 60 },
  minNoticeMinutes: { min: 0, max: 10_080, default: 60 },
  cancelDeadlineHours: { min: 0, max: 720, default: 24 },
  capacity: { min: 1, max: 500, default: 12 },
  eventDurationMin: { min: 5, max: 720, step: 5, default: 60 },
  rdvDurationMin: { min: 5, max: 480, step: 5, default: 60 },
  rdvBufferMin: { min: 0, max: 240 },
  offerQuantity: { min: 1, max: 100 },
  offerPriceCents: { min: 1, max: 1_000_000 },
  validityCount: { min: 1, max: 1000 },
  occurrenceCount: { min: 1, max: 730 },
  interval: { once: 1, daily: 31, weekly: 52, monthly: 12 },
  untilYears: 5,
  startMinute: { min: 0, max: 1439 },
  name: 80,
  title: 120,
  description: 2000,
  location: 200,
  offerLabel: 80,
  phone: 30,
  note: 500,
  questionLabel: 200,
  questionOptions: 20,
  answer: 2000,
  questions: 20,
  walletQuantity: 100,
} as const;

/** Event occurrences are materialised this many days ahead of local today. */
export const MATERIALIZE_DAYS = 400;
/** Renders call ensureHorizon only when min(generated_until) < today + this. */
export const HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS = 393;
/** At most this many occurrence rows are inserted by one call. */
export const MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS = 450;

/** A buy+book booking holds its seat this long (`hold_expires_at`). */
export const HOLD_MINUTES = 45;
/** Stripe Checkout `expires_at` for a buy+book purchase (Stripe minimum: 30). */
export const CHECKOUT_MINUTES = 31;
/** Stripe Checkout `expires_at` for a shop purchase. */
export const SHOP_CHECKOUT_HOURS = 24;
/** A pending purchase this long past `checkout_expires_at` is expired by the nightly job. */
export const STALE_PURCHASE_GRACE_MINUTES = 60;
/** Live holds (pending bookings) one customer may have at once. */
export const MAX_PENDING_HOLDS = 2;

export const THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC = 3600;
export const THROTTLE_ACTIONS = ["free", "buybook", "rdv", "shop"] as const;
export type ThrottleAction = (typeof THROTTLE_ACTIONS)[number];
export const THROTTLE_LIMITS: Record<ThrottleAction, { ip: number; email: number }> = {
  free: { ip: 20, email: 6 },
  buybook: { ip: 20, email: 6 },
  rdv: { ip: 20, email: 6 },
  shop: { ip: 20, email: 6 },
};
/** Sign-in / verification codes sent to one address per hour (auth hook). */
export const OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT = { limit: 5, windowSec: 3600 } as const;
/** `throttle` rows older than this are purged by the nightly job. */
export const THROTTLE_PURGE_DAYS = 2;

export const DEFAULT_COLOR = "#6366f1";

/* ───────────────────────── Engine error codes ───────────────────────── */

export const ENGINE_ERROR_CODES = [
  "full",
  "unavailable",
  "changed",
  "stale",
  "already_booked",
  "slot_taken",
  "no_ticket",
  "too_many_holds",
  "too_late_or_done",
  "not_found",
  "not_eligible",
  "in_use",
  "invalid",
  "throttled",
  "offer_too_short",
  "ticket_expires_before",
  "retire_paid",
  "timezone_locked",
] as const;
export type EngineErrorCode = (typeof ENGINE_ERROR_CODES)[number];

export function isEngineErrorCode(value: string): value is EngineErrorCode {
  return (ENGINE_ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export const ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<EngineErrorCode, string> = {
  full: "Complet",
  unavailable: "Cette séance n'est plus ouverte aux réservations.",
  changed: "La séance a changé, rafraîchissez la page.",
  stale: "Ceci a changé, rafraîchissez la page.",
  already_booked: "Vous êtes déjà inscrit(e).",
  slot_taken: "Ce créneau vient d'être pris.",
  no_ticket: "Aucun ticket utilisable pour cette séance.",
  too_many_holds: "Vous avez déjà deux paiements en cours. Terminez-les ou libérez une place.",
  too_late_or_done: "Cette inscription ne peut plus être annulée en ligne.",
  not_found: "Élément introuvable.",
  not_eligible: "Ce ticket n'est pas accepté pour cette séance.",
  in_use: "Cet élément a déjà servi : il a été archivé.",
  invalid: "Données invalides.",
  throttled: "Trop de demandes. Réessayez dans un moment.",
  offer_too_short: "La validité de cette offre ne couvre pas la date de la séance.",
  ticket_expires_before: "Ticket expiré à la date de cette séance. Déplacer quand même ?",
  retire_paid: "Un ticket payé ne peut pas être retiré.",
  timezone_locked: "Fuseau verrouillé : vous avez des séances planifiées.",
};

/* ───────────────────────────── Helpers ───────────────────────────── */

/** Trimmed, lower-cased e-mail: the `customer.email` key. */
export function normalizeEmail(email: string): string {
  return email.trim().toLowerCase();
}

/** « À l'unité » / « Carnet de 10 » unless the pro typed a label. */
export function offerLabel(offer: { label: string | null; quantity: number }): string {
  const typed = offer.label?.trim();
  if (typed) return typed;
  return offer.quantity === 1 ? "À l'unité" : `Carnet de ${offer.quantity}`;
}

/** « 3 mois », « 30 jours », « 1 jour »; null without validity. */
export function validityLabel(count: number | null, unit: ValidityUnit | null): string | null {
  if (count == null || unit == null) return null;
  if (unit === "month") return `${count} mois`;
  return count > 1 ? `${count} jours` : "1 jour";
}

/* ───────────────────────────── zod schemas ───────────────────────────── */

const intIn = (min: number, max: number) => z.number().int().min(min).max(max);
const optionalOverride = (min: number, max: number) => intIn(min, max).nullable();

export const dateKeySchema = z.iso.date();
export const hexColorSchema = z.string().regex(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/);
export const idSchema = z.string().min(1).max(64);

export const customerInputSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.name),
  email: z.email().max(254).transform(normalizeEmail),
  phone: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.phone).optional(),
});
export type CustomerInput = z.input<typeof customerInputSchema>;

export const answerInputSchema = z.object({
  questionId: idSchema,
  value: z.string().max(BOUNDS.answer),
});
export const answersInputSchema = z.array(answerInputSchema).max(BOUNDS.questions);
export type AnswerInput = z.infer<typeof answerInputSchema>;

export const bookingSettingsSchema = z.object({
  bookingHorizonDays: intIn(BOUNDS.bookingHorizonDays.min, BOUNDS.bookingHorizonDays.max),
  minNoticeMinutes: intIn(BOUNDS.minNoticeMinutes.min, BOUNDS.minNoticeMinutes.max),
  cancelDeadlineHours: intIn(BOUNDS.cancelDeadlineHours.min, BOUNDS.cancelDeadlineHours.max),
});
export type BookingSettings = z.infer<typeof bookingSettingsSchema>;

export const windowOverridesSchema = z.object({
  bookingHorizonDays: optionalOverride(BOUNDS.bookingHorizonDays.min, BOUNDS.bookingHorizonDays.max),
  minNoticeMinutes: optionalOverride(BOUNDS.minNoticeMinutes.min, BOUNDS.minNoticeMinutes.max),
  cancelDeadlineHours: optionalOverride(BOUNDS.cancelDeadlineHours.min, BOUNDS.cancelDeadlineHours.max),
});
export type WindowOverrides = z.infer<typeof windowOverridesSchema>;

export const questionInputSchema = z
  .object({
    id: idSchema.optional(),
    label: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.questionLabel),
    type: z.enum(QUESTION_TYPES),
    options: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.questionLabel)).max(BOUNDS.questionOptions).default([]),
    required: z.boolean().default(false),
  })
  .refine((q) => q.type !== "choice" || q.options.length >= 2, {
    message: "Une question à choix demande au moins deux options.",
    path: ["options"],
  });
export type QuestionInput = z.input<typeof questionInputSchema>;

export const ticketTypeInputSchema = z
  .object({
    name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.name),
    description: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.description).nullable().default(null),
    color: hexColorSchema.default(DEFAULT_COLOR),
    rdvEnabled: z.boolean().default(false),
    rdvFree: z.boolean().default(false),
    rdvDurationMin: z
      .number()
      .int()
      .min(BOUNDS.rdvDurationMin.min)
      .max(BOUNDS.rdvDurationMin.max)
      .multipleOf(BOUNDS.rdvDurationMin.step)
      .nullable()
      .default(null),
    rdvBufferBeforeMin: intIn(BOUNDS.rdvBufferMin.min, BOUNDS.rdvBufferMin.max).default(0),
    rdvBufferAfterMin: intIn(BOUNDS.rdvBufferMin.min, BOUNDS.rdvBufferMin.max).default(0),
    rdvOverrides: windowOverridesSchema.default({
      bookingHorizonDays: null,
      minNoticeMinutes: null,
      cancelDeadlineHours: null,
    }),
  })
  .refine((t) => !t.rdvEnabled || t.rdvDurationMin !== null, {
    message: "Indiquez la durée du rendez-vous.",
    path: ["rdvDurationMin"],
  })
  .refine((t) => !t.rdvFree || t.rdvEnabled, {
    message: "Le rendez-vous gratuit demande le RDV libre.",
    path: ["rdvFree"],
  });
export type TicketTypeInput = z.input<typeof ticketTypeInputSchema>;

export const offerInputSchema = z
  .object({
    ticketTypeId: idSchema,
    label: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.offerLabel).nullable().default(null),
    quantity: intIn(BOUNDS.offerQuantity.min, BOUNDS.offerQuantity.max),
    priceCents: intIn(BOUNDS.offerPriceCents.min, BOUNDS.offerPriceCents.max),
    validityCount: intIn(BOUNDS.validityCount.min, BOUNDS.validityCount.max).nullable().default(null),
    validityUnit: z.enum(VALIDITY_UNITS).nullable().default(null),
    isActive: z.boolean().default(true),
  })
  .refine((o) => (o.validityCount === null) === (o.validityUnit === null), {
    message: "Indiquez une durée et une unité de validité, ou aucune.",
    path: ["validityCount"],
  });
export type OfferInput = z.input<typeof offerInputSchema>;

/** Recurrence columns of `event` (spec § 8); refinements mirror the DB CHECKs. */
export const recurrenceRuleSchema = z
  .object({
    startDate: dateKeySchema,
    startMinute: intIn(BOUNDS.startMinute.min, BOUNDS.startMinute.max),
    freq: z.enum(RECURRENCE_FREQS),
    interval: z.number().int().min(1).max(52),
    weekdays: z.array(intIn(0, 6)).min(1).max(7).nullable(),
    monthlyBy: z.enum(MONTHLY_BY).nullable(),
    monthWeek: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2), z.literal(3), z.literal(4), z.literal(-1)]).nullable(),
    endMode: z.enum(RECURRENCE_ENDS),
    untilDate: dateKeySchema.nullable(),
    occurrenceCount: intIn(BOUNDS.occurrenceCount.min, BOUNDS.occurrenceCount.max).nullable(),
  })
  .superRefine((r, ctx) => {
    const fail = (path: string, message: string) => ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: [path], message });
    if (r.interval > BOUNDS.interval[r.freq]) fail("interval", "Intervalle trop grand.");
    if (r.freq === "once") {
      if (r.interval !== 1 || r.endMode !== "count" || r.occurrenceCount !== 1) fail("freq", "Une séance unique se répète une fois.");
      if (r.weekdays !== null || r.monthlyBy !== null || r.monthWeek !== null || r.untilDate !== null) fail("freq", "Champs de récurrence inattendus.");
    }
    if (r.freq === "weekly") {
      const days = r.weekdays ?? [];
      const sorted = [...new Set(days)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      if (days.length === 0) fail("weekdays", "Choisissez au moins un jour.");
      if (sorted.length !== days.length || sorted.some((d, i) => d !== days[i])) fail("weekdays", "Jours en double ou non triés.");
    } else if (r.weekdays !== null) fail("weekdays", "Jours réservés aux séries hebdomadaires.");
    if (r.freq === "monthly") {
      if (r.monthlyBy === null) fail("monthlyBy", "Choisissez le jour du mois.");
      if (r.monthlyBy === "weekday" && r.monthWeek === null) fail("monthWeek", "Choisissez la semaine du mois.");
      if (r.monthlyBy === "day_of_month" && r.monthWeek !== null) fail("monthWeek", "Semaine inattendue.");
    } else if (r.monthlyBy !== null || r.monthWeek !== null) fail("monthlyBy", "Réservé aux séries mensuelles.");
    if (r.endMode === "never" && (r.untilDate !== null || r.occurrenceCount !== null)) fail("endMode", "Fin inattendue.");
    if (r.endMode === "until") {
      if (r.untilDate === null || r.occurrenceCount !== null) fail("untilDate", "Indiquez la date de fin.");
      else if (r.untilDate < r.startDate) fail("untilDate", "La fin précède le début.");
      else {
        const [y, m, d] = r.startDate.split("-").map(Number);
        const max = `${String(y + BOUNDS.untilYears).padStart(4, "0")}-${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d).padStart(2, "0")}`;
        if (r.untilDate > max) fail("untilDate", "La série ne peut pas dépasser 5 ans.");
      }
    }
    if (r.endMode === "count" && (r.occurrenceCount === null || r.untilDate !== null)) fail("occurrenceCount", "Indiquez le nombre de séances.");
  });
export type RecurrenceRuleInput = z.infer<typeof recurrenceRuleSchema>;

export const eventInputSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.title),
  description: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.description).nullable().default(null),
  location: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.location).nullable().default(null),
  color: hexColorSchema.default(DEFAULT_COLOR),
  bookingMode: z.enum(BOOKING_MODES).default("tickets"),
  isPrivate: z.boolean().default(false),
  capacity: intIn(BOUNDS.capacity.min, BOUNDS.capacity.max).default(BOUNDS.capacity.default),
  durationMin: z
    .number()
    .int()
    .min(BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.min)
    .max(BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.max)
    .multipleOf(BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.step)
    .default(BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.default),
  rule: recurrenceRuleSchema,
  overrides: windowOverridesSchema.default({
    bookingHorizonDays: null,
    minNoticeMinutes: null,
    cancelDeadlineHours: null,
  }),
  ticketTypeIds: z.array(idSchema).max(50).default([]),
  questions: z.array(questionInputSchema).max(BOUNDS.questions).default([]),
});
export type EventInput = z.input<typeof eventInputSchema>;
export type EventInputParsed = z.infer<typeof eventInputSchema>;

export const availabilityRuleInputSchema = z
  .object({
    dayOfWeek: intIn(0, 6),
    startMinute: intIn(0, 1439),
    endMinute: intIn(1, 1440),
  })
  .refine((r) => r.startMinute < r.endMinute, { message: "Plage horaire invalide.", path: ["endMinute"] });
export type AvailabilityRuleInput = z.infer<typeof availabilityRuleInputSchema>;
```

Run this in Git Bash. Paste the URL between the single quotes. The command enters the worktree first, and the script refuses to run anywhere else, so it can never overwrite the main checkout's `.env.local`:

```bash
cd /c/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets && URL='<tickets-dev url>' node -e "if(require('path').relative('C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets',process.cwd())!==''){console.error('STOP: run from the tickets worktree');process.exit(1)}const fs=require('fs');const u=process.env.URL;const src=fs.readFileSync('C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.env.local','utf8').split(/\r?\n/).filter(l=>l&&!/^DATABASE_URL=/.test(l)&&!/^SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS=/.test(l));src.push('DATABASE_URL=\"'+u+'\"','SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS='+new URL(u).host);fs.writeFileSync('.env.local',src.join('\n')+'\n');console.log('tickets-dev host: '+new URL(u).host)"
```
Expected: `tickets-dev host: ep-…`. Note this host: every later migration step checks it.

Then run the env host check. Tasks 3, 4 and 5 chain the same check in front of every migration apply, in one `&&` command, so nothing is applied when it stops. Besides comparing the two `.env.local` files, it stops when a `DATABASE_URL` in the process environment points at another host: `scripts/load-env.ts` (dotenv 17, no `override`) would let that variable win over `.env.local`.

```bash
cd /c/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets && node -e "const fs=require('fs');const get=(f,k)=>{const m=new RegExp('^'+k+'=\"?([^\"\\r\\n]*)\"?','m').exec(fs.readFileSync(f,'utf8'));return m?m[1]:''};const wt=new URL(get('.env.local','DATABASE_URL')).host;const dev=new URL(get('C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.env.local','DATABASE_URL')).host;const saved=get('.env.local','SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS');console.log('worktree DATABASE_URL host: '+wt);console.log('SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS: '+saved);if(wt!==saved||wt===dev){console.error('STOP: .env.local is not on tickets-dev');process.exit(1)}if(process.env.DATABASE_URL&&new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL).host!==wt){console.error('STOP: a DATABASE_URL in the environment would override .env.local');process.exit(1)}console.log('OK: tickets-dev')"
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Rewrite `src/lib/admin-params.ts`**

Replace the whole file with:

```ts
// Types only from the schema: keeps drizzle out of client bundles that import these labels.
import type { userRole } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import {
  BOOKING_STATUSES,
  PURCHASE_STATUSES,
  type BookingStatus,
  type PurchaseStatus,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

export type UserRole = (typeof userRole.enumValues)[number];
export type { BookingStatus, PurchaseStatus };

/* ── Users ── */

export const USER_FILTERS = ["all", "client", "pro", "admin", "suspended"] as const;
export type UserFilter = (typeof USER_FILTERS)[number];
export const USER_FILTER_LABELS: Record<UserFilter, string> = {
  all: "Tous",
  client: "Clients",
  pro: "Pros",
  admin: "Admins",
  suspended: "Suspendus",
};
export const USERS_PAGE_SIZE = 25;

export const ROLE_LABELS: Record<UserRole, string> = {
  client: "Client",
  pro: "Pro",
  admin: "Admin",
};

/* ── Activity ── */

export const ACTIVITY_TABS = ["bookings", "purchases"] as const;
export type ActivityTab = (typeof ACTIVITY_TABS)[number];

// The tickets vocabulary (src/lib/tickets.ts) is the single source of these labels.
export { BOOKING_STATUS_LABELS, COVERAGE_LABELS, PURCHASE_STATUS_LABELS } from "@/lib/tickets";

/* ── Traffic ── */

export const TRAFFIC_RANGES = [7, 30, 90] as const;
export type TrafficRange = (typeof TRAFFIC_RANGES)[number];

/* ── Suspension ── */

/** Ban length in seconds; null = no end date. */
export const BAN_DURATIONS = {
  "1d": 86_400,
  "7d": 604_800,
  "30d": 2_592_000,
  forever: null,
} as const;
export type BanDuration = keyof typeof BAN_DURATIONS;
export const BAN_DURATION_LABELS: Record<BanDuration, string> = {
  "1d": "1 jour",
  "7d": "7 jours",
  "30d": "30 jours",
  forever: "Sans limite",
};

/* ── Parsers (search params & action inputs) ── */

function pick<T extends string>(allowed: readonly T[], value: unknown): T | null {
  return typeof value === "string" && (allowed as readonly string[]).includes(value)
    ? (value as T)
    : null;
}

export function parseUserFilter(value: unknown): UserFilter {
  return pick(USER_FILTERS, value) ?? "all";
}

export function parseActivityTab(value: unknown): ActivityTab {
  return pick(ACTIVITY_TABS, value) ?? "bookings";
}

export function parseBookingStatus(value: unknown): BookingStatus | null {
  return pick(BOOKING_STATUSES, value);
}

export function parsePurchaseStatus(value: unknown): PurchaseStatus | null {
  return pick(PURCHASE_STATUSES, value);
}

export function parseBanDuration(value: unknown): BanDuration | null {
  return pick(Object.keys(BAN_DURATIONS) as BanDuration[], value);
}

export function parseRange(value: unknown): TrafficRange {
  const n = typeof value === "string" ? Number(value) : NaN;
  return (TRAFFIC_RANGES as readonly number[]).includes(n) ? (n as TrafficRange) : 30;
}

/** 1-based page number from a search param; anything invalid → 1. */
export function parsePage(value: unknown): number {
  const n = typeof value === "string" ? Number(value) : NaN;
  return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 10_000 ? n : 1;
}

/**
 * Deleting an account needs its email retyped. Case and surrounding spaces are
 * ignored; an empty input never matches.
 */
export function deletionConfirmed(typed: string, email: string): boolean {
  const t = typed.trim().toLowerCase();
  return t.length > 0 && t === email.trim().toLowerCase();
}
```

```ts
import "server-only";
import { and, asc, eq, gte } from "drizzle-orm";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { account, professional, timeOff, user } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { userNotSuspended } from "@/lib/suspension";

/*
 * Tickets & événements, P2: the readers of the dropped booking tables
 * (dashboard, clients, payments, planning, prestations, packs, public booking)
 * are gone. P3 rebuilds the pro readers, P4 the public ones.
 */

export type AvailabilityRow = { dayOfWeek: number; startMinute: number; endMinute: number };

/** The pro's global weekly hours. Stubbed until the new availability_rule table exists (P2 Task 4). */
export async function getProAvailability(proId: string): Promise<AvailabilityRow[]> {
  void proId;
  return [];
}

export function getProTimeOff(proId: string) {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(timeOff)
    .where(and(eq(timeOff.professionalId, proId), gte(timeOff.endAt, new Date())))
    .orderBy(asc(timeOff.startAt));
}

/**
 * A suspended pro's page is offline (null), except where `includeSuspended` is
 * set: the thank-you page, so a customer who paid just before the suspension
 * still sees their confirmation.
 */
export async function getPublicProfile(
  slug: string,
  opts: { includeSuspended?: boolean } = {},
) {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ pro: professional })
    .from(professional)
    .innerJoin(user, eq(user.id, professional.userId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(professional.slug, slug),
        opts.includeSuspended ? undefined : userNotSuspended(),
      ),
    )
    .limit(1);
  const pro = rows[0]?.pro;
  if (!pro) return null;
  return { pro };
}

export async function getGoogleConnection(pro: {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
}) {
  const proRows = await db
    .select({ googleCalendarId: professional.googleCalendarId })
    .from(professional)
    .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id))
    .limit(1);

  const acct = await db
    .select({ accountId: account.accountId })
    .from(account)
    .where(
      and(eq(account.userId, pro.userId), eq(account.providerId, "google")),
    )
    .limit(1);

  const linkedEmail = acct.length
    ? (
        await db
          .select({ email: user.email })
          .from(user)
          .where(eq(user.id, pro.userId))
          .limit(1)
      )[0]?.email ?? null
    : null;

  return {
    configured: env.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ENABLED,
    connected: !!proRows[0]?.googleCalendarId,
    linked: acct.length > 0,
    accountEmail: linkedEmail,
  };
}
```

```ts
"use server";

import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { headers } from "next/headers";

import { isReservedSegment } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { professional, timeOff, user as userTable } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { createSkedulCalendar } from "@/lib/google-calendar";
import { getCurrentProfessional, getCurrentUser } from "@/lib/session";
import { isHexColor, presetColor } from "@/lib/color";
import { buildTimeOffInterval } from "@/lib/timeoff";
import { slugify } from "@/lib/utils";

type Res = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };

// Answer of the planning actions until P3 rebuilds them on the tickets engine.
const COMING_SOON: Res = {
  ok: false,
  error: "Bientôt disponible : cette action revient avec les tickets et événements.",
};

async function requirePro() {
  const pro = await getCurrentProfessional();
  if (!pro) redirect("/onboarding");
  return pro;
}

async function uniqueSlug(base: string): Promise<string> {
  const root = slugify(base) || "pro";
  let candidate = root;
  let n = 1;
  // ensure global uniqueness of professional slug; an app route ("admin",
  // "dashboard"…) counts as taken so a pro page never shadows it
  while (true) {
    if (!isReservedSegment(candidate)) {
      const existing = await db
        .select({ id: professional.id })
        .from(professional)
        .where(eq(professional.slug, candidate))
        .limit(1);
      if (existing.length === 0) return candidate;
    }
    candidate = `${root}-${++n}`;
  }
}

/* ─────────────────────── Onboarding / profile ──────────────────── */

export async function createProfessionalProfile(input: {
  businessName: string;
  headline?: string;
  bio?: string;
  timezone?: string;
}) {
  const user = await getCurrentUser();
  if (!user) redirect("/sign-in");

  const existing = await getCurrentProfessional();
  if (existing) redirect("/dashboard");

  const slug = await uniqueSlug(input.businessName);

  await db.insert(professional).values({
    userId: user.id,
    slug,
    businessName: input.businessName,
    headline: input.headline || null,
    bio: input.bio || null,
    timezone: input.timezone || "Europe/Paris",
  });

  // Promote plain clients to "pro" — never demote an admin.
  await db
    .update(userTable)
    .set({ role: "pro" })
    .where(and(eq(userTable.id, user.id), eq(userTable.role, "client")));

  redirect("/dashboard");
}

export async function updateProfile(input: {
  businessName: string;
  headline?: string;
  bio?: string;
  brandColor?: string;
  timezone?: string;
}) {
  const pro = await requirePro();
  await db
    .update(professional)
    .set({
      businessName: input.businessName,
      headline: input.headline || null,
      bio: input.bio || null,
      brandColor: input.brandColor || pro.brandColor,
      timezone: input.timezone || pro.timezone,
    })
    .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id));
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/settings");
  revalidatePath(`/${pro.slug}`);
}

export async function updateBranding(input: {
  themePreset?: string;
  customColor?: string | null;
  bannerUrl?: string | null;
  headerLayout?: string;
  avatarKind?: string | null;
  avatarStyle?: string | null;
  avatarSeed?: string | null;
  avatarUrl?: string | null;
}) {
  const pro = await requirePro();
  const layout = input.headerLayout === "vitrine" ? "vitrine" : "profil";
  const kind =
    input.avatarKind === "generated" ||
    input.avatarKind === "pack" ||
    input.avatarKind === "upload"
      ? input.avatarKind
      : null;

  // Resolve the active brand color from the chosen preset (or the pro's custom).
  // The custom color persists even while a named preset is active.
  const custom = isHexColor(input.customColor)
    ? (input.customColor as string)
    : pro.customColor;
  const preset = presetColor(input.themePreset);
  const themePreset = input.themePreset === "custom" || !preset ? "custom" : input.themePreset!;
  const brandColor =
    themePreset === "custom" ? (custom ?? pro.brandColor) : (preset as string);

  await db
    .update(professional)
    .set({
      brandColor,
      themePreset,
      customColor: custom ?? null,
      bannerUrl: input.bannerUrl ?? null,
      headerLayout: layout,
      avatarKind: kind,
      avatarStyle: kind === "generated" ? (input.avatarStyle ?? null) : null,
      avatarSeed: kind === "generated" ? (input.avatarSeed ?? null) : null,
      avatarUrl:
        kind === "pack" || kind === "upload" ? (input.avatarUrl ?? null) : null,
    })
    .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id));
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/settings");
  revalidatePath(`/${pro.slug}`);
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Availability ────────────────────── */

/** Stubbed until the new availability_rule table exists (P2 Task 4). */
export async function saveAvailability(
  rules: { dayOfWeek: number; startMinute: number; endMinute: number }[],
): Promise<void> {
  await requirePro();
  void rules;
}

/* ─────────────────────────── Indisponibilités ──────────────────── */

export async function createTimeOff(input: {
  startDate: string;
  endDate: string;
  allDay: boolean;
  startMinute?: number;
  endMinute?: number;
  reason?: string;
}): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }> {
  const pro = await requirePro();

  let interval;
  try {
    interval = buildTimeOffInterval(input, pro.timezone);
  } catch (e) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Entrée invalide.",
    };
  }

  await db.insert(timeOff).values({
    professionalId: pro.id,
    startAt: interval.startAt,
    endAt: interval.endAt,
    allDay: input.allDay,
    reason: input.reason?.trim() || null,
  });

  revalidatePath("/dashboard/unavailability");
  revalidatePath(`/${pro.slug}`);
  return { ok: true };
}

export async function deleteTimeOff(id: string) {
  const pro = await requirePro();
  await db
    .delete(timeOff)
    .where(and(eq(timeOff.id, id), eq(timeOff.professionalId, pro.id)));
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/unavailability");
  revalidatePath(`/${pro.slug}`);
}

export async function fetchManualDaySlots(

  eventTypeId: string;
  startISO: string;

/* ─────────────────────── Google Calendar ───────────────────────── */

/** After the Google OAuth redirect: create the Skedul calendar + store its id. */
export async function finalizeGoogleConnection(): Promise<{
  ok: boolean;
  error?: string;
}> {
  const pro = await getCurrentProfessional();
  if (!pro) return { ok: false, error: "Non authentifié." };
  if (!env.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ENABLED) return { ok: false, error: "Désactivé." };

  // Already connected → idempotent success.
  const existing = await db
    .select({ id: professional.googleCalendarId })
    .from(professional)
    .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id))
    .limit(1);
  if (existing[0]?.id) return { ok: true };

  try {
    const { accessToken } = await auth.api.getAccessToken({
      body: { providerId: "google" },
      headers: await headers(),
    });
    const calendarId = await createSkedulCalendar(accessToken);
    await db
      .update(professional)
      .set({ googleCalendarId: calendarId })
      .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id));
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[google] finalize connection failed:", err);
    return { ok: false, error: "Connexion à Google impossible." };
  }
}

/** Unlink Google and stop syncing (the Skedul calendar is left in place). */
export async function disconnectGoogleCalendar(): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> {
  const pro = await getCurrentProfessional();
  if (!pro) return { ok: false };
  try {
    await auth.api.unlinkAccount({
      body: { providerId: "google" },
      headers: await headers(),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[google] unlink failed:", err);
  }
  await db
    .update(professional)
    .set({ googleCalendarId: null })
    .where(eq(professional.id, pro.id));
  return { ok: true };
}
```

```ts
import "server-only";

import {
  and,
  count,
  desc,
  eq,
  gt,
  gte,
  ilike,
  isNotNull,
  or,
  sql,
  type SQL,
} from "drizzle-orm";
import type { AnyPgColumn } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { account, pageView, professional, session, user } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { ADMIN_TZ } from "@/lib/analytics";
import { stripeMode, type MoneyTotal } from "@/lib/admin-metrics";
import { USERS_PAGE_SIZE, type UserFilter } from "@/lib/admin-params";
import { userIsSuspended } from "@/lib/suspension";

/*
 * Tickets & événements, P2: the booking and pack tables are gone. Revenue,
 * booking counts, pending payments and the activity lists are stubbed with
 * their exported shapes unchanged; P3 rebuilds them on ticket_purchase
 * (src/lib/booking/revenue.ts) and booking.
 */

const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

/**
 * YYYY-MM-DD of a timestamp in ADMIN_TZ. The zone is inlined as a literal
 * (it is a constant) so SELECT and GROUP BY render the exact same SQL.
 */
function dayOf(column: AnyPgColumn) {
  return sql<string>`to_char(${column} at time zone ${sql.raw(`'${ADMIN_TZ}'`)}, 'YYYY-MM-DD')`;
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Overview ───────────────────────────── */

export async function getOverviewStats(now: Date = new Date()) {
  const since7 = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * DAY_MS);
  const since30 = new Date(now.getTime() - 30 * DAY_MS);

  const [users, newUsers, pros, revenue30d] = await Promise.all([
    db.select({ value: count() }).from(user),
    db.select({ value: count() }).from(user).where(gte(user.createdAt, since7)),
    db.select({ value: count() }).from(professional),
    getRevenue({ since: since30 }),
  ]);

  return {
    users: users[0]?.value ?? 0,
    newUsers7d: newUsers[0]?.value ?? 0,
    pros: pros[0]?.value ?? 0,
    activePros30d: 0,
    bookings30d: 0,
    revenue30d,
  };
}

/** Paid purchases summed per currency. Stubbed until P3 (ticket_purchase). */
export async function getRevenue(
  opts: { since?: Date; professionalId?: string } = {},
): Promise<MoneyTotal[]> {
  void opts;
  return [];
}

export function getDailySignups(since: Date) {
  const day = dayOf(user.createdAt);
  return db
    .select({ day, value: count() })
    .from(user)
    .where(gte(user.createdAt, since))
    .groupBy(day);
}

export function getDailyTraffic(since: Date) {
  const day = dayOf(pageView.createdAt);
  return db
    .select({
      day,
      views: count(),
      visitors: sql<string>`count(distinct ${pageView.visitorHash})`,
    })
    .from(pageView)
    .where(gte(pageView.createdAt, since))
    .groupBy(day);
}

export type ActivityEvent = {
  id: string;
  kind: "signup" | "pro" | "booking" | "purchase";
  at: Date;
  /** The account the event belongs to (links to /admin/users/[id]). */
  userId: string;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
};

/** The latest sign-ups and pro pages, merged by date (bookings and purchases return in P3). */
export async function getRecentActivity(limit = 10): Promise<ActivityEvent[]> {
  const [signups, pros] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({ id: user.id, at: user.createdAt, name: user.name, email: user.email })
      .from(user)
      .orderBy(desc(user.createdAt))
      .limit(limit),
    db
      .select({
        id: professional.id,
        at: professional.createdAt,
        userId: professional.userId,
        businessName: professional.businessName,
        slug: professional.slug,
      })
      .from(professional)
      .orderBy(desc(professional.createdAt))
      .limit(limit),
  ]);

  const events: ActivityEvent[] = [
    ...signups.map((r) => ({
      id: `signup-${r.id}`,
      kind: "signup" as const,
      at: r.at,
      userId: r.id,
      title: `Inscription · ${r.name}`,
      detail: r.email,
    })),
    ...pros.map((r) => ({
      id: `pro-${r.id}`,
      kind: "pro" as const,
      at: r.at,
      userId: r.userId,
      title: `Page pro créée · ${r.businessName}`,
      detail: `/${r.slug}`,
    })),
  ];
  return events.sort((a, b) => b.at.getTime() - a.at.getTime()).slice(0, limit);
}

export type AppHealth = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getAppHealth>>;

/** Integration modes and DB latency (pending payments return in P3). */
export async function getAppHealth(now: Date = new Date()) {
  void now;
  const started = performance.now();
  await db.execute(sql`select 1`);
  const dbLatencyMs = Math.round(performance.now() - started);

  return {
    appEnv: env.APP_ENV,
    appUrl: env.APP_URL,
    stripe: stripeMode(env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY, env.STRIPE_ENABLED),
    stripeWebhook: env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_CONFIGURED,
    resend: env.RESEND_ENABLED,
    blob: env.BLOB_ENABLED,
    google: env.GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ENABLED,
    dbLatencyMs,
    pendingBookings: 0,
    pendingPurchases: 0,
  };
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Users ───────────────────────────── */

export async function listUsers(opts: {
  q: string;
  filter: UserFilter;
  page: number;
}) {
  const conditions: (SQL | undefined)[] = [];
  if (opts.q) {
    const pattern = `%${opts.q.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`)}%`;
    conditions.push(
      or(
        ilike(user.name, pattern),
        ilike(user.email, pattern),
        ilike(professional.businessName, pattern),
      ),
    );
  }
  if (opts.filter === "client") conditions.push(eq(user.role, "client"));
  if (opts.filter === "admin") conditions.push(eq(user.role, "admin"));
  if (opts.filter === "pro") conditions.push(isNotNull(professional.id));
  if (opts.filter === "suspended") conditions.push(userIsSuspended());
  const where = and(...conditions);

  const [rows, totals] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({
        id: user.id,
        name: user.name,
        email: user.email,
        role: user.role,
        createdAt: user.createdAt,
        lastLoginAt: user.lastLoginAt,
        banned: user.banned,
        banExpires: user.banExpires,
        slug: professional.slug,
      })
      .from(user)
      .leftJoin(professional, eq(professional.userId, user.id))
      .where(where)
      // id breaks ties so offset paging never repeats or skips a row
      .orderBy(desc(user.createdAt), desc(user.id))
      .limit(USERS_PAGE_SIZE)
      .offset((opts.page - 1) * USERS_PAGE_SIZE),
    db
      .select({ value: count() })
      .from(user)
      .leftJoin(professional, eq(professional.userId, user.id))
      .where(where),
  ]);
  return { rows, total: totals[0]?.value ?? 0 };
}

/** A pro's catalogue and booking counts. Stubbed until P3. */
async function getProStats(professionalId: string, now: Date) {
  void professionalId;
  void now;
  return {
    eventTypes: 0,
    bookings: 0,
    upcoming: 0,
    customers: 0,
    packsSold: 0,
  };
}

export async function getUserDetail(userId: string, now: Date = new Date()) {
  const [found] = await db
    .select({
      id: user.id,
      name: user.name,
      email: user.email,
      image: user.image,
      emailVerified: user.emailVerified,
      role: user.role,
      createdAt: user.createdAt,
      lastLoginAt: user.lastLoginAt,
      banned: user.banned,
      banReason: user.banReason,
      banExpires: user.banExpires,
    })
    .from(user)
    .where(eq(user.id, userId))
    .limit(1);
  if (!found) return null;

  const [proRows, providers, sessions] = await Promise.all([
    db.select().from(professional).where(eq(professional.userId, userId)).limit(1),
    db
      .select({ providerId: account.providerId })
      .from(account)
      .where(eq(account.userId, userId)),
    db
      .select({
        id: session.id,
        createdAt: session.createdAt,
        expiresAt: session.expiresAt,
        ipAddress: session.ipAddress,
        userAgent: session.userAgent,
        impersonatedBy: session.impersonatedBy,
      })
      .from(session)
      .where(and(eq(session.userId, userId), gt(session.expiresAt, now)))
      .orderBy(desc(session.createdAt)),
  ]);
  const pro = proRows[0] ?? null;
  const [stats, revenue, deletion] = await Promise.all([
    pro ? getProStats(pro.id, now) : null,
    pro ? getRevenue({ professionalId: pro.id }) : [],
    getDeletionImpact(userId, pro?.id ?? null),
  ]);

  return {
    user: found,
    pro,
    providers: providers.map((p) => p.providerId),
    sessions,
    stats,
    revenue,
    deletion,
  };
}

/**
 * What deleting this account changes beyond its own pro page: tickets its
 * clients would lose and client records it has with other pros. Stubbed until
 * P3 (src/lib/booking/account.ts deletionImpact).
 */
async function getDeletionImpact(userId: string, professionalId: string | null) {
  void userId;
  void professionalId;
  return {
    creditsOutstanding: 0,
    clientRecordsElsewhere: 0,
  };
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Traffic ───────────────────────────── */

export async function getTrafficReport(since: Date) {
  const day = dayOf(pageView.createdAt);
  const visitors = () => sql<string>`count(distinct ${pageView.visitorHash})`;
  const source = sql<string>`coalesce(${pageView.source}, ${pageView.referrerHost}, 'Direct')`;
  const inRange = gte(pageView.createdAt, since);

  const [daily, devices, pages, pros, sources, countries] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({ day, views: count(), visitors: visitors() })
      .from(pageView)
      .where(inRange)
      .groupBy(day),
    db
      .select({ device: pageView.device, views: count() })
      .from(pageView)
      .where(inRange)
      .groupBy(pageView.device)
      .orderBy(desc(count())),
    db
      .select({ path: pageView.path, views: count(), visitors: visitors() })
      .from(pageView)
      .where(inRange)
      .groupBy(pageView.path)
      .orderBy(desc(count()))
      .limit(10),
    db
      .select({
        userId: professional.userId,
        businessName: professional.businessName,
        slug: professional.slug,
        views: count(),
      })
      .from(pageView)
      .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, pageView.professionalId))
      .where(inRange)
      .groupBy(professional.id)
      .orderBy(desc(count()))
      .limit(10),
    db
      .select({ source, visits: count() })
      .from(pageView)
      .where(and(inRange, eq(pageView.entry, true)))
      .groupBy(source)
      .orderBy(desc(count()))
      .limit(10),
    db
      .select({ country: pageView.country, views: count() })
      .from(pageView)
      .where(inRange)
      .groupBy(pageView.country)
      .orderBy(desc(count()))
      .limit(10),
  ]);
  return { daily, devices, pages, pros, sources, countries };
}
```

`src/app/api/stripe/webhook/route.ts` (whole file):

```ts
import type Stripe from "stripe";

import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { stripe } from "@/lib/stripe";

/**
 * Stripe webhook. Payments are rebuilt on the tickets engine in P4
 * (fulfillPurchase, releasePurchase); until then a verified event is only
 * logged and acknowledged, so Stripe does not retry it.
 */
export async function POST(request: Request) {
  if (!stripe || !env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) {
    return new Response("Stripe not configured", { status: 200 });
  }

  const signature = request.headers.get("stripe-signature");
  if (!signature) return new Response("Missing signature", { status: 400 });

  const payload = await request.text();
  let event: Stripe.Event;
  try {
    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(
      payload,
      signature,
      env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET,
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[stripe] signature verification failed:", err);
    return new Response("Invalid signature", { status: 400 });
  }

  console.warn(`[stripe] ${event.type} ignored: payments are being rebuilt`);
  return new Response("ok", { status: 200 });
}
```

### Task 3: Migration 0010 (drop the old domain)

**Files:**

- Create (generated): `drizzle/0010_domain_drop.sql`, `drizzle/meta/0010_snapshot.json`
