**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Task 2 code, which no longer references `eventType`, `classSession`, `availabilityRule`, `pack`, `customer`, `packPurchase`, `booking`, `question`, `bookingAnswer`, `bookingStatus`, `paymentType` or `purchaseStatus`.
- Produces: `schema.ts` exports
  - `userRole`, `questionType` (`["text","choice","boolean"]`), the support enums;
  - `user`, `session`, `account`, `verification`, `professional`, `timeOff`, `pageView`, `analyticsSalt`, `helpCategory`, `helpArticle`, `supportRequest`, `supportMessage`, `supportAttachment`;
  - `userRelations`, `professionalRelations` (only `user`, `timeOff`), `timeOffRelations`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite `src/lib/db/schema.ts` without the old domain**

Replace the whole file with:

```ts
import { relations } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  boolean,
  date,
  index,
  integer,
  pgEnum,
  pgTable,
  serial,
  text,
  timestamp,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import {
  SUPPORT_AUTHOR_ROLES,
  SUPPORT_MESSAGE_KINDS,
  SUPPORT_RESOLUTIONS,
  SUPPORT_STAGES,
  SUPPORT_TRACKS,
  SUPPORT_TYPES,
} from "../support";

const id = () =>
  text("id")
    .primaryKey()
    .$defaultFn(() => nanoid());

const timestamps = {
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true })
    .defaultNow()
    .notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true })
    .defaultNow()
    .notNull()
    .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
};

/* ───────────────────────────── Enums ───────────────────────────── */

export const userRole = pgEnum("user_role", ["client", "pro", "admin"]);
export const questionType = pgEnum("question_type", ["text", "choice", "boolean"]);
export const supportType = pgEnum("support_type", SUPPORT_TYPES);
export const supportTrack = pgEnum("support_track", SUPPORT_TRACKS);
export const supportStage = pgEnum("support_stage", SUPPORT_STAGES);
export const supportResolution = pgEnum("support_resolution", SUPPORT_RESOLUTIONS);
export const supportAuthorRole = pgEnum("support_author_role", SUPPORT_AUTHOR_ROLES);
export const supportMessageKind = pgEnum("support_message_kind", SUPPORT_MESSAGE_KINDS);

/* ──────────────────────── Better Auth tables ───────────────────── */

export const user = pgTable("user", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean("email_verified").default(false).notNull(),
  image: text("image"),
  role: userRole("role").default("client").notNull(),
  // Better Auth admin plugin (suspension)
  banned: boolean("banned").default(false).notNull(),
  banReason: text("ban_reason"),
  banExpires: timestamp("ban_expires", { withTimezone: true }),
  // stamped on every real sign-in (see databaseHooks in src/lib/auth.ts)
  lastLoginAt: timestamp("last_login_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  ...timestamps,
});

export const session = pgTable(
  "session",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    token: text("token").notNull().unique(),
    ipAddress: text("ip_address"),
    userAgent: text("user_agent"),
    // Better Auth admin plugin: id of the admin behind an impersonation session
    impersonatedBy: text("impersonated_by"),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(t.userId)],
);

export const account = pgTable(
  "account",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    accountId: text("account_id").notNull(),
    providerId: text("provider_id").notNull(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    accessToken: text("access_token"),
    refreshToken: text("refresh_token"),
    idToken: text("id_token"),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("access_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("refresh_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    scope: text("scope"),
    password: text("password"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(t.userId)],
);

export const verification = pgTable(
  "verification",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    identifier: text("identifier").notNull(),
    value: text("value").notNull(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(t.identifier)],
);

/* ─────────────────────────── Domain tables ─────────────────────── */

/** A professional's public business profile (one per pro user). */
export const professional = pgTable(
  "professional",
  {
    id: id(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .unique()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
    businessName: text("business_name").notNull(),
    headline: text("headline"),
    bio: text("bio"),
    avatarUrl: text("avatar_url"),
    avatarKind: text("avatar_kind"),
    avatarStyle: text("avatar_style"),
    avatarSeed: text("avatar_seed"),
    bannerUrl: text("banner_url"),
    brandColor: text("brand_color").default("#6366f1").notNull(),
    themePreset: text("theme_preset").default("violet").notNull(),
    customColor: text("custom_color"),
    headerLayout: text("header_layout").default("profil").notNull(),
    timezone: text("timezone").default("Europe/Paris").notNull(),
    currency: text("currency").default("eur").notNull(),
    stripeAccountId: text("stripe_account_id"),
    googleCalendarId: text("google_calendar_id"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("professional_slug_idx").on(t.slug)],
);

/** A one-off block of unavailability (time off) for a professional. */
export const timeOff = pgTable(
  "time_off",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: text("professional_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => professional.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // instant range; endAt is an EXCLUSIVE bound
    startAt: timestamp("start_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    endAt: timestamp("end_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    allDay: boolean("all_day").default(true).notNull(),
    reason: text("reason"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("time_off_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.startAt)],
);

/** One public page view from the cookieless tracker (see src/app/api/track). Immutable. */
export const pageView = pgTable(
  "page_view",
  {
    id: id(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true })
      .defaultNow()
      .notNull(),
    path: text("path").notNull(),
    // resolved from the slug at visit time, so it survives a slug change
    professionalId: text("professional_id").references(() => professional.id, {
      onDelete: "set null",
    }),
    referrerHost: text("referrer_host"),
    source: text("source"),
    // first view of a visit (external or no referrer)
    entry: boolean("entry").default(false).notNull(),
    device: text("device").notNull(),
    country: text("country"),
    // HMAC of IP + user agent keyed with the day's random salt (analytics_salt) —
    // the IP is never stored
    visitorHash: text("visitor_hash").notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("page_view_created_idx").on(t.createdAt),
    index("page_view_pro_created_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.createdAt),
  ],
);

/**
 * One random salt per Paris day for the visitor hash. Rows older than 2 days are
 * deleted, after which that day's hashes can no longer be linked back to an IP.
 */
export const analyticsSalt = pgTable("analytics_salt", {
  day: text("day").primaryKey(), // YYYY-MM-DD in ADMIN_TZ
  salt: text("salt").notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

/* ─────────────────────────── Help center ──────────────────────── */

export const helpCategory = pgTable("help_category", {
  id: id(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
  // one of HELP_ICONS (src/lib/support.ts)
  icon: text("icon").notNull().default("help"),
  position: integer("position").notNull().default(0),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const helpArticle = pgTable(
  "help_article",
  {
    id: id(),
    categoryId: text("category_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => helpCategory.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    question: text("question").notNull(),
    // plain text: blank line = paragraph, "- " = list item (parseAnswer)
    answer: text("answer").notNull(),
    position: integer("position").notNull().default(0),
    published: boolean("published").notNull().default(false),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("help_article_category_idx").on(t.categoryId, t.position)],
);

export const supportRequest = pgTable(
  "support_request",
  {
    id: id(),
    // human-facing "#42"
    number: serial("number").notNull().unique(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    type: supportType("type").notNull(),
    subject: text("subject").notNull(),
    track: supportTrack("track").notNull().default("support"),
    // always a step of `track` (TRACK_STEPS)
    stage: supportStage("stage").notNull().default("received"),
    // set = the request ended off-track (declined / closed); the bar freezes on `stage`
    resolution: supportResolution("resolution"),
    // estimated delivery, development track only
    eta: date("eta", { mode: "string" }),
    unreadByUser: boolean("unread_by_user").notNull().default(false),
    unreadByAdmin: boolean("unread_by_admin").notNull().default(true),
    lastActivityAt: timestamp("last_activity_at", { withTimezone: true })
      .defaultNow()
      .notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("support_request_user_idx").on(t.userId, t.lastActivityAt),
    index("support_request_unread_idx").on(t.unreadByAdmin),
  ],
);

export const supportMessage = pgTable(
  "support_message",
  {
    id: id(),
    requestId: text("request_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => supportRequest.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // NULL once the author's account is deleted (an admin's messages stay)
    authorId: text("author_id").references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
    authorRole: supportAuthorRole("author_role").notNull(),
    kind: supportMessageKind("kind").notNull().default("message"),
    body: text("body").notNull().default(""),
    // kind = stage_change: the step reached
    stage: supportStage("stage"),
    // kind = resolution: declined / closed, or NULL when the request was reopened
    resolution: supportResolution("resolution"),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [index("support_message_request_idx").on(t.requestId, t.createdAt)],
);

export const supportAttachment = pgTable(
  "support_attachment",
  {
    id: id(),
    messageId: text("message_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => supportMessage.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // private Blob pathname: support/<requesterUserId>/<nanoid>.<ext>
    pathname: text("pathname").notNull(),
    contentType: text("content_type").notNull(),
    size: integer("size").notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [index("support_attachment_message_idx").on(t.messageId)],
);

/* ───────────────────────────── Relations ───────────────────────── */

export const userRelations = relations(user, ({ one }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, {
    fields: [user.id],
    references: [professional.userId],
  }),
}));

export const professionalRelations = relations(professional, ({ one, many }) => ({
  user: one(user, { fields: [professional.userId], references: [user.id] }),
  timeOff: many(timeOff),
}));

export const timeOffRelations = relations(timeOff, ({ one }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, {
    fields: [timeOff.professionalId],
    references: [professional.id],
  }),
}));
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Rewrite `src/lib/db/schema.ts` with the new domain (contract §4.2)**

Replace the whole file with:

```ts
import { relations, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  bigint,
  boolean,
  check,
  date,
  index,
  integer,
  pgEnum,
  pgTable,
  primaryKey,
  serial,
  smallint,
  text,
  timestamp,
  unique,
  uniqueIndex,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import {
  SUPPORT_AUTHOR_ROLES,
  SUPPORT_MESSAGE_KINDS,
  SUPPORT_RESOLUTIONS,
  SUPPORT_STAGES,
  SUPPORT_TRACKS,
  SUPPORT_TYPES,
} from "../support";
import {
  ACTORS,
  BOOKING_COVERAGES,
  BOOKING_MODES,
  BOOKING_STATUSES,
  DEFAULT_COLOR,
  MONTHLY_BY,
  MOVEMENT_KINDS,
  OCCURRENCE_STATUSES,
  PURCHASE_STATUSES,
  QUESTION_TYPES,
  RECURRENCE_ENDS,
  RECURRENCE_FREQS,
  TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS,
  TICKET_STATUSES,
  VALIDITY_UNITS,
} from "../tickets";

const id = () =>
  text("id")
    .primaryKey()
    .$defaultFn(() => nanoid());

const timestamps = {
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true })
    .defaultNow()
    .notNull(),
  updatedAt: timestamp("updated_at", { withTimezone: true })
    .defaultNow()
    .notNull()
    .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
};

/* ───────────────────────────── Enums ───────────────────────────── */

export const userRole = pgEnum("user_role", ["client", "pro", "admin"]);
export const questionType = pgEnum("question_type", QUESTION_TYPES);
export const bookingMode = pgEnum("booking_mode", BOOKING_MODES);
export const recurrenceFreq = pgEnum("recurrence_freq", RECURRENCE_FREQS);
export const monthlyBy = pgEnum("monthly_by", MONTHLY_BY);
export const recurrenceEnd = pgEnum("recurrence_end", RECURRENCE_ENDS);
export const validityUnit = pgEnum("validity_unit", VALIDITY_UNITS);
export const occurrenceStatus = pgEnum("occurrence_status", OCCURRENCE_STATUSES);
export const bookingStatus = pgEnum("booking_status", BOOKING_STATUSES);
export const bookingCoverage = pgEnum("booking_coverage", BOOKING_COVERAGES);
export const actor = pgEnum("actor", ACTORS);
export const purchaseStatus = pgEnum("purchase_status", PURCHASE_STATUSES);
export const ticketStatus = pgEnum("ticket_status", TICKET_STATUSES);
export const ticketCloseReason = pgEnum("ticket_close_reason", TICKET_CLOSE_REASONS);
export const movementKind = pgEnum("movement_kind", MOVEMENT_KINDS);
export const supportType = pgEnum("support_type", SUPPORT_TYPES);
export const supportTrack = pgEnum("support_track", SUPPORT_TRACKS);
export const supportStage = pgEnum("support_stage", SUPPORT_STAGES);
export const supportResolution = pgEnum("support_resolution", SUPPORT_RESOLUTIONS);
export const supportAuthorRole = pgEnum("support_author_role", SUPPORT_AUTHOR_ROLES);
export const supportMessageKind = pgEnum("support_message_kind", SUPPORT_MESSAGE_KINDS);

/* ──────────────────────── Better Auth tables ───────────────────── */

export const user = pgTable("user", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  name: text("name").notNull(),
  email: text("email").notNull().unique(),
  emailVerified: boolean("email_verified").default(false).notNull(),
  image: text("image"),
  role: userRole("role").default("client").notNull(),
  // Better Auth admin plugin (suspension)
  banned: boolean("banned").default(false).notNull(),
  banReason: text("ban_reason"),
  banExpires: timestamp("ban_expires", { withTimezone: true }),
  // stamped on every real sign-in (see databaseHooks in src/lib/auth.ts)
  lastLoginAt: timestamp("last_login_at", { withTimezone: true }),
  ...timestamps,
});

export const session = pgTable(
  "session",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    token: text("token").notNull().unique(),
    ipAddress: text("ip_address"),
    userAgent: text("user_agent"),
    // Better Auth admin plugin: id of the admin behind an impersonation session
    impersonatedBy: text("impersonated_by"),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("session_user_id_idx").on(t.userId)],
);

export const account = pgTable(
  "account",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    accountId: text("account_id").notNull(),
    providerId: text("provider_id").notNull(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    accessToken: text("access_token"),
    refreshToken: text("refresh_token"),
    idToken: text("id_token"),
    accessTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("access_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    refreshTokenExpiresAt: timestamp("refresh_token_expires_at", {
      withTimezone: true,
    }),
    scope: text("scope"),
    password: text("password"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("account_user_id_idx").on(t.userId)],
);

export const verification = pgTable(
  "verification",
  {
    id: text("id").primaryKey(),
    identifier: text("identifier").notNull(),
    value: text("value").notNull(),
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("verification_identifier_idx").on(t.identifier)],
);

/* ─────────────────────────── Domain tables ─────────────────────── */

/** A professional's public business profile (one per pro user). */
export const professional = pgTable(
  "professional",
  {
    id: id(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .unique()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
    businessName: text("business_name").notNull(),
    headline: text("headline"),
    bio: text("bio"),
    avatarUrl: text("avatar_url"),
    avatarKind: text("avatar_kind"),
    avatarStyle: text("avatar_style"),
    avatarSeed: text("avatar_seed"),
    bannerUrl: text("banner_url"),
    brandColor: text("brand_color").default("#6366f1").notNull(),
    themePreset: text("theme_preset").default("violet").notNull(),
    customColor: text("custom_color"),
    headerLayout: text("header_layout").default("profil").notNull(),
    timezone: text("timezone").default("Europe/Paris").notNull(),
    currency: text("currency").default("eur").notNull(),
    stripeAccountId: text("stripe_account_id"),
    googleCalendarId: text("google_calendar_id"),
    // booking settings (spec § 2.13); events and RDV types may override each
    bookingHorizonDays: integer("booking_horizon_days").default(60).notNull(),
    minNoticeMinutes: integer("min_notice_minutes").default(60).notNull(),
    cancelDeadlineHours: integer("cancel_deadline_hours").default(24).notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("professional_slug_idx").on(t.slug),
    check("professional_horizon_chk", sql`booking_horizon_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365`),
    check("professional_notice_chk", sql`min_notice_minutes BETWEEN 0 AND 10080`),
    check("professional_cancel_chk", sql`cancel_deadline_hours BETWEEN 0 AND 720`),
  ],
);

/** `professional_id → professional ON DELETE CASCADE`, on every domain table (R2). */
const proRef = () =>
  text("professional_id")
    .notNull()
    .references(() => professional.id, { onDelete: "cascade" });

/** A one-off block of unavailability (time off) for a professional. */
export const timeOff = pgTable(
  "time_off",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: text("professional_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => professional.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // instant range; endAt is an EXCLUSIVE bound
    startAt: timestamp("start_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    endAt: timestamp("end_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    allDay: boolean("all_day").default(true).notNull(),
    reason: text("reason"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("time_off_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.startAt)],
);

/* ─────────────────────── Tickets & événements ─────────────────────
 * Spec § 6. FK rules: every table cascades DIRECTLY from professional (R2,
 * required for deleteUser); references inside the domain are NO ACTION (R3,
 * drizzle's default) except parent → child compositions, which cascade;
 * references to user and booking_answer → question are SET NULL (R4).
 * Every CHECK is named: mapDbError (src/lib/db/map-db-error.ts) reads names.
 * CHECK bodies are literal SQL (no ${} params) so drizzle-kit writes them as-is.
 */

/** What an event accepts; may be bookable as an RDV libre. */
export const ticketType = pgTable(
  "ticket_type",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    name: text("name").notNull(),
    description: text("description"),
    color: text("color").default(DEFAULT_COLOR).notNull(),
    position: integer("position").default(0).notNull(),
    rdvEnabled: boolean("rdv_enabled").default(false).notNull(),
    rdvFree: boolean("rdv_free").default(false).notNull(),
    rdvDurationMin: integer("rdv_duration_min"),
    rdvBufferBeforeMin: integer("rdv_buffer_before_min").default(0).notNull(),
    rdvBufferAfterMin: integer("rdv_buffer_after_min").default(0).notNull(),
    rdvBookingHorizonDays: integer("rdv_booking_horizon_days"),
    rdvMinNoticeMinutes: integer("rdv_min_notice_minutes"),
    rdvCancelDeadlineHours: integer("rdv_cancel_deadline_hours"),
    archivedAt: timestamp("archived_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("ticket_type_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
    check(
      "ticket_type_rdv_chk",
      sql`NOT rdv_enabled OR (rdv_duration_min IS NOT NULL AND rdv_duration_min BETWEEN 5 AND 480 AND rdv_duration_min % 5 = 0)`,
    ),
    check("ticket_type_rdv_free_chk", sql`NOT rdv_free OR rdv_enabled`),
    check(
      "ticket_type_buffers_chk",
      sql`rdv_buffer_before_min BETWEEN 0 AND 240 AND rdv_buffer_after_min BETWEEN 0 AND 240`,
    ),
    check(
      "ticket_type_overrides_chk",
      sql`(rdv_booking_horizon_days IS NULL OR rdv_booking_horizon_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365) AND (rdv_min_notice_minutes IS NULL OR rdv_min_notice_minutes BETWEEN 0 AND 10080) AND (rdv_cancel_deadline_hours IS NULL OR rdv_cancel_deadline_hours BETWEEN 0 AND 720)`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** N tickets of one type for a price (« À l'unité », « Carnet de 10 »). */
export const ticketOffer = pgTable(
  "ticket_offer",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketType.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    label: text("label"),
    quantity: integer("quantity").notNull(),
    priceCents: integer("price_cents").notNull(),
    validityCount: integer("validity_count"),
    validityUnit: validityUnit("validity_unit"),
    isActive: boolean("is_active").default(true).notNull(),
    position: integer("position").default(0).notNull(),
    archivedAt: timestamp("archived_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("ticket_offer_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
    index("ticket_offer_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    check("ticket_offer_quantity_chk", sql`quantity BETWEEN 1 AND 100`),
    check("ticket_offer_price_chk", sql`price_cents > 0`),
    check("ticket_offer_validity_chk", sql`(validity_count IS NULL) = (validity_unit IS NULL)`),
    check(
      "ticket_offer_validity_count_chk",
      sql`validity_count IS NULL OR validity_count BETWEEN 1 AND 1000`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** Weekly hours for RDV libre: global (ticket_type_id NULL) or per type (replaces the global ones). */
export const availabilityRule = pgTable(
  "availability_rule",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id").references(() => ticketType.id, {
      onDelete: "cascade",
    }),
    // 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday
    dayOfWeek: integer("day_of_week").notNull(),
    // minutes from local midnight; endMinute is exclusive (1440 = midnight)
    startMinute: integer("start_minute").notNull(),
    endMinute: integer("end_minute").notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("availability_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
    index("availability_ticket_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    check("availability_rule_dow_chk", sql`day_of_week BETWEEN 0 AND 6`),
    check(
      "availability_rule_range_chk",
      sql`start_minute >= 0 AND start_minute < end_minute AND end_minute <= 1440`,
    ),
  ],
);

/**
 * A series (one-off or recurring) with its booking mode and accepted types.
 * A « cette séance et les suivantes » edit ends a segment (until_date = d − 1)
 * and starts a new one with the same series_root_id.
 */
export const event = pgTable(
  "event",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    title: text("title").notNull(),
    description: text("description"),
    location: text("location"),
    color: text("color").default(DEFAULT_COLOR).notNull(),
    bookingMode: bookingMode("booking_mode").default("tickets").notNull(),
    isPrivate: boolean("is_private").default(false).notNull(),
    capacity: integer("capacity").notNull(),
    durationMin: integer("duration_min").notNull(),
    // recurrence (spec § 8); dates are local calendar dates in the pro's tz
    startDate: date("start_date", { mode: "string" }).notNull(),
    startMinute: integer("start_minute").notNull(),
    freq: recurrenceFreq("freq").notNull(),
    interval: smallint("interval").default(1).notNull(),
    weekdays: smallint("weekdays").array(),
    monthlyBy: monthlyBy("monthly_by"),
    monthWeek: smallint("month_week"),
    endMode: recurrenceEnd("end_mode").notNull(),
    untilDate: date("until_date", { mode: "string" }),
    occurrenceCount: integer("occurrence_count"),
    // NULL = the pro's setting
    bookingHorizonDays: integer("booking_horizon_days"),
    minNoticeMinutes: integer("min_notice_minutes"),
    cancelDeadlineHours: integer("cancel_deadline_hours"),
    // occurrences exist for every rule date ≤ generated_until
    generatedUntil: date("generated_until", { mode: "string" }).notNull(),
    // lineage, no FK; the engine always sets it (= id on the first segment)
    seriesRootId: text("series_root_id"),
    version: integer("version").default(1).notNull(),
    lastActionId: text("last_action_id"),
    archivedAt: timestamp("archived_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("event_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
    index("event_series_root_idx").on(t.seriesRootId),
    check("event_capacity_chk", sql`capacity BETWEEN 1 AND 500`),
    check("event_duration_chk", sql`duration_min BETWEEN 5 AND 720 AND duration_min % 5 = 0`),
    check("event_start_minute_chk", sql`start_minute BETWEEN 0 AND 1439`),
    check(
      "event_once_chk",
      sql`freq <> 'once' OR ("interval" = 1 AND end_mode = 'count' AND occurrence_count IS NOT NULL AND occurrence_count = 1 AND weekdays IS NULL AND monthly_by IS NULL AND month_week IS NULL AND until_date IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_daily_chk",
      sql`freq <> 'daily' OR ("interval" BETWEEN 1 AND 31 AND weekdays IS NULL AND monthly_by IS NULL AND month_week IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_weekly_chk",
      sql`freq <> 'weekly' OR ("interval" BETWEEN 1 AND 52 AND weekdays IS NOT NULL AND cardinality(weekdays) BETWEEN 1 AND 7 AND weekdays <@ '{0,1,2,3,4,5,6}'::smallint[] AND monthly_by IS NULL AND month_week IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_monthly_chk",
      sql`freq <> 'monthly' OR ("interval" BETWEEN 1 AND 12 AND weekdays IS NULL AND monthly_by IS NOT NULL AND ((monthly_by = 'day_of_month' AND month_week IS NULL) OR (monthly_by = 'weekday' AND month_week IS NOT NULL AND month_week IN (1, 2, 3, 4, -1))))`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_end_never_chk",
      sql`end_mode <> 'never' OR (until_date IS NULL AND occurrence_count IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_end_until_chk",
      sql`end_mode <> 'until' OR (until_date IS NOT NULL AND occurrence_count IS NULL AND until_date >= start_date)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_end_count_chk",
      sql`end_mode <> 'count' OR (occurrence_count IS NOT NULL AND occurrence_count BETWEEN 1 AND 730 AND until_date IS NULL)`,
    ),
    check(
      "event_overrides_chk",
      sql`(booking_horizon_days IS NULL OR booking_horizon_days BETWEEN 1 AND 365) AND (min_notice_minutes IS NULL OR min_notice_minutes BETWEEN 0 AND 10080) AND (cancel_deadline_hours IS NULL OR cancel_deadline_hours BETWEEN 0 AND 720)`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** The ticket types an event in `tickets` mode accepts (read live; empty = nobody). */
export const eventTicketType = pgTable(
  "event_ticket_type",
  {
    eventId: text("event_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => event.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketType.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: "event_ticket_type_pk", columns: [t.eventId, t.ticketTypeId] }),
    index("event_ticket_type_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    index("event_ticket_type_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
  ],
);

/** A registration question, on an event or on an RDV ticket type. */
export const question = pgTable(
  "question",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    eventId: text("event_id").references(() => event.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id").references(() => ticketType.id, {
      onDelete: "cascade",
    }),
    label: text("label").notNull(),
    type: questionType("type").notNull(),
    options: text("options").array(),
    required: boolean("required").default(false).notNull(),
    position: integer("position").default(0).notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    index("question_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
    index("question_event_idx").on(t.eventId),
    index("question_ticket_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    check("question_owner_chk", sql`num_nonnulls(event_id, ticket_type_id) = 1`),
  ],
);

/**
 * A séance of an event (event_id set, one per series_date) or a booked RDV
 * libre (event_id NULL, ticket_type_id set, capacity 1). Owns the seat counter.
 * EXCLUDE occurrence_rdv_excl lives in migration 0012 (drizzle-kit cannot express it).
 */
export const occurrence = pgTable(
  "occurrence",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    eventId: text("event_id").references(() => event.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id").references(() => ticketType.id),
    seriesDate: date("series_date", { mode: "string" }),
    startsAt: timestamp("starts_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    endsAt: timestamp("ends_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    // RDV: widened by the type's buffers; event séance: equal to starts/ends
    blockStart: timestamp("block_start", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    blockEnd: timestamp("block_end", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    capacity: integer("capacity").notNull(),
    seatsTaken: integer("seats_taken").default(0).notNull(),
    status: occurrenceStatus("status").default("scheduled").notNull(),
    locationOverride: text("location_override"),
    timeDetached: boolean("time_detached").default(false).notNull(),
    capacityOverride: boolean("capacity_override").default(false).notNull(),
    changedAt: timestamp("changed_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    cancelledAt: timestamp("cancelled_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    googleEventId: text("google_event_id"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("occurrence_event_series_uq").on(t.eventId, t.seriesDate),
    index("occurrence_pro_starts_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.startsAt),
    index("occurrence_ticket_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    check("occurrence_kind_chk", sql`num_nonnulls(event_id, ticket_type_id) = 1`),
    check("occurrence_series_date_chk", sql`(event_id IS NULL) = (series_date IS NULL)`),
    check("occurrence_seats_chk", sql`seats_taken BETWEEN 0 AND capacity`),
    check("occurrence_capacity_chk", sql`capacity BETWEEN 1 AND 500`),
    check("occurrence_rdv_capacity_chk", sql`event_id IS NOT NULL OR capacity = 1`),
    check(
      "occurrence_time_chk",
      sql`ends_at > starts_at AND block_start <= starts_at AND block_end >= ends_at`,
    ),
    check(
      "occurrence_cancelled_chk",
      sql`(status = 'cancelled') = (cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** A client of one pro, keyed by e-mail; may be linked to a verified account. */
export const customer = pgTable(
  "customer",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    userId: text("user_id").references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
    email: text("email").notNull(),
    name: text("name").notNull(),
    phone: text("phone"),
    // bumping it revokes every personal link (/api/c/<token>)
    linkVersion: integer("link_version").default(1).notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("customer_pro_email_uq").on(t.professionalId, t.email),
    index("customer_user_idx").on(t.userId),
    index("customer_email_idx").on(t.email),
    check("customer_email_chk", sql`email = lower(email) AND email <> ''`),
  ],
);

/** One checkout: the ONLY row that carries money (snapshot of the offer). */
export const ticketPurchase = pgTable(
  "ticket_purchase",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    customerId: text("customer_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => customer.id),
    offerId: text("offer_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketOffer.id),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketType.id),
    label: text("label").notNull(),
    quantity: integer("quantity").notNull(),
    amountCents: integer("amount_cents").notNull(),
    currency: text("currency").notNull(),
    validityCount: integer("validity_count"),
    validityUnit: validityUnit("validity_unit"),
    status: purchaseStatus("status").default("pending").notNull(),
    livemode: boolean("livemode").default(false).notNull(),
    checkoutSessionId: text("checkout_session_id"),
    checkoutUrl: text("checkout_url"),
    checkoutExpiresAt: timestamp("checkout_expires_at", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
    processingAt: timestamp("processing_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    paymentIntentId: text("payment_intent_id"),
    paidAt: timestamp("paid_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    fulfilledActionId: text("fulfilled_action_id"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("ticket_purchase_checkout_session_uq").on(t.checkoutSessionId),
    index("ticket_purchase_pro_status_paid_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.status, t.paidAt),
    index("ticket_purchase_customer_idx").on(t.customerId),
    index("ticket_purchase_status_expires_idx").on(t.status, t.checkoutExpiresAt),
    check("ticket_purchase_quantity_chk", sql`quantity BETWEEN 1 AND 100`),
    check("ticket_purchase_amount_chk", sql`amount_cents > 0`),
    check("ticket_purchase_paid_chk", sql`(status = 'paid') = (paid_at IS NOT NULL)`),
  ],
);

/** 1 client = 1 seat = at most 1 ticket, on a séance or an RDV. */
export const booking = pgTable(
  "booking",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    occurrenceId: text("occurrence_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => occurrence.id),
    customerId: text("customer_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => customer.id),
    status: bookingStatus("status").notNull(),
    coverage: bookingCoverage("coverage").notNull(),
    // buy + book: the purchase paying for this seat
    purchaseId: text("purchase_id").references(() => ticketPurchase.id),
    createdBy: actor("created_by").notNull(),
    holdExpiresAt: timestamp("hold_expires_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    confirmedAt: timestamp("confirmed_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    movedAt: timestamp("moved_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    endedAt: timestamp("ended_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    endedBy: actor("ended_by"),
    endedActionId: text("ended_action_id"),
    cancelReason: text("cancel_reason"),
    customerNotes: text("customer_notes"),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex("booking_active_uq")
      .on(t.occurrenceId, t.customerId)
      .where(sql`status IN ('pending', 'confirmed')`),
    unique("booking_purchase_uq").on(t.purchaseId),
    index("booking_occurrence_status_idx").on(t.occurrenceId, t.status),
    index("booking_customer_idx").on(t.customerId),
    index("booking_ended_action_idx").on(t.endedActionId),
    index("booking_hold_idx").on(t.holdExpiresAt).where(sql`status = 'pending'`),
    check(
      "booking_pending_chk",
      sql`status <> 'pending' OR (hold_expires_at IS NOT NULL AND purchase_id IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
    check("booking_free_chk", sql`coverage <> 'free' OR purchase_id IS NULL`),
    check(
      "booking_ended_chk",
      sql`(status IN ('cancelled', 'expired')) = (ended_at IS NOT NULL)`,
    ),
  ],
);

/** An answer to a question, snapshotted (question_label) onto the booking. */
export const bookingAnswer = pgTable(
  "booking_answer",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    bookingId: text("booking_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => booking.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    questionId: text("question_id").references(() => question.id, {
      onDelete: "set null",
    }),
    questionLabel: text("question_label").notNull(),
    value: text("value").notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("booking_answer_uq").on(t.bookingId, t.questionId),
    index("booking_answer_booking_idx").on(t.bookingId),
  ],
);

/** One ticket held by a client. The balance is a count(*): nothing can drift. */
export const ticket = pgTable(
  "ticket",
  {
    id: id(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    customerId: text("customer_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => customer.id),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketType.id),
    // NULL = offered by the pro
    purchaseId: text("purchase_id").references(() => ticketPurchase.id),
    status: ticketStatus("status").notNull(),
    bookingId: text("booking_id").references(() => booking.id),
    // first instant the ticket is no longer valid (local midnight after its last day)
    expiresAt: timestamp("expires_at", { withTimezone: true }),
    closedReason: ticketCloseReason("closed_reason"),
    closedNote: text("closed_note"),
    statusChangedAt: timestamp("status_changed_at", { withTimezone: true })
      .defaultNow()
      .notNull(),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("ticket_booking_uq").on(t.bookingId),
    index("ticket_customer_status_type_idx").on(t.customerId, t.status, t.ticketTypeId),
    index("ticket_by_purchase_idx").on(t.purchaseId),
    index("ticket_by_type_idx").on(t.ticketTypeId),
    index("ticket_expires_idx").on(t.expiresAt).where(sql`status = 'available'`),
    check("ticket_booked_chk", sql`(status = 'booked') = (booking_id IS NOT NULL)`),
    check("ticket_closed_chk", sql`(status = 'closed') = (closed_reason IS NOT NULL)`),
    check(
      "ticket_retire_paid_chk",
      sql`closed_reason IS DISTINCT FROM 'retire' OR purchase_id IS NULL`,
    ),
  ],
);

/**
 * Append-only ledger (convention, checked by invariant I4; no trigger). Order
 * by `seq`, never created_at. Rows are inserted by set-based CTEs, so `id`
 * has a database default.
 */
export const ticketMovement = pgTable(
  "ticket_movement",
  {
    id: text("id")
      .primaryKey()
      .default(sql`gen_random_uuid()::text`),
    seq: bigint("seq", { mode: "number" }).generatedAlwaysAsIdentity().notNull(),
    professionalId: proRef(),
    customerId: text("customer_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => customer.id),
    ticketId: text("ticket_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticket.id),
    actionId: text("action_id").notNull(),
    kind: movementKind("kind").notNull(),
    fromStatus: ticketStatus("from_status"),
    toStatus: ticketStatus("to_status").notNull(),
    ticketTypeId: text("ticket_type_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => ticketType.id),
    fromTicketTypeId: text("from_ticket_type_id").references(() => ticketType.id),
    bookingId: text("booking_id").references(() => booking.id),
    purchaseId: text("purchase_id").references(() => ticketPurchase.id),
    actor: actor("actor").notNull(),
    actorUserId: text("actor_user_id").references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
    reason: text("reason"),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    unique("ticket_movement_action_ticket_uq").on(t.actionId, t.ticketId),
    index("ticket_movement_customer_seq_idx").on(t.customerId, t.seq),
    index("ticket_movement_ticket_seq_idx").on(t.ticketId, t.seq),
    index("ticket_movement_action_idx").on(t.actionId),
    index("ticket_movement_pro_idx").on(t.professionalId),
  ],
);

/** Application rate limits (src/lib/throttle.ts) and `health:*` counters. No professional_id. */
export const throttle = pgTable("throttle", {
  key: text("key").primaryKey(),
  windowStart: timestamp("window_start", { withTimezone: true }).notNull(),
  hits: integer("hits").notNull(),
});

/** Better Auth `rateLimit` model (rateLimit.storage = "database"). No professional_id. */
export const rateLimit = pgTable("rate_limit", {
  id: text("id").primaryKey(),
  key: text("key").notNull().unique(),
  count: integer("count").notNull(),
  lastRequest: bigint("last_request", { mode: "number" }).notNull(),
});

/** One public page view from the cookieless tracker (see src/app/api/track). Immutable. */
export const pageView = pgTable(
  "page_view",
  {
    id: id(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true })
      .defaultNow()
      .notNull(),
    path: text("path").notNull(),
    // resolved from the slug at visit time, so it survives a slug change
    professionalId: text("professional_id").references(() => professional.id, {
      onDelete: "set null",
    }),
    referrerHost: text("referrer_host"),
    source: text("source"),
    // first view of a visit (external or no referrer)
    entry: boolean("entry").default(false).notNull(),
    device: text("device").notNull(),
    country: text("country"),
    // HMAC of IP + user agent keyed with the day's random salt (analytics_salt) —
    // the IP is never stored
    visitorHash: text("visitor_hash").notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("page_view_created_idx").on(t.createdAt),
    index("page_view_pro_created_idx").on(t.professionalId, t.createdAt),
  ],
);

/**
 * One random salt per Paris day for the visitor hash. Rows older than 2 days are
 * deleted, after which that day's hashes can no longer be linked back to an IP.
 */
export const analyticsSalt = pgTable("analytics_salt", {
  day: text("day").primaryKey(), // YYYY-MM-DD in ADMIN_TZ
  salt: text("salt").notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

/* ─────────────────────────── Help center ──────────────────────── */

export const helpCategory = pgTable("help_category", {
  id: id(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  slug: text("slug").notNull().unique(),
  // one of HELP_ICONS (src/lib/support.ts)
  icon: text("icon").notNull().default("help"),
  position: integer("position").notNull().default(0),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
});

export const helpArticle = pgTable(
  "help_article",
  {
    id: id(),
    categoryId: text("category_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => helpCategory.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    question: text("question").notNull(),
    // plain text: blank line = paragraph, "- " = list item (parseAnswer)
    answer: text("answer").notNull(),
    position: integer("position").notNull().default(0),
    published: boolean("published").notNull().default(false),
    ...timestamps,
  },
  (t) => [index("help_article_category_idx").on(t.categoryId, t.position)],
);

export const supportRequest = pgTable(
  "support_request",
  {
    id: id(),
    // human-facing "#42"
    number: serial("number").notNull().unique(),
    userId: text("user_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    type: supportType("type").notNull(),
    subject: text("subject").notNull(),
    track: supportTrack("track").notNull().default("support"),
    // always a step of `track` (TRACK_STEPS)
    stage: supportStage("stage").notNull().default("received"),
    // set = the request ended off-track (declined / closed); the bar freezes on `stage`
    resolution: supportResolution("resolution"),
    // estimated delivery, development track only
    eta: date("eta", { mode: "string" }),
    unreadByUser: boolean("unread_by_user").notNull().default(false),
    unreadByAdmin: boolean("unread_by_admin").notNull().default(true),
    lastActivityAt: timestamp("last_activity_at", { withTimezone: true })
      .defaultNow()
      .notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index("support_request_user_idx").on(t.userId, t.lastActivityAt),
    index("support_request_unread_idx").on(t.unreadByAdmin),
  ],
);

export const supportMessage = pgTable(
  "support_message",
  {
    id: id(),
    requestId: text("request_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => supportRequest.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // NULL once the author's account is deleted (an admin's messages stay)
    authorId: text("author_id").references(() => user.id, { onDelete: "set null" }),
    authorRole: supportAuthorRole("author_role").notNull(),
    kind: supportMessageKind("kind").notNull().default("message"),
    body: text("body").notNull().default(""),
    // kind = stage_change: the step reached
    stage: supportStage("stage"),
    // kind = resolution: declined / closed, or NULL when the request was reopened
    resolution: supportResolution("resolution"),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [index("support_message_request_idx").on(t.requestId, t.createdAt)],
);

export const supportAttachment = pgTable(
  "support_attachment",
  {
    id: id(),
    messageId: text("message_id")
      .notNull()
      .references(() => supportMessage.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
    // private Blob pathname: support/<requesterUserId>/<nanoid>.<ext>
    pathname: text("pathname").notNull(),
    contentType: text("content_type").notNull(),
    size: integer("size").notNull(),
    createdAt: timestamp("created_at", { withTimezone: true }).defaultNow().notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [index("support_attachment_message_idx").on(t.messageId)],
);

/* ───────────────────────────── Relations ───────────────────────── */

export const userRelations = relations(user, ({ one }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, {
    fields: [user.id],
    references: [professional.userId],
  }),
}));

export const professionalRelations = relations(professional, ({ one, many }) => ({
  user: one(user, { fields: [professional.userId], references: [user.id] }),
  timeOff: many(timeOff),
  ticketTypes: many(ticketType),
  events: many(event),
  customers: many(customer),
  availability: many(availabilityRule),
}));

export const timeOffRelations = relations(timeOff, ({ one }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, {
    fields: [timeOff.professionalId],
    references: [professional.id],
  }),
}));

export const ticketTypeRelations = relations(ticketType, ({ one, many }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, { fields: [ticketType.professionalId], references: [professional.id] }),
  offers: many(ticketOffer),
  eventLinks: many(eventTicketType),
  questions: many(question),
  availabilityRules: many(availabilityRule),
}));
export const ticketOfferRelations = relations(ticketOffer, ({ one }) => ({
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [ticketOffer.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
}));
export const availabilityRuleRelations = relations(availabilityRule, ({ one }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, { fields: [availabilityRule.professionalId], references: [professional.id] }),
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [availabilityRule.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
}));
export const eventRelations = relations(event, ({ one, many }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, { fields: [event.professionalId], references: [professional.id] }),
  ticketTypeLinks: many(eventTicketType),
  questions: many(question),
  occurrences: many(occurrence),
}));
export const eventTicketTypeRelations = relations(eventTicketType, ({ one }) => ({
  event: one(event, { fields: [eventTicketType.eventId], references: [event.id] }),
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [eventTicketType.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
}));
export const questionRelations = relations(question, ({ one, many }) => ({
  event: one(event, { fields: [question.eventId], references: [event.id] }),
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [question.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
  answers: many(bookingAnswer),
}));
export const occurrenceRelations = relations(occurrence, ({ one, many }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, { fields: [occurrence.professionalId], references: [professional.id] }),
  event: one(event, { fields: [occurrence.eventId], references: [event.id] }),
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [occurrence.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
  bookings: many(booking),
}));
export const customerRelations = relations(customer, ({ one, many }) => ({
  professional: one(professional, { fields: [customer.professionalId], references: [professional.id] }),
  user: one(user, { fields: [customer.userId], references: [user.id] }),
  bookings: many(booking),
  tickets: many(ticket),
  purchases: many(ticketPurchase),
}));
export const ticketPurchaseRelations = relations(ticketPurchase, ({ one, many }) => ({
  customer: one(customer, { fields: [ticketPurchase.customerId], references: [customer.id] }),
  offer: one(ticketOffer, { fields: [ticketPurchase.offerId], references: [ticketOffer.id] }),
  tickets: many(ticket),
}));
export const bookingRelations = relations(booking, ({ one, many }) => ({
  occurrence: one(occurrence, { fields: [booking.occurrenceId], references: [occurrence.id] }),
  customer: one(customer, { fields: [booking.customerId], references: [customer.id] }),
  purchase: one(ticketPurchase, { fields: [booking.purchaseId], references: [ticketPurchase.id] }),
  answers: many(bookingAnswer),
}));
export const bookingAnswerRelations = relations(bookingAnswer, ({ one }) => ({
  booking: one(booking, { fields: [bookingAnswer.bookingId], references: [booking.id] }),
  question: one(question, { fields: [bookingAnswer.questionId], references: [question.id] }),
}));
export const ticketRelations = relations(ticket, ({ one, many }) => ({
  customer: one(customer, { fields: [ticket.customerId], references: [customer.id] }),
  ticketType: one(ticketType, { fields: [ticket.ticketTypeId], references: [ticketType.id] }),
  purchase: one(ticketPurchase, { fields: [ticket.purchaseId], references: [ticketPurchase.id] }),
  booking: one(booking, { fields: [ticket.bookingId], references: [booking.id] }),
  movements: many(ticketMovement),
}));
export const ticketMovementRelations = relations(ticketMovement, ({ one }) => ({
  ticket: one(ticket, { fields: [ticketMovement.ticketId], references: [ticket.id] }),
}));
```

```ts
/** The pro's global weekly hours (ticket_type_id NULL); a type's own rules replace them for that type. */
export async function getProAvailability(proId: string): Promise<AvailabilityRow[]> {
  return db
    .select({
      dayOfWeek: availabilityRule.dayOfWeek,
      startMinute: availabilityRule.startMinute,
      endMinute: availabilityRule.endMinute,
    })
    .from(availabilityRule)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(availabilityRule.professionalId, proId),
        isNull(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(asc(availabilityRule.dayOfWeek), asc(availabilityRule.startMinute));
}
```

```ts
/** Replaces the pro's global weekly hours (rules of a ticket type are untouched). */
export async function saveAvailability(
  rules: { dayOfWeek: number; startMinute: number; endMinute: number }[],
): Promise<void> {
  const pro = await requirePro();
  // Rows the DB CHECKs would refuse are dropped, like the old `end > start` filter.
  const valid = rules.flatMap((r) => {
    const parsed = availabilityRuleInputSchema.safeParse(r);
    return parsed.success ? [parsed.data] : [];
  });
  const clear = db
    .delete(availabilityRule)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(availabilityRule.professionalId, pro.id),
        isNull(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId),
      ),
    );
  if (valid.length > 0) {
    // One batch = one transaction: the old hours never vanish without the new ones.
    await db.batch([
      clear,
      db
        .insert(availabilityRule)
        .values(valid.map((r) => ({ ...r, professionalId: pro.id }))),
    ]);
  } else {
    await clear;
  }
  revalidatePath("/dashboard/availability");
  revalidatePath(`/${pro.slug}`);
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { DB_SKIP, sameHost, testEmail, testIp } from "./db";

const BRANCH_A = "postgresql://neondb_owner:secret@ep-alpha-pooler.c-2.us-west-2.aws.neon.tech/neondb?sslmode=require";

test("sameHost compares host and port, not user, password, database or params", () => {
  assert.equal(
    sameHost(BRANCH_A, "postgresql://other:pw@ep-alpha-pooler.c-2.us-west-2.aws.neon.tech/otherdb"),
    true,
  );
  assert.equal(
    sameHost(BRANCH_A, "postgresql://neondb_owner:secret@ep-beta-pooler.c-2.us-west-2.aws.neon.tech/neondb?sslmode=require"),
    false,
  );
  assert.equal(sameHost("postgresql://u:p@localhost:5432/a", "postgresql://u:p@localhost:5433/a"), false);
});

test("sameHost throws on a malformed URL instead of answering", () => {
  assert.throws(() => sameHost("not a url", BRANCH_A));
});

test("DB_SKIP gives the reason when DATABASE_URL_TEST is unset", () => {
  if (process.env.DATABASE_URL_TEST) assert.equal(DB_SKIP, false);
  else assert.equal(DB_SKIP, "DATABASE_URL_TEST is not set (.env.test)");
});

test("testEmail is unique and lower-case", () => {
  const a = testEmail("Pro");
  assert.match(a, /^t-pro-[a-z0-9_-]{8}@skedul\.test$/);
  assert.notEqual(a, testEmail("Pro"));
});

test("testIp stays in the 198.18.0.0/15 benchmark range", () => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
    const parts = testIp().split(".").map(Number);
    assert.equal(parts.length, 4);
    assert.equal(parts[0], 198);
    assert.ok(parts[1] === 18 || parts[1] === 19, String(parts[1]));
    assert.ok(parts.every((p) => Number.isInteger(p) && p >= 0 && p <= 255));
  }
});
```

```ts
/**
 * Schema smoke tests on tickets-test: the manual SQL of migration 0012
 * (btree_gist, occurrence_rdv_excl, skedul_assert) and rule R2 (deleting a
 * pro's user removes every domain row, through NO ACTION references).
 */
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { pgErrorInfo, skedulCode } from "../lib/db/pg-error";
import * as schema from "../lib/db/schema";
import { addMinutes, createTestPro, dbTest, deleteTestUser, testEmail } from "./db";

async function failure(p: Promise<unknown>): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    await p;
  } catch (err) {
    return err;
  }
  assert.fail("expected the statement to fail");
}

// Fixed instants far in the future: no dependency on the clock or on DST.
const MON_0900 = new Date("2030-01-07T09:00:00.000Z");

dbTest("migration 0012 installed btree_gist and skedul_assert", async ({ db }) => {
  const ext = await db.execute<{ extname: string }>(
    sql`select extname from pg_extension where extname = 'btree_gist'`,
  );
  assert.equal(ext.rows.length, 1);
  const excl = await db.execute<{ conname: string }>(
    sql`select conname from pg_constraint where conname = 'occurrence_rdv_excl' and contype = 'x'`,
  );
  assert.equal(excl.rows.length, 1);
  const ok = await db.execute<{ ok: boolean }>(sql`select skedul_assert(true, 'unused') as ok`);
  assert.equal(ok.rows[0].ok, true);
});

dbTest("skedul_assert raises P0001 skedul:<code> and rolls its batch back", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = nanoid();
  const err = await failure(
    db.batch([
      db.insert(schema.ticketType).values({ id: typeId, professionalId: pro.proId, name: "Rollback" }),
      db.execute(sql`select skedul_assert(false, 'stale')`),
    ]),
  );
  assert.equal(pgErrorInfo(err).code, "P0001");
  assert.equal(skedulCode(err), "stale");
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: schema.ticketType.id })
    .from(schema.ticketType)
    .where(eq(schema.ticketType.id, typeId));
  assert.equal(rows.length, 0, "the insert before the assert was rolled back");

  // A scalar subquery on a missing row is NULL: IS NOT TRUE makes it raise too.
  const nullErr = await failure(db.execute(sql`select skedul_assert((select true where false), 'not_found')`));
  assert.equal(skedulCode(nullErr), "not_found");
});

dbTest("occurrence_rdv_excl refuses overlapping scheduled RDVs of one pro", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.ticketType).values({
    id: typeId,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    name: "Cours particulier",
    rdvEnabled: true,
    rdvDurationMin: 60,
  });
  const rdv = (start: Date, minutes: number) => ({
    id: nanoid(),
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId: typeId,
    startsAt: start,
    endsAt: addMinutes(start, minutes),
    blockStart: start,
    blockEnd: addMinutes(start, minutes),
    capacity: 1,
    seatsTaken: 1,
  });
  const first = rdv(MON_0900, 60);
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values(first);

  const overlap = await failure(db.batch([db.insert(schema.occurrence).values(rdv(addMinutes(MON_0900, 30), 60))]));
  assert.equal(pgErrorInfo(overlap).code, "23P01");
  assert.equal(pgErrorInfo(overlap).constraint, "occurrence_rdv_excl");

  // [) ranges: an RDV starting when the first one ends is allowed.
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values(rdv(addMinutes(MON_0900, 60), 60));

  // An event séance is outside the EXCLUDE (the engine guards it, E4).
  const eventId = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.event).values({
    id: eventId,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    title: "Atelier",
    capacity: 10,
    durationMin: 60,
    startDate: "2030-01-07",
    startMinute: 600,
    freq: "once",
    endMode: "count",
    occurrenceCount: 1,
    generatedUntil: "2030-01-07",
    seriesRootId: eventId,
  });
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values({
    id: nanoid(),
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    eventId,
    seriesDate: "2030-01-07",
    startsAt: MON_0900,
    endsAt: addMinutes(MON_0900, 60),
    blockStart: MON_0900,
    blockEnd: addMinutes(MON_0900, 60),
    capacity: 10,
  });

  // A cancelled RDV frees its slot.
  await db
    .update(schema.occurrence)
    .set({ status: "cancelled", cancelledAt: new Date(), seatsTaken: 0 })
    .where(eq(schema.occurrence.id, first.id));
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values(rdv(MON_0900, 30));

  // These hand-made RDVs have no booking: remove them so I1–I6 hold for the pro.
  await db.delete(schema.occurrence).where(eq(schema.occurrence.professionalId, pro.proId));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the harness**

Create `src/test/db.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * DB test harness (spec § 14). Run with:
 *   ./node_modules/.bin/tsx --env-file-if-exists=.env.test --test --test-concurrency=1 'src/**\/*.db.test.ts'
 * Skips when DATABASE_URL_TEST is unset; refuses to run when DATABASE_URL and
 * DATABASE_URL_TEST point at different hosts; builds the client only after
 * those guards. Each dbTest creates its own pro and deletes its user at the end
 * (the deleteUser cascade, rule R2), after checking I1–I6 for that pro.
 */
import test, { type TestContext } from "node:test";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import type { NeonHttpDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/neon-http";

import type * as schemaModule from "../lib/db/schema";

/** Same type as `Db` in src/lib/db/atomic.ts (Task 14); declared here because the harness lands first. */
export type Db = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schemaModule>;

export const DB_SKIP: string | false = process.env.DATABASE_URL_TEST
  ? false
  : "DATABASE_URL_TEST is not set (.env.test)";

/** Pure guard, unit-tested in src/test/db-guard.test.ts. */
export function sameHost(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  return new URL(a).host === new URL(b).host;
}

let dbPromise: Promise<Db> | null = null;

export function getTestDb(): Promise<Db> {
  dbPromise ??= (async () => {
    const testUrl = process.env.DATABASE_URL_TEST;
    const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
    if (!testUrl) throw new Error("DATABASE_URL_TEST is not set");
    if (!url || !sameHost(url, testUrl)) {
      throw new Error("Refusing to run: DATABASE_URL and DATABASE_URL_TEST point at different hosts.");
    }
    const { neon } = await import("@neondatabase/serverless");
    const { drizzle } = await import("drizzle-orm/neon-http");
    const schema = await import("../lib/db/schema");
    return drizzle({ client: neon(testUrl), schema });
  })();
  return dbPromise;
}

export type TestPro = { userId: string; proId: string; slug: string; tz: string; currency: string };

/** A pro (user role pro, e-mail verified) with default booking settings, Europe/Paris, eur. */
export async function createTestPro(db: Db, opts: { tz?: string } = {}): Promise<TestPro> {
  const schema = await import("../lib/db/schema");
  const tag = nanoid(10).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "x");
  const userId = nanoid();
  const proId = nanoid();
  const tz = opts.tz ?? "Europe/Paris";
  await db.insert(schema.user).values({
    id: userId,
    name: `Pro ${tag}`,
    email: `pro-${tag}@skedul.test`,
    emailVerified: true,
    role: "pro",
  });
  await db.insert(schema.professional).values({
    id: proId,
    userId,
    slug: `t-${tag}`,
    businessName: `Test ${tag}`,
    timezone: tz,
    currency: "eur",
  });
  return { userId, proId, slug: `t-${tag}`, tz, currency: "eur" };
}

/** The deleteUser path: one DELETE FROM "user" (everything cascades from professional). */
export async function deleteTestUser(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const { sql } = await import("drizzle-orm");
  await db.execute(sql`delete from "user" where id = ${userId}`);
}

export type DbHarness = { db: Db; pro: TestPro };

/**
 * node:test wrapper: skip without DATABASE_URL_TEST; fresh pro per test;
 * after `fn`: checkInvariants({ proId }) must be [] (wired by Task 15), then
 * the user is deleted (even when fn threw).
 */
export function dbTest(
  name: string,
  fn: (h: DbHarness, t: TestContext) => Promise<void>,
  opts: { timeout?: number; invariants?: boolean } = {},
): void {
  test(name, { skip: DB_SKIP, timeout: opts.timeout ?? 120_000 }, async (t) => {
    const db = await getTestDb();
    const pro = await createTestPro(db);
    try {
      await fn({ db, pro }, t);
      // Task 15 inserts here:
      // if (opts.invariants !== false) {
      //   const { checkInvariants } = await import("../lib/booking/invariants");
      //   assert.deepEqual(await checkInvariants(db, { proId: pro.proId }), [], "invariants I1–I6");
      // }
    } finally {
      await deleteTestUser(db, pro.userId);
    }
  });
}

/** Settles every promise; the caller counts outcomes (concurrency tests). */
export function inParallel<T>(fns: readonly (() => Promise<T>)[]): Promise<PromiseSettledResult<T>[]> {
  return Promise.allSettled(fns.map((f) => f()));
}

export const addMinutes = (d: Date, n: number): Date => new Date(d.getTime() + n * 60_000);
export const addDaysMs = (d: Date, n: number): Date => new Date(d.getTime() + n * 86_400_000);

/** Unique lower-case test address (never collides across runs: rate limits and throttle rows persist). */
export function testEmail(tag = "c"): string {
  return `t-${tag}-${nanoid(8)}@skedul.test`.toLowerCase();
}

/** A random IP in 198.18.0.0/15 (benchmark range): one Better Auth rate-limit bucket per call. */
export function testIp(): string {
  const n = Math.floor(Math.random() * 131_072);
  return `198.${18 + (n >> 16)}.${(n >> 8) & 255}.${n & 255}`;
}
```

<!-- G2 -->

## Contract deviations (G2)

1. **`src/lib/throttle.ts` does not import `Db` from `@/lib/db/atomic`.** Task 14 creates `atomic.ts`, and Task 13 runs before it. If Task 13 imported a module that does not exist yet, `tsc` would fail and the task would not end green. So `throttle.ts` declares a local `type ThrottleDb = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schema>`. This is the same instantiation as atomic's `Db`, so callers that pass an atomic `Db` (the auth hook in Task 32, the P4 actions) still typecheck. `throttle.db.test.ts` names the type as `Parameters<typeof consumeThrottle>[0]` for the same reason. Every exported function has the contract signature, with `db: ThrottleDb` in place of `db: Db`.
2. **`src/lib/recurrence.ts` adds two exports, `expandWalk` and `WalkResult`.** `expandDates(rule, from, to)` is defined as `expandWalk(rule, from, to).dates`. The extra export makes the « loop steps ≤ 431 » and `EXPAND_MAX_DATES` caps testable (contract §5.3). Nothing else changes.
3. **`recurrence.ts` throws `RangeError` on a rule it cannot walk.** That means: interval < 1, weekly without weekdays, monthly without `monthlyBy`, weekday-monthly without `monthWeek`, `count` without `occurrenceCount`, `until` without `untilDate`. The DB CHECKs already refuse all of these. Throwing removes any risk of an infinite loop if a rule is built wrongly in JS. The contract does not cover this case.
4. **`describeRecurrence` adds the year to the « jusqu'au » date when `untilDate` falls in a different year from `startDate`** (« , jusqu'au 30/06/2027 »). Within the same year the output is the contract's « , jusqu'au 30/06 ». Without the year, a series running into the next year has an ambiguous label.

**Clarifications** (the contract is silent; these are not deviations):
- **Scope.** The contract's task breakdown puts Tasks 7–13 in G2. The vocabulary and zod module (`src/lib/tickets.ts`) is Task 1 in G1; this group only uses it.
- **`SeriesEditPlan.remainingCount`** is `remainingCount(oldRule, d)`, per spec § 8 « Coupure et compteur ».
- **`describeSeriesEdit` wording** for the parts the contract does not spell out:
  - « N séances gardées à part » (orphans kept);
  - « N séances vides annulées ». N is `summary.cancelledEmpty`, which counts only the cancelEmpty rows that were still scheduled. An already-cancelled row whose date disappears still goes in the `cancelEmpty` bucket (spec § 8, contract §5.4), but the edit changes nothing for it, so the preview does not count it. This matches `summary.modified`, which also skips cancelled rows;
  - « N séances supprimées »;
  - « N séances ajoutées »;
  - « Aucun changement sur les séances » when nothing changes;
  - « (N tickets rendus) » appears only when N > 0.
- **`describeRecurrence` forms:** « le 1er » for day 1 of the month, and a singular « , 1 séance ».
- **`fitsAvailability`:** an RDV that ends exactly at the next local midnight counts as minute 1440. An RDV that crosses midnight never fits.
- **`onlineBookability`** returns "past" when `startsAt ≤ now`, as the contract says. With a notice of 0, this differs from the SQL `starts_at >= now` for exactly one instant, which is harmless.
- **DB tests on `throttle`.** Throttle rows have no `professional_id`, so the deleteUser cascade does not clean them up. The tests use unique keys and delete them in `finally`. The health test restores the previous value of `health:orphan_payments`.

**How the code was checked.** I ran every module and pure test below in a scratch copy of `src/lib`, using the contract's `tickets.ts`, `schema.ts` and `src/test/db.ts` and the repo's `node_modules`. Results: `tsx --test` passed (89 pure tests), `tsc --noEmit` was clean, and `eslint` was clean. The four `throttle.db.test.ts` tests passed `tsc` and `eslint`, but they did not run: no DB write was allowed while writing this plan.

All commands run from `C:/Users/Matheus/Documents/Codage/skedul/.claude/worktrees/tickets` in Git Bash. Never run `pnpm`.

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import {
  addDays,
  addMonthsClamped,
  compareKeys,
  dayNumber,
  dayOfWeek,
  daysInMonth,
  diffDays,
  formatDayMonth,
  formatDayMonthYear,
  fromDayNumber,
  instantAt,
  makeKey,
  maxKey,
  minKey,
  mondayOf,
  monthIndex,
  nthWeekdayOfMonth,
  parseKey,
  todayKey,
} from "./civil-date";

const TZ = "Europe/Paris";

test("parseKey reads a key and rejects malformed or impossible dates", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseKey("2026-10-12"), { y: 2026, m: 10, d: 12 });
  assert.deepEqual(parseKey("2028-02-29"), { y: 2028, m: 2, d: 29 });
  for (const bad of ["2026-13-01", "2026-00-10", "2026-02-29", "2026-04-31", "26-1-1", "2026-1-01", "", "2026-10-12T00:00"]) {
    assert.throws(() => parseKey(bad), /Invalid date key/, bad);
  }
});

test("makeKey normalises overflowing months and days", () => {
  assert.equal(makeKey(2026, 2, 31), "2026-03-03");
  assert.equal(makeKey(2026, 13, 1), "2027-01-01");
  assert.equal(makeKey(2026, 0, 1), "2025-12-01");
  assert.equal(makeKey(2026, 3, 0), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(makeKey(2026, 10, 12), "2026-10-12");
});

test("dayNumber and fromDayNumber round-trip around the epoch", () => {
  assert.equal(dayNumber("1970-01-01"), 0);
  assert.equal(dayNumber("1969-12-31"), -1);
  assert.equal(dayNumber("1970-01-02"), 1);
  for (const key of ["1969-12-31", "2000-02-29", "2026-03-29", "2026-10-25", "2099-12-31"]) {
    assert.equal(fromDayNumber(dayNumber(key)), key);
  }
});

test("addDays and diffDays cross months, years and leap days", () => {
  assert.equal(addDays("2026-12-31", 1), "2027-01-01");
  assert.equal(addDays("2026-03-01", -1), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(addDays("2028-03-01", -1), "2028-02-29");
  assert.equal(addDays("2026-10-25", 1), "2026-10-26");
  assert.equal(diffDays("2026-10-12", "2026-10-26"), 14);
  assert.equal(diffDays("2026-10-26", "2026-10-12"), -14);
  assert.equal(diffDays("2026-01-01", "2027-01-01"), 365);
  assert.equal(diffDays("2028-01-01", "2029-01-01"), 366);
});

test("dayOfWeek: 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday", () => {
  assert.equal(dayOfWeek("2026-10-14"), 3);
  assert.equal(dayOfWeek("2026-11-01"), 0);
  assert.equal(dayOfWeek("2026-10-30"), 5);
  assert.equal(dayOfWeek("2026-10-12"), 1);
  assert.equal(dayOfWeek("2026-10-17"), 6);
});

test("mondayOf returns the Monday of the ISO week (Sunday belongs to the week before)", () => {
  assert.equal(mondayOf("2026-10-12"), "2026-10-12");
  assert.equal(mondayOf("2026-10-14"), "2026-10-12");
  assert.equal(mondayOf("2026-10-18"), "2026-10-12");
  assert.equal(mondayOf("2026-11-01"), "2026-10-26");
  assert.equal(mondayOf("2027-01-01"), "2026-12-28");
});

test("daysInMonth and monthIndex", () => {
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2026, 2), 28);
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2028, 2), 29);
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2100, 2), 28);
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2000, 2), 29);
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2026, 4), 30);
  assert.equal(daysInMonth(2026, 12), 31);
  assert.equal(monthIndex("2026-01-15"), 2026 * 12);
  assert.equal(monthIndex("2026-12-01"), 2026 * 12 + 11);
});

test("addMonthsClamped clamps to the target month's last day", () => {
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-01-31", 1), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2028-01-31", 1), "2028-02-29");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-03-31", -1), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-08-31", 1), "2026-09-30");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-12-15", 1), "2027-01-15");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-01-15", -1), "2025-12-15");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-02-28", 12), "2027-02-28");
  assert.equal(addMonthsClamped("2026-10-12", 0), "2026-10-12");
});

test("nthWeekdayOfMonth: 1st..4th and last", () => {
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 11, 0, 1), "2026-11-01");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 12, 5, -1), "2026-12-25");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 10, 5, -1), "2026-10-30");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 10, 2, 2), "2026-10-13");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 2, 6, 4), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 12, 0, 1), "2026-12-06");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2027, 1, 0, 1), "2027-01-03");
  assert.equal(nthWeekdayOfMonth(2026, 3, 0, -1), "2026-03-29");
});

test("compareKeys, minKey, maxKey", () => {
  assert.equal(compareKeys("2026-10-12", "2026-10-13"), -1);
  assert.equal(compareKeys("2026-10-13", "2026-10-12"), 1);
  assert.equal(compareKeys("2026-10-12", "2026-10-12"), 0);
  assert.equal(minKey("2026-10-12", "2025-12-31"), "2025-12-31");
  assert.equal(maxKey("2026-10-12", "2025-12-31"), "2026-10-12");
});

test("todayKey is the local date in tz, not the UTC date", () => {
  // 22:30Z on 24 Oct 2026 is 00:30 on the 25th in Paris (summer time).
  assert.equal(todayKey(new Date("2026-10-24T22:30:00Z"), TZ), "2026-10-25");
  assert.equal(todayKey(new Date("2026-10-24T21:59:59Z"), TZ), "2026-10-24");
  assert.equal(todayKey(new Date("2026-10-24T22:30:00Z"), "UTC"), "2026-10-24");
});

test("instantAt follows Paris DST: shift forward, first instant, local midnight", () => {
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-03-29", 150, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-03-29T01:30:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-10-25", 150, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-10-25T00:30:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-03-01", 0, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-02-28T23:00:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-10-26", 0, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-10-25T23:00:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-10-12", 1140, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-10-12T17:00:00.000Z");
  // 1440 = the next local midnight
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-02-28", 1440, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-02-28T23:00:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(instantAt("2026-10-25", 1440, TZ).toISOString(), "2026-10-25T23:00:00.000Z");
});

test("formatDayMonth and formatDayMonthYear", () => {
  assert.equal(formatDayMonth("2026-10-12"), "12/10");
  assert.equal(formatDayMonth("2026-06-30"), "30/06");
  assert.equal(formatDayMonthYear("2026-10-12"), "12/10/2026");
  assert.equal(formatDayMonthYear("2027-01-03"), "03/01/2027");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/civil-date.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Civil (calendar) dates as "YYYY-MM-DD" keys (spec § 8). All arithmetic is on
 * whole days since 1970-01-01, through Date.UTC, which has no time zone and no
 * daylight saving time. Spec § 4: iterate by local date, never by adding
 * n × 86 400 000 ms to an instant. Instants are built only at the edge, by
 * `instantAt` (TZDate through datetime.instantFromWallClock).
 */
import {
  addDaysToKey,
  dayKeyInTz,
  dayOfWeekForKey,
  instantFromWallClock,
} from "@/lib/datetime";

/** A local calendar date, "YYYY-MM-DD". */
export type DateKey = string;

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const KEY_PATTERN = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/;

const pad2 = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

function isLeapYear(y: number): boolean {
  return (y % 4 === 0 && y % 100 !== 0) || y % 400 === 0;
}

export function daysInMonth(y: number, m: number): number {
  if (m === 2) return isLeapYear(y) ? 29 : 28;
  return m === 4 || m === 6 || m === 9 || m === 11 ? 30 : 31;
}

/** Parse a key; throws on a malformed one ("2026-13-01", "2026-02-30", "26-1-1"). */
export function parseKey(key: DateKey): { y: number; m: number; d: number } {
  const match = KEY_PATTERN.exec(key);
  if (!match) throw new Error(`Invalid date key: ${key}`);
  const y = Number(match[1]);
  const m = Number(match[2]);
  const d = Number(match[3]);
  if (m < 1 || m > 12 || d < 1 || d > daysInMonth(y, m)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid date key: ${key}`);
  }
  return { y, m, d };
}

/** Days since 1970-01-01 (may be negative). */
export function dayNumber(key: DateKey): number {
  const { y, m, d } = parseKey(key);
  return Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d) / DAY_MS;
}

export function fromDayNumber(n: number): DateKey {
  return new Date(n * DAY_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** Build a normalised key: month and day overflow roll over (2026-02-31 → 2026-03-03). */
export function makeKey(y: number, m: number, d: number): DateKey {
  return fromDayNumber(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d) / DAY_MS);
}

/** = datetime.addDaysToKey. */
export function addDays(key: DateKey, n: number): DateKey {
  return addDaysToKey(key, n);
}

/** b − a in days. */
export function diffDays(a: DateKey, b: DateKey): number {
  return dayNumber(b) - dayNumber(a);
}

/** 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday (= datetime.dayOfWeekForKey). */
export function dayOfWeek(key: DateKey): number {
  return dayOfWeekForKey(key);
}

/** Monday of the ISO week containing key. */
export function mondayOf(key: DateKey): DateKey {
  return addDays(key, -((dayOfWeek(key) + 6) % 7));
}

/** y*12 + (m−1). */
export function monthIndex(key: DateKey): number {
  const { y, m } = parseKey(key);
  return y * 12 + (m - 1);
}

/** Add months, clamping the day to the target month's length (2026-01-31 + 1 → 2026-02-28). */
export function addMonthsClamped(key: DateKey, n: number): DateKey {
  const { d } = parseKey(key);
  const target = monthIndex(key) + n;
  const y = Math.floor(target / 12);
  const m = target - y * 12 + 1;
  return `${String(y).padStart(4, "0")}-${pad2(m)}-${pad2(Math.min(d, daysInMonth(y, m)))}`;
}

/** nth (1..4) or last (−1) `weekday` (0..6) of month m (1..12) of year y. */
export function nthWeekdayOfMonth(
  y: number,
  m: number,
  weekday: number,
  nth: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | -1,
): DateKey {
  if (nth === -1) {
    const last = makeKey(y, m, daysInMonth(y, m));
    return addDays(last, -((dayOfWeek(last) - weekday + 7) % 7));
  }
  const first = makeKey(y, m, 1);
  return addDays(first, ((weekday - dayOfWeek(first) + 7) % 7) + (nth - 1) * 7);
}

export function compareKeys(a: DateKey, b: DateKey): number {
  return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;
}

export function minKey(a: DateKey, b: DateKey): DateKey {
  return a <= b ? a : b;
}

export function maxKey(a: DateKey, b: DateKey): DateKey {
  return a >= b ? a : b;
}

/** Local calendar date of `instant` in tz (= datetime.dayKeyInTz). */
export function todayKey(instant: Date, tz: string): DateKey {
  return dayKeyInTz(instant, tz);
}

/**
 * Instant of `minute` (0..1440) on local date `key` in tz (= datetime.
 * instantFromWallClock). A nonexistent spring-forward time is shifted forward
 * (02:30 on 2026-03-29 → 03:30), a repeated fall-back time takes the first
 * instant (02:30 on 2026-10-25 → 00:30Z). 1440 is the next local midnight.
 */
export function instantAt(key: DateKey, minute: number, tz: string): Date {
  const { y, m, d } = parseKey(key);
  return instantFromWallClock(y, m, d, minute, tz);
}

/** "12/10" (dd/MM). */
export function formatDayMonth(key: DateKey): string {
  const { m, d } = parseKey(key);
  return `${pad2(d)}/${pad2(m)}`;
}

/** "12/10/2026". */
export function formatDayMonthYear(key: DateKey): string {
  const { y } = parseKey(key);
  return `${formatDayMonth(key)}/${String(y).padStart(4, "0")}`;
}
```
