  ```ts
  export type RecurrenceRule = {
    startDate: DateKey; startMinute: number; durationMin: number; freq: RecurrenceFreq; interval: number;
    weekdays: number[] | null; monthlyBy: MonthlyBy | null; monthWeek: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | -1 | null;
    endMode: RecurrenceEnd; untilDate: DateKey | null; occurrenceCount: number | null;
  };
  export type ExpandedDate = { seriesDate: DateKey; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date };
  export const EXPAND_MAX_DATES: 1000;
  export type WalkResult = { dates: DateKey[]; steps: number; produced: number; capped: boolean }; // addition (deviation 2)
  export function expandWalk(rule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): WalkResult; // addition (deviation 2)
  export function expandDates(rule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): DateKey[];
  export function expand(rule: RecurrenceRule, tz: string, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): ExpandedDate[];
  export function lastSeriesDate(rule: RecurrenceRule): DateKey | null;
  export function countDatesBefore(rule: RecurrenceRule, d: DateKey): number;
  export function remainingCount(rule: RecurrenceRule, d: DateKey): number | null;
  export function describeRecurrence(rule: RecurrenceRule): string;
  export function monthlyDayWarning(rule: RecurrenceRule): string | null;
  export function dstNotes(rule: RecurrenceRule, tz: string, dates: readonly DateKey[]): { seriesDate: DateKey; kind: "shifted" | "ambiguous" }[];
  ```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import {
  addDays,
  dayOfWeek,
  diffDays,
  mondayOf,
  monthIndex,
  nthWeekdayOfMonth,
  parseKey,
  type DateKey,
} from "./civil-date";
import {
  countDatesBefore,
  describeRecurrence,
  dstNotes,
  expand,
  expandDates,
  expandWalk,
  EXPAND_MAX_DATES,
  lastSeriesDate,
  monthlyDayWarning,
  remainingCount,
  type RecurrenceRule,
} from "./recurrence";

const TZ = "Europe/Paris";

function rule(over: Partial<RecurrenceRule>): RecurrenceRule {
  return {
    startDate: "2026-10-14",
    startMinute: 1140,
    durationMin: 60,
    freq: "weekly",
    interval: 1,
    weekdays: [1, 4],
    monthlyBy: null,
    monthWeek: null,
    endMode: "never",
    untilDate: null,
    occurrenceCount: null,
    ...over,
  };
}

    "2026-10-19",
    "2026-10-22",
    "2026-10-26",
    "2026-10-29",
  ]);
});

test("weekly every 2 weeks counts weeks from the Monday of the start week", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(rule({ interval: 2 }), "2026-10-01", "2026-11-12"), [
    "2026-10-15",
    "2026-10-26",
    "2026-10-29",
    "2026-11-09",
    "2026-11-12",
  ]);
});

    "2026-10-18",
    "2026-10-19",
    "2026-10-25",
    "2026-10-26",
  ]);
});

test("monthly on the 31st skips the months without a 31st", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2026-01-31", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "day_of_month" });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-01-01", "2026-12-31"), [
    "2026-01-31",
    "2026-03-31",
    "2026-05-31",
    "2026-07-31",
    "2026-08-31",
    "2026-10-31",
    "2026-12-31",
  ]);
  assert.equal(monthlyDayWarning(r), "Certains mois n'ont pas de 31 : ils sont sautés.");
  assert.equal(monthlyDayWarning(rule({ startDate: "2026-01-15", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "day_of_month" })), null);
  assert.equal(monthlyDayWarning(rule({})), null);
});

test("monthly 1st Sunday and last Friday", () => {
  const firstSunday = rule({ startDate: "2026-11-01", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(firstSunday, "2026-11-01", "2027-01-31"), ["2026-11-01", "2026-12-06", "2027-01-03"]);
  const lastFriday = rule({ startDate: "2026-10-30", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: -1 });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(lastFriday, "2026-10-01", "2026-12-31"), ["2026-10-30", "2026-11-27", "2026-12-25"]);
});

test("monthly every 3 months jumps to the aligned month containing fromDate", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2026-01-15", freq: "monthly", interval: 3, weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "day_of_month" });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-05-01", "2027-02-01"), ["2026-07-15", "2026-10-15", "2027-01-15"]);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-04-16", "2026-07-15"), ["2026-07-15"]);
});

test("a monthly nth weekday before startDate in its first month is skipped", () => {
  // 2026-11-15 is a Sunday; the 1st Sunday of November (the 1st) precedes it.
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2026-11-15", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-11-01", "2027-01-31"), ["2026-12-06", "2027-01-03"]);
});

test("daily every 3 days, and once", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2026-10-12", freq: "daily", interval: 3, weekdays: null });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-10-13", "2026-10-22"), ["2026-10-15", "2026-10-18", "2026-10-21"]);
  const once = rule({ startDate: "2026-10-12", freq: "once", weekdays: null, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(once, "2026-10-01", "2026-10-31"), ["2026-10-12"]);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(once, "2026-10-13", "2026-10-31"), []);
});

test("until is inclusive and nothing is produced before startDate", () => {
  const r = rule({ endMode: "until", untilDate: "2026-10-22" });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-01-01", "2027-01-01"), ["2026-10-15", "2026-10-19", "2026-10-22"]);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-10-23", "2027-01-01"), []);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(rule({}), "2026-10-20", "2026-10-19"), []);
});

test("count walks from startDate: 5 weekly dates in total, even from after the 3rd", () => {
  const r = rule({ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 5 });
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-01-01", "2027-12-31"), [
    "2026-10-15",
    "2026-10-19",
    "2026-10-22",
    "2026-10-26",
    "2026-10-29",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-10-23", "2027-12-31"), ["2026-10-26", "2026-10-29"]);
  assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, "2026-10-30", "2027-12-31"), []);
});

test("a daily series started 4 years ago still produces 400 dates in a few steps", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2022-10-12", freq: "daily", weekdays: null });
  const walked = expandWalk(r, "2026-10-12", addDays("2026-10-12", 399));
  assert.equal(walked.dates.length, 400);
  assert.equal(walked.dates[0], "2026-10-12");
  assert.equal(walked.dates[399], addDays("2026-10-12", 399));
  assert.ok(walked.steps <= 431, `steps ${walked.steps}`);
  assert.equal(walked.capped, false);
});

test("an old weekly every-3-weeks series jumps without walking from its start", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2016-01-06", interval: 3, weekdays: [3] });
  const walked = expandWalk(r, "2026-10-12", "2026-12-31");
  assert.ok(walked.steps <= diffDays("2026-10-12", "2026-12-31") + 31, `steps ${walked.steps}`);
  for (const d of walked.dates) {
    assert.equal(dayOfWeek(d), 3);
    assert.equal((diffDays(mondayOf("2016-01-06"), mondayOf(d)) / 7) % 3, 0);
  }
  assert.ok(walked.dates.length >= 3);
});

test("EXPAND_MAX_DATES caps one call", () => {
  const r = rule({ startDate: "2020-01-01", freq: "daily", weekdays: null });
  const walked = expandWalk(r, "2020-01-01", "2026-01-01");
  assert.equal(EXPAND_MAX_DATES, 1000);
  assert.equal(walked.dates.length, EXPAND_MAX_DATES);
  assert.equal(walked.dates[999], addDays("2020-01-01", 999));
  assert.equal(walked.capped, true);
});

test("a rule that could loop forever is refused", () => {
  assert.throws(() => expandDates(rule({ interval: 0 }), "2026-10-12", "2026-12-31"), RangeError);
  assert.throws(() => expandDates(rule({ weekdays: [] }), "2026-10-12", "2026-12-31"), RangeError);
  assert.throws(() => expandDates(rule({ freq: "monthly", weekdays: null }), "2026-10-12", "2026-12-31"), RangeError);
});

/** Brute-force reference: test every local day from startDate. */
function reference(r: RecurrenceRule, from: DateKey, to: DateKey): DateKey[] {
  const out: DateKey[] = [];
  let produced = 0;
  for (let d = r.startDate; d <= to; d = addDays(d, 1)) {
    if (r.endMode === "until" && r.untilDate && d > r.untilDate) break;
    let match: boolean;
    if (r.freq === "once") match = d === r.startDate;
    else if (r.freq === "daily") match = diffDays(r.startDate, d) % r.interval === 0;
    else if (r.freq === "weekly") {
      const week = diffDays(mondayOf(r.startDate), mondayOf(d)) / 7;
      match = week % r.interval === 0 && (r.weekdays ?? []).includes(dayOfWeek(d));
    } else {
      const aligned = (monthIndex(d) - monthIndex(r.startDate)) % r.interval === 0;
      const { y, m, d: day } = parseKey(d);
      match =
        aligned &&
        (r.monthlyBy === "day_of_month"
          ? day === parseKey(r.startDate).d
          : d === nthWeekdayOfMonth(y, m, dayOfWeek(r.startDate), r.monthWeek ?? 1));
    }
    if (!match) continue;
    produced++;
    if (d >= from) out.push(d);
    if (r.endMode === "count" && produced >= (r.occurrenceCount ?? 0)) break;
  }
  return out;
}

test("the jumping expander matches a day-by-day reference on 300 seeded random rules", () => {
  // Park-Miller: deterministic, exact in double precision.
  let seed = 42;
  const rand = (n: number) => {
    seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647;
    return seed % n;
  };
  for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
    const startDate = addDays("2024-01-01", rand(900));
    const freq = (["daily", "weekly", "monthly"] as const)[rand(3)];
    const interval = 1 + rand(freq === "daily" ? 31 : freq === "weekly" ? 6 : 12);
    const weekdays = freq === "weekly" ? [...new Set([rand(7), rand(7), rand(7)])].sort((a, b) => a - b) : null;
    const monthlyBy = freq === "monthly" ? (rand(2) === 0 ? "day_of_month" : "weekday") : null;
    const monthWeek = monthlyBy === "weekday" ? ([1, 2, 3, 4, -1] as const)[rand(5)] : null;
    const endMode = (["never", "until", "count"] as const)[rand(3)];
    const r = rule({
      startDate,
      freq,
      interval,
      weekdays,
      monthlyBy,
      monthWeek,
      endMode,
      untilDate: endMode === "until" ? addDays(startDate, rand(800)) : null,
      occurrenceCount: endMode === "count" ? 1 + rand(40) : null,
    });
    const from = addDays(startDate, rand(700) - 100);
    const to = addDays(from, rand(500));
    assert.deepEqual(expandDates(r, from, to), reference(r, from, to), JSON.stringify({ r, from, to }));
    const d = addDays(startDate, rand(600));
    assert.equal(countDatesBefore(r, d), reference(r, r.startDate, addDays(d, -1)).length, JSON.stringify({ r, d }));
  }
});

test("expand builds instants on the wall clock, DST included", () => {
  const sundays = rule({ startDate: "2026-03-22", startMinute: 150, weekdays: [0] });
  const out = expand(sundays, TZ, "2026-03-22", "2026-03-29");
  assert.deepEqual(
    out.map((o) => [o.seriesDate, o.startsAt.toISOString(), o.endsAt.toISOString()]),
    [
      ["2026-03-22", "2026-03-22T01:30:00.000Z", "2026-03-22T02:30:00.000Z"],
      // 02:30 does not exist on 29 March: shifted forward to 03:30 (01:30Z)
      ["2026-03-29", "2026-03-29T01:30:00.000Z", "2026-03-29T02:30:00.000Z"],
    ],
  );
  const autumn = expand(rule({ startDate: "2026-10-25", startMinute: 150, weekdays: [0] }), TZ, "2026-10-25", "2026-10-25");
  // 02:30 happens twice on 25 October: the first instant (summer time) wins.
  assert.equal(autumn[0].startsAt.toISOString(), "2026-10-25T00:30:00.000Z");
});

test("dstNotes flags the shifted and the ambiguous dates only", () => {
  const sundays = rule({ startDate: "2026-03-22", startMinute: 150, weekdays: [0] });
  const dates = ["2026-03-22", "2026-03-29", "2026-10-18", "2026-10-25"];
  assert.deepEqual(dstNotes(sundays, TZ, dates), [
    { seriesDate: "2026-03-29", kind: "shifted" },
    { seriesDate: "2026-10-25", kind: "ambiguous" },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(dstNotes(rule({ startMinute: 1140, weekdays: [0] }), TZ, dates), []);
});

test("lastSeriesDate", () => {
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(rule({ startDate: "2026-10-12", freq: "once", weekdays: null, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1 })), "2026-10-12");
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(rule({ endMode: "until", untilDate: "2027-06-30" })), "2027-06-30");
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(rule({ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 3 })), "2026-10-22");
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(rule({})), null);
  const lastFriday = rule({ startDate: "2026-10-30", freq: "monthly", weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: -1, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 3 });
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(lastFriday), "2026-12-25");
  const yearly730 = rule({ startDate: "2026-01-31", freq: "monthly", interval: 12, weekdays: null, monthlyBy: "day_of_month", endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 730 });
  assert.equal(lastSeriesDate(yearly730), "2755-01-31");
});

test("countDatesBefore and remainingCount (« Il reste K séances »)", () => {
  assert.equal(countDatesBefore(rule({}), "2026-10-22"), 2);
  assert.equal(countDatesBefore(rule({}), "2026-10-14"), 0);
  assert.equal(countDatesBefore(rule({ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 2 }), "2026-11-30"), 2);
  assert.equal(countDatesBefore(rule({ startDate: "2022-10-12", freq: "daily", weekdays: null }), "2026-10-12"), diffDays("2022-10-12", "2026-10-12"));
  const ten = rule({ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 10 });
  assert.equal(remainingCount(ten, "2026-10-22"), 8);
  assert.equal(remainingCount(ten, "2026-10-01"), 10);
  assert.equal(remainingCount(ten, "2027-06-01"), 0);
  assert.equal(remainingCount(rule({}), "2026-10-22"), null);
});

test("describeRecurrence produces the French labels of spec § 8", () => {
  const monthly = { freq: "monthly" as const, weekdays: null };
  const cases: [Partial<RecurrenceRule>, string][] = [
    [{ startDate: "2026-10-12", freq: "once", weekdays: null, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1 }, "Le 12/10/2026"],
    [{ freq: "daily", weekdays: null }, "Tous les jours"],
    [{ freq: "daily", interval: 3, weekdays: null }, "Tous les 3 jours"],
    [{ weekdays: [1, 4] }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi et le jeudi"],
    [{ interval: 2, weekdays: [6] }, "Toutes les 2 semaines le samedi"],
    [{ weekdays: [1, 3, 5] }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi, le mercredi et le vendredi"],
    [{ weekdays: [0, 1] }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi et le dimanche"],
    [{ ...monthly, startDate: "2026-10-15", monthlyBy: "day_of_month" }, "Tous les mois le 15"],
    [{ ...monthly, startDate: "2026-10-01", monthlyBy: "day_of_month" }, "Tous les mois le 1er"],
    [{ ...monthly, startDate: "2026-11-01", monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: 1 }, "Tous les mois le 1er dimanche"],
    [{ ...monthly, startDate: "2026-10-13", monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: 2 }, "Tous les mois le 2e mardi"],
    [{ ...monthly, startDate: "2026-10-30", monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: -1 }, "Tous les mois le dernier vendredi"],
    [{ ...monthly, interval: 3, startDate: "2026-10-15", monthlyBy: "weekday", monthWeek: 3 }, "Tous les 3 mois le 3e jeudi"],
    [{ interval: 2, weekdays: [1, 3], endMode: "until", untilDate: "2026-06-30", startDate: "2026-01-05" }, "Toutes les 2 semaines le lundi et le mercredi, jusqu'au 30/06"],
    [{ endMode: "until", untilDate: "2027-06-30" }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi et le jeudi, jusqu'au 30/06/2027"],
    [{ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 10 }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi et le jeudi, 10 séances"],
    [{ endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 1 }, "Toutes les semaines le lundi et le jeudi, 1 séance"],
  ];
  for (const [over, label] of cases) {
    assert.equal(describeRecurrence(rule(over)), label);
    assert.ok(!describeRecurrence(rule(over)).includes("—"));
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/recurrence.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Recurrence expander (spec § 8): pure, on civil dates (src/lib/civil-date.ts).
 * A rule is the recurrence columns of `event` + its duration. Every supported
 * rule produces at most one date per local day, so `series_date` identifies an
 * occurrence (spec § 7). Instants come last: startsAt = the wall-clock start on
 * that local date (DST: shifted forward / first instant), endsAt = startsAt +
 * duration in elapsed milliseconds.
 */
import {
  addDays,
  dayOfWeek,
  diffDays,
  formatDayMonth,
  formatDayMonthYear,
  instantAt,
  makeKey,
  maxKey,
  minKey,
  mondayOf,
  monthIndex,
  nthWeekdayOfMonth,
  parseKey,
  todayKey,
  type DateKey,
} from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { wallClockMinutes } from "@/lib/datetime";
import type { MonthlyBy, RecurrenceEnd, RecurrenceFreq } from "@/lib/tickets";

/** The recurrence columns of `event` + duration: everything the expander needs. */
export type RecurrenceRule = {
  startDate: DateKey;
  startMinute: number;
  durationMin: number;
  freq: RecurrenceFreq;
  interval: number;
  weekdays: number[] | null;
  monthlyBy: MonthlyBy | null;
  monthWeek: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | -1 | null;
  endMode: RecurrenceEnd;
  untilDate: DateKey | null;
  occurrenceCount: number | null;
};

export type ExpandedDate = { seriesDate: DateKey; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date };

/** At most this many dates are produced by one call. */
export const EXPAND_MAX_DATES = 1000;

/** Far enough for any count rule (≤ 730 dates, monthly interval ≤ 12). */
const FAR_FUTURE: DateKey = "9999-12-31";

/** Throws on a rule the DB CHECKs would refuse in a way that could loop forever. */
function assertWalkable(rule: RecurrenceRule): void {
  parseKey(rule.startDate);
  if (!Number.isInteger(rule.interval) || rule.interval < 1) {
    throw new RangeError(`Invalid recurrence interval: ${rule.interval}`);
  }
  if (rule.freq === "weekly" && (!rule.weekdays || rule.weekdays.length === 0)) {
    throw new RangeError("A weekly rule needs at least one weekday");
  }
  if (rule.freq === "monthly" && rule.monthlyBy === null) {
    throw new RangeError("A monthly rule needs monthlyBy");
  }
  if (rule.monthlyBy === "weekday" && rule.monthWeek === null) {
    throw new RangeError("A monthly weekday rule needs monthWeek");
  }
  if (rule.endMode === "count" && (rule.occurrenceCount === null || rule.occurrenceCount < 1)) {
    throw new RangeError("A count rule needs occurrenceCount ≥ 1");
  }
  if (rule.endMode === "until" && rule.untilDate === null) {
    throw new RangeError("An until rule needs untilDate");
  }
}

export type WalkResult = {
  /** Dates in [lower, upper], ascending. */
  dates: DateKey[];
  /** Candidate dates examined (one per period for daily and monthly, one per weekday for weekly). */
  steps: number;
  /** Rule dates ≥ startDate seen by the walk (count mode: the running count). */
  produced: number;
  /** A cap (EXPAND_MAX_DATES or the step cap) stopped the walk early. */
  capped: boolean;
};

/**
 * The single walker behind expandDates and countDatesBefore. `fromRuleStart`
 * walks from startDate (count mode, or counting); otherwise never/until jump
 * arithmetically to the first aligned period containing `lower`. Candidates
 * come out in ascending order, so the walk stops at the first one > upper.
 */
function walk(
  rule: RecurrenceRule,
  lower: DateKey,
  upper: DateKey,
  opts: { fromRuleStart: boolean; maxDates: number },
): WalkResult {
  const result: WalkResult = { dates: [], steps: 0, produced: 0, capped: false };
  const limit = rule.endMode === "count" ? (rule.occurrenceCount ?? 0) : Infinity;
  const jumpFrom = opts.fromRuleStart ? rule.startDate : maxKey(lower, rule.startDate);

  let walkStart: DateKey;
  let next: () => DateKey | null | "skip";

  if (rule.freq === "once") {
    let done = false;
    walkStart = rule.startDate;
    next = () => {
      if (done) return null;
      done = true;
      return rule.startDate;
    };
  } else if (rule.freq === "daily") {
    let k = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(diffDays(rule.startDate, jumpFrom) / rule.interval));
    walkStart = addDays(rule.startDate, k * rule.interval);
    next = () => addDays(rule.startDate, k++ * rule.interval);
  } else if (rule.freq === "weekly") {
    // Monday-based weeks from the week of startDate; inside a week Mon..Sun.
    const offsets = [...new Set(rule.weekdays ?? [])]
      .map((d) => (d + 6) % 7)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const firstMonday = mondayOf(rule.startDate);
    const weeksToJump = Math.max(0, Math.floor(diffDays(firstMonday, mondayOf(jumpFrom)) / 7));
    let week = weeksToJump - (weeksToJump % rule.interval);
    let i = 0;
    walkStart = addDays(firstMonday, week * 7);
    next = () => {
      const date = addDays(firstMonday, week * 7 + offsets[i]);
      i++;
      if (i === offsets.length) {
        i = 0;
        week += rule.interval;
      }
      return date;
    };
  } else {
    const firstMonth = monthIndex(rule.startDate);
    const monthsToJump = Math.max(0, monthIndex(jumpFrom) - firstMonth);
    let k = monthsToJump - (monthsToJump % rule.interval);
    const { d: startDay } = parseKey(rule.startDate);
    const weekday = dayOfWeek(rule.startDate);
    const monthStart = (n: number) => {
      const idx = firstMonth + n;
      return { y: Math.floor(idx / 12), m: (idx % 12) + 1 };
    };
    const first = monthStart(k);
    walkStart = makeKey(first.y, first.m, 1);
    next = () => {
      const { y, m } = monthStart(k);
      k += rule.interval;
      if (makeKey(y, m, 1) > upper) return null;
      if (rule.monthlyBy === "day_of_month") {
        const date = makeKey(y, m, startDay);
        // RFC 5545: a month without that day is skipped (no 31 in April).
        return parseKey(date).d === startDay ? date : "skip";
      }
      return nthWeekdayOfMonth(y, m, weekday, rule.monthWeek ?? 1);
    };
  }

  const maxSteps = Math.max(0, diffDays(walkStart, upper)) + 31;
  for (;;) {
    if (result.steps >= maxSteps) {
      result.capped = true;
      break;
    }
    const date = next();
    if (date === null) break;
    result.steps++;
    if (date === "skip") continue;
    if (date > upper) break;
    if (date < rule.startDate) continue;
    result.produced++;
    if (date >= lower) {
      if (result.dates.length >= opts.maxDates) {
        result.capped = true;
        break;
      }
      result.dates.push(date);
    }
    if (result.produced >= limit) break;
  }
  return result;
}

function bounds(rule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): { lower: DateKey; upper: DateKey } {
  const lower = maxKey(fromDate, rule.startDate);
  const upper = rule.endMode === "until" && rule.untilDate ? minKey(toDate, rule.untilDate) : toDate;
  return { lower, upper };
}

/**
 * The rule's dates in [fromDate, toDate] with the walk statistics; exported for
 * the cap tests. Prefer expandDates.
 */
export function expandWalk(rule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): WalkResult {
  assertWalkable(rule);
  parseKey(fromDate);
  parseKey(toDate);
  const { lower, upper } = bounds(rule, fromDate, toDate);
  if (lower > upper) return { dates: [], steps: 0, produced: 0, capped: false };
  return walk(rule, lower, upper, {
    fromRuleStart: rule.endMode === "count",
    maxDates: EXPAND_MAX_DATES,
  });
}

/**
 * The rule's local dates in [fromDate, toDate] (inclusive), ascending. Never a
 * date before startDate; until inclusive; count walks from startDate and
 * counts every produced date. never/until jump arithmetically to the first
 * aligned period containing fromDate. Caps: EXPAND_MAX_DATES produced, and
 * (days from walk start to toDate) + 31 loop steps.
 */
export function expandDates(rule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): DateKey[] {
  return expandWalk(rule, fromDate, toDate).dates;
}

/** expandDates + instants: startsAt = instantAt(date, startMinute, tz), endsAt = startsAt + durationMin. */
export function expand(rule: RecurrenceRule, tz: string, fromDate: DateKey, toDate: DateKey): ExpandedDate[] {
  return expandDates(rule, fromDate, toDate).map((seriesDate) => {
    const startsAt = instantAt(seriesDate, rule.startMinute, tz);
    return { seriesDate, startsAt, endsAt: new Date(startsAt.getTime() + rule.durationMin * 60_000) };
  });
}

/** Last date the rule can produce: once → startDate; until → untilDate (upper bound); count → exact; never → null. */
export function lastSeriesDate(rule: RecurrenceRule): DateKey | null {
  assertWalkable(rule);
  if (rule.freq === "once") return rule.startDate;
  if (rule.endMode === "never") return null;
  if (rule.endMode === "until") return rule.untilDate;
  const { dates } = walk(rule, rule.startDate, FAR_FUTURE, {
    fromRuleStart: true,
    maxDates: EXPAND_MAX_DATES,
  });
  return dates.length > 0 ? dates[dates.length - 1] : null;
}

/** How many dates the rule produces strictly before d (from startDate; capped by occurrenceCount). */
export function countDatesBefore(rule: RecurrenceRule, d: DateKey): number {
  assertWalkable(rule);
  parseKey(d);
  if (d <= rule.startDate) return 0;
  const { lower, upper } = bounds(rule, rule.startDate, addDays(d, -1));
  if (lower > upper) return 0;
  return walk(rule, lower, upper, { fromRuleStart: true, maxDates: Infinity }).produced;
}

/** count mode: occurrenceCount − countDatesBefore(rule, d) (≥ 0); null otherwise (« Il reste K séances »). */
export function remainingCount(rule: RecurrenceRule, d: DateKey): number | null {
  if (rule.endMode !== "count" || rule.occurrenceCount === null) return null;
  return Math.max(0, rule.occurrenceCount - countDatesBefore(rule, d));
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Labels (FR) ───────────────────────────── */

const WEEKDAY_NAMES = ["dimanche", "lundi", "mardi", "mercredi", "jeudi", "vendredi", "samedi"];

/** « le lundi, le mercredi et le vendredi » */
function joinFr(items: readonly string[]): string {
  if (items.length <= 1) return items.join("");
  return `${items.slice(0, -1).join(", ")} et ${items[items.length - 1]}`;
}

function ordinalFr(n: number): string {
  return n === 1 ? "1er" : `${n}e`;
}

function every(interval: number, unit: "jour" | "semaine" | "mois"): string {
  if (unit === "semaine") return interval === 1 ? "Toutes les semaines" : `Toutes les ${interval} semaines`;
  if (unit === "jour") return interval === 1 ? "Tous les jours" : `Tous les ${interval} jours`;
  return interval === 1 ? "Tous les mois" : `Tous les ${interval} mois`;
}

/** « Toutes les 2 semaines le lundi et le mercredi, jusqu'au 30/06 » (spec § 8). */
export function describeRecurrence(rule: RecurrenceRule): string {
  if (rule.freq === "once") return `Le ${formatDayMonthYear(rule.startDate)}`;

  let base: string;
  if (rule.freq === "daily") {
    base = every(rule.interval, "jour");
  } else if (rule.freq === "weekly") {
    const days = [...new Set(rule.weekdays ?? [])].sort((a, b) => ((a + 6) % 7) - ((b + 6) % 7));
    base = `${every(rule.interval, "semaine")} ${joinFr(days.map((d) => `le ${WEEKDAY_NAMES[d]}`))}`;
  } else if (rule.monthlyBy === "day_of_month") {
    const { d } = parseKey(rule.startDate);
    base = `${every(rule.interval, "mois")} le ${d === 1 ? "1er" : d}`;
  } else {
    const which = rule.monthWeek === -1 ? "dernier" : ordinalFr(rule.monthWeek ?? 1);
    base = `${every(rule.interval, "mois")} le ${which} ${WEEKDAY_NAMES[dayOfWeek(rule.startDate)]}`;
  }

  if (rule.endMode === "until" && rule.untilDate) {
    const sameYear = rule.untilDate.slice(0, 4) === rule.startDate.slice(0, 4);
    return `${base}, jusqu'au ${sameYear ? formatDayMonth(rule.untilDate) : formatDayMonthYear(rule.untilDate)}`;
  }
  if (rule.endMode === "count" && rule.occurrenceCount !== null) {
    return `${base}, ${rule.occurrenceCount} séance${rule.occurrenceCount > 1 ? "s" : ""}`;
  }
  return base;
}

/** Monthly-by-day on 29/30/31: « Certains mois n'ont pas de 31 : ils sont sautés. »; else null. */
export function monthlyDayWarning(rule: RecurrenceRule): string | null {
  if (rule.freq !== "monthly" || rule.monthlyBy !== "day_of_month") return null;
  const { d } = parseKey(rule.startDate);
  return d >= 29 ? `Certains mois n'ont pas de ${d} : ils sont sautés.` : null;
}

/** Editor preview: dates whose wall-clock start is shifted (nonexistent) or ambiguous (repeated) in tz. */
export function dstNotes(
  rule: RecurrenceRule,
  tz: string,
  dates: readonly DateKey[],
): { seriesDate: DateKey; kind: "shifted" | "ambiguous" }[] {
  const notes: { seriesDate: DateKey; kind: "shifted" | "ambiguous" }[] = [];
  for (const seriesDate of dates) {
    const start = instantAt(seriesDate, rule.startMinute, tz);
    const sameWallClock = (instant: Date) =>
      todayKey(instant, tz) === seriesDate && wallClockMinutes(instant, tz) === rule.startMinute;
    if (!sameWallClock(start)) {
      notes.push({ seriesDate, kind: "shifted" });
    } else if ([30, 60, 120].some((min) => sameWallClock(new Date(start.getTime() + min * 60_000)))) {
      notes.push({ seriesDate, kind: "ambiguous" });
    }
  }
  return notes;
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { addDays, instantAt } from "./civil-date";
import type { RecurrenceRule } from "./recurrence";
import { describeSeriesEdit, planSeriesEdit, type SeriesEditInput, type SeriesRow } from "./series-plan";
import { MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS } from "./tickets";

const TZ = "Europe/Paris";

/** Old segment: Mon + Wed 19:00, 60 min, since 2026-09-07. */
const OLD: RecurrenceRule = {
  startDate: "2026-09-07",
  startMinute: 1140,
  durationMin: 60,
  freq: "weekly",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays: [1, 3],
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "never",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: null,
};

/** New segment from d = 2026-10-19: Mon + Thu 19:30. */
const NEW: RecurrenceRule = { ...OLD, startDate: "2026-10-19", startMinute: 1170, weekdays: [1, 4] };

function row(id: string, seriesDate: string, over: Partial<SeriesRow> = {}): SeriesRow {
  const startsAt = instantAt(seriesDate, OLD.startMinute, TZ);
  return {
    id,
    seriesDate,
    startsAt,
    endsAt: new Date(startsAt.getTime() + OLD.durationMin * 60_000),
    status: "scheduled",
    timeDetached: false,
    activeBookings: 0,
    ticketBookings: 0,
    hasHistory: false,
    ...over,
  };
}

const ROWS: SeriesRow[] = [
  row("r1", "2026-10-19", { activeBookings: 2, ticketBookings: 1, hasHistory: true }),
  row("r2", "2026-10-21", { activeBookings: 1, ticketBookings: 1, hasHistory: true }),
  row("r3", "2026-10-26", { status: "cancelled", hasHistory: true }),
  row("r4", "2026-10-28", { hasHistory: true }),
  row("r6", "2026-11-02", { timeDetached: true, activeBookings: 3, ticketBookings: 3, hasHistory: true }),
  row("r7", "2026-11-04"),
];

const INPUT: SeriesEditInput = {
  tz: TZ,
  fromDate: "2026-10-19",
  horizonEnd: "2026-11-04",
  oldRule: OLD,
  newRule: NEW,
  rows: ROWS,
};

test("planSeriesEdit puts every row in exactly one bucket", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit(INPUT);
  const placed = [
    ...plan.retime.map((r) => r.id),
    ...plan.reparentOnly,
    ...plan.delete,
    ...plan.cancelEmpty,
    ...plan.orphanBooked,
  ].sort();
  assert.deepEqual(placed, ROWS.map((r) => r.id).sort());
});

test("planSeriesEdit buckets: retime, reparentOnly, delete, cancelEmpty, orphanBooked", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit(INPUT);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.retime.map((r) => r.id), ["r1", "r3"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.reparentOnly, ["r6"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.delete, ["r7"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.cancelEmpty, ["r4"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.orphanBooked, ["r2"]);
});

test("retime takes the new wall-clock time on each local date (both sides of DST)", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit(INPUT);
  const [r1, r3] = plan.retime;
  // 19:30 Paris: summer time on 19 Oct, winter time on 26 Oct.
  assert.equal(r1.startsAt.toISOString(), "2026-10-19T17:30:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(r1.endsAt.toISOString(), "2026-10-19T18:30:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(r1.timeChanged, true);
  assert.equal(r3.startsAt.toISOString(), "2026-10-26T18:30:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(r3.timeChanged, true);
});

test("insert lists the new dates with no row, whatever the row's status", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit(INPUT);
  assert.deepEqual(
    plan.insert.map((i) => [i.seriesDate, i.startsAt.toISOString(), i.endsAt.toISOString()]),
    [
      ["2026-10-22", "2026-10-22T17:30:00.000Z", "2026-10-22T18:30:00.000Z"],
      ["2026-10-29", "2026-10-29T18:30:00.000Z", "2026-10-29T19:30:00.000Z"],
    ],
  );
  assert.equal(plan.generatedUntil, "2026-11-04");
});

test("summary counts only scheduled rows whose time changes, and their active bookings", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit(INPUT);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.summary, {
    modified: 1,
    notified: 2,
    orphans: 1,
    orphanTickets: 1,
    deleted: 1,
    cancelledEmpty: 1,
    inserted: 2,
  });
});

test("an unchanged time is not a modification; a duration change is", () => {
  const same = planSeriesEdit({ ...INPUT, newRule: { ...OLD, startDate: "2026-10-19" } });
  assert.equal(same.retime.every((r) => !r.timeChanged), true);
  assert.equal(same.summary.modified, 0);
  assert.equal(same.summary.notified, 0);
  const longer = planSeriesEdit({ ...INPUT, newRule: { ...OLD, startDate: "2026-10-19", durationMin: 90 } });
  // Same days, 90 min: r1, r2, r4, r7 are scheduled and retimed (r3 is cancelled, r6 detached).
  assert.deepEqual(longer.retime.map((r) => r.id), ["r1", "r2", "r3", "r4", "r7"]);
  assert.equal(longer.retime.every((r) => r.timeChanged), true);
  assert.equal(longer.summary.modified, 4);
  assert.equal(longer.summary.notified, 3);
});

test("a cancelled date that disappears without history is cancelEmpty, never delete", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit({
    ...INPUT,
    rows: [row("c1", "2026-10-21", { status: "cancelled" })],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(plan.cancelEmpty, ["c1"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.delete, []);
  // Already cancelled: the edit changes nothing for it, so the preview does not count it.
  assert.equal(plan.summary.cancelledEmpty, 0);
  assert.doesNotMatch(describeSeriesEdit(plan.summary, "keep"), /vide/);
});

test("new dates start at max(d, newRule.startDate)", () => {
  const plan = planSeriesEdit({ ...INPUT, newRule: { ...NEW, startDate: "2026-10-26" } });
  assert.deepEqual(plan.retime.map((r) => r.id), ["r3"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.orphanBooked, ["r1", "r2"]);
  assert.deepEqual(plan.insert.map((i) => i.seriesDate), ["2026-10-29"]);
});

test("insert is capped at MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS and generatedUntil follows the cap", () => {
  const daily: RecurrenceRule = { ...OLD, startDate: "2026-10-12", freq: "daily", weekdays: null };
  const capped = planSeriesEdit({
    tz: TZ,
    fromDate: "2026-10-12",
    horizonEnd: addDays("2026-10-12", 499),
    oldRule: daily,
    newRule: daily,
    rows: [],
  });
  assert.equal(capped.insert.length, MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS);
  assert.equal(capped.generatedUntil, addDays("2026-10-12", MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS - 1));
  assert.equal(capped.summary.inserted, MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS);
  const uncapped = planSeriesEdit({
    tz: TZ,
    fromDate: "2026-10-12",
    horizonEnd: addDays("2026-10-12", 399),
    oldRule: daily,
    newRule: daily,
    rows: [],
  });
  assert.equal(uncapped.insert.length, 400);
  assert.equal(uncapped.generatedUntil, addDays("2026-10-12", 399));
});

test("remainingCount comes from the old rule in count mode", () => {
  // Mon + Wed from 2026-09-07: 12 dates before 2026-10-19.
  const counted = planSeriesEdit({ ...INPUT, oldRule: { ...OLD, endMode: "count", occurrenceCount: 20 } });
  assert.equal(counted.remainingCount, 8);
  assert.equal(planSeriesEdit(INPUT).remainingCount, null);
});

test("describeSeriesEdit", () => {
  assert.equal(
    describeSeriesEdit(
      { modified: 12, notified: 3, orphans: 1, orphanTickets: 2, deleted: 0, cancelledEmpty: 0, inserted: 0 },
      "cancel",
    ),
    "12 séances modifiées, 3 inscrits prévenus, 1 séance annulée (2 tickets rendus)",
  );
  const summary = planSeriesEdit(INPUT).summary;
  assert.equal(
    describeSeriesEdit(summary, "cancel"),
    "1 séance modifiée, 2 inscrits prévenus, 1 séance annulée (1 ticket rendu), 1 séance vide annulée, 1 séance supprimée, 2 séances ajoutées",
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeSeriesEdit(summary, "keep"),
    "1 séance modifiée, 2 inscrits prévenus, 1 séance gardée à part, 1 séance vide annulée, 1 séance supprimée, 2 séances ajoutées",
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeSeriesEdit({ modified: 0, notified: 0, orphans: 2, orphanTickets: 0, deleted: 0, cancelledEmpty: 0, inserted: 0 }, "cancel"),
    "2 séances annulées",
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeSeriesEdit({ modified: 0, notified: 0, orphans: 0, orphanTickets: 0, deleted: 0, cancelledEmpty: 0, inserted: 0 }, "keep"),
    "Aucun changement sur les séances",
  );
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/series-plan.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Pure planner of « cette séance et les suivantes » (spec § 11 E7). It splits
 * the existing occurrences of the edited segment (series_date ≥ d) between
 * the new rule's dates, and lists the dates to insert. The engine
 * (src/lib/booking/series.ts) turns the plan into one guarded batch.
 */
import { instantAt, maxKey, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { expandDates, remainingCount, type RecurrenceRule } from "@/lib/recurrence";
import { MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS, type OccurrenceStatus } from "@/lib/tickets";

/** An existing occurrence of the edited segment with seriesDate ≥ d. */
export type SeriesRow = {
  id: string;
  seriesDate: DateKey;
  startsAt: Date;
  endsAt: Date;
  status: OccurrenceStatus;
  timeDetached: boolean;
  /** pending + confirmed bookings */
  activeBookings: number;
  /** confirmed bookings of coverage ticket */
  ticketBookings: number;
  /** any booking ever, whatever its status */
  hasHistory: boolean;
};

export type SeriesEditInput = {
  tz: string;
  /** d */
  fromDate: DateKey;
  /** today + MATERIALIZE_DAYS */
  horizonEnd: DateKey;
  oldRule: RecurrenceRule;
  newRule: RecurrenceRule;
  rows: readonly SeriesRow[];
};

export type SeriesEditSummary = {
  /** scheduled retime rows whose time changes */
  modified: number;
  /** active bookings on those rows (they get « séance modifiée ») */
  notified: number;
  /** orphanBooked rows */
  orphans: number;
  /** ticket bookings on orphanBooked rows (given back if the pro cancels them) */
  orphanTickets: number;
  deleted: number;
  /** cancelEmpty rows that were still scheduled (already-cancelled rows are not counted) */
  cancelledEmpty: number;
  inserted: number;
};

export type SeriesEditPlan = {
  /** not detached, date still produced by the new rule: new start and end (status kept) */
  retime: { id: string; seriesDate: DateKey; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; timeChanged: boolean }[];
  /** time_detached rows: keep their time, only change event_id */
  reparentOnly: string[];
  /** date gone, never booked, scheduled: physical delete */
  delete: string[];
  /** date gone, no active booking, but a history or already cancelled: → cancelled */
  cancelEmpty: string[];
  /** date gone, active bookings: kept isolated (time_detached) or cancelled (E6b) */
  orphanBooked: string[];
  /** new-rule dates without a row, ascending, at most MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS */
  insert: { seriesDate: DateKey; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date }[];
  /** the new segment's generated_until: the last inserted date when capped, else horizonEnd */
  generatedUntil: DateKey;
  /** count mode of the old rule: dates left from d (« Il reste K séances »); else null */
  remainingCount: number | null;
  summary: SeriesEditSummary;
};

export function planSeriesEdit(input: SeriesEditInput): SeriesEditPlan {
  const { tz, fromDate, horizonEnd, oldRule, newRule, rows } = input;
  const newDates = expandDates(newRule, maxKey(fromDate, newRule.startDate), horizonEnd);
  const produced = new Set(newDates);
  const instants = (seriesDate: DateKey) => {
    const startsAt = instantAt(seriesDate, newRule.startMinute, tz);
    return { startsAt, endsAt: new Date(startsAt.getTime() + newRule.durationMin * 60_000) };
  };

  const plan: SeriesEditPlan = {
    retime: [],
    reparentOnly: [],
    delete: [],
    cancelEmpty: [],
    orphanBooked: [],
    insert: [],
    generatedUntil: horizonEnd,
    remainingCount: remainingCount(oldRule, fromDate),
    summary: { modified: 0, notified: 0, orphans: 0, orphanTickets: 0, deleted: 0, cancelledEmpty: 0, inserted: 0 },
  };

  for (const row of rows) {
    if (row.timeDetached) {
      plan.reparentOnly.push(row.id);
    } else if (produced.has(row.seriesDate)) {
      const { startsAt, endsAt } = instants(row.seriesDate);
      const timeChanged =
        startsAt.getTime() !== row.startsAt.getTime() || endsAt.getTime() !== row.endsAt.getTime();
      plan.retime.push({ id: row.id, seriesDate: row.seriesDate, startsAt, endsAt, timeChanged });
      if (timeChanged && row.status === "scheduled") {
        plan.summary.modified++;
        plan.summary.notified += row.activeBookings;
      }
    } else if (row.activeBookings > 0) {
      plan.orphanBooked.push(row.id);
      plan.summary.orphans++;
      plan.summary.orphanTickets += row.ticketBookings;
    } else if (!row.hasHistory && row.status === "scheduled") {
      plan.delete.push(row.id);
    } else {
      plan.cancelEmpty.push(row.id);
      if (row.status === "scheduled") plan.summary.cancelledEmpty++;
    }
  }

  const existing = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.seriesDate));
  const missing = newDates.filter((d) => !existing.has(d));
  const capped = missing.length > MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS;
  for (const seriesDate of missing.slice(0, MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS)) {
    plan.insert.push({ seriesDate, ...instants(seriesDate) });
  }
  if (capped) plan.generatedUntil = plan.insert[plan.insert.length - 1].seriesDate;

  plan.summary.deleted = plan.delete.length;
  plan.summary.inserted = plan.insert.length;
  return plan;
}

const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} ${n > 1 ? many : one}`;

/** « 12 séances modifiées, 3 inscrits prévenus, 1 séance annulée (2 tickets rendus) ». */
export function describeSeriesEdit(summary: SeriesEditSummary, orphans: "keep" | "cancel"): string {
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (summary.modified > 0) parts.push(plural(summary.modified, "séance modifiée", "séances modifiées"));
  if (summary.notified > 0) parts.push(plural(summary.notified, "inscrit prévenu", "inscrits prévenus"));
  if (summary.orphans > 0) {
    if (orphans === "cancel") {
      const tickets =
        summary.orphanTickets > 0
          ? ` (${plural(summary.orphanTickets, "ticket rendu", "tickets rendus")})`
          : "";
      parts.push(`${plural(summary.orphans, "séance annulée", "séances annulées")}${tickets}`);
    } else {
      parts.push(plural(summary.orphans, "séance gardée à part", "séances gardées à part"));
    }
  }
  if (summary.cancelledEmpty > 0) {
    parts.push(plural(summary.cancelledEmpty, "séance vide annulée", "séances vides annulées"));
  }
  if (summary.deleted > 0) parts.push(plural(summary.deleted, "séance supprimée", "séances supprimées"));
  if (summary.inserted > 0) parts.push(plural(summary.inserted, "séance ajoutée", "séances ajoutées"));
  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(", ") : "Aucun changement sur les séances";
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import {
  expiryFromLastDay,
  lastValidDay,
  offerCovers,
  ticketExpiry,
  validAt,
  validityOf,
} from "./ticket-expiry";

const TZ = "Europe/Paris";
const iso = (d: Date | null) => (d === null ? null : d.toISOString());

test("ticketExpiry: no validity, no expiry", () => {
  assert.equal(ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z"), null, TZ), null);
});

test("ticketExpiry: one month from 31 January ends with February (clamped)", () => {
  // Paid 10:00 Paris on 31 Jan → valid through 28 Feb → expires 1 Mar 00:00 Paris.
  const at = ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-01-31T09:00:00Z"), { count: 1, unit: "month" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(at), "2026-02-28T23:00:00.000Z");
  const leap = ticketExpiry(new Date("2028-01-31T09:00:00Z"), { count: 1, unit: "month" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(leap), "2028-02-29T23:00:00.000Z");
  const year = ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-02-28T09:00:00Z"), { count: 12, unit: "month" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(year), "2027-02-28T23:00:00.000Z");
});

test("ticketExpiry: days count from the LOCAL purchase day, across the autumn change", () => {
  // 21:30Z on 24 Oct = 23:30 Paris → local day 24 Oct, +1 day → valid through 25 Oct.
  const at = ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-10-24T21:30:00Z"), { count: 1, unit: "day" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(at), "2026-10-25T23:00:00.000Z");
  // 22:30Z on 24 Oct is already 25 Oct in Paris.
  const late = ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-10-24T22:30:00Z"), { count: 1, unit: "day" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(late), "2026-10-26T23:00:00.000Z");
  const thirty = ticketExpiry(new Date("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z"), { count: 30, unit: "day" }, TZ);
  assert.equal(iso(thirty), "2026-11-11T23:00:00.000Z");
});

test("validityOf reads an offer or purchase snapshot", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(validityOf({ validityCount: 3, validityUnit: "month" }), { count: 3, unit: "month" });
  assert.equal(validityOf({ validityCount: null, validityUnit: null }), null);
  assert.equal(validityOf({ validityCount: 3, validityUnit: null }), null);
});

test("lastValidDay is the local day before expires_at", () => {
  assert.equal(lastValidDay(new Date("2026-02-28T23:00:00Z"), TZ), "2026-02-28");
  assert.equal(lastValidDay(new Date("2026-10-25T23:00:00Z"), TZ), "2026-10-25");
});

test("expiryFromLastDay is the local midnight after the chosen day", () => {
  assert.equal(expiryFromLastDay("2026-02-28", TZ).toISOString(), "2026-02-28T23:00:00.000Z");
  // 25 Oct starts in summer time: midnight at 22:00Z on the 24th.
  assert.equal(expiryFromLastDay("2026-10-24", TZ).toISOString(), "2026-10-24T22:00:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(lastValidDay(expiryFromLastDay("2026-03-28", TZ), TZ), "2026-03-28");
});

test("validAt: valid when no expiry or when it expires strictly after the start", () => {
  const start = new Date("2026-03-01T09:00:00Z");
  assert.equal(validAt(null, start), true);
  assert.equal(validAt(new Date("2026-03-01T09:00:00.001Z"), start), true);
  assert.equal(validAt(new Date("2026-03-01T09:00:00Z"), start), false);
  assert.equal(validAt(new Date("2026-02-28T23:00:00Z"), start), false);
});

test("offerCovers: a one-month offer bought on 31 Jan covers 28 Feb, not 1 Mar", () => {
  const now = new Date("2026-01-31T09:00:00Z");
  const month = { count: 1, unit: "month" } as const;
  assert.equal(offerCovers(now, month, TZ, new Date("2026-02-28T19:00:00Z")), true);
  assert.equal(offerCovers(now, month, TZ, new Date("2026-02-28T23:30:00Z")), false);
  assert.equal(offerCovers(now, null, TZ, new Date("2030-01-01T00:00:00Z")), true);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/ticket-expiry.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Ticket validity (spec § 9). A ticket bought with an N-day or N-month offer
 * is valid through local day (purchase day + N), months clamped to the
 * month's end, and expires at the local midnight that follows. `expires_at`
 * stores that first invalid instant; a ticket is usable on an occurrence when
 * expires_at > starts_at.
 */
import { addDays, addMonthsClamped, instantAt, todayKey, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import type { ValidityUnit } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type Validity = { count: number; unit: ValidityUnit } | null;

/** First instant a ticket is no longer valid: local midnight after (local date of paidAt + N days or months, months clamped). null = no expiry. */
export function ticketExpiry(paidAt: Date, validity: Validity, tz: string): Date | null {
  if (validity === null) return null;
  const paidDay = todayKey(paidAt, tz);
  const lastDay =
    validity.unit === "month" ? addMonthsClamped(paidDay, validity.count) : addDays(paidDay, validity.count);
  return expiryFromLastDay(lastDay, tz);
}

/** From an offer/purchase snapshot. */
export function validityOf(row: { validityCount: number | null; validityUnit: ValidityUnit | null }): Validity {
  if (row.validityCount === null || row.validityUnit === null) return null;
  return { count: row.validityCount, unit: row.validityUnit };
}

/** Last valid local day (« valable jusqu'au 12/01 inclus »): local date of (expiresAt − 1 ms). */
export function lastValidDay(expiresAt: Date, tz: string): DateKey {
  return todayKey(new Date(expiresAt.getTime() - 1), tz);
}

/** expires_at for a pro-chosen last day (Offrir): local midnight after lastDay. */
export function expiryFromLastDay(lastDay: DateKey, tz: string): Date {
  return instantAt(addDays(lastDay, 1), 0, tz);
}

/** expiresAt null or expiresAt > startsAt. */
export function validAt(expiresAt: Date | null, startsAt: Date): boolean {
  return expiresAt === null || expiresAt.getTime() > startsAt.getTime();
}

/** validAt(ticketExpiry(now, validity, tz), startsAt). */
export function offerCovers(now: Date, validity: Validity, tz: string, startsAt: Date): boolean {
  return validAt(ticketExpiry(now, validity, tz), startsAt);
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { effectiveWindow, fitsAvailability, lastBookableDate, onlineBookability, type BookingWindow } from "./booking-window";

const TZ = "Europe/Paris";
const PRO: BookingWindow = { bookingHorizonDays: 60, minNoticeMinutes: 60, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 };

test("effectiveWindow: each non-null override wins, null and missing fall back to the pro", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(effectiveWindow(PRO), PRO);
  assert.deepEqual(effectiveWindow(PRO, null), PRO);
  assert.deepEqual(
    effectiveWindow(PRO, { bookingHorizonDays: 7, minNoticeMinutes: null, cancelDeadlineHours: null }),
    { bookingHorizonDays: 7, minNoticeMinutes: 60, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(effectiveWindow(PRO, { minNoticeMinutes: 0, cancelDeadlineHours: 0 }), {
    bookingHorizonDays: 60,
    minNoticeMinutes: 0,
    cancelDeadlineHours: 0,
  });
});

test("lastBookableDate counts from the LOCAL today", () => {
  // 22:30Z on 24 Oct is already 25 Oct in Paris.
  assert.equal(lastBookableDate(new Date("2026-10-24T22:30:00Z"), TZ, 60), "2026-12-24");
  assert.equal(lastBookableDate(new Date("2026-10-24T21:30:00Z"), TZ, 60), "2026-12-23");
  assert.equal(lastBookableDate(new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z"), TZ, 1), "2026-10-13");
});

test("onlineBookability: past, notice, horizon, ok", () => {
  const now = new Date("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z"); // 10:00 Paris
  const at = (iso: string) => onlineBookability({ startsAt: new Date(iso), now, tz: TZ, window: PRO });
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z"), { ok: false, reason: "past" });
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-10-12T07:00:00Z"), { ok: false, reason: "past" });
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-10-12T08:59:00Z"), { ok: false, reason: "notice" });
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-10-12T09:00:00Z"), { ok: true });
  // today + 60 = 11 Dec: 23:30 Paris on the 11th is in, 00:30 on the 12th is out.
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-12-11T22:30:00Z"), { ok: true });
  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-12-11T23:30:00Z"), { ok: false, reason: "horizon" });
});

test("onlineBookability honours an event override", () => {
  const now = new Date("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z");
  const window = effectiveWindow(PRO, { bookingHorizonDays: 7, minNoticeMinutes: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(onlineBookability({ startsAt: new Date("2026-10-12T08:01:00Z"), now, tz: TZ, window }), { ok: true });
  assert.deepEqual(onlineBookability({ startsAt: new Date("2026-10-19T17:00:00Z"), now, tz: TZ, window }), { ok: true });
  assert.deepEqual(onlineBookability({ startsAt: new Date("2026-10-20T08:00:00Z"), now, tz: TZ, window }), {
    ok: false,
    reason: "horizon",
  });
});

test("fitsAvailability: inside one rule window of the local weekday", () => {
  const rules = [
    { dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 600, endMinute: 780 }, // Monday 10:00–13:00
    { dayOfWeek: 0, startMinute: 600, endMinute: 720 }, // Sunday 10:00–12:00
    { dayOfWeek: 5, startMinute: 1320, endMinute: 1440 }, // Friday 22:00–24:00
  ];
  const fits = (s: string, e: string) => fitsAvailability(rules, new Date(s), new Date(e), TZ);
  // Monday 12 Oct 2026, Paris = UTC+2
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z", "2026-10-12T09:00:00Z"), true);
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z", "2026-10-12T11:00:00Z"), true); // ends exactly 13:00
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-12T10:30:00Z", "2026-10-12T11:30:00Z"), false); // 12:30–13:30
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-12T07:30:00Z", "2026-10-12T08:30:00Z"), false); // starts 09:30
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-13T08:00:00Z", "2026-10-13T09:00:00Z"), false); // Tuesday
  // Sunday 29 March 2026 (spring forward): 10:00 Paris = 08:00Z
  assert.equal(fits("2026-03-29T08:00:00Z", "2026-03-29T09:00:00Z"), true);
  // Friday 16 Oct 23:00–24:00 Paris: ends at the next local midnight
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-16T21:00:00Z", "2026-10-16T22:00:00Z"), true);
  // Friday 23:30 → Saturday 00:30 crosses midnight
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-16T21:30:00Z", "2026-10-16T22:30:00Z"), false);
  assert.equal(fits("2026-10-12T09:00:00Z", "2026-10-12T09:00:00Z"), false);
  assert.equal(fitsAvailability([], new Date("2026-10-12T08:00:00Z"), new Date("2026-10-12T09:00:00Z"), TZ), false);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement.** Create `src/lib/booking-window.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Booking windows (spec § 2.13, § 7): horizon, minimum notice and client
 * cancellation deadline. An event (or an RDV ticket type) may override each
 * of the pro's three settings. The online rule here mirrors the SQL guard
 * ONLINE_OK of src/lib/booking/claims.ts; the pro path ignores both.
 */
import { addDays, dayOfWeek, instantAt, todayKey, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { wallClockMinutes } from "@/lib/datetime";
import type { WindowOverrides } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type BookingWindow = { bookingHorizonDays: number; minNoticeMinutes: number; cancelDeadlineHours: number };

/** Each field: the override when not null, else the pro's. */
export function effectiveWindow(pro: BookingWindow, override?: Partial<WindowOverrides> | null): BookingWindow {
  return {
    bookingHorizonDays: override?.bookingHorizonDays ?? pro.bookingHorizonDays,
    minNoticeMinutes: override?.minNoticeMinutes ?? pro.minNoticeMinutes,
    cancelDeadlineHours: override?.cancelDeadlineHours ?? pro.cancelDeadlineHours,
  };
}

/** today (local, tz) + horizonDays. */
export function lastBookableDate(now: Date, tz: string, horizonDays: number): DateKey {
  return addDays(todayKey(now, tz), horizonDays);
}

export type Bookability = { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: "past" | "notice" | "horizon" };

/** now + minNotice ≤ startsAt AND local date(startsAt) ≤ today + horizon; "past" when startsAt ≤ now. Mirrors ONLINE_OK (§6). */
export function onlineBookability(args: { startsAt: Date; now: Date; tz: string; window: BookingWindow }): Bookability {
  const { startsAt, now, tz, window } = args;
  if (startsAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) return { ok: false, reason: "past" };
  if (now.getTime() + window.minNoticeMinutes * 60_000 > startsAt.getTime()) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "notice" };
  }
  if (todayKey(startsAt, tz) > lastBookableDate(now, tz, window.bookingHorizonDays)) {
    return { ok: false, reason: "horizon" };
  }
  return { ok: true };
}

/** RDV: [startsAt, endsAt) inside one rule window of the local day of startsAt (rules already resolved). */
export function fitsAvailability(
  rules: readonly { dayOfWeek: number; startMinute: number; endMinute: number }[],
  startsAt: Date,
  endsAt: Date,
  tz: string,
): boolean {
  if (endsAt.getTime() <= startsAt.getTime()) return false;
  const day = todayKey(startsAt, tz);
  const startMinute = wallClockMinutes(startsAt, tz);
  let endMinute: number;
  if (todayKey(endsAt, tz) === day) {
    endMinute = wallClockMinutes(endsAt, tz);
  } else if (endsAt.getTime() === instantAt(addDays(day, 1), 0, tz).getTime()) {
    endMinute = 1440;
  } else {
    return false;
  }
  const dow = dayOfWeek(day);
  return rules.some((r) => r.dayOfWeek === dow && r.startMinute <= startMinute && endMinute <= r.endMinute);
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { clientCanCancel, clientCancelDeadline } from "./cancellation";

const START = new Date("2026-10-20T17:00:00Z");
const hoursBefore = (h: number) => new Date(START.getTime() - h * 3_600_000);
const CREATED = new Date("2026-10-10T10:00:00Z");

const check = (over: Partial<Parameters<typeof clientCanCancel>[0]>) =>
  clientCanCancel({
    startsAt: START,
    now: hoursBefore(25),
    deadlineHours: 24,
    bookingCreatedAt: CREATED,
    occurrenceChangedAt: null,
    movedAt: null,
    ...over,
  });

test("clientCancelDeadline subtracts elapsed hours", () => {
  assert.equal(clientCancelDeadline(START, 24).toISOString(), "2026-10-19T17:00:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(clientCancelDeadline(START, 0).toISOString(), START.toISOString());
});

test("before the deadline the client cancels; after it, only the pro can", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(25) }), { allowed: true, reason: "before_deadline" });
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(23) }), { allowed: false, reason: "too_late" });
  // exactly at the deadline: startsAt − deadline > now is false
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(24) }), { allowed: false, reason: "too_late" });
});

test("a pro change after the booking re-opens cancellation until the start", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(23), occurrenceChangedAt: new Date("2026-10-15T09:00:00Z") }), {
    allowed: true,
    reason: "changed_by_pro",
  });
  // a change BEFORE the booking does not count
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(23), occurrenceChangedAt: new Date("2026-10-01T09:00:00Z") }), {
    allowed: false,
    reason: "too_late",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: hoursBefore(1), movedAt: new Date("2026-10-15T09:00:00Z") }), {
    allowed: true,
    reason: "changed_by_pro",
  });
});

test("nobody cancels online once the occurrence has started", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: START }), { allowed: false, reason: "started" });
  assert.deepEqual(check({ now: new Date(START.getTime() + 60_000), movedAt: CREATED }), {
    allowed: false,
    reason: "started",
  });
});

test("deadline 0: allowed until the start", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check({ deadlineHours: 0, now: new Date(START.getTime() - 60_000) }), {
    allowed: true,
    reason: "before_deadline",
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Implement.** Create `src/lib/cancellation.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Client cancellation rule (spec § 2.7, E5): the client cancels a confirmed
 * booking up to `deadline` hours before the start; when the pro changed the
 * occurrence's time after the booking, or moved the client, the client may
 * cancel until the start. Mirrors the EXISTS of cancelByClient
 * (src/lib/booking/cancel.ts). Hours are elapsed hours, like make_interval.
 */
export function clientCancelDeadline(startsAt: Date, deadlineHours: number): Date {
  return new Date(startsAt.getTime() - deadlineHours * 3_600_000);
}

export type ClientCancelCheck =
  | { allowed: true; reason: "before_deadline" | "changed_by_pro" }
  | { allowed: false; reason: "started" | "too_late" };

/** startsAt > now AND (startsAt − deadline > now OR occurrenceChangedAt > bookingCreatedAt OR movedAt set). Mirrors cancelByClient's EXISTS. */
export function clientCanCancel(args: {
  startsAt: Date;
  now: Date;
  deadlineHours: number;
  bookingCreatedAt: Date;
  occurrenceChangedAt: Date | null;
  movedAt: Date | null;
}): ClientCancelCheck {
  const now = args.now.getTime();
  if (args.startsAt.getTime() <= now) return { allowed: false, reason: "started" };
  if (clientCancelDeadline(args.startsAt, args.deadlineHours).getTime() > now) {
    return { allowed: true, reason: "before_deadline" };
  }
  const changedAfterBooking =
    args.occurrenceChangedAt !== null && args.occurrenceChangedAt.getTime() > args.bookingCreatedAt.getTime();
  if (changedAfterBooking || args.movedAt !== null) return { allowed: true, reason: "changed_by_pro" };
  return { allowed: false, reason: "too_late" };
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { GUEST_COOKIE, GUEST_COOKIE_MAX_AGE, guestLinkKey, signGuestLink, verifyGuestLink } from "./guest-link";

const KEY = guestLinkKey("test-secret-0123456789abcdefghijklmnop");
const LINK = { customerId: "V1StGXR8_Z5jdHi6B-myT", linkVersion: 3 };

test("constants", () => {
  assert.equal(GUEST_COOKIE, "skedul_guest");
  assert.equal(GUEST_COOKIE_MAX_AGE, 2_592_000);
});

test("a signed link verifies and round-trips", () => {
  const token = signGuestLink(LINK, KEY);
  assert.match(token, /^V1StGXR8_Z5jdHi6B-myT\.3\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
  assert.deepEqual(verifyGuestLink(token, KEY), LINK);
  assert.equal(signGuestLink(LINK, KEY), token);
});

test("the key depends on the secret", () => {
  assert.equal(guestLinkKey("a").length, 32);
  assert.notDeepEqual(guestLinkKey("a"), guestLinkKey("b"));
  const token = signGuestLink(LINK, guestLinkKey("other-secret"));
  assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(token, KEY), null);
});

test("tampering with any part is refused", () => {
  const token = signGuestLink(LINK, KEY);
  const [id, version, mac] = token.split(".");
  const flipped = mac.slice(0, -1) + (mac.endsWith("A") ? "B" : "A");
  assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(`${id}.${version}.${flipped}`, KEY), null);
  assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(`${id}.4.${mac}`, KEY), null);
  assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(`${id}x.${version}.${mac}`, KEY), null);
  assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(`${id}.${version}.${mac}A`, KEY), null);
});

test("a bumped link_version yields a different token (revocation is the caller's comparison)", () => {
  const v3 = signGuestLink(LINK, KEY);
  const v4 = signGuestLink({ ...LINK, linkVersion: 4 }, KEY);
  assert.notEqual(v3, v4);
  assert.deepEqual(verifyGuestLink(v4, KEY), { ...LINK, linkVersion: 4 });
});

test("malformed tokens are refused", () => {
  const mac = signGuestLink(LINK, KEY).split(".")[2];
  for (const bad of [
    "",
    "abc",
    `${LINK.customerId}.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.3.${mac}.x`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.0.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.03.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.-3.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.3.5.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.abc.${mac}`,
    `.3.${mac}`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.3.`,
    `${LINK.customerId}.3.${mac.slice(0, 20)}+/`,
  ]) {
    assert.equal(verifyGuestLink(bad, KEY), null, bad);
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/guest-link.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Personal link (spec § 12): every client e-mail points to /api/c/<token>,
 * which sets the `skedul_guest` cookie for this pro's path. The token is
 * `customerId.linkVersion.mac`, MAC = HMAC-SHA256 under a key derived from
 * BETTER_AUTH_SECRET. Bumping customer.link_version revokes every link: the
 * caller compares the verified version with the row's. Pure (node:crypto).
 */
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from "node:crypto";

export const GUEST_COOKIE = "skedul_guest" as const;
/** 30 days, in seconds (2_592_000). */
export const GUEST_COOKIE_MAX_AGE: number = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60;

export type GuestLink = { customerId: string; linkVersion: number };

const VERSION = /^[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$/;
const PART = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/;

/** HMAC-SHA256(secret, "skedul-guest-link-v1"). */
export function guestLinkKey(secret: string): Buffer {
  return createHmac("sha256", secret).update("skedul-guest-link-v1").digest();
}

function mac(link: GuestLink, key: Buffer): string {
  return createHmac("sha256", key).update(`${link.customerId}|${link.linkVersion}`).digest("base64url");
}

/** `${customerId}.${linkVersion}.${base64url(HMAC-SHA256(key, `${customerId}|${linkVersion}`))}`. */
export function signGuestLink(link: GuestLink, key: Buffer): string {
  return `${link.customerId}.${link.linkVersion}.${mac(link, key)}`;
}

/** The link when the token has exactly 3 parts, an integer version ≥ 1 and a matching MAC (constant time); else null. */
export function verifyGuestLink(token: string, key: Buffer): GuestLink | null {
  const parts = token.split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null;
  const [customerId, versionText, given] = parts;
  if (!PART.test(customerId) || !VERSION.test(versionText) || !PART.test(given)) return null;
  const link = { customerId, linkVersion: Number(versionText) };
  const expected = Buffer.from(mac(link, key));
  const actual = Buffer.from(given);
  if (actual.length !== expected.length || !timingSafeEqual(actual, expected)) return null;
  return link;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement.** Create `src/lib/lanes.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Side-by-side layout of overlapping intervals for the planning (spec § 7):
 * occurrences that overlap share their column width. Intervals are [start,
 * end) in any unit (ms or minutes); touching intervals do not overlap.
 */
export type LaneItem = { id: string; start: number; end: number };
/** lane: 0-based column; lanes: columns used by the item's overlap cluster. */
export type LaneSlot = { lane: number; lanes: number };

/** Sorted by (start, end desc, id); clusters of transitively overlapping items; greedy first free lane; every item gets its cluster's lane count; touching intervals do not overlap. */
export function layoutLanes(items: readonly LaneItem[]): Map<string, LaneSlot> {
  const sorted = [...items].sort(
    (a, b) => a.start - b.start || b.end - a.end || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0),
  );
  const result = new Map<string, LaneSlot>();
  let cluster: { id: string; lane: number }[] = [];
  let laneEnds: number[] = [];
  let clusterEnd = -Infinity;

  const close = () => {
    for (const member of cluster) result.set(member.id, { lane: member.lane, lanes: laneEnds.length });
    cluster = [];
    laneEnds = [];
  };

  for (const item of sorted) {
    if (item.start >= clusterEnd) close();
    let lane = laneEnds.findIndex((end) => end <= item.start);
    if (lane === -1) {
      lane = laneEnds.length;
      laneEnds.push(item.end);
    } else {
      laneEnds[lane] = item.end;
    }
    cluster.push({ id: item.id, lane });
    clusterEnd = Math.max(clusterEnd, item.end);
  }
  close();
  return result;
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { classifyTypes, consumptionOrder, customerAlerts, isUsable, type TypeFacts } from "./wallet-rules";

const facts = (ticketTypeId: string, over: Partial<TypeFacts> = {}): TypeFacts => ({
  ticketTypeId,
  archived: false,
  rdvEnabled: false,
  linkedToUpcomingEvent: true,
  ...over,
});

test("classifyTypes: archived wins, then orphan (not RDV and not linked), else ok", () => {
  const classes = classifyTypes([
    facts("linked"),
    facts("rdv", { rdvEnabled: true, linkedToUpcomingEvent: false }),
    facts("orphan", { linkedToUpcomingEvent: false }),
    facts("archived", { archived: true }),
    facts("archived-orphan", { archived: true, linkedToUpcomingEvent: false }),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.fromEntries(classes), {
    linked: "ok",
    rdv: "ok",
    orphan: "orphan",
    archived: "archived",
    "archived-orphan": "archived",
  });
});

test("customerAlerts: red for usable tickets of an archived type, orange for an orphan type", () => {
  const classes = classifyTypes([
    facts("ok"),
    facts("arch", { archived: true }),
    facts("orph", { linkedToUpcomingEvent: false }),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([{ ticketTypeId: "ok", usable: 4 }], classes), { red: false, orange: false });
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([{ ticketTypeId: "arch", usable: 1 }], classes), { red: true, orange: false });
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([{ ticketTypeId: "orph", usable: 2 }], classes), { red: false, orange: true });
  assert.deepEqual(
    customerAlerts(
      [
        { ticketTypeId: "arch", usable: 1 },
        { ticketTypeId: "orph", usable: 1 },
      ],
      classes,
    ),
    { red: true, orange: true },
  );
  // no usable ticket left: the alert disappears by itself
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([{ ticketTypeId: "arch", usable: 0 }], classes), { red: false, orange: false });
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([{ ticketTypeId: "unknown", usable: 3 }], classes), { red: false, orange: false });
  assert.deepEqual(customerAlerts([], classes), { red: false, orange: false });
});

test("isUsable: available and not expired at the given instant", () => {
  const at = new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z");
  assert.equal(isUsable({ status: "available", expiresAt: null }, at), true);
  assert.equal(isUsable({ status: "available", expiresAt: new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:01Z") }, at), true);
  assert.equal(isUsable({ status: "available", expiresAt: new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z") }, at), false);

    { id: "n2", expiresAt: null, createdAt: d("2026-01-02T00:00:00Z") },
    { id: "late", expiresAt: d("2026-12-31T23:00:00Z"), createdAt: d("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z") },
    { id: "b", expiresAt: d("2026-11-30T23:00:00Z"), createdAt: d("2026-02-01T00:00:00Z") },
    { id: "n1", expiresAt: null, createdAt: d("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z") },
    { id: "a", expiresAt: d("2026-11-30T23:00:00Z"), createdAt: d("2026-02-01T00:00:00Z") },
    { id: "old", expiresAt: d("2026-11-30T23:00:00Z"), createdAt: d("2026-01-15T00:00:00Z") },
  ];
  const input = [...tickets];
  assert.deepEqual(
    consumptionOrder(tickets).map((t) => t.id),
    ["old", "a", "b", "late", "n1", "n2"],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(tickets, input, "the input array is not mutated");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/lib/wallet-rules.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Wallet rules (spec § 9): ticket-type classes behind the client-list alerts,
 * the balance predicate and the consumption order. Pure; the facts come from
 * src/lib/booking/wallet.ts (loadTypeFacts) and the SQL twins live in
 * src/lib/booking/eligibility.ts.
 */
import type { TicketStatus } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type TypeFacts = {
  ticketTypeId: string;
  archived: boolean;
  rdvEnabled: boolean;
  /** linked to a non-archived tickets-mode event (private counts) with a future scheduled séance */
  linkedToUpcomingEvent: boolean;
};

/** ok, 🔴 archived, 🟠 orphan (active, not rdv_enabled, not linked). */
export type TypeClass = "ok" | "archived" | "orphan";

export function classifyTypes(types: readonly TypeFacts[]): Map<string, TypeClass> {
  const classes = new Map<string, TypeClass>();
  for (const t of types) {
    const cls: TypeClass = t.archived ? "archived" : !t.rdvEnabled && !t.linkedToUpcomingEvent ? "orphan" : "ok";
    classes.set(t.ticketTypeId, cls);
  }
  return classes;
}

/** usable = available and not expired (isUsable), counted per type. */
export type Holding = { ticketTypeId: string; usable: number };

/** red: usable tickets of an archived type; orange: usable tickets of an orphan type. */
export function customerAlerts(
  holdings: readonly Holding[],
  classes: ReadonlyMap<string, TypeClass>,
): { red: boolean; orange: boolean } {
  let red = false;
  let orange = false;
  for (const h of holdings) {
    if (h.usable <= 0) continue;
    const cls = classes.get(h.ticketTypeId);
    if (cls === "archived") red = true;
    if (cls === "orphan") orange = true;
  }
  return { red, orange };
}

/** status available AND (expiresAt null OR expiresAt > at). */
export function isUsable(t: { status: TicketStatus; expiresAt: Date | null }, at: Date): boolean {
  return t.status === "available" && (t.expiresAt === null || t.expiresAt.getTime() > at.getTime());
}

/** Ascending, nulls last (ORDER BY expires_at ASC NULLS LAST). */
function compareExpiry(a: Date | null, b: Date | null): number {
  if (a === null || b === null) return a === b ? 0 : a === null ? 1 : -1;
  return a.getTime() - b.getTime();
}

/** expiresAt asc nulls last, createdAt, id; returns a new array. */
export function consumptionOrder<T extends { id: string; expiresAt: Date | null; createdAt: Date }>(
  tickets: readonly T[],
): T[] {
  return [...tickets].sort(
    (a, b) =>
      compareExpiry(a.expiresAt, b.expiresAt) ||
      a.createdAt.getTime() - b.createdAt.getTime() ||
      (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0),
  );
}
```

```ts
/**
 * The IP alone, hashed with the same daily salt: the per-IP key of the app
 * rate limits (src/lib/throttle.ts, spec § 12). No IP is ever stored in clear.
 */
export function ipHash(salt: string, ip: string): string {
  return createHmac("sha256", salt).update(ip).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
}
```

```ts
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { HEALTH_KEYS, nextWindow, otpThrottleKey, throttleKey } from "./throttle";

const T0 = new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z");
const later = (sec: number) => new Date(T0.getTime() + sec * 1000);

test("nextWindow opens a window, counts inside it, and reopens once it has elapsed", () => {
  const first = nextWindow(null, T0, 3600);
  assert.deepEqual(first, { windowStart: T0, hits: 1 });
  const second = nextWindow(first, later(60), 3600);
  assert.deepEqual(second, { windowStart: T0, hits: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(nextWindow(second, later(3599), 3600), { windowStart: T0, hits: 3 });
  // windowStart ≤ now − windowSec: exactly one window later is a new window
  assert.deepEqual(nextWindow(second, later(3600), 3600), { windowStart: later(3600), hits: 1 });
  assert.deepEqual(nextWindow(second, later(9000), 3600), { windowStart: later(9000), hits: 1 });
});

test("throttleKey: the IP hash is used as given, the e-mail is normalised and hashed", () => {
  assert.equal(throttleKey("free", "ip", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef"), "t:free:ip:0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef");
  const key = throttleKey("buybook", "email", "Lea@Example.com ");
  assert.match(key, /^t:buybook:e:[0-9a-f]{32}$/);
  assert.equal(key, throttleKey("buybook", "email", "lea@example.com"));
  assert.notEqual(key, throttleKey("buybook", "email", "leo@example.com"));
  assert.notEqual(key, throttleKey("shop", "email", "lea@example.com"));
  assert.ok(!key.includes("@") && !key.includes("lea"));
});

test("otpThrottleKey is the e-mail key of the « otp » action", () => {
  assert.equal(otpThrottleKey("Lea@Example.com"), throttleKey("otp", "email", "lea@example.com"));
  assert.match(otpThrottleKey("lea@example.com"), /^t:otp:e:[0-9a-f]{32}$/);
});

test("health keys live under the health: prefix", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(HEALTH_KEYS, {
    orphanPayments: "health:orphan_payments",
    invariantViolations: "health:invariant_violations",
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the failing DB throttle test.** Create `src/lib/throttle.db.test.ts`. It uses the Task 6 harness. Throttle rows are not tied to a pro, so every key is unique and deleted in `finally`. The health test restores the previous row. The `Db` type comes from `consumeThrottle`, because `src/lib/db/atomic.ts` only arrives in Task 14.

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { throttle } from "./db/schema";
import { dbTest, inParallel, testEmail } from "../test/db";
import {
  bumpHealth,
  checkActionThrottle,
  consumeThrottle,
  HEALTH_KEYS,
  nextWindow,
  readHealth,
  setHealth,
  throttleKey,
  type ThrottleState,
} from "./throttle";

type Db = Parameters<typeof consumeThrottle>[0];

const T0 = new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00Z");
const later = (sec: number) => new Date(T0.getTime() + sec * 1000);

async function dropKeys(db: Db, keys: readonly string[]): Promise<void> {
  await db.execute(sql`delete from throttle where key = any(${sql.param([...keys])}::text[])`);
}

async function readRow(db: Db, key: string): Promise<ThrottleState | null> {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(throttle).where(eq(throttle.key, key));
  return row ? { windowStart: row.windowStart, hits: row.hits } : null;
}

dbTest("consumeThrottle allows `limit` hits per window, refuses the next, reopens after the window", async ({ db }) => {
  const key = `t:test:ip:${nanoid()}`;
  const limit = { limit: 3, windowSec: 3600 };
  try {
    const instants = [later(0), later(60), later(120), later(180), later(3600), later(3660)];
    const allowed: boolean[] = [];
    let twin: ThrottleState | null = null;
    for (const at of instants) {
      allowed.push(await consumeThrottle(db, key, limit, at));
      twin = nextWindow(twin, at, limit.windowSec);
    }
    assert.deepEqual(allowed, [true, true, true, false, true, true]);
    assert.deepEqual(await readRow(db, key), twin);
    assert.deepEqual(twin, { windowStart: later(3600), hits: 2 });
  } finally {
    await dropKeys(db, [key]);
  }
});

dbTest("concurrent consumes are counted exactly once each", async ({ db }) => {
  const key = `t:test:ip:${nanoid()}`;
  try {
    const results = await inParallel(
      Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => () => consumeThrottle(db, key, { limit: 5, windowSec: 3600 }, T0)),
    );
    const allowed = results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled" && r.value === true).length;
    assert.equal(results.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected").length, 0);
    assert.equal(allowed, 5);
    assert.equal((await readRow(db, key))?.hits, 10);
  } finally {
    await dropKeys(db, [key]);
  }
});

dbTest("checkActionThrottle caps an e-mail at 6 per hour and an IP at 20 per hour", async ({ db }) => {
  const ip = nanoid(32);
  const otherIp = nanoid(32);
  const emails = Array.from({ length: 4 }, () => testEmail("thr"));
  const keys = [
    throttleKey("free", "ip", ip),
    throttleKey("free", "ip", otherIp),
    ...emails.map((e) => throttleKey("free", "email", e)),
  ];
  try {
    const first: boolean[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) first.push(await checkActionThrottle(db, { action: "free", ipHash: ip, email: emails[0] }, T0));
    assert.deepEqual(first, [true, true, true, true, true, true, false]);
    // Same IP, other addresses: the IP key reaches 20 on the 20th call overall.
    const more: boolean[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
      more.push(await checkActionThrottle(db, { action: "free", ipHash: ip, email: emails[1 + Math.floor(i / 6)] }, T0));
    }
    assert.deepEqual(more.slice(0, 13), Array(13).fill(true));
    assert.equal(more[13], false, "21st call from the IP");
    assert.equal((await readRow(db, keys[0]))?.hits, 21);
    // The e-mail is normalised before hashing.
    assert.equal(await checkActionThrottle(db, { action: "free", ipHash: otherIp, email: emails[0].toUpperCase() }, later(60)), false);
  } finally {
    await dropKeys(db, keys);
  }
});

dbTest("health counters: set, bump and read", async ({ db }) => {
  const key = HEALTH_KEYS.orphanPayments;
  const before = await readRow(db, key);
  try {
    await setHealth(db, key, 7, T0);
    await bumpHealth(db, key, 2, later(60));
    const health = await readHealth(db);
    assert.deepEqual(health[key], { value: 9, at: later(60) });
  } finally {
    if (before) {
      await db.update(throttle).set({ hits: before.hits, windowStart: before.windowStart }).where(eq(throttle.key, key));
    } else {
      await dropKeys(db, [key]);
    }
  }
});
```
