- [ ] **Step 8: Implement.** Create `src/lib/throttle.ts`. The consume statement is the binding SQL of contract §5.8.

```ts
/**
 * Application rate limits (spec § 12) and the admin health counters, both on
 * the `throttle` table (no professional_id). One fixed window per key: a
 * single atomic upsert either opens a new window or counts one more hit, so
 * concurrent requests are counted exactly. No IP or e-mail is stored in
 * clear: the caller hashes the IP with the day's salt (`ipHash`,
 * src/lib/visitor-hash.ts); the e-mail is hashed here with an unsalted
 * sha256, so its cap survives the daily salt rotation.
 * `health:*` rows hold counters, not windows: the nightly purge (E12) must
 * skip them. No server-only: the auth hook, the actions and the tests use it.
 */
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import type { NeonHttpDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/neon-http";

import * as schema from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { normalizeEmail, THROTTLE_LIMITS, THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC, type ThrottleAction } from "@/lib/tickets";

/** Same type as `Db` of src/lib/db/atomic.ts (Task 14), declared here because this module comes first. */
type ThrottleDb = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schema>;

export type ThrottleState = { windowStart: Date; hits: number };
export type ThrottleLimit = { limit: number; windowSec: number };

/** Pure twin of the SQL upsert: a new window when prev is null or windowStart ≤ now − windowSec, else hits + 1. */
export function nextWindow(prev: ThrottleState | null, now: Date, windowSec: number): ThrottleState {
  if (prev === null || prev.windowStart.getTime() <= now.getTime() - windowSec * 1000) {
    return { windowStart: now, hits: 1 };
  }
  return { windowStart: prev.windowStart, hits: prev.hits + 1 };
}

/** `t:<action>:ip:<ipHash>` / `t:<action>:e:<sha256(normalizedEmail) first 32 hex>`; action "otp" for the auth code cap. */
export function throttleKey(action: ThrottleAction | "otp", kind: "ip" | "email", value: string): string {
  if (kind === "ip") return `t:${action}:ip:${value}`;
  const digest = createHash("sha256").update(normalizeEmail(value)).digest("hex").slice(0, 32);
  return `t:${action}:e:${digest}`;
}

export function otpThrottleKey(email: string): string {
  return throttleKey("otp", "email", email);
}

/** Atomic upsert on `throttle` (one statement); true while hits ≤ limit. */
export async function consumeThrottle(db: ThrottleDb, key: string, limit: ThrottleLimit, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  const at = sql`${now.toISOString()}::timestamptz`;
  const elapsed = sql`throttle.window_start <= ${at} - make_interval(secs => ${limit.windowSec}::int)`;
  const result = await db.execute<{ hits: number }>(sql`
    insert into throttle (key, window_start, hits) values (${key}, ${at}, 1)
    on conflict (key) do update set
      window_start = case when ${elapsed} then ${at} else throttle.window_start end,
      hits = case when ${elapsed} then 1 else throttle.hits + 1 end
    returning hits`);
  return Number(result.rows[0]?.hits) <= limit.limit;
}

/** P4 actions: consumes the IP key and the e-mail key (THROTTLE_LIMITS, THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC); false if either is over. */
export async function checkActionThrottle(
  db: ThrottleDb,
  args: { action: ThrottleAction; ipHash: string; email: string },
  now: Date,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const limits = THROTTLE_LIMITS[args.action];
  const [ipOk, emailOk] = await Promise.all([
    consumeThrottle(db, throttleKey(args.action, "ip", args.ipHash), { limit: limits.ip, windowSec: THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC }, now),
    consumeThrottle(db, throttleKey(args.action, "email", args.email), { limit: limits.email, windowSec: THROTTLE_WINDOW_SEC }, now),
  ]);
  return ipOk && emailOk;
}

/* ───────────────────────── Health counters ───────────────────────── */

export const HEALTH_KEYS = {
  orphanPayments: "health:orphan_payments",
  invariantViolations: "health:invariant_violations",
} as const;
export type HealthKey = (typeof HEALTH_KEYS)[keyof typeof HEALTH_KEYS];

/** value += by; window_start = now (time of the last change). */
export async function bumpHealth(db: ThrottleDb, key: HealthKey, by: number, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  await db.execute(sql`
    insert into throttle (key, window_start, hits) values (${key}, ${now.toISOString()}::timestamptz, ${by}::int)
    on conflict (key) do update set hits = throttle.hits + excluded.hits, window_start = excluded.window_start`);
}

/** value = value; window_start = now. */
export async function setHealth(db: ThrottleDb, key: HealthKey, value: number, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  await db.execute(sql`
    insert into throttle (key, window_start, hits) values (${key}, ${now.toISOString()}::timestamptz, ${value}::int)
    on conflict (key) do update set hits = excluded.hits, window_start = excluded.window_start`);
}

export async function readHealth(db: ThrottleDb): Promise<Partial<Record<HealthKey, { value: number; at: Date }>>> {
  const keys: HealthKey[] = Object.values(HEALTH_KEYS);
  const rows = await db.select().from(schema.throttle).where(inArray(schema.throttle.key, keys));
  const out: Partial<Record<HealthKey, { value: number; at: Date }>> = {};
  for (const row of rows) {
    const key = keys.find((k) => k === row.key);
    if (key) out[key] = { value: row.hits, at: row.windowStart };
  }
  return out;
}
```

<!-- G3 -->

**Contract deviations (G3).** All are additive or fill gaps the contract leaves open. None renames a contract export or changes its signature.

1. **New test helper `src/test/rows.ts` (Task 14).** It holds raw builder inserts for ticket types, offers, customers, events, links, séances, RDV, purchases, bookings, tickets and movements. Tasks 14 to 17 need it because the engine that `fixtures.ts` uses (catalog, wallet) does not exist until Tasks 17 and 19. Invariant I4 needs a movement history, so `rowTicket` writes one by default. The file is not named `*.test.ts`.
2. **`types.ts` exports a third helper, `failOf(res)`.** It turns a failed `tryAtomic` result into an `EngineFail`, and maps `duplicate` to `stale`. Every engine writer can use it.
3. **`answers.ts` validation:**
   - It also exports `BOOLEAN_ANSWERS`.
   - `buildAnswers` also rejects a duplicate answer and a value longer than `BOUNDS.answer` (`invalid`).
   - A choice or boolean value that is not allowed fails with « Réponse invalide pour : <label> ». The contract did not fix this message.
4. **Eligibility queries:**
   - `ELIGIBLE` is wrapped in parentheses.
   - `pickTicket` and `usableTickets` also require `t.professional_id = o.professional_id`. This is a safety check and does not change results for valid ids.
5. **`checkInvariants` I2a also flags a booked ticket whose booking belongs to another customer.** Spec § 14 says the link is 1:1. All six invariants run in one read-only `db.batch`, so one round trip.
6. **`ensureHorizon` changes:**
   - After each version-guarded `generated_until` update, it runs `G((select version …) = v, 'stale')`. A segment that is split or deleted between the read and the batch therefore triggers the single re-plan that spec § 10 asks for, instead of a silent skip. Both readings of spec § 7 still hold: no phantom row, and the segment is regenerated.
   - `generated_until` is set to min(last rule date, horizon end), or to the last inserted date when the row cap is hit. A finished series (once, count or until) therefore has `generated_until ≥ last date`, and `minGeneratedUntil` ignores it.
   - When a capped call fits zero rows, `draftOccurrences` returns `generatedUntil = from − 1`.
7. **`createEvent` returns `not_found` for an unknown pro** (a programming error), in addition to `invalid` and `stale`.
8. **`createCustomer`:**
   - It trims the e-mail before zod, because `z.email()` rejects surrounding spaces (verified).
   - It keeps the existing phone when none is given.
   - `created` comes from `RETURNING (xmax = 0)`.
9. **`offerTickets` returns `invalid` for an expiry that is not after `now`, or a note longer than `BOUNDS.note`.** As a result, when the caller gives no `now`, the fixture `giveTickets` acts at the wall clock. If the expiry is less than 1 s ahead or already past, it acts 1 s before that expiry instead. An expiry that a test built from its own earlier clock (e.g. `addMinutes(now, 1)`) therefore never makes the fixture throw, however slow the round trips since then. An explicit `now` is still honoured (documented in `fixtures.ts`).
10. **Catalog details:**
    - `updateOffer` and `deleteOffer` treat an archived offer as `not_found`.
    - Offer `position` is max + 1 within its type.
    - `setAvailabilityRules` accepts at most 168 rules.
    - An empty label or description is stored as NULL.
11. **Extra tests in `eligibility.db.test.ts`.** It also tests the DB fragments `assertHoldQuota`, `expireElapsedHolds`, `loadQuestions` and `insertAnswers`. The file map has no other DB file for holds and answers; `holds.test.ts` stays pure.
12. **`revenue.ts` also exports the type `RevenueScope`.** `occurrences.db.test.ts` contains one plain `test()` for `horizonEnd`/`needsHorizon`, which runs with the DB suite.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing pure test for `mapDbError`.** Create `src/lib/db/map-db-error.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { mapDbError } from "./map-db-error";

/** A NeonDbError as thrown inside db.batch. */
function pg(code: string, extra: { constraint?: string; message?: string } = {}): Error {
  return Object.assign(new Error(extra.message ?? `pg ${code}`), { code, constraint: extra.constraint });
}

/** The same error as thrown outside a batch (DrizzleQueryError with a cause). */
function wrapped(inner: Error): Error {
  return new Error("Failed query: …\nparams: …", { cause: inner });
}

const both = (inner: Error) => [inner, wrapped(inner)];

test("P0001 skedul:already_paid is a duplicate (success without effect)", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("P0001", { message: "skedul:already_paid" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "duplicate" });
  }
});

test("P0001 skedul:<engine code> maps to that code", () => {
  for (const code of ["changed", "stale", "unavailable", "no_ticket", "too_many_holds", "too_late_or_done", "not_found"] as const) {
    for (const err of both(pg("P0001", { message: `skedul:${code}` }))) {
      assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code });
    }
  }
});

test("P0001 with an unknown skedul code, or without the prefix, is unknown", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("P0001", { message: "skedul:boom" }), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("P0001", { message: "custom raise" }), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
});

test("23514 maps named CHECKs: seats → full, retire on a paid ticket → retire_paid, others → invalid", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("23514", { constraint: "occurrence_seats_chk" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code: "full" });
  }
  for (const err of both(pg("23514", { constraint: "ticket_retire_paid_chk" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code: "retire_paid" });
  }
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("23514", { constraint: "event_capacity_chk" }), "write"), { type: "code", code: "invalid" });
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("23514"), "delete"), { type: "code", code: "invalid" });
});

test("23505 booking_active_uq → already_booked; any other unique violation is unknown (rethrown)", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("23505", { constraint: "booking_active_uq" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code: "already_booked" });
  }
  for (const constraint of ["ticket_movement_action_ticket_uq", "ticket_pkey", "customer_pro_email_uq"]) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("23505", { constraint }), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
  }
});

test("23P01 occurrence_rdv_excl → slot_taken; another exclusion is unknown", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("23P01", { constraint: "occurrence_rdv_excl" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code: "slot_taken" });
  }
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("23P01", { constraint: "other_excl" }), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
});

test("23503 → in_use when deleting, stale when writing", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("23503", { constraint: "ticket_ticket_type_id_ticket_type_id_fk" }))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "delete"), { type: "code", code: "in_use" });
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "code", code: "stale" });
  }
});

test("40P01 is a deadlock (atomic replays the batch once)", () => {
  for (const err of both(pg("40P01"))) {
    assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(err, "write"), { type: "deadlock" });
  }
});

test("anything else is unknown", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(pg("22001"), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError(new Error("fetch failed"), "write"), { type: "unknown" });
  assert.deepEqual(mapDbError("not an error", "write"), { type: "unknown" });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `mapDbError`.** Create `src/lib/db/map-db-error.ts` (contract §5.10, verified):

```ts
/**
 * Postgres error → engine outcome (spec § 10 table). Pure; reads both error
 * shapes through pgErrorInfo (in batch: NeonDbError; outside: DrizzleQueryError.cause).
 */
import { isEngineErrorCode, type EngineErrorCode } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { pgErrorInfo, skedulCode } from "./pg-error";

export type MappedDbError =
  | { type: "code"; code: EngineErrorCode }
  /** skedul:already_paid: a duplicate fulfillment, a success without effect. */
  | { type: "duplicate" }
  /** 40P01: atomic() replays the same batch once. */
  | { type: "deadlock" }
  /** Anything else (incl. 23505 other than booking_active_uq): rethrow. */
  | { type: "unknown" };

/** Named CHECKs with their own code; every other named CHECK maps to `invalid`. */
export const CHECK_CODES: Readonly<Record<string, EngineErrorCode>> = {
  occurrence_seats_chk: "full",
  ticket_retire_paid_chk: "retire_paid",
};

export function mapDbError(err: unknown, ctx: "write" | "delete"): MappedDbError {
  const info = pgErrorInfo(err);
  switch (info.code) {
    case "P0001": {
      const code = skedulCode(err);
      if (code === "already_paid") return { type: "duplicate" };
      return code && isEngineErrorCode(code) ? { type: "code", code } : { type: "unknown" };
    }
    case "23514":
      return { type: "code", code: (info.constraint && CHECK_CODES[info.constraint]) || "invalid" };
    case "23505":
      return info.constraint === "booking_active_uq"
        ? { type: "code", code: "already_booked" }
        : { type: "unknown" };
    case "23P01":
      return info.constraint === "occurrence_rdv_excl"
        ? { type: "code", code: "slot_taken" }
        : { type: "unknown" };
    case "23503":
      return { type: "code", code: ctx === "delete" ? "in_use" : "stale" };
    case "40P01":
      return { type: "deadlock" };
    default:
      return { type: "unknown" };
  }
}
```

```ts
/**
 * Raw row builders for the engine-core DB tests (Tasks 14 to 17), usable
 * before the catalog and wallet engine exist. Builder inserts, nanoid ids,
 * every timestamp from `now`. They bypass every engine guard on purpose: a test
 * that keeps the default invariant check (src/test/db.ts) must still leave a
 * consistent state. Later tests use the engine-backed src/test/fixtures.ts.
 */
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import type { Db } from "../lib/db/atomic";
import * as schema from "../lib/db/schema";
import type {
  BookingCoverage,
  BookingMode,
  BookingStatus,
  MovementKind,
  OccurrenceStatus,
  PurchaseStatus,
  TicketStatus,
} from "../lib/tickets";
import { testEmail, type TestPro } from "./db";

const MINUTE = 60_000;
const DAY = 86_400_000;

type TypeOverrides = Omit<Partial<typeof schema.ticketType.$inferInsert>, "id" | "professionalId">;

export async function rowType(db: Db, pro: TestPro, over: TypeOverrides = {}, now = new Date()): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.ticketType).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    name: `Type ${id}`,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
    ...over,
  });
  return id;
}

/** A paid RDV libre type (60 min). */
export function rowRdvType(db: Db, pro: TestPro, over: TypeOverrides = {}): Promise<string> {
  return rowType(db, pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60, ...over });
}

export async function rowOffer(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  ticketTypeId: string,
  over: { quantity?: number; priceCents?: number; archivedAt?: Date | null } = {},
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.ticketOffer).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId,
    quantity: over.quantity ?? 1,
    priceCents: over.priceCents ?? 2500,
    archivedAt: over.archivedAt ?? null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function rowCustomer(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  over: { email?: string; name?: string; userId?: string | null } = {},
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.customer).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    email: over.email ?? testEmail(),
    name: over.name ?? `Client ${id}`,
    userId: over.userId ?? null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

/** A one-off event row (no séance: add them with rowSeance). */
export async function rowEvent(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  over: { bookingMode?: BookingMode; capacity?: number; isPrivate?: boolean; archivedAt?: Date | null } = {},
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  await db.insert(schema.event).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    title: `Event ${id}`,
    bookingMode: over.bookingMode ?? "tickets",
    isPrivate: over.isPrivate ?? false,
    capacity: over.capacity ?? 12,
    durationMin: 60,
    startDate: "2026-01-01",
    startMinute: 600,
    freq: "once",
    interval: 1,
    endMode: "count",
    occurrenceCount: 1,
    generatedUntil: "2026-01-01",
    seriesRootId: id,
    archivedAt: over.archivedAt ?? null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function rowLink(db: Db, pro: TestPro, eventId: string, ticketTypeId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<void> {
  await db.insert(schema.eventTicketType).values({ eventId, ticketTypeId, professionalId: pro.proId, createdAt: now });
}

/** A séance of `eventId`, 60 min, starting `startsAt` (default now + 2 days). */
export async function rowSeance(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  eventId: string,
  over: { startsAt?: Date; capacity?: number; seatsTaken?: number; status?: OccurrenceStatus; seriesDate?: string } = {},
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  const startsAt = over.startsAt ?? new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY);
  const endsAt = new Date(startsAt.getTime() + 60 * MINUTE);
  const status = over.status ?? "scheduled";
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    eventId,
    seriesDate: over.seriesDate ?? startsAt.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    startsAt,
    endsAt,
    blockStart: startsAt,
    blockEnd: endsAt,
    capacity: over.capacity ?? 12,
    seatsTaken: over.seatsTaken ?? 0,
    status,
    cancelledAt: status === "cancelled" ? now : null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

/** A booked RDV libre of `ticketTypeId` (capacity 1), default seats_taken 1 and starting now + 2 days. */
export async function rowRdv(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  ticketTypeId: string,
  over: { startsAt?: Date; durationMin?: number; seatsTaken?: number; status?: OccurrenceStatus } = {},
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  const startsAt = over.startsAt ?? new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY);
  const endsAt = new Date(startsAt.getTime() + (over.durationMin ?? 60) * MINUTE);
  const status = over.status ?? "scheduled";
  await db.insert(schema.occurrence).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId,
    startsAt,
    endsAt,
    blockStart: startsAt,
    blockEnd: endsAt,
    capacity: 1,
    seatsTaken: over.seatsTaken ?? 1,
    status,
    cancelledAt: status === "cancelled" ? now : null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function rowPurchase(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  args: {
    customerId: string;
    offerId: string;
    ticketTypeId: string;
    status?: PurchaseStatus;
    quantity?: number;
    amountCents?: number;
    currency?: string;
    livemode?: boolean;
    paymentIntentId?: string | null;
    /** default: a fresh id when paid, else null */
    fulfilledActionId?: string | null;
    paidAt?: Date;
  },
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  const status = args.status ?? "pending";
  await db.insert(schema.ticketPurchase).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    customerId: args.customerId,
    offerId: args.offerId,
    ticketTypeId: args.ticketTypeId,
    label: "Offre de test",
    quantity: args.quantity ?? 1,
    amountCents: args.amountCents ?? 2500,
    currency: args.currency ?? "eur",
    status,
    livemode: args.livemode ?? false,
    checkoutExpiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 31 * MINUTE),
    paymentIntentId: args.paymentIntentId ?? null,
    paidAt: status === "paid" ? (args.paidAt ?? now) : null,
    fulfilledActionId:
      args.fulfilledActionId === undefined ? (status === "paid" ? nanoid() : null) : args.fulfilledActionId,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function rowBooking(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  args: {
    occurrenceId: string;
    customerId: string;
    status?: BookingStatus;
    coverage?: BookingCoverage;
    purchaseId?: string | null;
    holdExpiresAt?: Date;
  },
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  const status = args.status ?? "confirmed";
  const ended = status === "cancelled" || status === "expired";
  await db.insert(schema.booking).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: args.occurrenceId,
    customerId: args.customerId,
    status,
    coverage: args.coverage ?? "guest",
    purchaseId: args.purchaseId ?? null,
    createdBy: "pro",
    holdExpiresAt: status === "pending" ? (args.holdExpiresAt ?? new Date(now.getTime() + 45 * MINUTE)) : null,
    confirmedAt: status === "confirmed" ? now : null,
    endedAt: ended ? now : null,
    endedBy: ended ? "system" : null,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  return id;
}

export async function rowMovement(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  args: {
    ticketId: string;
    customerId: string;
    ticketTypeId: string;
    kind: MovementKind;
    fromStatus: TicketStatus | null;
    toStatus: TicketStatus;
    bookingId?: string | null;
    purchaseId?: string | null;
  },
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<void> {
  await db.insert(schema.ticketMovement).values({
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    customerId: args.customerId,
    ticketId: args.ticketId,
    actionId: nanoid(),
    kind: args.kind,
    fromStatus: args.fromStatus,
    toStatus: args.toStatus,
    ticketTypeId: args.ticketTypeId,
    bookingId: args.bookingId ?? null,
    purchaseId: args.purchaseId ?? null,
    actor: "system",
    createdAt: now,
  });
}

const SECOND_MOVE: Record<Exclude<TicketStatus, "available">, MovementKind> = {
  booked: "spend",
  used: "pointage",
  expired: "expire",
  closed: "close",
};

/**
 * A ticket in `status` (booked needs bookingId) with the movements that lead
 * there (purchase or offer, then spend / pointage / expire / close), unless
 * `movements: false` (invariant I4 tests).
 */
export async function rowTicket(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  args: {
    customerId: string;
    ticketTypeId: string;
    status?: TicketStatus;
    bookingId?: string | null;
    purchaseId?: string | null;
    expiresAt?: Date | null;
    movements?: boolean;
  },
  now = new Date(),
): Promise<string> {
  const id = nanoid();
  const status = args.status ?? "available";
  await db.insert(schema.ticket).values({
    id,
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    customerId: args.customerId,
    ticketTypeId: args.ticketTypeId,
    purchaseId: args.purchaseId ?? null,
    status,
    bookingId: status === "booked" ? (args.bookingId ?? null) : null,
    expiresAt: args.expiresAt ?? null,
    closedReason: status === "closed" ? "autre" : null,
    statusChangedAt: now,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  if (args.movements === false) return id;
  const base = { ticketId: id, customerId: args.customerId, ticketTypeId: args.ticketTypeId, purchaseId: args.purchaseId ?? null };
  await rowMovement(
    db,
    pro,
    { ...base, kind: args.purchaseId ? "purchase" : "offer", fromStatus: null, toStatus: "available" },
    now,
  );
  if (status !== "available") {
    await rowMovement(
      db,
      pro,
      {
        ...base,
        kind: SECOND_MOVE[status],
        fromStatus: "available",
        toStatus: status,
        bookingId: status === "booked" ? (args.bookingId ?? null) : null,
      },
      now,
    );
  }
  return id;
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { addMinutes, dbTest, testEmail } from "../../test/db";
import {
  rowBooking,
  rowCustomer,
  rowEvent,
  rowOffer,
  rowPurchase,
  rowRdv,
  rowRdvType,
  rowSeance,
  rowTicket,
  rowType,
} from "../../test/rows";
import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  R,
  RET,
  SEAT,
  atomic,
  lockRows,
  textArray,
  tryAtomic,
  ts,
  type BatchCtx,
} from "./atomic";
import { pgErrorInfo } from "./pg-error";
import * as schema from "./schema";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

async function occurrenceRow(db: BatchCtx["db"], id: string) {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.occurrence).where(eq(schema.occurrence.id, id));
  return row;
}

async function bookingRow(db: BatchCtx["db"], id: string) {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.booking).where(eq(schema.booking.id, id));
  return row;
}

dbTest("atomic: an empty batch sends nothing and keeps the given aid", async ({ db }) => {
  const done = await atomic(db, async () => () => [], { aid: "fixed-aid" });
  assert.deepEqual(done, { aid: "fixed-aid", results: [] });
});

dbTest("G: a false predicate rolls back the statements before it", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const id = nanoid();
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db.insert(schema.customer).values({
      id,
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      email: testEmail(),
      name: "Rollback",
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    }),
    G(db, sql`false`, "stale"),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(res, { ok: false, code: "stale" });
  const rows = await db.select().from(schema.customer).where(eq(schema.customer.id, id));
  assert.equal(rows.length, 0);
});

dbTest("G: a NULL predicate (a row that vanished) raises too", async ({ db }) => {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    G(db, sql`(select name from customer where id = ${"missing"}) = 'x'`, "not_found"),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(res, { ok: false, code: "not_found" });
});

dbTest("tryAtomic: a PlanError becomes its code and detail", async ({ db }) => {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    throw new PlanError("not_found", "no such occurrence");
  });
  assert.deepEqual(res, { ok: false, code: "not_found", message: "no such occurrence" });
});

dbTest("tryAtomic: skedul:already_paid is reported as duplicate", async ({ db }) => {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [G(db, sql`false`, "already_paid")]);
  assert.deepEqual(res, { ok: false, code: "duplicate" });
});

dbTest("tryAtomic: replanOnStale re-runs plan() exactly once, and only for stale", async ({ db }) => {
  let plans = 0;
  const failWith = (code: "stale" | "full") => async () => {
    plans++;
    return () => [G(db, sql`false`, code)];
  };

  assert.deepEqual(await tryAtomic(db, failWith("stale")), { ok: false, code: "stale" });
  assert.equal(plans, 1, "no re-plan without the option");

  plans = 0;
  assert.deepEqual(await tryAtomic(db, failWith("stale"), { replanOnStale: true }), { ok: false, code: "stale" });
  assert.equal(plans, 2, "one re-plan, then stale is returned");

  plans = 0;
  assert.deepEqual(await tryAtomic(db, failWith("full"), { replanOnStale: true }), { ok: false, code: "full" });
  assert.equal(plans, 1, "other codes are never re-planned");

  plans = 0;
  const staleOnce = async () => {
    plans++;
    const pass = plans > 1;
    return () => [G(db, sql.raw(pass ? "true" : "false"), "stale")];
  };
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, staleOnce, { replanOnStale: true });
  assert.equal(res.ok, true);
  assert.equal(plans, 2);
});

dbTest("tryAtomic: afterPlan runs once, between the first plan and its batch", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  let plans = 0;
  let hooks = 0;
  let staged: string | null = null;
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      plans++;
      const first = plans === 1;
      return () => [
        // the row staged by the hook is visible to the FIRST batch: the hook ran before it
        G(db, sql`exists (select 1 from customer where id = ${staged ?? "none"})`, "not_found"),
        G(db, sql.raw(first ? "false" : "true"), "stale"),
      ];
    },
    {
      replanOnStale: true,
      afterPlan: async () => {
        hooks++;
        staged = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
      },
    },
  );
  assert.equal(res.ok, true);
  assert.equal(plans, 2);
  assert.equal(hooks, 1, "the hook is not re-run by the re-plan");
});

dbTest("textArray binds ONE text[] parameter; lockRows dedupes and sorts", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const a = await rowCustomer(db, pro, { name: "A" });
  const b = await rowCustomer(db, pro, { name: "B" });
  const c = await rowCustomer(db, pro, { name: "C" });
  const done = await atomic(db, async () => () => [
    lockRows(db, "customer", [c, a, b, a]),
    db.execute(sql`update customer set name = 'Renamed' where id = any(${textArray([a, b])})`),
  ]);
  const locked = done.results[0] as { rows: { id: string }[] };
  assert.deepEqual(
    locked.rows.map((r) => r.id),
    [a, b, c].sort(),
  );
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: schema.customer.id, name: schema.customer.name })
    .from(schema.customer)
    .where(inArray(schema.customer.id, [a, b, c]));
  const names = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.name]));
  assert.deepEqual(names, { [a]: "Renamed", [b]: "Renamed", [c]: "C" });
});

dbTest("R + SEAT(o, 0): an elapsed hold expires and an RDV left with 0 seats is cancelled", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await rowOffer(db, pro, typeId);
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const purchaseId = await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
  const o = await rowRdv(db, pro, typeId, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY) });
  const b = await rowBooking(db, pro, {
    occurrenceId: o,
    customerId,
    status: "pending",
    coverage: "ticket",
    purchaseId,
    holdExpiresAt: addMinutes(now, -1),
  });

  const done = await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    return [lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o]), R(c, o), SEAT(c, o, 0)];
  });

  const occ = await occurrenceRow(db, o);
  assert.equal(occ.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal(occ.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(occ.cancelledAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  const bk = await bookingRow(db, b);
  assert.equal(bk.status, "expired");
  assert.equal(bk.endedBy, "system");
  assert.equal(bk.endedActionId, done.aid);
  assert.equal(bk.endedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
});

dbTest("R only expires elapsed holds; SEAT counts them once", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await rowOffer(db, pro, typeId);
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro, { capacity: 3 });
  const o = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId, { capacity: 3, seatsTaken: 2 });
  const holds: string[] = [];
  for (const holdExpiresAt of [addMinutes(now, -5), addMinutes(now, 30)]) {
    const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
    const purchaseId = await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
    holds.push(
      await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: o, customerId, status: "pending", coverage: "ticket", purchaseId, holdExpiresAt }),
    );
  }

  await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    return [lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o]), R(c, o), SEAT(c, o, 0)];
  });

  assert.equal((await bookingRow(db, holds[0])).status, "expired");
  assert.equal((await bookingRow(db, holds[1])).status, "pending");
  const occ = await occurrenceRow(db, o);
  assert.equal(occ.seatsTaken, 1);
  assert.equal(occ.status, "scheduled", "an event séance is never cancelled by SEAT");
});

dbTest("SEAT(o, +1) on a full occurrence maps to full", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const o = await rowRdv(db, pro, typeId);
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: o, customerId: await rowCustomer(db, pro), coverage: "guest" });
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    return [lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o]), SEAT(c, o, 1)];
  });
  assert.deepEqual(res, { ok: false, code: "full" });
  assert.equal((await occurrenceRow(db, o)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("SEAT extraSeat (pro « +1 place ») raises the capacity of a full séance", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro, { bookingMode: "free", capacity: 1 });
  const o = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId, { capacity: 1, seatsTaken: 1 });
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: o, customerId: await rowCustomer(db, pro), coverage: "free" });
  const second = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const now = new Date();
  await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    return [
      lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o]),
      SEAT(c, o, 1, { extraSeat: true }),
      db.insert(schema.booking).values({
        id: nanoid(),
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        occurrenceId: o,
        customerId: second,
        status: "confirmed",
        coverage: "guest",
        createdBy: "pro",
        confirmedAt: now,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      }),
    ];
  });
  const occ = await occurrenceRow(db, o);
  assert.equal(occ.seatsTaken, 2);
  assert.equal(occ.capacity, 2);
  assert.equal(occ.capacityOverride, true);
});

dbTest("RET gives back every ticket of the bookings this action ended, one return movement each", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const rdvA = await rowRdv(db, pro, typeId, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY) });
  const rdvB = await rowRdv(db, pro, typeId, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 3 * DAY) });
  const bookingA = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: rdvA, customerId, coverage: "ticket" });
  const bookingB = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: rdvB, customerId, coverage: "ticket" });
  const ticketA = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, status: "booked", bookingId: bookingA });
  const ticketB = await rowTicket(db, pro, {
    customerId,
    ticketTypeId: typeId,
    status: "booked",
    bookingId: bookingB,
    expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() - 3_600_000),
  });

  const done = await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    return [
      lockRows(db, "occurrence", [rdvB, rdvA]),
      db.execute(sql`
        update booking set status = 'cancelled', ended_at = ${ts(now)}, ended_by = 'pro',
          ended_action_id = ${aid}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
        where id = any(${textArray([bookingA, bookingB])})`),
      SEAT(c, rdvA, 0),
      SEAT(c, rdvB, 0),
      RET(c, { actor: "pro", userId: pro.userId }),
    ];
  });

  const tickets = await db.select().from(schema.ticket).where(inArray(schema.ticket.id, [ticketA, ticketB]));
  const byId = new Map(tickets.map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  assert.equal(byId.get(ticketA)?.status, "available");
  assert.equal(byId.get(ticketA)?.bookingId, null);
  assert.equal(byId.get(ticketB)?.status, "expired", "a ticket past its expiry comes back expired");
  assert.equal(byId.get(ticketB)?.bookingId, null);

  const moves = await db
    .select()
    .from(schema.ticketMovement)
    .where(eq(schema.ticketMovement.actionId, done.aid));
  assert.equal(moves.length, 2);
  const moveOf = new Map(moves.map((m) => [m.ticketId, m]));
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketA)?.kind, "return");
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketA)?.fromStatus, "booked");
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketA)?.toStatus, "available");
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketA)?.bookingId, bookingA);
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketA)?.actorUserId, pro.userId);
  assert.equal(moveOf.get(ticketB)?.toStatus, "expired");

  for (const o of [rdvA, rdvB]) {
    const occ = await occurrenceRow(db, o);
    assert.equal(occ.status, "cancelled");
    assert.equal(occ.seatsTaken, 0);
  }
});

dbTest("23503: stale when writing, in_use when deleting", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const write = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db.insert(schema.booking).values({
      id: nanoid(),
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      occurrenceId: "missing-occurrence",
      customerId,
      status: "confirmed",
      coverage: "guest",
      createdBy: "pro",
      confirmedAt: now,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    }),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(write, { ok: false, code: "stale" });

  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
  const del = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [db.delete(schema.ticketType).where(eq(schema.ticketType.id, typeId))], {
    ctx: "delete",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(del, { ok: false, code: "in_use" });
});

dbTest("tryAtomic rethrows a unique violation other than booking_active_uq", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const email = testEmail();
  const customerRow = () => ({
    id: nanoid(),
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    email,
    name: "Twin",
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });
  await assert.rejects(
    tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
      db.insert(schema.customer).values(customerRow()),
      db.insert(schema.customer).values(customerRow()),
    ]),
    (err: unknown) => {
      assert.equal(pgErrorInfo(err).code, "23505");
      assert.equal(pgErrorInfo(err).constraint, "customer_pro_email_uq");
      return true;
    },
  );
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement `atomic`.** Create `src/lib/db/atomic.ts` (contract §5.9, verified):

```ts
/**
 * The one write mechanism of the booking domain (spec § 10). Every multi-row
 * write is ONE `db.batch` = one non-interactive READ COMMITTED transaction:
 * lock first, small statements, every guard followed by a raising assert, ids
 * made in JS, rows marked by the action id `aid`, the clock passed in as $now.
 * No server-only: the actions, the seed and the DB tests all use it.
 */
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import { sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";
import type { BatchItem } from "drizzle-orm/batch";
import type { NeonHttpDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/neon-http";

import type { Actor, EngineErrorCode } from "@/lib/tickets";
import type * as schema from "./schema";
import { mapDbError } from "./map-db-error";

export type Db = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schema>;
export type Query = BatchItem<"pg">;
/** What `plan()` returns once its reads are done: the batch for one action id. */
export type BuildBatch = (aid: string) => Query[];
/** Shared by every fragment builder of one batch. */
export type BatchCtx = { db: Db; aid: string; now: Date };
export type AtomicOk = { aid: string; results: unknown[] };
export type AssertCode = EngineErrorCode | "already_paid";
export type ActorRef = { actor: Actor; userId: string | null };

/** Thrown by a `plan()` read that already knows the answer (not found, invalid input…). */
export class PlanError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: EngineErrorCode,
    readonly detail?: string,
  ) {
    super(detail ?? code);
    this.name = "PlanError";
  }
}

export function newActionId(): string {
  return nanoid();
}

/**
 * Runs `plan()` (reads + JS ids), then its batch in one transaction. A 40P01
 * replays the SAME statements once. Every other error is thrown as is.
 */
export async function atomic(
  db: Db,
  plan: () => Promise<BuildBatch>,
  opts: { aid?: string; afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<AtomicOk> {
  const build = await plan();
  if (opts.afterPlan) await opts.afterPlan();
  const aid = opts.aid ?? newActionId();
  const queries = build(aid);
  if (queries.length === 0) return { aid, results: [] };
  const run = () => db.batch(queries as [Query, ...Query[]]);
  try {
    return { aid, results: await run() };
  } catch (err) {
    if (mapDbError(err, "write").type !== "deadlock") throw err;
    return { aid, results: await run() };
  }
}

export type TryAtomicResult =
  | ({ ok: true } & AtomicOk)
  | { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode; message?: string }
  | { ok: false; code: "duplicate" };

/**
 * `atomic` + mapping (src/lib/db/map-db-error.ts). PlanError → its code.
 * With `replanOnStale`, a `stale` re-runs `plan()` once: new reads, new ids,
 * new aid (E7 following, E8, ensureHorizon). `afterPlan` (tests only) runs
 * between the FIRST plan and its batch, to stage a deterministic race.
 * Unknown errors (programming errors, other 23505) are rethrown.
 */
export async function tryAtomic(
  db: Db,
  plan: () => Promise<BuildBatch>,
  opts: { ctx?: "write" | "delete"; replanOnStale?: boolean; afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<TryAtomicResult> {
  const ctx = opts.ctx ?? "write";
  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
    try {
      const done = await atomic(db, plan, { afterPlan: attempt === 0 ? opts.afterPlan : undefined });
      return { ok: true, ...done };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof PlanError) return { ok: false, code: err.code, message: err.detail };
      const mapped = mapDbError(err, ctx);
      if (mapped.type === "duplicate") return { ok: false, code: "duplicate" };
      if (mapped.type !== "code") throw err;
      if (mapped.code === "stale" && opts.replanOnStale && attempt === 0) continue;
      return { ok: false, code: mapped.code };
    }
  }
}

/* ───────────────────────── Parameter helpers ───────────────────────── */

/** A Date as a timestamptz parameter (`$n::timestamptz`). */
export function ts(d: Date): SQL {
  return sql`${d.toISOString()}::timestamptz`;
}

/** Strings as ONE text[] parameter. A bare JS array would expand to ($1, $2…). */
export function textArray(values: readonly string[]): SQL {
  return sql`${sql.param([...values])}::text[]`;
}

/** Dates as ONE timestamptz[] parameter. */
export function tsArray(values: readonly Date[]): SQL {
  return sql`${sql.param(values.map((d) => d.toISOString()))}::timestamptz[]`;
}

/** YYYY-MM-DD keys as ONE date[] parameter. */
export function dateArray(keys: readonly string[]): SQL {
  return sql`${sql.param([...keys])}::date[]`;
}

/* ───────────────────────── Statements ───────────────────────── */

/** `select skedul_assert(pred, code)`: raises P0001 `skedul:<code>` unless pred IS TRUE. */
export function G(db: Db, pred: SQL, code: AssertCode): Query {
  return db.execute(sql`select skedul_assert((${pred})::boolean, ${code}::text)`);
}

export type LockTable = "professional" | "event" | "occurrence" | "customer" | "booking" | "ticket_purchase";

/**
 * `select id from <table> where id = any($ids) order by id for update`.
 * Global order (spec § 10): professional → event → occurrence → customer →
 * booking → purchase → ticket; ascending id inside a table.
 */
export function lockRows(db: Db, table: LockTable, ids: readonly string[]): Query {
  const sorted = [...new Set(ids)].sort();
  return db.execute(
    sql`select id from ${sql.identifier(table)} where id = any(${textArray(sorted)}) order by id for update`,
  );
}

/** R(o): lazy release of elapsed holds on an occurrence this batch already locked. */
export function R(c: BatchCtx, occurrenceId: string): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update booking set status = 'expired', ended_at = ${ts(c.now)}, ended_by = 'system',
      ended_action_id = ${c.aid}, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    where occurrence_id = ${occurrenceId} and status = 'pending' and hold_expires_at < ${ts(c.now)}`);
}

/** rel(o): bookings of o ended by this action, evaluated INSIDE the SEAT statement. */
export function rel(c: BatchCtx, occurrenceId: string): SQL {
  return sql`(select count(*)::int from booking where occurrence_id = ${occurrenceId} and ended_action_id = ${c.aid})`;
}

/**
 * SEAT(o, delta): seats_taken += delta − rel(o). Called ONCE per occurrence and
 * action, after every booking end on o in this batch. An RDV falling to 0 seats
 * is cancelled. occurrence_seats_chk raises `full`. `extraSeat` (pro « +1
 * place », event séances only) raises capacity to fit.
 */
export function SEAT(c: BatchCtx, occurrenceId: string, delta: -1 | 0 | 1, opts: { extraSeat?: boolean } = {}): Query {
  const next = sql`(seats_taken + ${delta}::int - ${rel(c, occurrenceId)})`;
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update occurrence set
      seats_taken = ${next},
      capacity = ${opts.extraSeat ? sql`greatest(capacity, ${next})` : sql`capacity`},
      capacity_override = ${opts.extraSeat ? sql`(capacity_override or ${next} > capacity)` : sql`capacity_override`},
      status = case when event_id is null and ${next} <= 0 then 'cancelled'::occurrence_status else status end,
      cancelled_at = case when event_id is null and ${next} <= 0 then coalesce(cancelled_at, ${ts(c.now)}) else cancelled_at end,
      updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    where id = ${occurrenceId}`);
}

/**
 * RET(aid): ONCE per action, gives back the tickets of every booking ended by
 * this action (→ available, or expired if past expires_at), one `return`
 * movement per ticket, in one statement.
 */
export function RET(c: BatchCtx, by: ActorRef): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    with u as (
      update ticket t set
        status = case when t.expires_at is not null and t.expires_at <= ${ts(c.now)}
          then 'expired'::ticket_status else 'available'::ticket_status end,
        booking_id = null, status_changed_at = ${ts(c.now)}, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
      from booking b
      where t.booking_id = b.id and b.ended_action_id = ${c.aid}
      returning t.id, t.professional_id, t.customer_id, t.ticket_type_id, t.purchase_id, t.status, b.id as booking_id
    )
    insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status,
      to_status, ticket_type_id, booking_id, purchase_id, actor, actor_user_id, created_at)
    select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${c.aid}, 'return', 'booked', status,
      ticket_type_id, booking_id, purchase_id, ${by.actor}::actor, ${by.userId}, ${ts(c.now)}
    from u`);
}

/**
 * Aggregate seat release (E4 step 3, sweepHolds): for every occurrence with
 * bookings ended by this action, seats_taken −= N; an RDV at 0 is cancelled.
 * Never combine with SEAT on the same occurrence in one action.
 */
export function releaseEndedSeats(c: BatchCtx): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update occurrence o set
      seats_taken = o.seats_taken - r.n,
      status = case when o.event_id is null and o.seats_taken - r.n <= 0 then 'cancelled'::occurrence_status else o.status end,
      cancelled_at = case when o.event_id is null and o.seats_taken - r.n <= 0 then coalesce(o.cancelled_at, ${ts(c.now)}) else o.cancelled_at end,
      updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    from (select occurrence_id, count(*)::int as n from booking where ended_action_id = ${c.aid} group by occurrence_id) r
    where o.id = r.occurrence_id`);
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { count, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

import { ticketMovement, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";

import { dbTest, type DbHarness } from "../../test/db";
import {
  rowBooking,
  rowCustomer,
  rowEvent,
  rowMovement,
  rowOffer,
  rowPurchase,
  rowRdv,
  rowRdvType,
  rowSeance,
  rowTicket,
  rowType,
} from "../../test/rows";
import { checkInvariants, type InvariantId } from "./invariants";
import { paidPurchaseOf, revenueByCurrency } from "./revenue";

const BAD = { invariants: false };

async function expectViolation(h: DbHarness, invariant: InvariantId, rowId: string): Promise<void> {
  const found = await checkInvariants(h.db, { proId: h.pro.proId });
  assert.ok(
    found.some((v) => v.invariant === invariant && v.rowId === rowId),
    `expected ${invariant} on ${rowId}, got ${JSON.stringify(found)}`,
  );
}

dbTest("a consistent pro has no violation", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await rowOffer(db, pro, typeId, { quantity: 2 });
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);
  const seance = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId, { seatsTaken: 2 });
  const alice = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const bob = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const paid = await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId: alice, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, status: "paid", quantity: 2 });
  const aliceBooking = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId: alice, coverage: "ticket" });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId: alice, ticketTypeId: typeId, purchaseId: paid, status: "booked", bookingId: aliceBooking });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId: alice, ticketTypeId: typeId, purchaseId: paid });
  const pending = await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId: bob, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId: bob, status: "pending", coverage: "ticket", purchaseId: pending });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId: bob, ticketTypeId: typeId, status: "used" });
  const rdvType = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const rdv = await rowRdv(db, pro, rdvType);
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: rdv, customerId: bob, coverage: "guest" });
  await rowRdv(db, pro, rdvType, {
    startsAt: new Date(Date.now() + 5 * 86_400_000),
    seatsTaken: 0,
    status: "cancelled",
  });

  assert.deepEqual(await checkInvariants(db, { proId: pro.proId }), []);
});

dbTest(
  "I1: seats_taken differs from the active bookings",

  },

);

dbTest(
  "I2: a booked ticket on a cancelled booking, and a confirmed ticket booking without ticket",

    const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);

    const stray = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, status: "booked", bookingId: ended });
    const bare = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId, coverage: "ticket" });
    await expectViolation(h, "I2", stray);
    await expectViolation(h, "I2", bare);

);

dbTest(

);

  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await rowOffer(db, pro, typeId);

    const purchaseId = await rowPurchase(db, pro, {
      customerId,
      offerId,
      ticketTypeId: typeId,
      status: "paid",
      amountCents: args.amountCents,
      currency: args.currency,
      livemode: args.livemode,
      paymentIntentId: args.livemode ? "pi_live_test" : null,
      paidAt: args.paidAt,

    await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, purchaseId });
  };
  await paid({ amountCents: 2500 });
  await paid({ amountCents: 1000, paidAt: old });
  await paid({ amountCents: 4000, livemode: true });
  await paid({ amountCents: 700, currency: "chf" });
  await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, amountCents: 99_999 });

  assert.deepEqual(await revenueByCurrency(db, { live: false, professionalId: pro.proId }), [
    { currency: "chf", cents: 700 },
    { currency: "eur", cents: 3500 },
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(await revenueByCurrency(db, { live: true, professionalId: pro.proId }), [
    { currency: "eur", cents: 4000 },
  ]);
  const since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 86_400_000);
  assert.deepEqual(await revenueByCurrency(db, { live: false, professionalId: pro.proId, since }), [
    { currency: "chf", cents: 700 },
    { currency: "eur", cents: 2500 },
  ]);
  const [{ n }] = await db
    .select({ n: count() })
    .from(ticketPurchase)
    .where(paidPurchaseOf({ live: false, professionalId: pro.proId }));
  assert.equal(n, 3);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the invariants.** Create `src/lib/booking/invariants.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Invariants I1 to I6 (spec § 14), checked after every DB test
 * (src/test/db.ts) and every night (runNightly). Each invariant is ONE
 * read-only statement (one snapshot); the six run in one db.batch, so one
 * round trip. [] = consistent.
 */
import { sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";

import type { Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";

export type InvariantId = "I1" | "I2" | "I3" | "I4" | "I5" | "I6";
export type InvariantViolation = { invariant: InvariantId; rowId: string; detail: string };

type Row = { invariant: InvariantId; row_id: string; detail: string };

export async function checkInvariants(db: Db, opts: { proId?: string } = {}): Promise<InvariantViolation[]> {
  const pro = (alias: string): SQL =>
    opts.proId ? sql`and ${sql.raw(alias)}.professional_id = ${opts.proId}` : sql``;

  const results = await db.batch([
    // I1: seats_taken = pending + confirmed bookings
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I1' as invariant, o.id as row_id,
        'seats_taken ' || o.seats_taken || ' but ' || a.n || ' active bookings' as detail
      from occurrence o
      cross join lateral (
        select count(*)::int as n from booking b
        where b.occurrence_id = o.id and b.status in ('pending', 'confirmed')
      ) a
      where o.seats_taken <> a.n ${pro("o")}
      order by o.id`),
    // I2a: a booked ticket points at a confirmed `ticket` booking of the same customer
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I2' as invariant, t.id as row_id,
        'booked ticket on booking ' || coalesce(b.id, 'missing') || ' (' || coalesce(b.status::text, '-')
          || ', ' || coalesce(b.coverage::text, '-') || ')' as detail
      from ticket t
      left join booking b on b.id = t.booking_id
      where t.status = 'booked'
        and (b.id is null or b.status <> 'confirmed' or b.coverage <> 'ticket' or b.customer_id <> t.customer_id)
        ${pro("t")}
      order by t.id`),
    // I2b: a confirmed `ticket` booking has a booked ticket
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I2' as invariant, b.id as row_id, 'confirmed ticket booking without a booked ticket' as detail
      from booking b
      where b.status = 'confirmed' and b.coverage = 'ticket'
        and not exists (select 1 from ticket t where t.booking_id = b.id and t.status = 'booked')
        ${pro("b")}
      order by b.id`),
    // I3: paid → exactly `quantity` tickets; not paid → none
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I3' as invariant, p.id as row_id,
        p.status || ' purchase of ' || p.quantity || ' with ' || c.n || ' tickets' as detail
      from ticket_purchase p
      cross join lateral (select count(*)::int as n from ticket t where t.purchase_id = p.id) c
      where ((p.status = 'paid' and c.n <> p.quantity) or (p.status <> 'paid' and c.n > 0)) ${pro("p")}
      order by p.id`),
    // I4: the last movement (by seq) matches the ticket's status and type
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I4' as invariant, t.id as row_id,
        case when m.id is null then 'no movement'
          else 'last movement ' || m.kind || ' to ' || m.to_status || '/' || m.ticket_type_id
            || ' but ticket is ' || t.status || '/' || t.ticket_type_id end as detail
      from ticket t
      left join lateral (
        select mv.id, mv.kind, mv.to_status, mv.ticket_type_id from ticket_movement mv
        where mv.ticket_id = t.id order by mv.seq desc limit 1
      ) m on true
      where (m.id is null or m.to_status <> t.status or m.ticket_type_id <> t.ticket_type_id) ${pro("t")}
      order by t.id`),
    // I5a: no active booking on a cancelled occurrence
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I5' as invariant, b.id as row_id, 'active booking on cancelled occurrence ' || o.id as detail
      from booking b
      join occurrence o on o.id = b.occurrence_id
      where o.status = 'cancelled' and b.status in ('pending', 'confirmed') ${pro("b")}
      order by b.id`),
    // I5b: an RDV is scheduled exactly when its one seat is taken
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I5' as invariant, o.id as row_id, 'RDV ' || o.status || ' with seats_taken ' || o.seats_taken as detail
      from occurrence o
      where o.event_id is null and (o.status = 'scheduled') <> (o.seats_taken = 1) ${pro("o")}
      order by o.id`),
    // I6: paid → paid_at and fulfilled_action_id; livemode → payment_intent_id
    db.execute<Row>(sql`
      select 'I6' as invariant, p.id as row_id,
        case when p.status = 'paid' and p.paid_at is null then 'paid without paid_at'
          when p.status = 'paid' and p.fulfilled_action_id is null then 'paid without fulfilled_action_id'
          else 'livemode without payment_intent_id' end as detail
      from ticket_purchase p
      where ((p.status = 'paid' and (p.paid_at is null or p.fulfilled_action_id is null))
        or (p.livemode and p.payment_intent_id is null)) ${pro("p")}
      order by p.id`),
  ]);

  return results.flatMap((r) =>
    r.rows.map((row) => ({ invariant: row.invariant, rowId: row.row_id, detail: row.detail })),
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the revenue predicate.** Create `src/lib/booking/revenue.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * « Encaissé » (spec § 9): ONE predicate over ticket_purchase, shared by the
 * dashboard, /dashboard/payments and admin-queries.ts (re-exported by
 * src/server/revenue.ts in P3). Mock and Stripe test keys write
 * livemode = false, live keys livemode = true: the caller passes `live` from
 * the Stripe mode in use, so test environments still show figures.
 */
import { asc, sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";

import { ts, type Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";

export type RevenueScope = { live: boolean; professionalId?: string; since?: Date };

/**
 * status = 'paid' AND livemode = live [AND professional_id] [AND paid_at ≥ since].
 * Columns are qualified with "ticket_purchase": use it in queries on that
 * table without an alias.
 */
export function paidPurchaseOf(opts: RevenueScope): SQL {
  const parts: SQL[] = [
    sql`${ticketPurchase.status} = 'paid'`,
    sql`${ticketPurchase.livemode} = ${opts.live}`,
  ];
  if (opts.professionalId) parts.push(sql`${ticketPurchase.professionalId} = ${opts.professionalId}`);
  if (opts.since) parts.push(sql`${ticketPurchase.paidAt} >= ${ts(opts.since)}`);
  return sql`(${sql.join(parts, sql` and `)})`;
}

/** Σ amount_cents of the paid purchases in scope, per currency (ordered by currency). */
export async function revenueByCurrency(db: Db, opts: RevenueScope): Promise<{ currency: string; cents: number }[]> {
  return db
    .select({
      currency: ticketPurchase.currency,
      cents: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${ticketPurchase.amountCents}), 0)`.mapWith(Number),
    })
    .from(ticketPurchase)
    .where(paidPurchaseOf(opts))
    .groupBy(ticketPurchase.currency)
    .orderBy(asc(ticketPurchase.currency));
}
```

```ts
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { buildAnswers, type QuestionRow } from "./answers";

const q = (over: Partial<QuestionRow> & Pick<QuestionRow, "id" | "label">): QuestionRow => ({
  type: "text",
  options: null,
  required: false,
  position: 0,
  ...over,
});

const level = q({ id: "q1", label: "Votre niveau ?", type: "choice", options: ["Débutant", "Confirmé"], required: true });
const ball = q({ id: "q2", label: "Avez-vous un ballon ?", type: "boolean", position: 1 });
const note = q({ id: "q3", label: "Une remarque ?", position: 2 });
const questions = [level, ball, note];

test("trims, keeps question order and snapshots the label", () => {
  const res = buildAnswers(
    questions,
    [
      { questionId: "q3", value: "  Genou fragile " },
      { questionId: "q1", value: "Débutant" },
      { questionId: "q2", value: "Oui" },
    ],
    { enforceRequired: true },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(res, {
    ok: true,
    rows: [
      { questionId: "q1", questionLabel: "Votre niveau ?", value: "Débutant" },
      { questionId: "q2", questionLabel: "Avez-vous un ballon ?", value: "Oui" },
      { questionId: "q3", questionLabel: "Une remarque ?", value: "Genou fragile" },
    ],
  });
});

test("empty answers are skipped; a missing required one fails only for clients", () => {
  const answers = [{ questionId: "q3", value: "   " }];
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, answers, { enforceRequired: true }), {
    ok: false,
    message: "Merci de répondre à : Votre niveau ?",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, answers, { enforceRequired: false }), { ok: true, rows: [] });
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, undefined, { enforceRequired: false }), { ok: true, rows: [] });
});

test("a choice must be one of the options and a boolean Oui or Non", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, [{ questionId: "q1", value: "Expert" }], { enforceRequired: false }), {
    ok: false,
    message: "Réponse invalide pour : Votre niveau ?",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, [{ questionId: "q2", value: "peut-être" }], { enforceRequired: false }), {
    ok: false,
    message: "Réponse invalide pour : Avez-vous un ballon ?",
  });
});

test("an unknown question, a duplicate answer or an over-long value is invalid", () => {
  const invalid = { ok: false, message: "Données invalides." };
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers(questions, [{ questionId: "nope", value: "x" }], { enforceRequired: false }), invalid);
  assert.deepEqual(
    buildAnswers(
      questions,
      [
        { questionId: "q3", value: "a" },
        { questionId: "q3", value: "b" },
      ],
      { enforceRequired: false },
    ),
    invalid,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    buildAnswers(questions, [{ questionId: "q3", value: "x".repeat(2001) }], { enforceRequired: false }),
    invalid,
  );
});

test("no question: any answer is invalid, none is fine", () => {
  assert.deepEqual(buildAnswers([], [], { enforceRequired: true }), { ok: true, rows: [] });
  assert.equal(buildAnswers([], [{ questionId: "q1", value: "x" }], { enforceRequired: true }).ok, false);
});
```

```ts
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

import { CHECKOUT_MINUTES, HOLD_MINUTES } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { checkoutExpiry, holdExpiry, seatsShown } from "./holds";

const now = new Date("2026-10-12T10:00:00.000Z");

test("a hold lasts 45 minutes, longer than the 31-minute Checkout session", () => {
  assert.equal(holdExpiry(now).toISOString(), "2026-10-12T10:45:00.000Z");
  assert.equal(checkoutExpiry(now, "buybook").toISOString(), "2026-10-12T10:31:00.000Z");
  assert.ok(HOLD_MINUTES > CHECKOUT_MINUTES, "the seat must outlive the payment page");
  assert.ok(CHECKOUT_MINUTES >= 30, "Stripe refuses an expires_at under 30 minutes");
});

test("a shop checkout lasts 24 hours", () => {
  assert.equal(checkoutExpiry(now, "shop").toISOString(), "2026-10-13T10:00:00.000Z");
});

test("seats shown add back the elapsed holds and never go below 0", () => {
  assert.equal(seatsShown({ capacity: 12, seatsTaken: 12, elapsedHolds: 0 }), 0);
  assert.equal(seatsShown({ capacity: 12, seatsTaken: 12, elapsedHolds: 2 }), 2);
  assert.equal(seatsShown({ capacity: 12, seatsTaken: 5, elapsedHolds: 0 }), 7);
  assert.equal(seatsShown({ capacity: 3, seatsTaken: 5, elapsedHolds: 0 }), 0);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create the engine result types.** Create `src/lib/booking/types.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Engine result shapes. The engine never sends e-mails and never calls Stripe
 * or Google: it returns effect descriptors that carry ids only, executed after
 * commit by src/server/notify.ts (P3/P4), which re-reads names, dates and
 * amounts by id.
 */
import type { EngineErrorCode } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type Effect =
  /** client: confirmation of a booking, whatever its coverage */
  | { type: "email.booking_confirmed"; bookingId: string }
  /** pro: a client booked (never when the pro registered someone) */
  | { type: "email.pro_new_booking"; bookingId: string }
  /** client: their booking was cancelled (by themselves or by the pro) */
  | { type: "email.booking_cancelled"; bookingId: string; by: "client" | "pro" }
  /** pro: a client cancelled */
  | { type: "email.pro_booking_cancelled"; bookingId: string }
  /** clients of a cancelled séance (tickets given back) */
  | { type: "email.occurrence_cancelled"; occurrenceId: string; bookingIds: string[] }
  /** clients of a séance whose date, time, duration, title or effective location changed */
  | { type: "email.occurrence_changed"; occurrenceId: string; bookingIds: string[] }
  /** client moved by the pro */
  | { type: "email.attendee_moved"; bookingId: string; fromOccurrenceId: string }
  /** receipt with expiry, after a fulfilled purchase */
  | { type: "email.purchase_receipt"; purchaseId: string }
  /** paid after the hold ended: the tickets went to the wallet */
  | { type: "email.late_payment"; purchaseId: string }
  /** optional e-mail after « Offrir » */
  | { type: "email.tickets_offered"; customerId: string; actionId: string; quantity: number }
  /** re-sync the Google event of this occurrence (P3) */
  | { type: "google.sync_occurrence"; occurrenceId: string }
  /** expire a still-open Checkout session (pending purchase whose seat ended) */
  | { type: "stripe.expire_session"; purchaseId: string; checkoutSessionId: string };

export type EngineOk<T extends object = object> = { ok: true; effects: Effect[] } & T;
export type EngineFail = { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode; message?: string };
export type EngineResult<T extends object = object> = EngineOk<T> | EngineFail;

/** A pro acting from the back-office. */
export type ProActor = { professionalId: string; userId: string };

/** One google.sync_occurrence effect per distinct occurrence id, in first-seen order. */
export function syncEffects(occurrenceIds: readonly string[]): Effect[] {
  return [...new Set(occurrenceIds)].map(
    (occurrenceId): Effect => ({ type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId }),
  );
}

/** `{ ok: false, code }`, with `message` only when one is given. */
export function fail(code: EngineErrorCode, message?: string): EngineFail {
  return message === undefined ? { ok: false, code } : { ok: false, code, message };
}

/**
 * A failed `tryAtomic` outcome as an engine failure. `duplicate`
 * (skedul:already_paid) only means something to fulfillment, which handles it
 * itself; anywhere else it is reported as `stale`.
 */
export function failOf(res: { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode | "duplicate"; message?: string }): EngineFail {
  return res.code === "duplicate" ? fail("stale") : fail(res.code, res.message);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement answers.** Create `src/lib/booking/answers.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Registration questions and answers (moved from src/server/booking-core.ts).
 * Questions belong to an event, or to a ticket type for the RDV libre; answers
 * are snapshotted (question_label) onto the booking, so they survive a
 * question's deletion (booking_answer.question_id → question SET NULL).
 */
import { asc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import type { BatchCtx, Db, Query } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { bookingAnswer, question } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { BOUNDS, ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES, type AnswerInput, type QuestionType } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type QuestionRow = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  type: QuestionType;
  options: string[] | null;
  required: boolean;
  position: number;
};
export type AnswerRow = { questionId: string; questionLabel: string; value: string };

/** The answers a boolean question accepts. */
export const BOOLEAN_ANSWERS = ["Oui", "Non"] as const;

/** Questions of an event, or the RDV questions of a ticket type, by position. */
export async function loadQuestions(
  db: Db,
  owner: { eventId: string } | { ticketTypeId: string },
): Promise<QuestionRow[]> {
  const where = "eventId" in owner ? eq(question.eventId, owner.eventId) : eq(question.ticketTypeId, owner.ticketTypeId);
  return db
    .select({
      id: question.id,
      label: question.label,
      type: question.type,
      options: question.options,
      required: question.required,
      position: question.position,
    })
    .from(question)
    .where(where)
    .orderBy(asc(question.position), asc(question.createdAt), asc(question.id));
}

/**
 * Validates answers against the questions, in question order. Values are
 * trimmed and an empty one is skipped; a required question is enforced only
 * when `enforceRequired` (clients, never the pro). A choice must be one of the
 * options, a boolean « Oui » or « Non ». An answer to an unknown question, a
 * duplicate answer or an over-long value is invalid.
 */
export function buildAnswers(
  questions: readonly QuestionRow[],
  answers: readonly AnswerInput[] | undefined,
  opts: { enforceRequired: boolean },
): { ok: true; rows: AnswerRow[] } | { ok: false; message: string } {
  const invalid = { ok: false as const, message: ENGINE_ERROR_MESSAGES.invalid };
  const known = new Set(questions.map((q) => q.id));
  const byId = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const a of answers ?? []) {
    if (!known.has(a.questionId) || byId.has(a.questionId)) return invalid;
    byId.set(a.questionId, a.value.trim());
  }

  const rows: AnswerRow[] = [];
  for (const q of questions) {
    const value = byId.get(q.id) ?? "";
    if (!value) {
      if (q.required && opts.enforceRequired) return { ok: false, message: `Merci de répondre à : ${q.label}` };
      continue;
    }
    if (value.length > BOUNDS.answer) return invalid;
    const accepted =
      q.type === "choice"
        ? (q.options ?? []).includes(value)
        : q.type === "boolean"
          ? (BOOLEAN_ANSWERS as readonly string[]).includes(value)
          : true;
    if (!accepted) return { ok: false, message: `Réponse invalide pour : ${q.label}` };
    rows.push({ questionId: q.id, questionLabel: q.label, value });
  }
  return { ok: true, rows };
}

/** One multi-row INSERT into booking_answer (nanoid ids, timestamps = now); [] when there is nothing to insert. */
export function insertAnswers(
  c: BatchCtx,
  args: { professionalId: string; bookingId: string; rows: readonly AnswerRow[] },
): Query[] {
  if (args.rows.length === 0) return [];
  return [
    c.db.insert(bookingAnswer).values(
      args.rows.map((r) => ({
        id: nanoid(),
        professionalId: args.professionalId,
        bookingId: args.bookingId,
        questionId: r.questionId,
        questionLabel: r.questionLabel,
        value: r.value,
        createdAt: c.now,
        updatedAt: c.now,
      })),
    ),
  ];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement holds.** Create `src/lib/booking/holds.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Seat holds of the buy + book path (spec § 11 E1, E4, E11): a pending booking
 * keeps its seat until hold_expires_at; the Checkout session expires before
 * the hold. An elapsed hold is released lazily by R(o) before every claim, or
 * in bulk by sweepHolds; correctness never waits for the nightly job.
 */
import { sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";

import { G, ts, type BatchCtx, type Query } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { CHECKOUT_MINUTES, HOLD_MINUTES, MAX_PENDING_HOLDS, SHOP_CHECKOUT_HOURS } from "@/lib/tickets";

const MINUTE = 60_000;

/** now + HOLD_MINUTES: booking.hold_expires_at. */
export function holdExpiry(now: Date): Date {
  return new Date(now.getTime() + HOLD_MINUTES * MINUTE);
}

/** ticket_purchase.checkout_expires_at: now + CHECKOUT_MINUTES (buy + book) or SHOP_CHECKOUT_HOURS (shop). */
export function checkoutExpiry(now: Date, kind: "buybook" | "shop"): Date {
  const minutes = kind === "buybook" ? CHECKOUT_MINUTES : SHOP_CHECKOUT_HOURS * 60;
  return new Date(now.getTime() + minutes * MINUTE);
}

/**
 * G(live holds of the customer < MAX_PENDING_HOLDS, 'too_many_holds'), séances
 * and RDV together (a customer row belongs to one pro). Only LIVE holds count
 * (hold_expires_at > now): an elapsed one not yet swept does not block. Put it
 * BEFORE the new pending booking's INSERT. The caller must already hold the
 * customer row lock in the same batch (lockRows(db, "customer", …): E1 and
 * E4 « buy »). Under READ COMMITTED, two concurrent holds of one customer
 * would otherwise both pass this count.
 */
export function assertHoldQuota(c: BatchCtx, customerId: string): Query {
  return G(
    c.db,
    sql`(select count(*)::int from booking
      where customer_id = ${customerId} and status = 'pending' and hold_expires_at > ${ts(c.now)})
      < ${MAX_PENDING_HOLDS}::int`,
    "too_many_holds",
  );
}

/**
 * R over many occurrences: expires the elapsed holds of every booking whose
 * occurrence is returned by `occurrenceIds`, a subquery (`select id from …`;
 * for a JS list: sql`select unnest(${textArray(ids)})`), marking them with
 * the action id. Lock those occurrences first, then follow with
 * releaseEndedSeats (atomic.ts).
 */
export function expireElapsedHolds(c: BatchCtx, occurrenceIds: SQL): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update booking set status = 'expired', ended_at = ${ts(c.now)}, ended_by = 'system',
      ended_action_id = ${c.aid}, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    where occurrence_id in (${occurrenceIds}) and status = 'pending' and hold_expires_at < ${ts(c.now)}`);
}

/** Seats a visitor sees (spec § 7): capacity − seats taken + elapsed holds not yet released, never below 0. */
export function seatsShown(o: { capacity: number; seatsTaken: number; elapsedHolds: number }): number {
  return Math.max(0, o.capacity - o.seatsTaken + o.elapsedHolds);
}
```
