- [ ] **Step 7: Write the failing DB test.** Create `src/lib/booking/eligibility.db.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { SEAT, atomic, lockRows, releaseEndedSeats, tryAtomic, type BatchCtx, type Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, bookingAnswer, occurrence, question, ticket, ticketMovement } from "@/lib/db/schema";

import { addMinutes, dbTest, type TestPro } from "../../test/db";
import {
  rowBooking,
  rowCustomer,
  rowEvent,
  rowLink,
  rowOffer,
  rowPurchase,
  rowRdv,
  rowRdvType,
  rowSeance,
  rowTicket,
  rowType,
} from "../../test/rows";
import { insertAnswers, loadQuestions } from "./answers";
import { assertSpent, spendTicket, usableTickets } from "./eligibility";
import { assertHoldQuota, expireElapsedHolds } from "./holds";

const DAY = 86_400_000;

/** The batch of a client ticket claim on an event séance (E2 shape). */
function claimBatch(c: BatchCtx, pro: TestPro, args: { occurrenceId: string; customerId: string; bookingId: string; ticketId?: string }) {
  return [
    lockRows(c.db, "occurrence", [args.occurrenceId]),
    SEAT(c, args.occurrenceId, 1),
    c.db.insert(booking).values({
      id: args.bookingId,
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      occurrenceId: args.occurrenceId,
      customerId: args.customerId,
      status: "confirmed",
      coverage: "ticket",
      createdBy: "client",
      confirmedAt: c.now,
      createdAt: c.now,
      updatedAt: c.now,
    }),
    spendTicket(c, {
      bookingId: args.bookingId,
      customerId: args.customerId,
      occurrenceId: args.occurrenceId,
      ticketId: args.ticketId,
      by: { actor: "client", userId: null },
    }),
    assertSpent(c, args.bookingId),
  ];
}

async function seatsOf(db: Db, id: string): Promise<number> {
  const [row] = await db.select({ n: occurrence.seatsTaken }).from(occurrence).where(eq(occurrence.id, id));
  return row.n;
}

dbTest("ELIGIBLE: mode, live link, archived type, expiry at the séance, RDV paid vs free, status", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const linked = await rowType(db, pro);
  const unlinked = await rowType(db, pro);
  const archived = await rowType(db, pro, { archivedAt: now });
  const rdvPaid = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const rdvFree = await rowRdvType(db, pro, { rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 15 });

  const ticketsEvent = await rowEvent(db, pro, { bookingMode: "tickets" });
  await rowLink(db, pro, ticketsEvent, linked);
  await rowLink(db, pro, ticketsEvent, archived);
  const freeEvent = await rowEvent(db, pro, { bookingMode: "free" });
  await rowLink(db, pro, freeEvent, linked);
  const seance = await rowSeance(db, pro, ticketsEvent, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY) });
  const freeSeance = await rowSeance(db, pro, freeEvent, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * DAY) });
  const holder = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const paidRdv = await rowRdv(db, pro, rdvPaid, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 3 * DAY) });
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: paidRdv, customerId: holder });
  const freeRdv = await rowRdv(db, pro, rdvFree, { startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 4 * DAY), durationMin: 15 });
  await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: freeRdv, customerId: holder });
  const [{ startsAt }] = await db.select({ startsAt: occurrence.startsAt }).from(occurrence).where(eq(occurrence.id, seance));

  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const good = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: linked, expiresAt: addMinutes(startsAt, 1) });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: linked, expiresAt: startsAt }); // expires at the start: not valid
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: linked, status: "used" });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: unlinked });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: archived });
  const rdvTicket = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: rdvPaid });
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: rdvFree });

  const ids = async (occurrenceId: string) => (await usableTickets(db, { customerId, occurrenceId })).map((t) => t.id);
  assert.deepEqual(await ids(seance), [good]);
  assert.deepEqual(await ids(freeSeance), [], "a free-mode event accepts no ticket, even linked");
  assert.deepEqual(await ids(paidRdv), [rdvTicket]);
  assert.deepEqual(await ids(freeRdv), [], "a free RDV never consumes a ticket");

  const [usable] = await usableTickets(db, { customerId, occurrenceId: seance });
  assert.equal(usable.expiresAt?.getTime(), addMinutes(startsAt, 1).getTime());
  assert.equal(usable.ticketTypeId, linked);
  assert.equal(usable.purchaseId, null);

  // links are read live: unlinking makes the ticket unusable at once
  await db.execute(sql`delete from event_ticket_type where event_id = ${ticketsEvent} and ticket_type_id = ${linked}`);
  assert.deepEqual(await ids(seance), []);
});

dbTest("consumption order: soonest expiry first, no expiry last, then oldest, then id", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);
  await rowLink(db, pro, eventId, typeId);
  const seance = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId);
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const at = (seconds: number) => new Date(now.getTime() + seconds * 1000);
  const never = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId }, at(-40));
  const later = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * DAY) }, at(-30));
  const soonOld = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * DAY) }, at(-20));
  const soonNew = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * DAY) }, at(-10));

  const order = (await usableTickets(db, { customerId, occurrenceId: seance })).map((t) => t.id);
  assert.deepEqual(order, [soonOld, soonNew, later, never]);

  const bookingId = nanoid();
  const done = await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => claimBatch({ db, aid, now }, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId, bookingId }));
  const [spent] = await db.select().from(ticket).where(eq(ticket.bookingId, bookingId));
  assert.equal(spent.id, soonOld, "the spend takes the first ticket of the consumption order");
  assert.equal(spent.status, "booked");
  const moves = await db.select().from(ticketMovement).where(eq(ticketMovement.actionId, done.aid));
  assert.equal(moves.length, 1);
  assert.equal(moves[0].kind, "spend");
  assert.equal(moves[0].fromStatus, "available");
  assert.equal(moves[0].toStatus, "booked");
  assert.equal(moves[0].bookingId, bookingId);
  assert.equal(moves[0].actor, "client");
  assert.equal(await seatsOf(db, seance), 1);
});

dbTest("a forced ticket is spent only if eligible; no eligible ticket raises no_ticket", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const otherType = await rowType(db, pro);
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);
  await rowLink(db, pro, eventId, typeId);
  const seance = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId);
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId, expiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * DAY) });
  const chosen = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
  const foreign = await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId, ticketTypeId: otherType });

  const refused = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) =>
    claimBatch({ db, aid, now }, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId, bookingId: nanoid(), ticketId: foreign }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(refused, { ok: false, code: "no_ticket" });
  assert.equal(await seatsOf(db, seance), 0, "the whole batch was rolled back");

  const bookingId = nanoid();
  const forced = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) =>
    claimBatch({ db, aid, now }, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId, bookingId, ticketId: chosen }),
  );
  assert.equal(forced.ok, true);
  const [spent] = await db.select({ id: ticket.id }).from(ticket).where(eq(ticket.bookingId, bookingId));
  assert.equal(spent.id, chosen, "the forced ticket wins over the consumption order");

  const nobody = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const none = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) =>
    claimBatch({ db, aid, now }, pro, { occurrenceId: seance, customerId: nobody, bookingId: nanoid() }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(none, { ok: false, code: "no_ticket" });
});

dbTest("assertHoldQuota counts live holds only; expireElapsedHolds marks them with the action id", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await rowOffer(db, pro, typeId);
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  const seances: string[] = [];
  const holds: string[] = [];
  for (const [i, minutes] of [30, 20, -5].entries()) {
    const o = await rowSeance(db, pro, eventId, { seatsTaken: 1, startsAt: new Date(now.getTime() + (2 + i) * DAY) });
    const purchaseId = await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId, offerId, ticketTypeId: typeId });
    seances.push(o);
    holds.push(
      await rowBooking(db, pro, {
        occurrenceId: o,
        customerId,
        status: "pending",
        coverage: "ticket",
        purchaseId,
        holdExpiresAt: addMinutes(now, minutes),
      }),
    );
  }

  const quota = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) => [assertHoldQuota({ db, aid, now }, customerId)]);
  assert.deepEqual(quota, { ok: false, code: "too_many_holds" }, "two live holds: a third is refused");
  const later = addMinutes(now, 25); // the 20-minute hold has elapsed too
  const fine = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) => [assertHoldQuota({ db, aid, now: later }, customerId)]);
  assert.equal(fine.ok, true, "elapsed holds do not count, swept or not");

  const done = await atomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    const ids = sql`select id from occurrence where event_id = ${eventId}`;
    return [
      db.execute(sql`select id from occurrence where event_id = ${eventId} order by id for update`),
      expireElapsedHolds(c, ids),
      releaseEndedSeats(c),
    ];
  });
  const rows = await db.select().from(booking).where(eq(booking.endedActionId, done.aid));
  assert.deepEqual(
    rows.map((r) => r.id),
    [holds[2]],
  );
  assert.equal(rows[0].status, "expired");
  assert.equal(await seatsOf(db, seances[2]), 0);
  assert.equal(await seatsOf(db, seances[0]), 1);
});

dbTest("loadQuestions orders by position; insertAnswers writes one row per answer", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await rowRdvType(db, pro);
  const second = nanoid();
  const first = nanoid();
  await db.insert(question).values([
    { id: second, professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, label: "Deuxième", type: "text", position: 1, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now },
    { id: first, professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, label: "Première", type: "choice", options: ["A", "B"], required: true, position: 0, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now },
  ]);
  const questions = await loadQuestions(db, { ticketTypeId: typeId });
  assert.deepEqual(
    questions.map((q) => [q.id, q.label, q.type, q.options, q.required, q.position]),
    [
      [first, "Première", "choice", ["A", "B"], true, 0],
      [second, "Deuxième", "text", null, false, 1],
    ],
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: "none" }), []);

  const rdv = await rowRdv(db, pro, typeId);
  const bookingId = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: rdv, customerId: await rowCustomer(db, pro) });
  const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid: nanoid(), now };
  assert.deepEqual(insertAnswers(c, { professionalId: pro.proId, bookingId, rows: [] }), []);
  await atomic(db, async () => (aid) =>
    insertAnswers({ db, aid, now }, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      bookingId,
      rows: [
        { questionId: first, questionLabel: "Première", value: "B" },
        { questionId: second, questionLabel: "Deuxième", value: "Libre" },
      ],
    }),
  );
  const saved = await db.select().from(bookingAnswer).where(eq(bookingAnswer.bookingId, bookingId));
  assert.equal(saved.length, 2);
  assert.ok(saved.every((a) => a.professionalId === pro.proId && a.createdAt.getTime() === now.getTime()));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement eligibility.** Create `src/lib/booking/eligibility.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * THE eligibility rule (spec § 9), one SQL fragment shared by the spend (E2,
 * E4) and the « utilisable ici » reads. Links are read live: an inner EXISTS on
 * event_ticket_type, so an empty list accepts nobody.
 */
import { sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";

import { G, ts, type ActorRef, type BatchCtx, type Db, type Query } from "@/lib/db/atomic";

/**
 * Over the aliases t = ticket, tt = its ticket_type, o = occurrence, e = event
 * LEFT JOINed on o.event_id. Valid AT THE SÉANCE (expires_at > o.starts_at),
 * not at now: the pro may register someone on a past séance.
 */
export const ELIGIBLE: SQL = sql`(
  t.status = 'available' and tt.archived_at is null
  and (t.expires_at is null or t.expires_at > o.starts_at)
  and (
    (o.event_id is not null and e.booking_mode = 'tickets'
      and exists (select 1 from event_ticket_type l where l.event_id = o.event_id and l.ticket_type_id = t.ticket_type_id))
    or (o.event_id is null and o.ticket_type_id = t.ticket_type_id and tt.rdv_enabled and not tt.rdv_free)
  )
)`;

/** The FROM clause ELIGIBLE expects, for one customer and one occurrence. */
function eligibleFrom(customerId: string, occurrenceId: string): SQL {
  return sql`from ticket t
    join ticket_type tt on tt.id = t.ticket_type_id
    join occurrence o on o.id = ${occurrenceId}
    left join event e on e.id = o.event_id
    where t.customer_id = ${customerId} and t.professional_id = o.professional_id and ${ELIGIBLE}`;
}

const CONSUMPTION_ORDER = sql`order by t.expires_at asc nulls last, t.created_at, t.id`;

/**
 * Scalar subquery: the ticket to spend (consumption order: soonest expiry,
 * then oldest), or the forced `ticketId` if it is eligible. FOR UPDATE OF t
 * SKIP LOCKED: two concurrent spends never pick the same row; NULL when none.
 */
export function pickTicket(args: { customerId: string; occurrenceId: string; ticketId?: string | null }): SQL {
  const forced = args.ticketId ? sql` and t.id = ${args.ticketId}` : sql``;
  return sql`(select t.id ${eligibleFrom(args.customerId, args.occurrenceId)}${forced}
    ${CONSUMPTION_ORDER}
    limit 1
    for update of t skip locked)`;
}

/**
 * One statement: book the picked ticket on `bookingId` and write its `spend`
 * movement (marked with the action id). The booking INSERT must come before
 * it in the batch (FK); follow with assertSpent.
 */
export function spendTicket(
  c: BatchCtx,
  args: { bookingId: string; customerId: string; occurrenceId: string; ticketId?: string | null; by: ActorRef },
): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    with u as (
      update ticket set status = 'booked', booking_id = ${args.bookingId},
        status_changed_at = ${ts(c.now)}, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
      where id = ${pickTicket(args)} and status = 'available'
      returning id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id, purchase_id
    )
    insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status,
      to_status, ticket_type_id, booking_id, purchase_id, actor, actor_user_id, created_at)
    select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${c.aid}, 'spend', 'available', 'booked',
      ticket_type_id, ${args.bookingId}, purchase_id, ${args.by.actor}::actor, ${args.by.userId}, ${ts(c.now)}
    from u`);
}

/** G(a ticket is booked on this booking, 'no_ticket'). */
export function assertSpent(c: BatchCtx, bookingId: string): Query {
  return G(c.db, sql`exists (select 1 from ticket where booking_id = ${bookingId})`, "no_ticket");
}

/** « Utilisable ici »: the customer's eligible tickets for the occurrence, in consumption order, without lock. */
export async function usableTickets(
  db: Db,
  args: { customerId: string; occurrenceId: string },
): Promise<{ id: string; ticketTypeId: string; expiresAt: Date | null; purchaseId: string | null }[]> {
  const res = await db.execute<{
    id: string;
    ticket_type_id: string;
    expires_at: string | null;
    purchase_id: string | null;
  }>(sql`select t.id, t.ticket_type_id, t.expires_at, t.purchase_id
    ${eligibleFrom(args.customerId, args.occurrenceId)}
    ${CONSUMPTION_ORDER}`);
  return res.rows.map((r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    ticketTypeId: r.ticket_type_id,
    expiresAt: r.expires_at === null ? null : new Date(r.expires_at),
    purchaseId: r.purchase_id,
  }));
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, asc, eq, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import {
  availabilityRule,
  bookingAnswer,
  eventTicketType,
  professional,
  question,
  ticketOffer,
  ticketType,
} from "@/lib/db/schema";

import { createTestPro, dbTest, deleteTestUser } from "../../test/db";
import { rowBooking, rowCustomer, rowEvent, rowLink, rowPurchase, rowRdv, rowTicket } from "../../test/rows";
import {
  createOffer,
  createTicketType,
  deleteOffer,
  deleteTicketType,
  hasDomainData,
  setAvailabilityRules,
  setTicketTypeArchived,
  setTypeQuestions,
  updateBookingSettings,
  updateOffer,
  updateTicketType,
} from "./catalog";

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T): Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.equal(res.ok, true, JSON.stringify(res));
  return res as Extract<T, { ok: true }>;
}

dbTest("createTicketType appends with defaults; zod errors come back as invalid with their message", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const first = must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "  Cours collectif " } }, now));
  const second = must(
    await createTicketType(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      type: { name: "Cours particulier", rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60, rdvBufferAfterMin: 10 },
    }),
  );
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(ticketType)
    .where(eq(ticketType.professionalId, pro.proId))
    .orderBy(asc(ticketType.position));
  assert.deepEqual(
    rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.name, r.position, r.color, r.rdvEnabled, r.rdvDurationMin, r.rdvBufferAfterMin]),
    [
      [first.ticketTypeId, "Cours collectif", 0, "#6366f1", false, null, 0],
      [second.ticketTypeId, "Cours particulier", 1, "#6366f1", true, 60, 10],
    ],
  );
  assert.equal(rows[0].createdAt.getTime(), now.getTime());

  assert.deepEqual(
    await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "RDV", rdvEnabled: true } }),
    { ok: false, code: "invalid", message: "Indiquez la durée du rendez-vous." },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Gratuit", rdvFree: true, rdvEnabled: false, rdvDurationMin: 15 } }),
    { ok: false, code: "invalid", message: "Le rendez-vous gratuit demande le RDV libre." },
  );
});

dbTest("updateTicketType and setTicketTypeArchived stay inside the pro", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const other = await createTestPro(db);
  try {
    const { ticketTypeId } = must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Avant" } }));
    assert.deepEqual(
      await updateTicketType(db, { professionalId: other.proId, ticketTypeId, type: { name: "Volé" } }),
      { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
    );
    must(await updateTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId, type: { name: "Après", color: "#16a34a" } }));
    must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId, archived: true }));
    let [row] = await db.select().from(ticketType).where(eq(ticketType.id, ticketTypeId));
    assert.equal(row.name, "Après");
    assert.equal(row.color, "#16a34a");
    assert.ok(row.archivedAt);
    must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId, archived: false }));
    [row] = await db.select().from(ticketType).where(eq(ticketType.id, ticketTypeId));
    assert.equal(row.archivedAt, null);
    assert.deepEqual(
      await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: other.proId, ticketTypeId, archived: true }),
      { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
    );
  } finally {
    await deleteTestUser(db, other.userId);
  }
});

dbTest("deleteTicketType deletes an unused type (offers cascade) and archives one with history", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const unused = must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Jamais servi" } }));
  const unusedOffer = must(
    await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId: unused.ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 1500 } }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await deleteTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: unused.ticketTypeId }), {
    ok: true,
    effects: [],
    outcome: "deleted",
  });
  assert.equal((await db.select().from(ticketOffer).where(eq(ticketOffer.id, unusedOffer.offerId))).length, 0);

  const used = must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Déjà vendu" } }));
  const usedOffer = must(
    await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId: used.ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 1500 } }),
  );
  const eventId = await rowEvent(db, pro);
  await rowLink(db, pro, eventId, used.ticketTypeId);
  await rowTicket(db, pro, { customerId: await rowCustomer(db, pro), ticketTypeId: used.ticketTypeId });
  assert.deepEqual(await deleteTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: used.ticketTypeId }), {
    ok: true,
    effects: [],
    outcome: "archived",
  });
  const [row] = await db.select().from(ticketType).where(eq(ticketType.id, used.ticketTypeId));
  assert.ok(row.archivedAt, "archived instead of deleted");
  assert.equal((await db.select().from(ticketOffer).where(eq(ticketOffer.id, usedOffer.offerId))).length, 1, "offers untouched");
  assert.equal(
    (await db.select().from(eventTicketType).where(eq(eventTicketType.ticketTypeId, used.ticketTypeId))).length,
    1,
    "links untouched",
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await deleteTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: "missing" }), {
    ok: false,
    code: "not_found",
  });
});

dbTest("offers: refused on an archived type; edit; delete, or archive once bought", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const { ticketTypeId } = must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Collectif" } }));
  const unit = must(await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 2500 } }));
  const pack = must(
    await createOffer(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      offer: { ticketTypeId, label: "  ", quantity: 10, priceCents: 19000, validityCount: 6, validityUnit: "month" },
    }),
  );
  const offers = await db
    .select()
    .from(ticketOffer)
    .where(eq(ticketOffer.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId))
    .orderBy(asc(ticketOffer.position));
  assert.deepEqual(
    offers.map((o) => [o.id, o.position, o.label, o.quantity, o.validityCount, o.validityUnit]),
    [
      [unit.offerId, 0, null, 1, null, null],
      [pack.offerId, 1, null, 10, 6, "month"],
    ],
  );

  const free = await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 0 } });
  assert.equal(!free.ok && free.code, "invalid", "no offer at 0 €");
  assert.deepEqual(
    await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 900, validityCount: 3 } }),
    { ok: false, code: "invalid", message: "Indiquez une durée et une unité de validité, ou aucune." },
  );

  must(
    await updateOffer(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      offerId: pack.offerId,
      offer: { ticketTypeId: "ignored", label: "Carnet été", quantity: 8, priceCents: 15000, isActive: false },
    }),
  );
  const [edited] = await db.select().from(ticketOffer).where(eq(ticketOffer.id, pack.offerId));
  assert.deepEqual(
    [edited.ticketTypeId, edited.label, edited.quantity, edited.priceCents, edited.validityCount, edited.isActive],
    [ticketTypeId, "Carnet été", 8, 15000, null, false],
  );

  assert.deepEqual(await deleteOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offerId: pack.offerId }), {
    ok: true,
    effects: [],
    outcome: "deleted",
  });
  const customerId = await rowCustomer(db, pro);
  await rowPurchase(db, pro, { customerId, offerId: unit.offerId, ticketTypeId });
  assert.deepEqual(await deleteOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offerId: unit.offerId }), {
    ok: true,
    effects: [],
    outcome: "archived",
  });
  assert.deepEqual(
    await updateOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offerId: unit.offerId, offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 1 } }),
    { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
    "an archived offer is no longer editable",
  );

  must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId, archived: true }));
  assert.deepEqual(
    await createOffer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 2500 } }),
    { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
  );
});

dbTest("setAvailabilityRules replaces the global hours, or one type's hours, in one batch", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const { ticketTypeId } = must(
    await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Appel", rdvEnabled: true, rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 15 } }),
  );
  const global = async () =>
    db
      .select({ d: availabilityRule.dayOfWeek, s: availabilityRule.startMinute, e: availabilityRule.endMinute })
      .from(availabilityRule)
      .where(and(eq(availabilityRule.professionalId, pro.proId), isNull(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId)))
      .orderBy(asc(availabilityRule.dayOfWeek), asc(availabilityRule.startMinute));

  must(
    await setAvailabilityRules(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId: null,
      rules: [
        { dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 540, endMinute: 720 },
        { dayOfWeek: 1, startMinute: 840, endMinute: 1080 },
      ],
    }),
  );
  must(
    await setAvailabilityRules(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId,
      rules: [{ dayOfWeek: 2, startMinute: 720, endMinute: 840 }],
    }),
  );
  must(
    await setAvailabilityRules(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId: null,
      rules: [{ dayOfWeek: 3, startMinute: 600, endMinute: 780 }],
    }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await global(), [{ d: 3, s: 600, e: 780 }], "the global hours were replaced");
  const typed = await db.select().from(availabilityRule).where(eq(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId));
  assert.equal(typed.length, 1, "the type's own hours are untouched");

  assert.equal(
    (
      await setAvailabilityRules(db, {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        ticketTypeId: null,
        rules: [{ dayOfWeek: 3, startMinute: 780, endMinute: 600 }],
      })
    ).ok,
    false,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(await global(), [{ d: 3, s: 600, e: 780 }], "an invalid list changes nothing");
  assert.deepEqual(
    await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: "missing", rules: [] }),
    { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
  );
});

dbTest("setTypeQuestions keeps known ids, inserts new ones, deletes the rest; answers keep their label", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const { ticketTypeId } = must(
    await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Particulier", rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 } }),
  );
  must(
    await setTypeQuestions(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId,
      questions: [
        { label: "Objectif ?", type: "text", required: true },
        { label: "Niveau ?", type: "choice", options: ["Débutant", "Confirmé"] },
      ],
    }),
  );
  const initial = await db
    .select()
    .from(question)
    .where(eq(question.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId))
    .orderBy(asc(question.position));
  const [objective, levelQ] = initial;
  assert.deepEqual(
    initial.map((q) => [q.label, q.type, q.options, q.required, q.position]),
    [
      ["Objectif ?", "text", null, true, 0],
      ["Niveau ?", "choice", ["Débutant", "Confirmé"], false, 1],
    ],
  );

  const rdv = await rowRdv(db, pro, ticketTypeId);
  const bookingId = await rowBooking(db, pro, { occurrenceId: rdv, customerId: await rowCustomer(db, pro) });
  const now = new Date();
  await db.insert(bookingAnswer).values({
    id: nanoid(),
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    bookingId,
    questionId: levelQ.id,
    questionLabel: levelQ.label,
    value: "Débutant",
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
  });

  must(
    await setTypeQuestions(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId,
      questions: [
        { label: "Matériel ?", type: "boolean" },
        { id: objective.id, label: "Votre objectif ?", type: "text", required: true },
      ],
    }),
  );
  const after = await db
    .select()
    .from(question)
    .where(eq(question.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId))
    .orderBy(asc(question.position));
  assert.deepEqual(
    after.map((q) => [q.label, q.type, q.position]),
    [
      ["Matériel ?", "boolean", 0],
      ["Votre objectif ?", "text", 1],
    ],
  );
  assert.equal(after[1].id, objective.id, "the kept question keeps its id");
  const [answer] = await db.select().from(bookingAnswer).where(eq(bookingAnswer.bookingId, bookingId));
  assert.equal(answer.questionId, null, "the deleted question's answers are detached (SET NULL)");
  assert.equal(answer.questionLabel, "Niveau ?");

  assert.equal(
    (
      await setTypeQuestions(db, {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        ticketTypeId,
        questions: [{ label: "Choix ?", type: "choice", options: ["Seul"] }],
      })
    ).ok,
    false,
    "a choice needs two options",
  );
});

dbTest("updateBookingSettings validates the bounds; hasDomainData sees the catalog", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  assert.equal(await hasDomainData(db, pro.proId), false);
  must(
    await updateBookingSettings(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      settings: { bookingHorizonDays: 90, minNoticeMinutes: 0, cancelDeadlineHours: 48 },
    }),
  );
  const [row] = await db.select().from(professional).where(eq(professional.id, pro.proId));
  assert.deepEqual([row.bookingHorizonDays, row.minNoticeMinutes, row.cancelDeadlineHours], [90, 0, 48]);
  for (const settings of [
    { bookingHorizonDays: 366, minNoticeMinutes: 0, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 },
    { bookingHorizonDays: 60, minNoticeMinutes: 10_081, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 },
    { bookingHorizonDays: 60, minNoticeMinutes: 60, cancelDeadlineHours: 721 },
  ]) {
    const res = await updateBookingSettings(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, settings });
    assert.equal(res.ok, false);
    assert.equal(!res.ok && res.code, "invalid");
  }
  assert.deepEqual(
    await updateBookingSettings(db, {
      professionalId: "missing",
      settings: { bookingHorizonDays: 60, minNoticeMinutes: 60, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 },
    }),
    { ok: false, code: "not_found" },
  );

  must(await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: "Premier" } }));
  assert.equal(await hasDomainData(db, pro.proId), true);
});
```

Expected: FAIL. The file does not load (`Error: Cannot find module './catalog'`), then `ℹ fail 1`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the catalog.** Create `src/lib/booking/catalog.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Catalog writes (spec § 9 « Archiver ou supprimer », decision 13): ticket
 * types, offers, RDV hours and questions, booking settings. Used by the seed,
 * bootstrap-content and the P3 actions (src/server/pro-catalog.ts). Every
 * statement filters professional_id; every write is one tryAtomic, so CHECK
 * and FK errors come back as engine codes.
 */
import { and, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import { z } from "zod";

import { G, PlanError, textArray, tryAtomic, ts, type Db, type Query } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { availabilityRule, professional, question, ticketOffer, ticketType } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import {
  availabilityRuleInputSchema,
  bookingSettingsSchema,
  BOUNDS,
  offerInputSchema,
  questionInputSchema,
  ticketTypeInputSchema,
  type AvailabilityRuleInput,
  type BookingSettings,
  type OfferInput,
  type QuestionInput,
  type TicketTypeInput,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

import { fail, failOf, type EngineFail, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/** zod → data, or `invalid` with the first issue's message. */
function parse<S extends z.ZodType>(schema: S, input: unknown): { ok: true; data: z.output<S> } | EngineFail {
  const r = schema.safeParse(input);
  return r.success ? { ok: true, data: r.data } : fail("invalid", r.error.issues[0]?.message);
}

const typeExists = (professionalId: string, ticketTypeId: string, opts: { active?: boolean } = {}) =>
  sql`exists (select 1 from ticket_type where id = ${ticketTypeId} and professional_id = ${professionalId}${
    opts.active ? sql` and archived_at is null` : sql``
  })`;

function typeColumns(t: z.output<typeof ticketTypeInputSchema>) {
  return {
    name: t.name,
    description: t.description || null,
    color: t.color,
    rdvEnabled: t.rdvEnabled,
    rdvFree: t.rdvFree,
    rdvDurationMin: t.rdvDurationMin,
    rdvBufferBeforeMin: t.rdvBufferBeforeMin,
    rdvBufferAfterMin: t.rdvBufferAfterMin,
    rdvBookingHorizonDays: t.rdvOverrides.bookingHorizonDays,
    rdvMinNoticeMinutes: t.rdvOverrides.minNoticeMinutes,
    rdvCancelDeadlineHours: t.rdvOverrides.cancelDeadlineHours,
  };
}

function offerColumns(o: z.output<typeof offerInputSchema>) {
  return {
    label: o.label || null,
    quantity: o.quantity,
    priceCents: o.priceCents,
    validityCount: o.validityCount,
    validityUnit: o.validityUnit,
    isActive: o.isActive,
  };
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Ticket types ───────────────────────────── */

/** Inserts a type at the end of the list (position = max + 1). Errors: invalid. */
export async function createTicketType(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; type: TicketTypeInput },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketTypeId: string }>> {
  const parsed = parse(ticketTypeInputSchema, input.type);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const ticketTypeId = nanoid();
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db.insert(ticketType).values({
      id: ticketTypeId,
      professionalId: input.professionalId,
      ...typeColumns(parsed.data),
      position: sql`(select coalesce(max(position) + 1, 0) from ticket_type where professional_id = ${input.professionalId})`,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    }),
  ]);
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return { ok: true, effects: [], ticketTypeId };
}

/** Replaces the editable fields (archived or not). Errors: invalid, not_found. */
export async function updateTicketType(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string; type: TicketTypeInput },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = parse(ticketTypeInputSchema, input.type);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(ticketType)
      .set({ ...typeColumns(parsed.data), updatedAt: now })
      .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, input.ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, input.professionalId))),
    G(db, typeExists(input.professionalId, input.ticketTypeId), "not_found"),
  ]);
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/** Sets or clears archived_at; offers and event links are left untouched (unarchiving restores everything). Errors: not_found. */
export async function setTicketTypeArchived(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string; archived: boolean },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(ticketType)
      .set({ archivedAt: input.archived ? sql`coalesce(archived_at, ${ts(now)})` : null, updatedAt: now })
      .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, input.ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, input.professionalId))),
    G(db, typeExists(input.professionalId, input.ticketTypeId), "not_found"),
  ]);
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/**
 * « Supprimer »: DELETE (offers, links, questions and hours cascade). A type
 * with tickets, purchases or RDV occurrences raises 23503 (in_use) and is
 * archived instead. Errors: not_found.
 */
export async function deleteTicketType(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ outcome: "deleted" | "archived" }>> {
  const where = and(eq(ticketType.id, input.ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, input.professionalId));
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const [row] = await db.select({ id: ticketType.id }).from(ticketType).where(where);
      if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      return () => [db.delete(ticketType).where(where)];
    },
    { ctx: "delete" },
  );
  if (res.ok) return { ok: true, effects: [], outcome: "deleted" };
  if (res.code !== "in_use") return failOf(res);
  const archived = await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { ...input, archived: true }, now);
  return archived.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [], outcome: "archived" } : archived;
}

/* ───────────────────────────── Offers ───────────────────────────── */

/** Adds an offer at the end of its type's list. Errors: invalid, not_found (type unknown or archived). */
export async function createOffer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; offer: OfferInput },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ offerId: string }>> {
  const parsed = parse(offerInputSchema, input.offer);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const o = parsed.data;
  const offerId = nanoid();
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [type] = await db
      .select({ id: ticketType.id })
      .from(ticketType)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(ticketType.id, o.ticketTypeId),
          eq(ticketType.professionalId, input.professionalId),
          isNull(ticketType.archivedAt),
        ),
      );
    if (!type) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    return () => [
      db.insert(ticketOffer).values({
        id: offerId,
        professionalId: input.professionalId,
        ticketTypeId: o.ticketTypeId,
        ...offerColumns(o),
        position: sql`(select coalesce(max(position) + 1, 0) from ticket_offer where ticket_type_id = ${o.ticketTypeId})`,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      }),
      G(db, typeExists(input.professionalId, o.ticketTypeId, { active: true }), "not_found"),
    ];
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return { ok: true, effects: [], offerId };
}

/**
 * Edits an offer (ticketTypeId is immutable and ignored). Pending purchases
 * keep their snapshot. Errors: invalid, not_found (unknown or archived offer).
 */
export async function updateOffer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; offerId: string; offer: OfferInput },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = parse(offerInputSchema, input.offer);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(ticketOffer)
      .set({ ...offerColumns(parsed.data), updatedAt: now })
      .where(
        and(
          eq(ticketOffer.id, input.offerId),
          eq(ticketOffer.professionalId, input.professionalId),
          isNull(ticketOffer.archivedAt),
        ),
      ),
    G(
      db,
      sql`exists (select 1 from ticket_offer where id = ${input.offerId} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and archived_at is null)`,
      "not_found",
    ),
  ]);
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/** « Supprimer »: DELETE; an offer already bought (23503) gets archived_at instead. Errors: not_found. */
export async function deleteOffer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; offerId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ outcome: "deleted" | "archived" }>> {
  const where = and(
    eq(ticketOffer.id, input.offerId),
    eq(ticketOffer.professionalId, input.professionalId),
    isNull(ticketOffer.archivedAt),
  );
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const [row] = await db.select({ id: ticketOffer.id }).from(ticketOffer).where(where);
      if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      return () => [db.delete(ticketOffer).where(where)];
    },
    { ctx: "delete" },
  );
  if (res.ok) return { ok: true, effects: [], outcome: "deleted" };
  if (res.code !== "in_use") return failOf(res);
  const archived = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(ticketOffer)
      .set({ archivedAt: sql`coalesce(archived_at, ${ts(now)})`, updatedAt: now })
      .where(and(eq(ticketOffer.id, input.offerId), eq(ticketOffer.professionalId, input.professionalId))),
    G(
      db,
      sql`exists (select 1 from ticket_offer where id = ${input.offerId} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId})`,
      "not_found",
    ),
  ]);
  return archived.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [], outcome: "archived" } : failOf(archived);
}

/* ─────────────────────── RDV hours, questions, settings ─────────────────────── */

const rulesSchema = z.array(availabilityRuleInputSchema).max(7 * 24);

/**
 * Replaces, in one batch, the global weekly hours (ticketTypeId null) or the
 * hours of one type (which then replace the global ones for that type; an
 * empty list falls back to the global hours). Errors: invalid, not_found.
 */
export async function setAvailabilityRules(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string | null; rules: AvailabilityRuleInput[] },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = parse(rulesSchema, input.rules);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const { professionalId, ticketTypeId } = input;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    if (ticketTypeId) {
      const [type] = await db
        .select({ id: ticketType.id })
        .from(ticketType)
        .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, professionalId)));
      if (!type) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    }
    const rows = parsed.data.map((r) => ({
      id: nanoid(),
      professionalId,
      ticketTypeId,
      dayOfWeek: r.dayOfWeek,
      startMinute: r.startMinute,
      endMinute: r.endMinute,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    }));
    return () => {
      const scope = ticketTypeId ? eq(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId) : isNull(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId);
      const qs: Query[] = [db.delete(availabilityRule).where(and(eq(availabilityRule.professionalId, professionalId), scope))];
      if (ticketTypeId) qs.push(G(db, typeExists(professionalId, ticketTypeId), "not_found"));
      if (rows.length > 0) qs.push(db.insert(availabilityRule).values(rows));
      return qs;
    };
  });
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

const questionsSchema = z.array(questionInputSchema).max(BOUNDS.questions);

/**
 * Replaces the RDV questions of a type, in one batch: questions sent with a
 * known id are updated in place, the others inserted, the missing ones
 * deleted. Position = list order. Past answers keep question_label (their
 * question_id is SET NULL). Errors: invalid, not_found.
 */
export async function setTypeQuestions(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string; questions: QuestionInput[] },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = parse(questionsSchema, input.questions);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const { professionalId, ticketTypeId } = input;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [type] = await db
      .select({ id: ticketType.id })
      .from(ticketType)
      .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, professionalId)));
    if (!type) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    const existing = new Set(
      (
        await db
          .select({ id: question.id })
          .from(question)
          .where(and(eq(question.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId), eq(question.professionalId, professionalId)))
      ).map((q) => q.id),
    );
    const kept: string[] = [];
    const rows = parsed.data.map((q, position) => {
      const keep = q.id !== undefined && existing.has(q.id) && !kept.includes(q.id);
      const id = keep && q.id ? q.id : nanoid();
      if (keep) kept.push(id);
      return {
        keep,
        id,
        values: {
          label: q.label,
          type: q.type,
          options: q.type === "choice" ? q.options : null,
          required: q.required,
          position,
        },
      };
    });
    return () => {
      const qs: Query[] = [
        db
          .delete(question)
          .where(
            and(
              eq(question.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId),
              eq(question.professionalId, professionalId),
              sql`${question.id} <> all(${textArray(kept)})`,
            ),
          ),
      ];
      for (const r of rows.filter((x) => x.keep)) {
        qs.push(
          db
            .update(question)
            .set({ ...r.values, updatedAt: now })
            .where(and(eq(question.id, r.id), eq(question.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId))),
        );
      }
      const added = rows.filter((x) => !x.keep);
      if (added.length > 0) {
        qs.push(
          db.insert(question).values(
            added.map((r) => ({
              id: r.id,
              professionalId,
              ticketTypeId,
              ...r.values,
              createdAt: now,
              updatedAt: now,
            })),
          ),
        );
      }
      qs.push(G(db, typeExists(professionalId, ticketTypeId), "not_found"));
      return qs;
    };
  });
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/** The pro's booking settings (horizon, notice, client cancellation deadline). Errors: invalid, not_found. */
export async function updateBookingSettings(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; settings: BookingSettings },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = parse(bookingSettingsSchema, input.settings);
  if (!parsed.ok) return parsed;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(professional)
      .set({ ...parsed.data, updatedAt: now })
      .where(eq(professional.id, input.professionalId)),
    G(db, sql`exists (select 1 from professional where id = ${input.professionalId})`, "not_found"),
  ]);
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/** True when the pro has any ticket_type, event, customer or time_off row (bootstrap-content guard). */
export async function hasDomainData(db: Db, professionalId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ found: boolean }>(sql`select (
    exists (select 1 from ticket_type where professional_id = ${professionalId})
    or exists (select 1 from event where professional_id = ${professionalId})
    or exists (select 1 from customer where professional_id = ${professionalId})
    or exists (select 1 from time_off where professional_id = ${professionalId})
  ) as found`);
  return res.rows[0]?.found === true;
}
```

```ts
import type { RecurrenceRuleInput } from "@/lib/tickets";

  minGeneratedUntil,
  needsHorizon,

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T): Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.equal(res.ok, true, JSON.stringify(res));
  return res as Extract<T, { ok: true }>;
}

const DAILY_NEVER = (startDate: string): RecurrenceRuleInput => ({
  startDate,
  startMinute: 600,
  freq: "daily",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays: null,
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "never",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: null,
});

const ONCE = (startDate: string, startMinute: number): RecurrenceRuleInput => ({
  startDate,
  startMinute,
  freq: "once",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays: null,
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "count",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: 1,
});

/** The expander's rule for an input rule (durationMin is an event column). */
function asRule(rule: RecurrenceRuleInput, durationMin = 60): RecurrenceRule {
  return { ...rule, durationMin };
}

async function rowsOf(db: Parameters<typeof createEvent>[0], eventId: string) {
  return db.select().from(occurrence).where(eq(occurrence.eventId, eventId)).orderBy(asc(occurrence.seriesDate));
}

async function eventRow(db: Parameters<typeof createEvent>[0], eventId: string) {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(event).where(eq(event.id, eventId));
  return row;
}

});

dbTest("an event created 10 days ago gets exactly the missing dates; a second call inserts nothing", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const past = addDaysMs(now, -10);
  const startDate = todayKey(past, pro.tz);
  const rule = DAILY_NEVER(startDate);
  const created = must(await createEvent(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, event: { title: "Ancien", rule } }, past));
  assert.equal(created.occurrenceCount, 401);
  assert.equal(await minGeneratedUntil(db, pro.proId, now), horizonEnd(past, pro.tz));

  const missing = expandDates(asRule(rule), addDays(horizonEnd(past, pro.tz), 1), horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
  assert.ok(missing.length >= 9 && missing.length <= 11, "about ten days (a DST change may shift local midnight)");
  assert.deepEqual(await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now), { ok: true, effects: [], inserted: missing.length, complete: true });
  const rows = await rowsOf(db, created.eventId);
  assert.deepEqual(
    rows.map((r) => r.seriesDate),
    expandDates(asRule(rule), startDate, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz)),
  );
  assert.equal((await eventRow(db, created.eventId)).generatedUntil, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
  assert.deepEqual(await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now), { ok: true, effects: [], inserted: 0, complete: true });
});

dbTest("ensureHorizon re-plans once when the event changes between its read and its batch", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const past = addDaysMs(now, -10);
  const rule = DAILY_NEVER(todayKey(past, pro.tz));
  const created = must(await createEvent(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, event: { title: "Bougé", rule } }, past));
  const missing = expandDates(asRule(rule), addDays(horizonEnd(past, pro.tz), 1), horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
  let hooks = 0;
  const res = must(
    await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now, {
      afterPlan: async () => {
        hooks++;
        await db.execute(sql`update event set version = version + 1 where id = ${created.eventId}`);
      },
    }),
  );
  assert.equal(hooks, 1);
  assert.deepEqual([res.inserted, res.complete], [missing.length, true], "the re-plan used the new version");
});

dbTest("a split during ensureHorizon leaves no phantom séance after the new until_date", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const past = addDaysMs(now, -10);
  const created = must(
    await createEvent(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, event: { title: "Coupé", rule: DAILY_NEVER(todayKey(past, pro.tz)) } }, past),
  );
  const oldEnd = horizonEnd(past, pro.tz);
  const res = must(
    await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now, {
      afterPlan: async () => {
        // what a split does to the old segment: version + 1, end at a date already generated
        await db.execute(sql`
          update event set version = version + 1, end_mode = 'until', until_date = ${addDays(todayKey(now, pro.tz), 5)}::date
          where id = ${created.eventId}`);
      },
    }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual([res.inserted, res.complete], [0, true]);
  const beyond = await db
    .select({ id: occurrence.id })
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(sql`${occurrence.eventId} = ${created.eventId} and ${occurrence.seriesDate} > ${oldEnd}::date`);
  assert.equal(beyond.length, 0, "nothing was generated past the old horizon");
  assert.equal(await minGeneratedUntil(db, pro.proId, now), null, "the segment now ends before its generated_until");
});

dbTest("archived events are skipped by ensureHorizon and minGeneratedUntil", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const past = addDaysMs(now, -10);
  const created = must(
    await createEvent(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, event: { title: "Archivé", rule: DAILY_NEVER(todayKey(past, pro.tz)) } }, past),
  );
  await db.update(event).set({ archivedAt: now }).where(eq(event.id, created.eventId));
  assert.deepEqual(await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now), { ok: true, effects: [], inserted: 0, complete: true });
  assert.equal(await minGeneratedUntil(db, pro.proId, now), null);
  const late = await db
    .select({ id: occurrence.id })
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(and(eq(occurrence.eventId, created.eventId), gt(occurrence.seriesDate, horizonEnd(past, pro.tz))));
  assert.equal(late.length, 0);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement occurrences.** Create `src/lib/booking/occurrences.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Materialised séances (spec § 7). Creating an event inserts its occurrences
 * over [start_date, min(last date, today + MATERIALIZE_DAYS)] in the same
 * batch; ensureHorizon keeps every active segment MATERIALIZE_DAYS ahead (the
 * nightly job, and after() on the pro planning and the public page when
 * needsHorizon says so). Inserts are ON CONFLICT (event_id, series_date) DO
 * NOTHING: a moved or cancelled row is never recreated.
 *
 * event.generated_until: every rule date ≤ generated_until has its row. It is
 * min(last rule date, horizon end), or the last inserted date when a call hit
 * MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS. A series is complete once generated_until ≥ its last
 * date (never for an endless one).
 */
import { and, asc, eq, isNull, lt, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { addDays, compareKeys, minKey, todayKey, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  dateArray,
  lockRows,
  textArray,
  tryAtomic,
  ts,
  tsArray,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
  type Query,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { event, professional, question } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { EXPAND_MAX_DATES, expand, lastSeriesDate, type RecurrenceRule } from "@/lib/recurrence";
import {
  eventInputSchema,
  HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS,
  MATERIALIZE_DAYS,
  MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS,
  type EventInput,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

import { fail, failOf, type EngineResult } from "./types";

export type EventRow = typeof event.$inferSelect;
export type OccurrenceDraft = { id: string; seriesDate: DateKey; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date };

function monthWeekOf(n: number | null): RecurrenceRule["monthWeek"] {
  return n === 1 || n === 2 || n === 3 || n === 4 || n === -1 ? n : null;
}

/** The recurrence of an event row (durationMin included). */
export function ruleOf(e: EventRow): RecurrenceRule {
  return {
    startDate: e.startDate,
    startMinute: e.startMinute,
    durationMin: e.durationMin,
    freq: e.freq,
    interval: e.interval,
    weekdays: e.weekdays,
    monthlyBy: e.monthlyBy,
    monthWeek: monthWeekOf(e.monthWeek),
    endMode: e.endMode,
    untilDate: e.untilDate,
    occurrenceCount: e.occurrenceCount,
  };
}

/** Local today (tz) + MATERIALIZE_DAYS: occurrences exist up to this date. */
export function horizonEnd(now: Date, tz: string): DateKey {
  return addDays(todayKey(now, tz), MATERIALIZE_DAYS);
}

/** Render-time trigger (spec § 7): an active series is generated less than HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS ahead. */
export function needsHorizon(minGeneratedUntil: DateKey | null, now: Date, tz: string): boolean {
  if (minGeneratedUntil === null) return false;
  return compareKeys(minGeneratedUntil, addDays(todayKey(now, tz), HORIZON_REFRESH_DAYS)) < 0;
}

/**
 * The rule's dates in [from, to] as drafts with fresh ids, at most maxRows.
 * generatedUntil = `to` when everything fits, else the last draft's date
 * (from − 1 when not even one fits).
 */
export function draftOccurrences(
  rule: RecurrenceRule,
  tz: string,
  from: DateKey,
  to: DateKey,
  maxRows: number,
): { drafts: OccurrenceDraft[]; generatedUntil: DateKey } {
  const dates = expand(rule, tz, from, to);
  const capped = dates.length > maxRows || dates.length >= EXPAND_MAX_DATES;
  const kept = dates.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxRows));
  const drafts = kept.map((d) => ({ id: nanoid(), seriesDate: d.seriesDate, startsAt: d.startsAt, endsAt: d.endsAt }));
  if (!capped) return { drafts, generatedUntil: to };
  return { drafts, generatedUntil: drafts.length > 0 ? drafts[drafts.length - 1].seriesDate : addDays(from, -1) };
}

/**
 * One INSERT … SELECT FROM unnest(ids, dates, starts, ends) ON CONFLICT
 * (event_id, series_date) DO NOTHING. With `guardVersion` (ensureHorizon), a
 * row is inserted only while the event still has that version, is not
 * archived and the date is ≤ until_date: a concurrent split never gets
 * phantom séances.
 */
export function insertOccurrences(
  c: BatchCtx,
  args: { professionalId: string; eventId: string; capacity: number; drafts: readonly OccurrenceDraft[]; guardVersion?: number },
): Query {
  const guard =
    args.guardVersion === undefined
      ? sql``
      : sql`where exists (select 1 from event ev where ev.id = ${args.eventId} and ev.version = ${args.guardVersion}::int
          and ev.archived_at is null and (ev.until_date is null or v.d <= ev.until_date))`;
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    insert into occurrence (id, professional_id, event_id, series_date, starts_at, ends_at, block_start, block_end,
      capacity, seats_taken, status, created_at, updated_at)
    select v.id, ${args.professionalId}, ${args.eventId}, v.d, v.s, v.e, v.s, v.e,
      ${args.capacity}::int, 0, 'scheduled'::occurrence_status, ${ts(c.now)}, ${ts(c.now)}
    from unnest(${textArray(args.drafts.map((d) => d.id))}, ${dateArray(args.drafts.map((d) => d.seriesDate))},
      ${tsArray(args.drafts.map((d) => d.startsAt))}, ${tsArray(args.drafts.map((d) => d.endsAt))}) as v(id, d, s, e)
    ${guard}
    on conflict (event_id, series_date) do nothing`);
}

/**
 * Creates an event (first segment: series_root_id = id, version 1), its
 * links (only this pro's types, else `invalid`), its questions and its
 * occurrences, in ONE batch. Effects: none (empty séances are not synced).
 * Errors: invalid, not_found (unknown pro).
 */
export async function createEvent(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; event: EventInput },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ eventId: string; occurrenceCount: number }>> {
  const parsed = eventInputSchema.safeParse(input.event);
  if (!parsed.success) return fail("invalid", parsed.error.issues[0]?.message);
  const e = parsed.data;
  const { professionalId } = input;
  const eventId = nanoid();
  const typeIds = [...new Set(e.ticketTypeIds)];
  let occurrenceCount = 0;

  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [pro] = await db
      .select({ tz: professional.timezone })
      .from(professional)
      .where(eq(professional.id, professionalId));
    if (!pro) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    const rule: RecurrenceRule = { ...e.rule, durationMin: e.durationMin };
    const last = lastSeriesDate(rule);
    const end = horizonEnd(now, pro.tz);
    const to = last === null ? end : minKey(last, end);
    const { drafts, generatedUntil } = draftOccurrences(rule, pro.tz, rule.startDate, to, MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS);
    occurrenceCount = drafts.length;

    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const qs: Query[] = [
        db.insert(event).values({
          id: eventId,
          professionalId,
          title: e.title,
          description: e.description || null,
          location: e.location || null,
          color: e.color,
          bookingMode: e.bookingMode,
          isPrivate: e.isPrivate,
          capacity: e.capacity,
          durationMin: e.durationMin,
          startDate: rule.startDate,
          startMinute: rule.startMinute,
          freq: rule.freq,
          interval: rule.interval,
          weekdays: rule.weekdays,
          monthlyBy: rule.monthlyBy,
          monthWeek: rule.monthWeek,
          endMode: rule.endMode,
          untilDate: rule.untilDate,
          occurrenceCount: rule.occurrenceCount,
          bookingHorizonDays: e.overrides.bookingHorizonDays,
          minNoticeMinutes: e.overrides.minNoticeMinutes,
          cancelDeadlineHours: e.overrides.cancelDeadlineHours,
          generatedUntil,
          seriesRootId: eventId,
          version: 1,
          lastActionId: aid,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        }),
      ];
      if (typeIds.length > 0) {
        qs.push(
          db.execute(sql`
            insert into event_ticket_type (event_id, ticket_type_id, professional_id, created_at)
            select ${eventId}, id, ${professionalId}, ${ts(now)} from ticket_type
            where id = any(${textArray(typeIds)}) and professional_id = ${professionalId}`),
          G(
            db,
            sql`(select count(*)::int from event_ticket_type where event_id = ${eventId}) = ${typeIds.length}::int`,
            "invalid",
          ),
        );
      }
      if (e.questions.length > 0) {
        qs.push(
          db.insert(question).values(
            e.questions.map((q, position) => ({
              id: nanoid(),
              professionalId,
              eventId,
              label: q.label,
              type: q.type,
              options: q.type === "choice" ? q.options : null,
              required: q.required,
              position,
              createdAt: now,
              updatedAt: now,
            })),
          ),
        );
      }
      if (drafts.length > 0) {
        qs.push(insertOccurrences(c, { professionalId, eventId, capacity: e.capacity, drafts }));
      }
      return qs;
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return { ok: true, effects: [], eventId, occurrenceCount };
}

/**
 * Brings every active segment of the pro to today + MATERIALIZE_DAYS (or its
 * last date), at most MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS rows per call, in ONE batch:
 * lock the events (first rank of the lock order, id asc), version-guarded
 * inserts, version-guarded generated_until, then G(version unchanged,
 * 'stale'). A segment edited between the read and the batch (split, delete)
 * makes the batch stale: it re-plans once on fresh rows. `complete` = nothing
 * left to generate. Effects: none.
 */
export async function ensureHorizon(
  db: Db,
  professionalId: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<{ inserted: number; complete: boolean }>> {
  let insertAt: number[] = [];
  let complete = true;

  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const [pro] = await db
        .select({ tz: professional.timezone })
        .from(professional)
        .where(eq(professional.id, professionalId));
      if (!pro) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      const end = horizonEnd(now, pro.tz);
      const rows = await db
        .select()
        .from(event)
        .where(and(eq(event.professionalId, professionalId), isNull(event.archivedAt), lt(event.generatedUntil, end)))
        .orderBy(asc(event.id));

      complete = true;
      let budget = MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS;
      const work: { row: EventRow; drafts: OccurrenceDraft[]; generatedUntil: DateKey }[] = [];
      for (const row of rows) {
        const rule = ruleOf(row);
        const last = lastSeriesDate(rule);
        const target = last === null ? end : minKey(last, end);
        if (compareKeys(row.generatedUntil, target) >= 0) continue;
        if (budget === 0) {
          complete = false;
          break;
        }
        const d = draftOccurrences(rule, pro.tz, addDays(row.generatedUntil, 1), target, budget);
        budget -= d.drafts.length;
        if (compareKeys(d.generatedUntil, target) < 0) complete = false;
        work.push({ row, drafts: d.drafts, generatedUntil: d.generatedUntil });
      }

      return (aid) => {
        insertAt = [];
        if (work.length === 0) return [];
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
        const qs: Query[] = [lockRows(db, "event", work.map((w) => w.row.id))];
        for (const w of work) {
          if (w.drafts.length > 0) {
            insertAt.push(qs.length);
            qs.push(
              insertOccurrences(c, {
                professionalId,
                eventId: w.row.id,
                capacity: w.row.capacity,
                drafts: w.drafts,
                guardVersion: w.row.version,
              }),
            );
          }
          qs.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              update event set generated_until = ${w.generatedUntil}::date, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
              where id = ${w.row.id} and version = ${w.row.version}::int and archived_at is null`),
            G(db, sql`(select version from event where id = ${w.row.id}) = ${w.row.version}::int`, "stale"),
          );
        }
        return qs;
      };
    },
    { replanOnStale: true, afterPlan: opts.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const inserted = insertAt.reduce(
    (n, i) => n + ((res.results[i] as { rowCount?: number | null } | undefined)?.rowCount ?? 0),
    0,
  );
  return { ok: true, effects: [], inserted, complete };
}

/**
 * min(generated_until) over the pro's non-archived segments that can still
 * produce dates; null when there is none. `now` keeps the engine signature
 * uniform (the comparison with today is needsHorizon's job).
 */
export async function minGeneratedUntil(db: Db, professionalId: string, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<DateKey | null> {
  void now;
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(event)
    .where(and(eq(event.professionalId, professionalId), isNull(event.archivedAt)));
  let min: DateKey | null = null;
  for (const row of rows) {
    const last = lastSeriesDate(ruleOf(row));
    if (last !== null && compareKeys(row.generatedUntil, last) >= 0) continue;
    if (min === null || compareKeys(row.generatedUntil, min) < 0) min = row.generatedUntil;
  }
  return min;
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

import { instantAt } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { customer, ticketMovement, ticketOffer } from "@/lib/db/schema";

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T): Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.equal(res.ok, true, JSON.stringify(res));
  return res as Extract<T, { ok: true }>;
}

async function customerRow(db: Parameters<typeof upsertCustomer>[0], id: string) {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(customer).where(eq(customer.id, id));
  return row;
}

dbTest("upsertCustomer normalises the e-mail, fills blanks only and never overwrites the name", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const email = testEmail();
  const first = await upsertCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, email: `  ${email.toUpperCase()} `, name: " Léa " });
  const again = await upsertCustomer(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    email,
    name: "Quelqu'un d'autre",
    phone: " 06 00 00 00 00 ",
    userId: pro.userId,
  });
  assert.equal(again.customerId, first.customerId);
  let row = await customerRow(db, first.customerId);
  assert.deepEqual([row.email, row.name, row.phone, row.userId, row.linkVersion], [email, "Léa", "06 00 00 00 00", pro.userId, 1]);

  await upsertCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, email, name: "Encore", phone: "07 11 11 11 11", userId: null });
  row = await customerRow(db, first.customerId);
  assert.deepEqual([row.name, row.phone, row.userId], ["Léa", "06 00 00 00 00", pro.userId], "nothing filled is replaced");
});

dbTest("createCustomer: the pro's name and phone win; created tells a new row", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const email = testEmail();
  const created = must(await createCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, name: "Rafael", email: ` ${email} ` }));
  assert.equal(created.created, true);
  const updated = must(await createCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, name: "Rafael Souza", email, phone: "0612" }));
  assert.deepEqual([updated.customerId, updated.created], [created.customerId, false]);
  must(await createCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, name: "Rafael S.", email }));
  const row = await customerRow(db, created.customerId);

  const bad = await createCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, name: "X", email: "pas-une-adresse" });

  const blank = await createCustomer(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, name: "  ", email: testEmail() });
  assert.equal(!blank.ok && blank.code, "invalid");
});

});

dbTest("offerTickets: N available tickets and N offer movements in one action", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);

  const expiresAt = addDaysMs(now, 30);
  const res = must(
    await offerTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        actorUserId: pro.userId,
        customerId,
        ticketTypeId: typeId,
        quantity: 3,
        expiresAt,
        reason: " Geste commercial ",
        notify: true,
      },
      now,
    ),
  );
  assert.equal(res.ticketIds.length, 3);
  const tickets = await ticketsOf(db, customerId);
  assert.deepEqual(new Set(tickets.map((t) => t.id)), new Set(res.ticketIds));
  assert.ok(
    tickets.every(
      (t) =>
        t.status === "available" &&
        t.purchaseId === null &&
        t.ticketTypeId === typeId &&
        t.expiresAt?.getTime() === expiresAt.getTime() &&
        t.createdAt.getTime() === now.getTime(),
    ),
  );
  const moves = await db.select().from(ticketMovement).where(eq(ticketMovement.customerId, customerId));
  assert.equal(moves.length, 3);
  const actionId = moves[0].actionId;
  assert.ok(
    moves.every(
      (m) =>
        m.actionId === actionId &&
        m.kind === "offer" &&
        m.fromStatus === null &&
        m.toStatus === "available" &&
        m.actor === "pro" &&
        m.actorUserId === pro.userId &&
        m.reason === "Geste commercial",
    ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [{ type: "email.tickets_offered", customerId, actionId, quantity: 3 }]);

  const [quiet] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(
    (await movementsOf(db, quiet)).map((m) => [m.kind, m.fromStatus, m.toStatus]),
    [["offer", null, "available"]],
  );
});

    const foreign = await makeCustomer(db, other);

    must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, archived: true }));
    assert.deepEqual(await offerTickets(db, base, now), { ok: false, code: "not_found" });
    assert.deepEqual(await ticketsOf(db, customerId), []);

  }
});

dbTest("walletBalance counts usable tickets per type, with the soonest expiry", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const typeB = await makeType(db, pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 2);
  const soon = addDaysMs(now, 10);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: soon });
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: addDaysMs(now, -1), now: addDaysMs(now, -30) });
  // no `now`: the fixture acts 1 s before an expiry that is already past (not usable)
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeB, 1, { expiresAt: addDaysMs(now, -2) });
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeB, 1);

  const balance = await walletBalance(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId }, now);
  const expected = [
    { ticketTypeId: typeA, usable: 3, nextExpiry: soon },
    { ticketTypeId: typeB, usable: 1, nextExpiry: null },
  ].sort((x, y) => (x.ticketTypeId < y.ticketTypeId ? -1 : 1));
  assert.deepEqual(
    balance.map((b) => ({ ...b, nextExpiry: b.nextExpiry ? b.nextExpiry.getTime() : null })),
    expected.map((b) => ({ ...b, nextExpiry: b.nextExpiry ? b.nextExpiry.getTime() : null })),
  );
});

dbTest("fixtures: makeEvent is a one-off at 10:00 local in three days; makeOffer has the defaults", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(db, pro, typeId);
  const [offer] = await db.select().from(ticketOffer).where(eq(ticketOffer.id, offerId));
  assert.deepEqual([offer.quantity, offer.priceCents, offer.validityCount], [1, 2500, null]);
  const { occurrences } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  assert.equal(occurrences.length, 1);
  assert.equal(occurrences[0].seriesDate, dayIn(pro, 3));
  assert.equal(occurrences[0].startsAt.getTime(), instantAt(dayIn(pro, 3), 600, pro.tz).getTime());
  assert.equal(occurrences[0].capacity, 12);
});
```
