- [ ] **Step 3: Implement wallet part 1.** Create `src/lib/booking/wallet.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Customers and the client file (spec § 11 E1, E8). Every statement filters
 * professional_id. Part 1 (Task 19): identity, « Offrir », balance. Part 2
 * (Task 29): convert, close, point, unpoint, move, type facts.
 */
import { and, asc, eq, gt, isNull, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import { z } from "zod";

import { G, PlanError, textArray, tryAtomic, ts, type Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { customer, ticket, ticketType } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { BOUNDS, customerInputSchema, normalizeEmail } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { fail, failOf, type Effect, type EngineResult } from "./types";

const customerExists = (professionalId: string, customerId: string) =>
  sql`exists (select 1 from customer where id = ${customerId} and professional_id = ${professionalId})`;

/**
 * Anonymous identity (E1, E3, E4, E9), ONE statement: insert, or on
 * (professional_id, email) fill only the blanks: phone and user_id are
 * COALESCEd, the name is NEVER overwritten by anonymous input. `userId` only
 * when a verified session carries the same e-mail (the caller checks). The
 * caller validated the input (customerInputSchema); a CHECK error throws.
 */
export async function upsertCustomer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; email: string; name: string; phone?: string | null; userId?: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<{ customerId: string }> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ id: string }>(sql`
    insert into customer (id, professional_id, email, name, phone, user_id, link_version, created_at, updated_at)
    values (${nanoid()}, ${input.professionalId}, ${normalizeEmail(input.email)}, ${input.name.trim()},
      ${input.phone?.trim() || null}, ${input.userId ?? null}, 1, ${ts(now)}, ${ts(now)})
    on conflict (professional_id, email) do update set
      phone = coalesce(customer.phone, excluded.phone),
      user_id = coalesce(customer.user_id, excluded.user_id),
      updated_at = excluded.updated_at
    returning id`);
  return { customerId: res.rows[0].id };
}

/**
 * « Nouveau client » (pro): the same upsert, but the pro's name wins, and the
 * phone too when one is given. `created` = a new row. Errors: invalid.
 */
export async function createCustomer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; name: string; email: string; phone?: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ customerId: string; created: boolean }>> {
  const parsed = customerInputSchema.safeParse({
    name: input.name,
    email: input.email.trim(),
    phone: input.phone ?? undefined,
  });
  if (!parsed.success) return fail("invalid", parsed.error.issues[0]?.message);
  const { name, email } = parsed.data;
  const phone = parsed.data.phone || null;
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db.execute(sql`
      insert into customer (id, professional_id, email, name, phone, link_version, created_at, updated_at)
      values (${nanoid()}, ${input.professionalId}, ${email}, ${name}, ${phone}, 1, ${ts(now)}, ${ts(now)})
      on conflict (professional_id, email) do update set
        name = excluded.name,
        phone = coalesce(excluded.phone, customer.phone),
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at
      returning id, (xmax = 0) as created`),
  ]);
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const [row] = (res.results[0] as { rows: { id: string; created: boolean }[] }).rows;
  return { ok: true, effects: [], customerId: row.id, created: row.created };
}

const customerEditSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.name),
  phone: z.string().trim().max(BOUNDS.phone).nullable(),
});

/** « Modifier nom / téléphone ». Errors: invalid, not_found. */
export async function updateCustomer(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; customerId: string; name: string; phone: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const parsed = customerEditSchema.safeParse({ name: input.name, phone: input.phone });
  if (!parsed.success) return fail("invalid", parsed.error.issues[0]?.message);
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db
      .update(customer)
      .set({ name: parsed.data.name, phone: parsed.data.phone || null, updatedAt: now })
      .where(and(eq(customer.id, input.customerId), eq(customer.professionalId, input.professionalId))),
    G(db, customerExists(input.professionalId, input.customerId), "not_found"),
  ]);
  return res.ok ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : failOf(res);
}

/** « Déconnecter ses liens »: link_version + 1 revokes every personal link of the customer. Errors: not_found. */
export async function revokeLinks(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; customerId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ linkVersion: number }>> {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => () => [
    db.execute(sql`
      update customer set link_version = link_version + 1, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
      where id = ${input.customerId} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}
      returning link_version`),
    G(db, customerExists(input.professionalId, input.customerId), "not_found"),
  ]);
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const [row] = (res.results[0] as { rows: { link_version: number }[] }).rows;
  return { ok: true, effects: [], linkVersion: row.link_version };
}

/**
 * « Offrir N » (E8): N tickets (JS ids, purchase_id NULL, available, optional
 * expiry) for a customer of the pro, of an active type of the pro, and their
 * `offer` movements, in ONE statement; then G(N movements for this action,
 * 'stale'). Effects: email.tickets_offered when `notify`.
 * Errors: invalid (quantity, expiry not in the future, note too long), not_found, stale.
 */
export async function offerTickets(
  db: Db,
  input: {
    professionalId: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    customerId: string;
    ticketTypeId: string;
    quantity: number;
    expiresAt: Date | null;
    reason?: string | null;
    notify: boolean;
  },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  const n = input.quantity;
  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 1 || n > BOUNDS.walletQuantity) return fail("invalid");
  if (input.expiresAt && input.expiresAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) return fail("invalid");
  const reason = input.reason?.trim() || null;
  if (reason && reason.length > BOUNDS.note) return fail("invalid");
  const p = input.professionalId;
  const ticketIds = Array.from({ length: n }, () => nanoid());
  const expiresAt = input.expiresAt ? ts(input.expiresAt) : sql`null::timestamptz`;

  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [c] = await db
      .select({ id: customer.id })
      .from(customer)
      .where(and(eq(customer.id, input.customerId), eq(customer.professionalId, p)));
    const [t] = await db
      .select({ id: ticketType.id })
      .from(ticketType)
      .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, input.ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, p), isNull(ticketType.archivedAt)));
    if (!c || !t) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    return (aid) => [
      db.execute(sql`
        with t as (
          insert into ticket (id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id, purchase_id, status, booking_id,
            expires_at, status_changed_at, created_at, updated_at)
          select x.id, c.professional_id, c.id, tt.id, null, 'available'::ticket_status, null,
            ${expiresAt}, ${ts(now)}, ${ts(now)}, ${ts(now)}
          from unnest(${textArray(ticketIds)}) as x(id)
          join customer c on c.id = ${input.customerId} and c.professional_id = ${p}
          join ticket_type tt on tt.id = ${input.ticketTypeId} and tt.professional_id = ${p} and tt.archived_at is null
          returning id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id
        )
        insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status, to_status,
          ticket_type_id, actor, actor_user_id, reason, created_at)
        select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${aid}, 'offer'::movement_kind, null, 'available'::ticket_status,
          ticket_type_id, 'pro'::actor, ${input.actorUserId}, ${reason}, ${ts(now)}
        from t`),
      G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from ticket_movement where action_id = ${aid}) = ${n}::int`, "stale"),
    ];
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const effects: Effect[] = input.notify
    ? [{ type: "email.tickets_offered", customerId: input.customerId, actionId: res.aid, quantity: n }]
    : [];
  return { ok: true, effects, ticketIds };
}

/**
 * Usable tickets per type (the balance predicate of spec § 9: available and
 * not expired at `now`), with the soonest expiry; ordered by type id.
 */
export async function walletBalance(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; customerId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<{ ticketTypeId: string; usable: number; nextExpiry: Date | null }[]> {
  return db
    .select({
      ticketTypeId: ticket.ticketTypeId,
      usable: sql<number>`count(*)::int`,
      nextExpiry: sql<Date | null>`min(${ticket.expiresAt})`.mapWith(ticket.expiresAt),
    })
    .from(ticket)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(ticket.professionalId, input.professionalId),
        eq(ticket.customerId, input.customerId),
        eq(ticket.status, "available"),
        or(isNull(ticket.expiresAt), gt(ticket.expiresAt, now)),
      ),
    )
    .groupBy(ticket.ticketTypeId)
    .orderBy(asc(ticket.ticketTypeId));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the engine-backed fixtures.** Create `src/test/fixtures.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Engine-backed fixtures for the DB tests of Tasks 19 to 35: every row is
 * made through the engine (catalog, occurrences, wallet), so the state is
 * always consistent (checkInvariants runs after every dbTest). Each maker
 * throws on an engine failure: a fixture that fails is a broken test.
 */
import { asc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { createOffer, createTicketType } from "../lib/booking/catalog";
import { createEvent } from "../lib/booking/occurrences";
import { createCustomer, offerTickets } from "../lib/booking/wallet";
import { addDays, todayKey, type DateKey } from "../lib/civil-date";
import type { Db } from "../lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, occurrence, ticket, ticketMovement } from "../lib/db/schema";
import type { BookingMode, OfferInput, RecurrenceRuleInput, TicketTypeInput } from "../lib/tickets";
import { testEmail, type TestPro } from "./db";

export type OccurrenceRow = typeof occurrence.$inferSelect;
export type BookingRow = typeof booking.$inferSelect;
export type TicketRow = typeof ticket.$inferSelect;

function check<T extends { ok: boolean }>(what: string, res: T): Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${what} failed: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
  return res as Extract<T, { ok: true }>;
}

/** Local date `days` after today in the pro's time zone. */
export function dayIn(pro: TestPro, days: number, now: Date = new Date()): DateKey {
  return addDays(todayKey(now, pro.tz), days);
}

/** createTicketType with { name: "Type <nanoid>" } + overrides. */
export async function makeType(db: Db, pro: TestPro, over: Partial<TicketTypeInput> = {}): Promise<string> {
  const res = await createTicketType(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, type: { name: `Type ${nanoid(6)}`, ...over } });
  return check("makeType", res).ticketTypeId;
}

/** createOffer with { quantity 1, priceCents 2500, no validity } + overrides. */
export async function makeOffer(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  ticketTypeId: string,
  over: Partial<OfferInput> = {},
): Promise<string> {
  const res = await createOffer(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    offer: { ticketTypeId, quantity: 1, priceCents: 2500, ...over },
  });
  return check("makeOffer", res).offerId;
}

/**
 * createEvent: tickets mode, capacity 12, 60 min, once on dayIn(pro, 3) at
 * 10:00 (600), + overrides. A `rule` override is merged over that one-off
 * rule, so a recurring one must give every field it changes (e.g. freq,
 * weekdays, endMode and occurrenceCount: null). Occurrences by starts_at.
 */
export async function makeEvent(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  over: {
    title?: string;
    bookingMode?: BookingMode;
    capacity?: number;
    durationMin?: number;
    isPrivate?: boolean;
    ticketTypeIds?: string[];
    rule?: Partial<RecurrenceRuleInput>;
  } = {},
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<{ eventId: string; occurrences: OccurrenceRow[] }> {
  const rule: RecurrenceRuleInput = {
    startDate: dayIn(pro, 3, now),
    startMinute: 600,
    freq: "once",
    interval: 1,
    weekdays: null,
    monthlyBy: null,
    monthWeek: null,
    endMode: "count",
    untilDate: null,
    occurrenceCount: 1,
    ...over.rule,
  };
  const res = await createEvent(
    db,
    {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      event: {
        title: over.title ?? `Cours ${nanoid(6)}`,
        bookingMode: over.bookingMode ?? "tickets",
        capacity: over.capacity ?? 12,
        durationMin: over.durationMin ?? 60,
        isPrivate: over.isPrivate ?? false,
        ticketTypeIds: over.ticketTypeIds ?? [],
        rule,
      },
    },
    now,
  );
  const { eventId } = check("makeEvent", res);
  return { eventId, occurrences: await occurrencesOf(db, eventId) };
}

/** createCustomer with a unique testEmail(). */
export async function makeCustomer(db: Db, pro: TestPro, over: { name?: string; email?: string } = {}): Promise<string> {
  const res = await createCustomer(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    name: over.name ?? `Client ${nanoid(6)}`,
    email: over.email ?? testEmail(),
  });
  return check("makeCustomer", res).customerId;
}

/**
 * offerTickets by the pro's user, without e-mail. offerTickets refuses an
 * expiry that is not after its `now`. With an explicit `opts.now`, the
 * fixture acts at that instant (e.g. { now: T − 30 days, expiresAt: T − 1 day }
 * for a ticket that is already expired). Without one, it acts at the wall
 * clock. If `expiresAt` is less than 1 s ahead or already past, it acts 1 s
 * before that expiry instead: tests build expiries from their own earlier clock
 * (addMinutes(now, 1)), and slow round trips since then must not turn them into
 * a refusal.
 */
export async function giveTickets(
  db: Db,
  pro: TestPro,
  customerId: string,
  ticketTypeId: string,
  quantity: number,
  opts: { expiresAt?: Date | null; now?: Date } = {},
): Promise<string[]> {
  const expiresAt = opts.expiresAt ?? null;
  const now =
    opts.now ??
    (expiresAt && expiresAt.getTime() <= Date.now() + 1_000
      ? new Date(expiresAt.getTime() - 1_000)
      : new Date());
  const res = await offerTickets(
    db,
    {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      actorUserId: pro.userId,
      customerId,
      ticketTypeId,
      quantity,
      expiresAt,
      notify: false,
    },
    now,
  );
  return check("giveTickets", res).ticketIds;
}

export async function occurrencesOf(db: Db, eventId: string): Promise<OccurrenceRow[]> {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(eq(occurrence.eventId, eventId))
    .orderBy(asc(occurrence.startsAt), asc(occurrence.id));
}

export async function getOccurrence(db: Db, id: string): Promise<OccurrenceRow> {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(occurrence).where(eq(occurrence.id, id));
  if (!row) throw new Error(`occurrence ${id} not found`);
  return row;
}

export async function getBooking(db: Db, id: string): Promise<BookingRow> {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(booking).where(eq(booking.id, id));
  if (!row) throw new Error(`booking ${id} not found`);
  return row;
}

/** The customer's tickets, oldest first. */
export async function ticketsOf(db: Db, customerId: string): Promise<TicketRow[]> {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(ticket)
    .where(eq(ticket.customerId, customerId))
    .orderBy(asc(ticket.createdAt), asc(ticket.id));
}

/** Movements of a ticket ordered by seq. */
export async function movementsOf(
  db: Db,
  ticketId: string,
): Promise<{ kind: string; fromStatus: string | null; toStatus: string; actionId: string }[]> {
  return db
    .select({
      kind: ticketMovement.kind,
      fromStatus: ticketMovement.fromStatus,
      toStatus: ticketMovement.toStatus,
      actionId: ticketMovement.actionId,
    })
    .from(ticketMovement)
    .where(eq(ticketMovement.ticketId, ticketId))
    .orderBy(asc(ticketMovement.seq));
}
```

<!-- G4 -->

## Contract deviations (G4)

1. **Test-only `opts` on `startBuyAndBook` and `bookRdv`.** Contract §5.16 declares them as `(db, input, now?)`. Both now take a 4th optional parameter `opts: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> }`, which is passed through to `tryAtomic`. §1 allows this ("before an optional test-only `opts`"). Without it there is no deterministic way to write the binding Task 20 minimum "an offer edited after the read → `stale`", or the in-batch `changed`, notice and horizon guards.
2. **`activeBookingOf(db, input, now = new Date())`.** It takes `now` and ignores pending bookings whose hold has elapsed at `now` (the next E1 releases that hold through `R(o)`). It returns `checkoutUrl` only while the purchase is pending AND `checkout_expires_at > now`. The Checkout session closes at 31 min but the hold lasts 45 min, so between the two « Reprendre le paiement » gets `checkoutUrl: null` instead of a dead URL, while `purchaseId` stays set so « Libérer ma place » still works.
3. **Extra exports.**
   - `claims.ts` also exports `assertCustomer`, `readOffer`, `OfferRow`, `OfferScope`, `offerUnchanged`, `insertPurchase` and `BookRdvData`. These are internal to the engine; there is still no barrel.
   - `fulfillment.ts` exports `ShopPurchaseData`, which only names the inline return type of §5.17.
   - `fulfillment.ts` (E9) reuses the `claims.ts` helpers. As a result E1, E4 « buy » and E9 share one offer snapshot, one `stale` guard and one purchase insert.
   - Failed batches are mapped everywhere with Task 16's `failOf` (from `./types`); G4 adds no local copy of it.
4. **Plan-time codes the contract leaves open:**
   - The offer is off sale, its type is archived, or the type is not linked (E1) → `unavailable`. The binding Task 20 test expects "archived type → `unavailable`".
   - E4: the offer is not of the RDV type → `unavailable`.
   - E9: the offer is missing or off sale → `not_found`.
   - E4: the start is outside notice or horizon, or outside the weekly hours, at plan time → `unavailable`. The binding batch slot guard keeps `slot_taken`.
   - `registerPro`: a `ticketId` with a coverage other than ticket → `invalid`; a forced ticket that is not eligible → `not_eligible`.
   - Customer notes longer than `BOUNDS.note` → `invalid`.
5. **E4 « buy » also locks the customer**, after the RDV occurrences and in the global order, as E1 does. Otherwise the hold quota can be raced. §6 lists only professional → RDV occurrences.
6. **Task 22's "RDV hold → cancelled"** builds its RDV hold with direct inserts, because `bookRdv` only arrives in Task 24. Task 24 adds the same case made through the engine: an elapsed RDV hold is freed by the next E4.

Assumption, not a deviation: `expireElapsedHolds(c, ids: SQL)` (Task 16) gets a bare `select id from occurrence …` subquery, with no parentheses. That works whether Task 16 embeds it as `occurrence_id in (<SQL>)` or as `occurrence_id = any(<SQL>)`.

## Group G4: claims E1–E4, shop purchase E9, fulfillment E10, release E11

These rules apply to Tasks 20–24:

- **Prerequisites:**
  - Tasks 1–19 are done.
  - `.env.test` points at **tickets-test** (Task 5). Without it, the DB tests are skipped, and every task below requires `ℹ skipped 0`.
  - `dbTest` checks `checkInvariants({ proId }) == []` after each test (Task 15). Every test below therefore also proves I1–I6.
- **Imports and timing:**
  - Tests import engine modules through `@/…`. tsx resolves the `paths` in tsconfig.
  - Séances default to 3 days ahead at 10:00 Paris (`makeEvent`). That is inside the default 60 min notice and the 60-day horizon.
- **Global clock:** `sweepHolds` and `expireStalePurchases` act on the whole branch, not on one pro. DB tests run one at a time (`--test-concurrency=1`) and each deletes its pro, so only the current test's rows exist. Assertions check this pro's rows and use `>=` on the global counts.
- **Unique Stripe ids:** `checkout_session_id` is UNIQUE across the branch, so tests use `cs_test_${nanoid()}`.
- **Health counter:** the orphan-payment test bumps the global `health:orphan_payments` row. That row persists on tickets-test, so the test compares before and after.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing DB test**

Create `src/lib/booking/claims-buy.db.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { setTicketTypeArchived } from "@/lib/booking/catalog";
import { activeBookingOf, attachCheckoutSession, startBuyAndBook, type BuyAndBookInput } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { bookingAnswer, question, ticketPurchase, timeOff } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import type { OfferInput } from "@/lib/tickets";
import { addMinutes, dbTest, type DbHarness } from "@/test/db";
import { getBooking, getOccurrence, makeCustomer, makeEvent, makeOffer, makeType } from "@/test/fixtures";

const codeOf = (r: { ok: true } | { ok: false; code: string }): string => (r.ok ? "ok" : r.code);

/** A tickets-mode séance in 3 days at 10:00 (Paris), one linked type, one offer, one customer. */
async function setup(
  h: DbHarness,
  over: { capacity?: number; isPrivate?: boolean; offer?: Partial<OfferInput> } = {},
) {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId, { quantity: 4, priceCents: 8500, ...over.offer });
  const { eventId, occurrences } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, {
    ticketTypeIds: [typeId],
    capacity: over.capacity ?? 12,
    isPrivate: over.isPrivate ?? false,
  });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  return { typeId, offerId, eventId, occ: occurrences[0], customerId };
}

function buyInput(h: DbHarness, s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setup>>, over: Partial<BuyAndBookInput> = {}): BuyAndBookInput {
  return {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    customerId: s.customerId,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    offerId: s.offerId,
    ...over,
  };
}

dbTest("E1 holds a seat, snapshots the offer and stores the answers", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h);
  await h.db.insert(question).values({
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    eventId: s.eventId,
    label: "Votre niveau ?",
    type: "text",
    required: true,
    position: 0,
  });
  const [q] = await h.db.select().from(question).where(eq(question.eventId, s.eventId));
  const now = new Date();

  const res = await startBuyAndBook(
    h.db,
    buyInput(h, s, { answers: [{ questionId: q.id, value: "  Débutant " }], customerNotes: " Première fois " }),
    now,
  );
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, []);
  assert.equal(res.amountCents, 8500);
  assert.equal(res.quantity, 4);
  assert.equal(res.currency, "eur");
  assert.equal(res.label, "Carnet de 4");
  assert.equal(res.checkoutExpiresAt.getTime(), addMinutes(now, 31).getTime());

  const b = await getBooking(h.db, res.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "pending");
  assert.equal(b.coverage, "ticket");
  assert.equal(b.createdBy, "client");
  assert.equal(b.purchaseId, res.purchaseId);
  assert.equal(b.customerNotes, "Première fois");
  assert.equal(b.holdExpiresAt?.getTime(), addMinutes(now, 45).getTime());
  assert.equal(b.createdAt.getTime(), now.getTime());

  const [p] = await h.db.select().from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, res.purchaseId));
  assert.equal(p.status, "pending");
  assert.equal(p.offerId, s.offerId);
  assert.equal(p.ticketTypeId, s.typeId);
  assert.equal(p.customerId, s.customerId);
  assert.equal(p.label, "Carnet de 4");
  assert.equal(p.amountCents, 8500);
  assert.equal(p.quantity, 4);
  assert.equal(p.checkoutExpiresAt.getTime(), addMinutes(now, 31).getTime());

  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);

  const answers = await h.db.select().from(bookingAnswer).where(eq(bookingAnswer.bookingId, res.bookingId));
  assert.equal(answers.length, 1);
  assert.equal(answers[0].value, "Débutant");
  assert.equal(answers[0].questionLabel, "Votre niveau ?");
});

dbTest("E1 refuses a missing required answer (invalid, no seat taken)", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h);
  await h.db.insert(question).values({
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    eventId: s.eventId,
    label: "Votre niveau ?",
    type: "text",
    required: true,
    position: 0,
  });
  const res = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s));
  assert.equal(codeOf(res), "invalid");
  assert.ok(!res.ok && /Votre niveau/.test(res.message ?? ""), JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E1 refuses a private event and an archived type (unavailable)", async (h) => {
  const hidden = await setup(h, { isPrivate: true });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, hidden))), "unavailable");

  const s = await setup(h);
  const archived = await setTicketTypeArchived(h.db, {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId: s.typeId,
    archived: true,
  });
  assert.ok(archived.ok);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s))), "unavailable");
});

dbTest("E1 respects notice and horizon, in the plan and under the lock", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h);
  const start = s.occ.startsAt;
  // Plan: inside the 60 min notice, then 70 days ahead with a 60-day horizon.
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), addMinutes(start, -30))), "unavailable");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), addMinutes(start, -70 * 1440))), "unavailable");

  // Batch: the pro raises the notice to 7 days after the plan read.
  const notice = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), new Date(), {
    afterPlan: async () => {
      await h.db.execute(sql`update professional set min_notice_minutes = 10080 where id = ${h.pro.proId}`);
    },
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(notice), "unavailable");
  await h.db.execute(sql`update professional set min_notice_minutes = 60 where id = ${h.pro.proId}`);

  // Batch: the event's own horizon drops to 1 day after the plan read.
  const horizon = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), new Date(), {
    afterPlan: async () => {
      await h.db.execute(sql`update event set booking_horizon_days = 1 where id = ${s.eventId}`);
    },
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(horizon), "unavailable");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E1 refuses a séance under a time off (batch guard)", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h);
  await h.db.insert(timeOff).values({
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    startAt: addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, -60),
    endAt: addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, 30),
    allDay: false,
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s))), "unavailable");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E1 maps a moved séance to changed and an edited offer to stale", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h);
  // Plan: the client saw another start.
  const seen = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s, { seenStartsAt: addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, 1) }));
  assert.equal(codeOf(seen), "changed");

  // Batch: the pro moves the séance between the read and the batch.
  const moved = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), new Date(), {
    afterPlan: async () => {
      await h.db.execute(sql`
        update occurrence set starts_at = starts_at + interval '1 hour', ends_at = ends_at + interval '1 hour',
          block_start = block_start + interval '1 hour', block_end = block_end + interval '1 hour'
        where id = ${s.occ.id}`);
    },
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(moved), "changed");

  // Batch: the pro edits the offer's price between the read and the batch.
  const s2 = await setup(h);
  const edited = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s2), new Date(), {
    afterPlan: async () => {
      await h.db.execute(sql`update ticket_offer set price_cents = 9000 where id = ${s2.offerId}`);
    },
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(edited), "stale");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s2.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E1 refuses an offer whose validity ends before the séance", async (h) => {
  const s = await setup(h, { offer: { validityCount: 1, validityUnit: "day" } });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s))), "offer_too_short");
});

dbTest("E1 double submit, hold quota and last seat", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const s = await setup(h, { capacity: 3 });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), now)), "ok");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), now)), "already_booked");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);

  // A second séance: 2 live holds; a third one is refused.
  const { occurrences: [second] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [s.typeId] });
  const { occurrences: [third] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [s.typeId] });
  const on = (o: typeof second) => buyInput(h, s, { occurrenceId: o.id, seenStartsAt: o.startsAt });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, on(second), now)), "ok");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, on(third), now)), "too_many_holds");
  // 46 minutes later both holds have elapsed: they no longer count.
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, on(third), addMinutes(now, 46))), "ok");

  // Last seat: the CHECK raises full.
  const one = await setup(h, { capacity: 1 });
  const other = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, one), now)), "ok");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, one, { customerId: other }), now)), "full");
});

dbTest("E1 releases an elapsed hold on the same séance before taking the seat (R)", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const s = await setup(h, { capacity: 1 });
  const first = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), now);
  assert.ok(first.ok, JSON.stringify(first));
  const other = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const second = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s, { customerId: other }), addMinutes(now, 46));
  assert.ok(second.ok, JSON.stringify(second));

  const expired = await getBooking(h.db, first.bookingId);
  assert.equal(expired.status, "expired");
  assert.equal(expired.endedBy, "system");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("attachCheckoutSession and activeBookingOf", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const s = await setup(h);
  const res = await startBuyAndBook(h.db, buyInput(h, s), now);
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));

  const before = await activeBookingOf(h.db, { occurrenceId: s.occ.id, customerId: s.customerId }, now);
  assert.deepEqual(before, { bookingId: res.bookingId, status: "pending", purchaseId: res.purchaseId, checkoutUrl: null });

  const url = "https://checkout.stripe.com/c/pay/cs_test_1";
  const attached = await attachCheckoutSession(h.db, {
    purchaseId: res.purchaseId,
    checkoutSessionId: `cs_test_${res.purchaseId}`,
    checkoutUrl: url,
  });
  assert.ok(attached.ok);
  const after = await activeBookingOf(h.db, { occurrenceId: s.occ.id, customerId: s.customerId }, now);
  assert.equal(after?.checkoutUrl, url);

  // Checkout expired (31 min) but the hold is still live (45 min): no dead URL, the purchase stays releasable.
  const lapsed = await activeBookingOf(h.db, { occurrenceId: s.occ.id, customerId: s.customerId }, addMinutes(now, 40));
  assert.deepEqual(lapsed, { bookingId: res.bookingId, status: "pending", purchaseId: res.purchaseId, checkoutUrl: null });

  // An elapsed hold is not « active » any more.
  assert.equal(await activeBookingOf(h.db, { occurrenceId: s.occ.id, customerId: s.customerId }, addMinutes(now, 46)), null);

  // A purchase that is no longer pending cannot take a session.
  await h.db.execute(sql`update ticket_purchase set status = 'expired' where id = ${res.purchaseId}`);
  const late = await attachCheckoutSession(h.db, {
    purchaseId: res.purchaseId,
    checkoutSessionId: `cs_test_late_${res.purchaseId}`,
    checkoutUrl: url,
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(late), "stale");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/lib/booking/claims.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Taking a seat (spec § 11): E1 buy + book, E2 held ticket and pro « Inscrire »,
 * E3 free registration, E4 RDV libre. Every write is ONE tryAtomic batch:
 * lock first (professional → occurrence → customer), R + SEAT once per
 * occurrence, every guard followed by an assert, ids made in the plan. The
 * engine never calls Stripe: E1 and E4 « buy » return the pending purchase;
 * the P4/P5 action creates the Checkout session, then attachCheckoutSession.
 */
import { and, eq, gt, inArray, or, sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { effectiveWindow, onlineBookability, type BookingWindow } from "@/lib/booking-window";
import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  R,
  SEAT,
  lockRows,
  ts,
  tryAtomic,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
  type Query,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import {
  booking,
  customer,
  event,
  eventTicketType,
  occurrence,
  professional,
  ticketOffer,
  ticketPurchase,
  ticketType,
} from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { offerCovers, validityOf } from "@/lib/ticket-expiry";
import {
  BOUNDS,
  offerLabel,
  type Actor,
  type AnswerInput,
  type BookingCoverage,
  type BookingMode,
  type BookingStatus,
  type OccurrenceStatus,
  type ValidityUnit,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

import { buildAnswers, insertAnswers, loadQuestions } from "./answers";
import { assertHoldQuota, checkoutExpiry, holdExpiry } from "./holds";
import { fail, failOf, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/* ───────────────────────── Shared types ───────────────────────── */

/** What every client claim on an event séance carries. */
type ClientClaimBase = {
  professionalId: string;
  occurrenceId: string;
  /** from upsertCustomer (or the verified wallet owner) */
  customerId: string;
  /** occurrence.starts_at the client saw: G(starts_at = seen, 'changed') */
  seenStartsAt: Date;
  answers?: AnswerInput[];
  customerNotes?: string | null;
};

export type BuyAndBookInput = ClientClaimBase & { offerId: string };
export type BuyAndBookData = {
  purchaseId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  checkoutExpiresAt: Date;
  amountCents: number;
  currency: string;
  label: string;
  quantity: number;
  ticketTypeName: string;
};

/** Test-only hook: runs once between the first plan and its batch (deterministic races). */
type RaceOpts = { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> };

/* ─────────────── Engine-internal helpers (also used by fulfillment.ts) ─────────────── */

/** Customer ids are per pro: a customer of another pro is not_found. */
export async function assertCustomer(db: Db, professionalId: string, customerId: string): Promise<void> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({ id: customer.id })
    .from(customer)
    .where(and(eq(customer.id, customerId), eq(customer.professionalId, professionalId)));
  if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
}

/** An offer of this pro with its type, read for a purchase snapshot. */
export type OfferRow = {
  offerId: string;
  ticketTypeId: string;
  ticketTypeName: string;
  /** offerLabel(): « À l'unité », « Carnet de 10 » or the pro's label */
  label: string;
  quantity: number;
  priceCents: number;
  validityCount: number | null;
  validityUnit: ValidityUnit | null;
  /** active, not archived, type not archived */
  onSale: boolean;
  rdvEnabled: boolean;
  rdvFree: boolean;
  /** the pro's currency and zone */
  currency: string;
  tz: string;
};

export async function readOffer(db: Db, professionalId: string, offerId: string): Promise<OfferRow | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({
      offerId: ticketOffer.id,
      ticketTypeId: ticketOffer.ticketTypeId,
      ticketTypeName: ticketType.name,
      label: ticketOffer.label,
      quantity: ticketOffer.quantity,
      priceCents: ticketOffer.priceCents,
      validityCount: ticketOffer.validityCount,
      validityUnit: ticketOffer.validityUnit,
      isActive: ticketOffer.isActive,
      archivedAt: ticketOffer.archivedAt,
      typeArchivedAt: ticketType.archivedAt,
      rdvEnabled: ticketType.rdvEnabled,
      rdvFree: ticketType.rdvFree,
      currency: professional.currency,
      tz: professional.timezone,
    })
    .from(ticketOffer)
    .innerJoin(ticketType, eq(ticketType.id, ticketOffer.ticketTypeId))
    .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, ticketOffer.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(ticketOffer.id, offerId), eq(ticketOffer.professionalId, professionalId)));
  if (!row) return null;
  return {
    offerId: row.offerId,
    ticketTypeId: row.ticketTypeId,
    ticketTypeName: row.ticketTypeName,
    label: offerLabel({ label: row.label, quantity: row.quantity }),
    quantity: row.quantity,
    priceCents: row.priceCents,
    validityCount: row.validityCount,
    validityUnit: row.validityUnit,
    onSale: row.isActive && row.archivedAt === null && row.typeArchivedAt === null,
    rdvEnabled: row.rdvEnabled,
    rdvFree: row.rdvFree,
    currency: row.currency,
    tz: row.tz,
  };
}

/** Where the offer is used: linked to the séance's event (E1), the RDV type itself (E4), or the shop (E9). */
export type OfferScope =
  | { kind: "event"; occurrenceId: string }
  | { kind: "rdv"; ticketTypeId: string }
  | { kind: "shop" };

/**
 * The offer is still on sale with the snapshot the plan read (price,
 * quantity, validity) and still usable in `scope`. Asserted with 'stale'.
 */
export function offerUnchanged(o: OfferRow, professionalId: string, scope: OfferScope): SQL {
  const link =
    scope.kind === "event"
      ? sql`join occurrence o on o.id = ${scope.occurrenceId}
        join event_ticket_type l on l.event_id = o.event_id and l.ticket_type_id = f.ticket_type_id`
      : sql``;
  const rdv =
    scope.kind === "rdv"
      ? sql`and f.ticket_type_id = ${scope.ticketTypeId} and tt.rdv_enabled and not tt.rdv_free`
      : sql``;
  return sql`exists (
    select 1 from ticket_offer f
    join ticket_type tt on tt.id = f.ticket_type_id
    ${link}
    where f.id = ${o.offerId} and f.professional_id = ${professionalId}
      and f.is_active and f.archived_at is null and tt.archived_at is null
      and f.price_cents = ${o.priceCents}::int and f.quantity = ${o.quantity}::int
      and f.validity_count is not distinct from ${o.validityCount}::int
      and f.validity_unit is not distinct from ${o.validityUnit}::validity_unit
      ${rdv}
  )`;
}

/** INSERT ticket_purchase: pending, snapshot of the offer, the pro's currency. */
export function insertPurchase(
  c: BatchCtx,
  p: { id: string; professionalId: string; customerId: string; offer: OfferRow; checkoutExpiresAt: Date },
): Query {
  return c.db.insert(ticketPurchase).values({
    id: p.id,
    professionalId: p.professionalId,
    customerId: p.customerId,
    offerId: p.offer.offerId,
    ticketTypeId: p.offer.ticketTypeId,
    label: p.offer.label,
    quantity: p.offer.quantity,
    amountCents: p.offer.priceCents,
    currency: p.offer.currency,
    validityCount: p.offer.validityCount,
    validityUnit: p.offer.validityUnit,
    status: "pending",
    checkoutExpiresAt: p.checkoutExpiresAt,
    createdAt: c.now,
    updatedAt: c.now,
  });
}

/* ───────────────────────── Claim helpers ───────────────────────── */

/** Customer notes: trimmed, empty → null, at most BOUNDS.note characters. */
function cleanNotes(notes: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const value = notes?.trim() ?? "";
  if (value.length > BOUNDS.note) throw new PlanError("invalid", "Votre message est trop long.");
  return value === "" ? null : value;
}

/** An EVENT séance of this pro, its event and the pro's settings. An RDV occurrence is not_found here. */
type EventTarget = {
  occurrenceId: string;
  eventId: string;
  startsAt: Date;
  status: OccurrenceStatus;
  bookingMode: BookingMode;
  isPrivate: boolean;
  archived: boolean;
  tz: string;
  window: BookingWindow;
};

async function readEventTarget(db: Db, professionalId: string, occurrenceId: string): Promise<EventTarget> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({
      eventId: event.id,
      startsAt: occurrence.startsAt,
      status: occurrence.status,
      bookingMode: event.bookingMode,
      isPrivate: event.isPrivate,
      archivedAt: event.archivedAt,
      eventHorizonDays: event.bookingHorizonDays,
      eventNoticeMinutes: event.minNoticeMinutes,
      eventCancelHours: event.cancelDeadlineHours,
      tz: professional.timezone,
      bookingHorizonDays: professional.bookingHorizonDays,
      minNoticeMinutes: professional.minNoticeMinutes,
      cancelDeadlineHours: professional.cancelDeadlineHours,
    })
    .from(occurrence)
    .innerJoin(event, eq(event.id, occurrence.eventId))
    .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, occurrence.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, occurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, professionalId)));
  if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
  return {
    occurrenceId,
    eventId: row.eventId,
    startsAt: row.startsAt,
    status: row.status,
    bookingMode: row.bookingMode,
    isPrivate: row.isPrivate,
    archived: row.archivedAt !== null,
    tz: row.tz,
    window: effectiveWindow(
      {
        bookingHorizonDays: row.bookingHorizonDays,
        minNoticeMinutes: row.minNoticeMinutes,
        cancelDeadlineHours: row.cancelDeadlineHours,
      },
      {
        bookingHorizonDays: row.eventHorizonDays,
        minNoticeMinutes: row.eventNoticeMinutes,
        cancelDeadlineHours: row.eventCancelHours,
      },
    ),
  };
}

/** Plan-time twin of ONLINE_OK + CHANGED; the batch re-asserts both under the lock. */
function assertOnline(t: EventTarget, mode: "tickets" | "free", seenStartsAt: Date, now: Date): void {
  if (t.status !== "scheduled" || t.bookingMode !== mode || t.isPrivate || t.archived) {
    throw new PlanError("unavailable");
  }
  if (t.startsAt.getTime() !== seenStartsAt.getTime()) throw new PlanError("changed");
  if (!onlineBookability({ startsAt: t.startsAt, now, tz: t.tz, window: t.window }).ok) {
    throw new PlanError("unavailable");
  }
}

/**
 * ONLINE_OK(mode): the séance is open to online claims in `mode` (spec § 7):
 * scheduled, public, not archived, notice and local-date horizon (event
 * overrides, else the pro's), no overlapping time off. Asserted 'unavailable'.
 */
function onlineOk(c: BatchCtx, a: { professionalId: string; occurrenceId: string; mode: "tickets" | "free" }): SQL {
  return sql`exists (
    select 1 from occurrence o
    join event e on e.id = o.event_id
    join professional p on p.id = o.professional_id
    where o.id = ${a.occurrenceId} and o.professional_id = ${a.professionalId} and o.status = 'scheduled'
      and e.booking_mode = ${a.mode}::booking_mode and not e.is_private and e.archived_at is null
      and o.starts_at >= ${ts(c.now)} + make_interval(mins => coalesce(e.min_notice_minutes, p.min_notice_minutes))
      and (o.starts_at at time zone p.timezone)::date
          <= (${ts(c.now)} at time zone p.timezone)::date + coalesce(e.booking_horizon_days, p.booking_horizon_days)
      and not exists (select 1 from time_off t where t.professional_id = o.professional_id
                      and t.start_at < o.ends_at and t.end_at > o.starts_at)
  )`;
}

/** CHANGED: the séance still starts when the client saw it. Asserted 'changed'. */
function unchangedStart(occurrenceId: string, seenStartsAt: Date): SQL {
  return sql`(select starts_at from occurrence where id = ${occurrenceId}) = ${ts(seenStartsAt)}`;
}

/** The offer's type is accepted by the séance's event (live link). */
async function offerLinkedToEvent(db: Db, offerId: string, eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ eventId: eventTicketType.eventId })
    .from(eventTicketType)
    .innerJoin(ticketOffer, eq(ticketOffer.ticketTypeId, eventTicketType.ticketTypeId))
    .where(and(eq(ticketOffer.id, offerId), eq(eventTicketType.eventId, eventId)));
  return rows.length > 0;
}

type NewBooking = {
  id: string;
  professionalId: string;
  occurrenceId: string;
  customerId: string;
  coverage: BookingCoverage;
  createdBy: Actor;
  customerNotes: string | null;
} & ({ status: "confirmed" } | { status: "pending"; purchaseId: string; holdExpiresAt: Date });

function insertBooking(c: BatchCtx, b: NewBooking): Query {
  return c.db.insert(booking).values({
    id: b.id,
    professionalId: b.professionalId,
    occurrenceId: b.occurrenceId,
    customerId: b.customerId,
    status: b.status,
    coverage: b.coverage,
    purchaseId: b.status === "pending" ? b.purchaseId : null,
    createdBy: b.createdBy,
    holdExpiresAt: b.status === "pending" ? b.holdExpiresAt : null,
    confirmedAt: b.status === "confirmed" ? c.now : null,
    customerNotes: b.customerNotes,
    createdAt: c.now,
    updatedAt: c.now,
  });
}

/* ───────────────────────── E1: buy + book ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * E1. Plan: séance scheduled and open online (tickets mode, public, not
 * archived, notice, horizon), seen start, offer on sale and linked, offer
 * validity covers the séance, answers. Batch: lock occurrence → customer,
 * R(o), SEAT(o, +1), G(ONLINE_OK), G(CHANGED), G(offer unchanged), hold
 * quota, INSERT purchase (pending) and booking (pending, hold), answers.
 * Effects: none (the action creates the Checkout session).
 */
export async function startBuyAndBook(
  db: Db,
  input: BuyAndBookInput,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: RaceOpts = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<BuyAndBookData>> {
  const planned: { data?: BuyAndBookData } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const notes = cleanNotes(input.customerNotes);
      const [target] = await Promise.all([
        readEventTarget(db, input.professionalId, input.occurrenceId),
        assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
      ]);
      assertOnline(target, "tickets", input.seenStartsAt, now);
      const [offer, linked, questions] = await Promise.all([
        readOffer(db, input.professionalId, input.offerId),
        offerLinkedToEvent(db, input.offerId, target.eventId),
        loadQuestions(db, { eventId: target.eventId }),
      ]);
      if (!offer) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      if (!offer.onSale || !linked) throw new PlanError("unavailable");
      if (!offerCovers(now, validityOf(offer), target.tz, target.startsAt)) throw new PlanError("offer_too_short");
      const answers = buildAnswers(questions, input.answers, { enforceRequired: true });
      if (!answers.ok) throw new PlanError("invalid", answers.message);
      const answerRows = answers.rows;

      const purchaseId = nanoid();
      const bookingId = nanoid();
      const checkoutExpiresAt = checkoutExpiry(now, "buybook");
      planned.data = {
        purchaseId,
        bookingId,
        checkoutExpiresAt,
        amountCents: offer.priceCents,
        currency: offer.currency,
        label: offer.label,
        quantity: offer.quantity,
        ticketTypeName: offer.ticketTypeName,
      };

      return (aid) => {
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
        const { professionalId, occurrenceId, customerId } = input;
        return [
          lockRows(db, "occurrence", [occurrenceId]),
          lockRows(db, "customer", [customerId]),
          R(c, occurrenceId),
          SEAT(c, occurrenceId, 1),
          G(db, onlineOk(c, { professionalId, occurrenceId, mode: "tickets" }), "unavailable"),
          G(db, unchangedStart(occurrenceId, input.seenStartsAt), "changed"),
          G(db, offerUnchanged(offer, professionalId, { kind: "event", occurrenceId }), "stale"),
          assertHoldQuota(c, customerId),
          insertPurchase(c, { id: purchaseId, professionalId, customerId, offer, checkoutExpiresAt }),
          insertBooking(c, {
            id: bookingId,
            professionalId,
            occurrenceId,
            customerId,
            status: "pending",
            coverage: "ticket",
            purchaseId,
            holdExpiresAt: holdExpiry(now),
            createdBy: "client",
            customerNotes: notes,
          }),
          ...insertAnswers(c, { professionalId, bookingId, rows: answerRows }),
        ];
      };
    },
    { afterPlan: opts.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  if (!planned.data) throw new Error("startBuyAndBook: the plan produced no data");
  return { ok: true, effects: [], ...planned.data };
}

/**
 * After Stripe answered (E1, E4 buy, E9): one guarded UPDATE. A purchase that
 * is no longer pending → stale (the action then expires the session).
 */
export async function attachCheckoutSession(
  db: Db,
  input: { purchaseId: string; checkoutSessionId: string; checkoutUrl: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const rows = await db
    .update(ticketPurchase)
    .set({ checkoutSessionId: input.checkoutSessionId, checkoutUrl: input.checkoutUrl, updatedAt: now })
    .where(and(eq(ticketPurchase.id, input.purchaseId), eq(ticketPurchase.status, "pending")))
    .returning({ id: ticketPurchase.id });
  return rows.length === 1 ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : fail("stale");
}

/**
 * « Reprendre le paiement »: the customer's active booking on the occurrence
 * (confirmed, or pending with a live hold at `now`). `purchaseId` while that
 * purchase is pending (« Libérer ma place »); `checkoutUrl` only while it is
 * pending AND its Checkout session is still open (checkout_expires_at > now).
 * The session closes at 31 min but the hold lasts 45 min: in between the URL
 * would be dead, so it is withheld.
 */
export async function activeBookingOf(
  db: Db,
  input: { occurrenceId: string; customerId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<{ bookingId: string; status: BookingStatus; purchaseId: string | null; checkoutUrl: string | null } | null> {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({
      bookingId: booking.id,
      status: booking.status,
      purchaseId: ticketPurchase.id,
      purchaseStatus: ticketPurchase.status,
      checkoutUrl: ticketPurchase.checkoutUrl,
      checkoutExpiresAt: ticketPurchase.checkoutExpiresAt,
    })
    .from(booking)
    .leftJoin(ticketPurchase, eq(ticketPurchase.id, booking.purchaseId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(booking.occurrenceId, input.occurrenceId),
        eq(booking.customerId, input.customerId),
        inArray(booking.status, ["pending", "confirmed"]),
        or(eq(booking.status, "confirmed"), gt(booking.holdExpiresAt, now)),
      ),
    );
  if (!row) return null;
  const pendingPurchase = row.purchaseStatus === "pending";
  const resumable =
    pendingPurchase && row.checkoutExpiresAt !== null && row.checkoutExpiresAt.getTime() > now.getTime();
  return {
    bookingId: row.bookingId,
    status: row.status,
    purchaseId: pendingPurchase ? row.purchaseId : null,
    checkoutUrl: resumable ? row.checkoutUrl : null,
  };
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { startBuyAndBook } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { fulfillPurchase, markProcessing, startPurchase, type PaymentFacts } from "@/lib/booking/fulfillment";
import { ticketMovement, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { HEALTH_KEYS, readHealth } from "@/lib/throttle";
import { ticketExpiry } from "@/lib/ticket-expiry";
import { addMinutes, dbTest, inParallel, type DbHarness } from "@/test/db";
import { getBooking, getOccurrence, makeCustomer, makeEvent, makeOffer, makeType, ticketsOf } from "@/test/fixtures";

const codeOf = (r: { ok: true } | { ok: false; code: string }): string => (r.ok ? "ok" : r.code);

/** Unique Stripe ids: checkout_session_id is UNIQUE across the whole test branch. */
function payment(livemode = false): PaymentFacts {
  return { sessionId: `cs_test_${nanoid()}`, paymentIntentId: `pi_test_${nanoid()}`, livemode };
}

async function shop(h: DbHarness, quantity: number) {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId, {
    quantity,
    priceCents: 19000,
    validityCount: 6,
    validityUnit: "month",
  });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  return { typeId, offerId, customerId };
}

async function purchaseMovements(h: DbHarness, purchaseId: string) {
  return h.db
    .select({ kind: ticketMovement.kind, ticketId: ticketMovement.ticketId, toStatus: ticketMovement.toStatus })
    .from(ticketMovement)
    .where(eq(ticketMovement.purchaseId, purchaseId));
}

dbTest("E9 startPurchase creates a pending shop purchase from the offer snapshot", async (h) => {
  const s = await shop(h, 10);
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId: s.offerId, customerId: s.customerId }, now);
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, []);
  assert.equal(res.quantity, 10);
  assert.equal(res.amountCents, 19000);
  assert.equal(res.label, "Carnet de 10");
  assert.equal(res.checkoutExpiresAt.getTime(), addMinutes(now, 24 * 60).getTime());

  const [p] = await h.db.select().from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, res.purchaseId));
  assert.equal(p.status, "pending");
  assert.equal(p.validityCount, 6);
  assert.equal(p.validityUnit, "month");
  assert.equal(p.currency, "eur");

  // An offer taken off sale is not found.
  await h.db.execute(sql`update ticket_offer set is_active = false where id = ${s.offerId}`);
  const off = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId: s.offerId, customerId: s.customerId });
  assert.equal(codeOf(off), "not_found");
});

dbTest("T4: two concurrent deliveries give the tickets once", async (h) => {
  const s = await shop(h, 3);
  const started = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId: s.offerId, customerId: s.customerId });
  assert.ok(started.ok, JSON.stringify(started));
  const facts = payment();
  const now = new Date();

  const settled = await inParallel([
    () => fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, facts, now),
    () => fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, facts, now),
  ]);
  const outcomes = settled.map((r) => (r.status === "fulfilled" ? r.value.outcome : `threw: ${String(r.reason)}`)).sort();
  assert.deepEqual(outcomes, ["duplicate", "fulfilled"]);
  const winner = settled.find((r) => r.status === "fulfilled" && r.value.outcome === "fulfilled");
  assert.ok(winner && winner.status === "fulfilled");
  assert.deepEqual(winner.value.effects, [{ type: "email.purchase_receipt", purchaseId: started.purchaseId }]);
  const loser = settled.find((r) => r.status === "fulfilled" && r.value.outcome === "duplicate");
  assert.ok(loser && loser.status === "fulfilled");
  assert.deepEqual(loser.value.effects, []);

  const tickets = await ticketsOf(h.db, s.customerId);
  assert.equal(tickets.length, 3);
  assert.ok(tickets.every((t) => t.status === "available" && t.purchaseId === started.purchaseId));
  const expected = ticketExpiry(now, { count: 6, unit: "month" }, h.pro.tz);
  assert.ok(tickets.every((t) => t.expiresAt?.getTime() === expected?.getTime()));
  const moves = await purchaseMovements(h, started.purchaseId);
  assert.equal(moves.length, 3);
  assert.ok(moves.every((m) => m.kind === "purchase" && m.toStatus === "available"));

  const [p] = await h.db.select().from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, started.purchaseId));
  assert.equal(p.status, "paid");
  assert.equal(p.paidAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(p.checkoutSessionId, facts.sessionId);
  assert.equal(p.paymentIntentId, facts.paymentIntentId);
  assert.ok(p.fulfilledActionId);

  // A third, late delivery is a no-op too.
  const again = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, facts);
  assert.equal(again.outcome, "duplicate");
  assert.equal((await ticketsOf(h.db, s.customerId)).length, 3);
});

dbTest("T14: a paid buy + book confirms the booking and books ticket no 1", async (h) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);

  const done = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, payment());

  assert.deepEqual(done.effects, [
    { type: "email.purchase_receipt", purchaseId: started.purchaseId },
    { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: started.bookingId },

    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: occ.id },
  ]);

  const b = await getBooking(h.db, started.bookingId);

  assert.equal(booked[0].bookingId, started.bookingId);
  assert.equal(tickets.filter((t) => t.status === "available").length, 3);

  const moves = await purchaseMovements(h, started.purchaseId);
  assert.equal(moves.length, 4);

  assert.equal(new Set(moves.map((m) => m.ticketId)).size, 4);
  const spends = await h.db
    .select({ id: ticketMovement.id })
    .from(ticketMovement)
    .where(and(eq(ticketMovement.customerId, customerId), eq(ticketMovement.kind, "spend")));
  assert.equal(spends.length, 0);
});

dbTest("E10 pays a purchase that already expired: the tickets go to the wallet", async (h) => {
  const s = await shop(h, 2);
  const started = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId: s.offerId, customerId: s.customerId });
  assert.ok(started.ok, JSON.stringify(started));
  await h.db.execute(sql`update ticket_purchase set status = 'expired' where id = ${started.purchaseId}`);

  const done = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, payment(true));
  assert.equal(done.outcome, "fulfilled");
  assert.equal(done.bookingConfirmed, false);
  assert.deepEqual(done.effects, [{ type: "email.purchase_receipt", purchaseId: started.purchaseId }]);
  const tickets = await ticketsOf(h.db, s.customerId);
  assert.equal(tickets.length, 2);
  assert.ok(tickets.every((t) => t.status === "available"));
  const [p] = await h.db.select().from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, started.purchaseId));
  assert.equal(p.status, "paid");
  assert.equal(p.livemode, true);
});

dbTest("E10 orphan payment: no purchase, counted for the admin health card", async (h) => {
  const before = (await readHealth(h.db))[HEALTH_KEYS.orphanPayments]?.value ?? 0;
  const done = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, `missing_${nanoid()}`, payment());
  assert.equal(done.outcome, "orphan");
  assert.deepEqual(done.ticketIds, []);
  assert.deepEqual(done.effects, []);
  const after = (await readHealth(h.db))[HEALTH_KEYS.orphanPayments]?.value ?? 0;
  assert.equal(after, before + 1);
});

  const [p] = await h.db.select().from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, started.purchaseId));

  const done = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, started.purchaseId, payment());
  assert.equal(done.outcome, "fulfilled");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await markProcessing(h.db, started.purchaseId)), "stale");
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/lib/booking/fulfillment.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * E9 shop purchase and E10 fulfillment (spec § 11). Replaces the deleted
 * src/lib/payments.ts. Three callers deliver payments (P4): the Stripe webhook
 * (`checkout.session.completed` paid, `async_payment_succeeded`) and the mock
 * route. Money received ALWAYS yields tickets, even from `expired`; a booking
 * that ended is never resurrected; a duplicate delivery is a no-op.
 */
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  lockRows,
  textArray,
  ts,
  tryAtomic,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
  type Query,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, professional, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { bumpHealth, HEALTH_KEYS } from "@/lib/throttle";
import { ticketExpiry, validityOf } from "@/lib/ticket-expiry";

import { assertCustomer, insertPurchase, offerUnchanged, readOffer } from "./claims";
import { checkoutExpiry } from "./holds";
import { fail, failOf, syncEffects, type Effect, type EngineOk, type EngineResult } from "./types";

export type ShopPurchaseData = {
  purchaseId: string;
  checkoutExpiresAt: Date;
  amountCents: number;
  currency: string;
  label: string;
  quantity: number;
  ticketTypeName: string;
};

/**
 * E9. Plan: offer of this pro, on sale (active, not archived, type not
 * archived), customer of this pro. Batch: G(offer unchanged, 'stale'), INSERT
 * ticket_purchase (pending, snapshot, checkout_expires_at = now + 24 h). No
 * seat, no lock. Effects: none.
 */
export async function startPurchase(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; offerId: string; customerId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<ShopPurchaseData>> {
  const planned: { data?: ShopPurchaseData } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [offer] = await Promise.all([
      readOffer(db, input.professionalId, input.offerId),
      assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
    ]);
    if (!offer || !offer.onSale) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    const purchaseId = nanoid();
    const checkoutExpiresAt = checkoutExpiry(now, "shop");
    planned.data = {
      purchaseId,
      checkoutExpiresAt,
      amountCents: offer.priceCents,
      currency: offer.currency,
      label: offer.label,
      quantity: offer.quantity,
      ticketTypeName: offer.ticketTypeName,
    };
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      return [
        G(db, offerUnchanged(offer, input.professionalId, { kind: "shop" }), "stale"),
        insertPurchase(c, {
          id: purchaseId,
          professionalId: input.professionalId,
          customerId: input.customerId,
          offer,
          checkoutExpiresAt,
        }),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  if (!planned.data) throw new Error("startPurchase: the plan produced no data");
  return { ok: true, effects: [], ...planned.data };
}

export type PaymentFacts = { sessionId: string | null; paymentIntentId: string | null; livemode: boolean };
export type FulfillOutcome = "fulfilled" | "duplicate" | "orphan";
type FulfillData = { outcome: FulfillOutcome; ticketIds: string[]; bookingConfirmed: boolean };

function noop(outcome: "duplicate" | "orphan"): EngineOk<FulfillData> {
  return { ok: true, effects: [], outcome, ticketIds: [], bookingConfirmed: false };
}

/**
 * The purchase's N tickets and their N `purchase` movements in ONE statement.
 * Ticket n° 1 is `booked` to the purchase's booking when that booking is
 * confirmed (by the previous statement of this batch); every other ticket is
 * `available`. One movement per ticket, never a `spend` (spec § 9).
 */
function insertPurchasedTickets(c: BatchCtx, purchaseId: string, ticketIds: readonly string[], expiresAt: Date | null): Query {
  const expiry = expiresAt ? ts(expiresAt) : sql`null::timestamptz`;
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    with b as (select id from booking where purchase_id = ${purchaseId} and status = 'confirmed'),
    t as (
      insert into ticket (id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id, purchase_id, status, booking_id,
        expires_at, status_changed_at, created_at, updated_at)
      select x.id, p.professional_id, p.customer_id, p.ticket_type_id, p.id,
        case when x.n = 1 and exists (select 1 from b) then 'booked'::ticket_status else 'available'::ticket_status end,
        case when x.n = 1 then (select id from b) else null end,
        ${expiry}, ${ts(c.now)}, ${ts(c.now)}, ${ts(c.now)}
      from unnest(${textArray(ticketIds)}) with ordinality as x(id, n)
      join ticket_purchase p on p.id = ${purchaseId}
      returning id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id, purchase_id, status, booking_id
    )
    insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status, to_status,
      ticket_type_id, booking_id, purchase_id, actor, created_at)
    select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${c.aid}, 'purchase', null, status, ticket_type_id, booking_id,
      purchase_id, 'system', ${ts(c.now)}
    from t`);
}

/**
 * E10. Plan: the purchase (+ the pro's zone, + its booking if any). Unknown
 * purchase → `orphan` (logged, health counter bumped; refund by hand in
 * Stripe). Already paid → `duplicate`. N ticket ids and expires_at
 * (ticketExpiry(now, snapshot, tz)) are computed in JS. Batch: lock the
 * booking, UPDATE purchase → paid FROM pending OR expired, G(fulfilled by
 * this aid, 'already_paid') (a concurrent duplicate raises here → no-op),
 * confirm a STILL pending booking, tickets + movements. Never fails on
 * business grounds; an unexpected error is thrown (the webhook answers 500,
 * Stripe retries).
 */
export async function fulfillPurchase(
  db: Db,
  purchaseId: string,
  payment: PaymentFacts,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineOk<FulfillData>> {
  const [purchase] = await db
    .select({
      status: ticketPurchase.status,
      quantity: ticketPurchase.quantity,
      validityCount: ticketPurchase.validityCount,
      validityUnit: ticketPurchase.validityUnit,
      tz: professional.timezone,
    })
    .from(ticketPurchase)
    .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, ticketPurchase.professionalId))
    .where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, purchaseId));
  if (!purchase) {
    console.warn(`[stripe] orphan payment ${payment.sessionId ?? "-"}/${payment.paymentIntentId ?? "-"}`);
    await bumpHealth(db, HEALTH_KEYS.orphanPayments, 1, now);
    return noop("orphan");
  }
  if (purchase.status === "paid") return noop("duplicate");

  const [held] = await db.select({ id: booking.id }).from(booking).where(eq(booking.purchaseId, purchaseId));
  const ticketIds = Array.from({ length: purchase.quantity }, () => nanoid());
  const expiresAt = ticketExpiry(now, validityOf(purchase), purchase.tz);

  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => (aid) => {
    const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
    const queries: Query[] = [];
    if (held) queries.push(lockRows(db, "booking", [held.id]));
    queries.push(
      db.execute(sql`
        update ticket_purchase set status = 'paid', paid_at = ${ts(now)}, livemode = ${payment.livemode}::boolean,
          checkout_session_id = coalesce(${payment.sessionId}::text, checkout_session_id),
          payment_intent_id = ${payment.paymentIntentId}::text, fulfilled_action_id = ${aid}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
        where id = ${purchaseId} and status in ('pending', 'expired')`),
      G(db, sql`(select fulfilled_action_id from ticket_purchase where id = ${purchaseId}) = ${aid}`, "already_paid"),
      db.execute(sql`
        update booking set status = 'confirmed', confirmed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
        where purchase_id = ${purchaseId} and status = 'pending'`),
      insertPurchasedTickets(c, purchaseId, ticketIds, expiresAt),
    );
    return queries;
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    if (res.code === "duplicate") return noop("duplicate");
    throw new Error(`fulfillPurchase ${purchaseId}: unexpected ${res.code}`);
  }

  const [after] = await db
    .select({ id: booking.id, confirmedAt: booking.confirmedAt, occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId })
    .from(booking)
    .where(eq(booking.purchaseId, purchaseId));
  // Confirmed by THIS fulfillment (step 4 stamps confirmed_at = now), even if a cancellation already followed.
  const bookingConfirmed = after?.confirmedAt?.getTime() === now.getTime();
  const effects: Effect[] = [{ type: "email.purchase_receipt", purchaseId }];
  if (after && bookingConfirmed) {
    effects.push(
      { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: after.id },
      { type: "email.pro_new_booking", bookingId: after.id },
      ...syncEffects([after.occurrenceId]),
    );
  } else if (after) {
    effects.push({ type: "email.late_payment", purchaseId });
  }
  return { ok: true, effects, outcome: "fulfilled", ticketIds, bookingConfirmed };
}

/**
 * `checkout.session.completed` with payment_status `unpaid` (delayed method):
 * processing_at = now (first instant kept) while pending; the nightly job then
 * leaves the purchase alone. Not pending → stale.
 */
export async function markProcessing(db: Db, purchaseId: string, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const rows = await db.execute<{ id: string }>(sql`
    update ticket_purchase set processing_at = coalesce(processing_at, ${ts(now)}), updated_at = ${ts(now)}
    where id = ${purchaseId} and status = 'pending'
    returning id`);
  return rows.rows.length === 1 ? { ok: true, effects: [] } : fail("stale");
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { startBuyAndBook } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { fulfillPurchase, markProcessing, startPurchase } from "@/lib/booking/fulfillment";
import { expireStalePurchases, releasePurchase, sweepHolds } from "@/lib/booking/release";
import { instantAt } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { booking, occurrence, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { addMinutes, dbTest, type DbHarness } from "@/test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeOffer,
  makeType,
  ticketsOf,
} from "@/test/fixtures";

const codeOf = (r: { ok: true } | { ok: false; code: string }): string => (r.ok ? "ok" : r.code);

async function purchaseStatus(h: DbHarness, purchaseId: string): Promise<string> {
  const [p] = await h.db.select({ status: ticketPurchase.status }).from(ticketPurchase).where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, purchaseId));
  return p.status;
}

/** A buy + book hold on a tickets-mode séance (capacity 1 by default). */
async function hold(h: DbHarness, now: Date, opts: { capacity?: number; quantity?: number } = {}) {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId, { quantity: opts.quantity ?? 1 });
  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], capacity: opts.capacity ?? 1 });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const res = await startBuyAndBook(
    h.db,
    { professionalId: h.pro.proId, occurrenceId: occ.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt, offerId },
    now,
  );
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  return { typeId, offerId, occ, customerId, purchaseId: res.purchaseId, bookingId: res.bookingId };
}

dbTest("T5: sweep, then a late payment: booking expired, N tickets available", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const s = await hold(h, now, { quantity: 4 });

  const swept = await sweepHolds(h.db, addMinutes(now, 46));
  assert.ok(swept.expiredBookings >= 1);
  const ended = await getBooking(h.db, s.bookingId);
  assert.equal(ended.status, "expired");
  assert.equal(ended.endedBy, "system");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);

  const paid = await fulfillPurchase(
    h.db,
    s.purchaseId,
    { sessionId: `cs_test_${nanoid()}`, paymentIntentId: `pi_test_${nanoid()}`, livemode: false },
    addMinutes(now, 50),
  );
  assert.equal(paid.outcome, "fulfilled");
  assert.equal(paid.bookingConfirmed, false);
  assert.deepEqual(paid.effects, [
    { type: "email.purchase_receipt", purchaseId: s.purchaseId },
    { type: "email.late_payment", purchaseId: s.purchaseId },
  ]);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, s.bookingId)).status, "expired");
  const tickets = await ticketsOf(h.db, s.customerId);
  assert.equal(tickets.length, 4);
  assert.ok(tickets.every((t) => t.status === "available" && t.bookingId === null));
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, s.purchaseId), "paid");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("releasePurchase frees the seat once (idempotent)", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const s = await hold(h, now);

  const first = await releasePurchase(h.db, s.purchaseId, addMinutes(now, 5));
  assert.ok(first.ok, JSON.stringify(first));
  assert.equal(first.released, true);
  assert.deepEqual(first.effects, [{ type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: s.occ.id }]);
  const b = await getBooking(h.db, s.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "expired");
  assert.equal(b.endedBy, "system");
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, s.purchaseId), "expired");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);

  const second = await releasePurchase(h.db, s.purchaseId, addMinutes(now, 6));
  assert.ok(second.ok, JSON.stringify(second));
  assert.equal(second.released, false);
  assert.deepEqual(second.effects, []);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);

  // The freed seat can be taken again.
  const other = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const again = await startBuyAndBook(
    h.db,
    { professionalId: h.pro.proId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id, customerId: other, seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt, offerId: s.offerId },
    addMinutes(now, 7),
  );
  assert.ok(again.ok, JSON.stringify(again));
});

dbTest("releasePurchase: shop purchase, paid purchase, unknown purchase", async (h) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId, { quantity: 2 });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);

  const shop = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId, customerId });
  assert.ok(shop.ok, JSON.stringify(shop));
  const released = await releasePurchase(h.db, shop.purchaseId);
  assert.ok(released.ok && released.released === false, JSON.stringify(released));
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, shop.purchaseId), "expired");

  const bought = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId, customerId });
  assert.ok(bought.ok, JSON.stringify(bought));
  await fulfillPurchase(h.db, bought.purchaseId, { sessionId: null, paymentIntentId: null, livemode: false });
  const untouched = await releasePurchase(h.db, bought.purchaseId);
  assert.ok(untouched.ok && untouched.released === false, JSON.stringify(untouched));
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, bought.purchaseId), "paid");
  assert.equal((await ticketsOf(h.db, customerId)).length, 2);

  assert.equal(codeOf(await releasePurchase(h.db, `missing_${nanoid()}`)), "not_found");
});

dbTest("sweepHolds cancels an RDV whose hold elapsed and leaves live holds alone", async (h) => {
  const now = new Date();
  // A live event hold (10 minutes old at sweep time) must survive.
  const live = await hold(h, now, { capacity: 3 });

  // A buy + book RDV hold, written by hand (bookRdv arrives in Task 24).
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 });
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const startsAt = instantAt(dayIn(h.pro, 4), 600, h.pro.tz);
  const endsAt = addMinutes(startsAt, 60);
  const ids = { occurrence: nanoid(), purchase: nanoid(), booking: nanoid() };
  const early = addMinutes(now, -50);
  await h.db.batch([
    h.db.insert(occurrence).values({
      id: ids.occurrence,
      professionalId: h.pro.proId,
      ticketTypeId: typeId,
      startsAt,
      endsAt,
      blockStart: startsAt,
      blockEnd: endsAt,
      capacity: 1,
      seatsTaken: 1,
    }),
    h.db.insert(ticketPurchase).values({
      id: ids.purchase,
      professionalId: h.pro.proId,
      customerId,
      offerId,
      ticketTypeId: typeId,
      label: "À l'unité",
      quantity: 1,
      amountCents: 2500,
      currency: "eur",
      checkoutExpiresAt: addMinutes(early, 31),
    }),
    h.db.insert(booking).values({
      id: ids.booking,
      professionalId: h.pro.proId,
      occurrenceId: ids.occurrence,
      customerId,
      status: "pending",
      coverage: "ticket",
      purchaseId: ids.purchase,
      createdBy: "client",
      holdExpiresAt: addMinutes(early, 45),
    }),
  ]);

  const swept = await sweepHolds(h.db, addMinutes(now, 10));
  assert.ok(swept.expiredBookings >= 1);
  const rdv = await getOccurrence(h.db, ids.occurrence);
  assert.equal(rdv.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(rdv.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.ok(rdv.cancelledAt);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, ids.booking)).status, "expired");

  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, live.bookingId)).status, "pending");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, live.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("expireStalePurchases: grace period, processing purchases kept", async (h) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, typeId);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const now = new Date();
  const plain = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId, customerId }, now);
  const slow = await startPurchase(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, offerId, customerId }, now);
  assert.ok(plain.ok && slow.ok);
  assert.ok((await markProcessing(h.db, slow.purchaseId, addMinutes(now, 1))).ok);

  // 24 h checkout + 59 min: still inside the 60 min grace.
  await expireStalePurchases(h.db, addMinutes(now, 24 * 60 + 59));
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, plain.purchaseId), "pending");

  const done = await expireStalePurchases(h.db, addMinutes(now, 24 * 60 + 61));
  assert.ok(done.expired >= 1);
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, plain.purchaseId), "expired");
  assert.equal(await purchaseStatus(h, slow.purchaseId), "pending");
});
```
