- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/lib/booking/release.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * E11: releasing seats held by unpaid checkouts (spec § 11). Correctness never
 * depends on the nightly job: R(o) also releases elapsed holds before every
 * claim on o, and E4 releases the RDVs it overlaps.
 */
import { and, count, eq, isNull, lt, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import {
  PlanError,
  SEAT,
  atomic,
  lockRows,
  releaseEndedSeats,
  ts,
  tryAtomic,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { STALE_PURCHASE_GRACE_MINUTES } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { expireElapsedHolds } from "./holds";
import { failOf, syncEffects, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/**
 * Idempotent. Callers (P4): the `checkout.session.expired` and
 * `async_payment_failed` webhooks, « Libérer ma place » (after
 * sessions.expire), a Stripe error right after E1/E4. Batch: lock the
 * booking's occurrence; booking pending → expired (aid); SEAT(o, 0) (an RDV
 * at 0 is cancelled); purchase pending → expired. A paid purchase is left
 * alone. Effects: google.sync_occurrence when a booking ended. Errors: not_found.
 */
export async function releasePurchase(
  db: Db,
  purchaseId: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ released: boolean }>> {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ id: ticketPurchase.id, occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId })
      .from(ticketPurchase)
      .leftJoin(booking, eq(booking.purchaseId, ticketPurchase.id))
      .where(eq(ticketPurchase.id, purchaseId));
    if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    const occurrenceId = row.occurrenceId;
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const expirePurchase = db.execute(sql`
        update ticket_purchase set status = 'expired', updated_at = ${ts(now)}
        where id = ${purchaseId} and status = 'pending'`);
      if (occurrenceId === null) return [expirePurchase];
      return [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [occurrenceId]),
        db.execute(sql`
          update booking set status = 'expired', ended_at = ${ts(now)}, ended_by = 'system',
            ended_action_id = ${aid}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where purchase_id = ${purchaseId} and status = 'pending'`),
        SEAT(c, occurrenceId, 0),
        expirePurchase,
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const ended = await db
    .select({ occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId })
    .from(booking)
    .where(eq(booking.endedActionId, res.aid));
  return { ok: true, effects: syncEffects(ended.map((b) => b.occurrenceId)), released: ended.length > 0 };
}

/**
 * Nightly step 1 (E12). Batch: lock every occurrence with an elapsed hold (id
 * asc; EXISTS because Postgres refuses FOR UPDATE with DISTINCT), expire those
 * bookings (aid), then release the seats in aggregate (N per occurrence; an
 * RDV at 0 is cancelled). No batch at all when nothing elapsed.
 */
export async function sweepHolds(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ expiredBookings: number }> {
  const elapsed = sql`select o.id from occurrence o
    where exists (select 1 from booking b
                  where b.occurrence_id = o.id and b.status = 'pending' and b.hold_expires_at < ${ts(now)})`;
  const done = await atomic(db, async () => {
    const [pending] = await db
      .select({ n: count() })
      .from(booking)
      .where(and(eq(booking.status, "pending"), lt(booking.holdExpiresAt, now)));
    if (!pending || pending.n === 0) return () => [];
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      return [
        db.execute(sql`${elapsed} order by o.id for update`),
        expireElapsedHolds(c, elapsed),
        releaseEndedSeats(c),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (done.results.length === 0) return { expiredBookings: 0 };
  const [ended] = await db.select({ n: count() }).from(booking).where(eq(booking.endedActionId, done.aid));
  return { expiredBookings: ended?.n ?? 0 };
}

/**
 * Nightly step 2 (E11, E12): purchases still pending STALE_PURCHASE_GRACE_MINUTES
 * after checkout_expires_at, without processing_at, → expired. A payment that
 * still arrives is fulfilled from `expired` (E10).
 */
export async function expireStalePurchases(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ expired: number }> {
  const cutoff = new Date(now.getTime() - STALE_PURCHASE_GRACE_MINUTES * 60_000);
  const rows = await db
    .update(ticketPurchase)
    .set({ status: "expired", updatedAt: now })
    .where(
      and(
        eq(ticketPurchase.status, "pending"),
        isNull(ticketPurchase.processingAt),
        lt(ticketPurchase.checkoutExpiresAt, cutoff),
      ),
    )
    .returning({ id: ticketPurchase.id });
  return { expired: rows.length };
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { bookWithHeldTicket, registerFree, registerPro } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { ticket, ticketMovement } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { addMinutes, dbTest, inParallel, type DbHarness } from "@/test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeType,
  movementsOf,
  ticketsOf,
  type OccurrenceRow,
} from "@/test/fixtures";

type Outcome = { ok: true } | { ok: false; code: string };
const codeOf = (r: Outcome): string => (r.ok ? "ok" : r.code);
const codesOf = (settled: PromiseSettledResult<Outcome>[]): string[] =>
  settled.map((s) => (s.status === "fulfilled" ? codeOf(s.value) : `threw: ${String(s.reason)}`));
const countOf = (codes: string[], code: string): number => codes.filter((c) => c === code).length;

function held(h: DbHarness, occ: OccurrenceRow, customerId: string) {
  return {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
    actorUserId: null,
  };
}

function pro(h: DbHarness, occ: OccurrenceRow, customerId: string) {
  return {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    actorUserId: h.pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId,
    asGuest: false,
  };
}

dbTest(
  "T1: 20 concurrent spends on 5 seats: exactly 5 win",
  async (h) => {
    const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
    const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], capacity: 5 });
    const customers = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)));
    await Promise.all(customers.map((c) => giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, c, typeId, 1)));

    const codes = codesOf(await inParallel(customers.map((c) => () => bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, occ, c)))));
    assert.equal(countOf(codes, "ok"), 5, codes.join(","));
    assert.equal(countOf(codes, "full"), 15, codes.join(","));
    assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 5);
    const booked = await h.db
      .select({ id: ticket.id })
      .from(ticket)
      .where(and(eq(ticket.professionalId, h.pro.proId), eq(ticket.status, "booked")));
    assert.equal(booked.length, 5);
  },
  { timeout: 240_000 },
);

dbTest(

dbTest("E2 spends the soonest-expiring valid ticket and confirms", async (h) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const [forever] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  const [soon] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, typeId, 1, { expiresAt: addMinutes(occ.startsAt, 1440) });
  const [tooSoon] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, typeId, 1, { expiresAt: addMinutes(occ.startsAt, -60) });

  const res = await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, occ, customerId));
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.equal(res.ticketId, soon);
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [
    { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "email.pro_new_booking", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: occ.id },
  ]);
  const b = await getBooking(h.db, res.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "confirmed");
  assert.equal(b.coverage, "ticket");
  assert.equal(b.createdBy, "client");
  assert.ok(b.confirmedAt);
  const moves = await movementsOf(h.db, soon);
  assert.deepEqual(moves.map((m) => m.kind), ["offer", "spend"]);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);

  // The same client again on the same séance.
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, occ, customerId))), "already_booked");
  // A second séance: the ticket without expiry is next; the one expiring before the séance never is.
  const { occurrences: [later] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const next = await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, later, customerId));
  assert.ok(next.ok, JSON.stringify(next));
  assert.equal(next.ticketId, forever);
  const { occurrences: [third] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, third, customerId))), "no_ticket");
  const left = (await ticketsOf(h.db, customerId)).find((t) => t.id === tooSoon);
  assert.equal(left?.status, "available");
});

dbTest("E2 client refuses a free-mode séance and a type the event does not accept", async (h) => {

  const otherType = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);

  const { occurrences: [free] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "free" });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, free, customerId))), "unavailable");

  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookWithHeldTicket(h.db, held(h, occ, customerId))), "no_ticket");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E3 registerFree: confirmed free booking, already booked, full, wrong mode", async (h) => {
  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "free", capacity: 2 });
  const [a, b, c] = await Promise.all([makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)]);
  const input = (customerId: string) => ({
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
  });

  const res = await registerFree(h.db, input(a));
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [
    { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "email.pro_new_booking", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: occ.id },
  ]);
  const row = await getBooking(h.db, res.bookingId);
  assert.equal(row.coverage, "free");
  assert.equal(row.status, "confirmed");
  assert.equal(row.purchaseId, null);

  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerFree(h.db, input(a))), "already_booked");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerFree(h.db, input(b))), "ok");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerFree(h.db, input(c))), "full");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 2);

  const { occurrences: [paid] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "tickets" });
  assert.equal(
    codeOf(await registerFree(h.db, { ...input(c), occurrenceId: paid.id, seenStartsAt: paid.startsAt })),
    "unavailable",
  );
});

dbTest("registerPro: guest on a « Sur place » séance, private and past séances, forced ticket", async (h) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const otherType = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);

  const { occurrences: [none] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "none" });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerPro(h.db, pro(h, none, customerId))), "not_eligible");
  const guest = await registerPro(h.db, { ...pro(h, none, customerId), asGuest: true });
  assert.ok(guest.ok, JSON.stringify(guest));
  assert.equal(guest.coverage, "guest");
  assert.equal(guest.ticketId, null);
  assert.deepEqual(guest.effects, [
    { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: guest.bookingId },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: none.id },
  ]);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, guest.bookingId)).createdBy, "pro");

  // A private séance two days ago: the pro path ignores privacy, notice and horizon.
  const [first, second] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, typeId, 2);
  const { occurrences: [past] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, {
    ticketTypeIds: [typeId],
    isPrivate: true,
    rule: { startDate: dayIn(h.pro, -2) },
  });
  const forced = await registerPro(h.db, { ...pro(h, past, customerId), ticketId: second });
  assert.ok(forced.ok, JSON.stringify(forced));
  assert.equal(forced.coverage, "ticket");
  assert.equal(forced.ticketId, second);
  const secondMoves = await movementsOf(h.db, second);
  assert.equal(secondMoves[secondMoves.length - 1].kind, "spend");
  const [actor] = await h.db
    .select({ actor: ticketMovement.actor, actorUserId: ticketMovement.actorUserId })
    .from(ticketMovement)
    .where(and(eq(ticketMovement.ticketId, second), eq(ticketMovement.kind, "spend")));
  assert.deepEqual(actor, { actor: "pro", actorUserId: h.pro.userId });

  // A forced ticket the séance does not accept.
  const [foreign] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, otherType, 1);
  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerPro(h.db, { ...pro(h, occ, customerId), ticketId: foreign })), "not_eligible");
  // Without forcing, the remaining eligible ticket is spent.
  const auto = await registerPro(h.db, pro(h, occ, customerId));
  assert.ok(auto.ok, JSON.stringify(auto));
  assert.equal(auto.ticketId, first);
});

dbTest("registerPro: « +1 place » raises the capacity; a cancelled séance is refused", async (h) => {
  const { occurrences: [occ] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "free", capacity: 1 });
  const [a, b, c] = await Promise.all([makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)]);

  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerPro(h.db, pro(h, occ, a))), "ok");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerPro(h.db, pro(h, occ, b))), "full");
  const extra = await registerPro(h.db, { ...pro(h, occ, b), extraSeat: true });
  assert.ok(extra.ok, JSON.stringify(extra));
  assert.equal(extra.coverage, "free");
  const after = await getOccurrence(h.db, occ.id);
  assert.equal(after.capacity, 2);
  assert.equal(after.seatsTaken, 2);
  assert.equal(after.capacityOverride, true);

  const { occurrences: [gone] } = await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { bookingMode: "free" });
  await h.db.execute(sql`update occurrence set status = 'cancelled', cancelled_at = ${new Date().toISOString()}::timestamptz where id = ${gone.id}`);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await registerPro(h.db, pro(h, gone, c))), "unavailable");
});
```

```ts

/* ───────────────── E2 / E3: held ticket, free registration, pro « Inscrire » ───────────────── */

/** Effects of a confirmed booking: the client always; the pro only when the client booked (spec § 13.6). */
function confirmedEffects(bookingId: string, occurrenceId: string, byClient: boolean): Effect[] {
  const effects: Effect[] = [{ type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId }];
  if (byClient) effects.push({ type: "email.pro_new_booking", bookingId });
  return [...effects, ...syncEffects([occurrenceId])];
}

async function bookedTicketOf(db: Db, bookingId: string): Promise<string> {
  const [row] = await db.select({ id: ticket.id }).from(ticket).where(eq(ticket.bookingId, bookingId));
  if (!row) throw new Error(`booking ${bookingId}: no booked ticket after a committed spend`);
  return row.id;
}

/**
 * E2 client: spend a held ticket on an event séance in `tickets` mode
 * (requireWalletOwner is the P4 action's job). Batch: lock occurrence, R,
 * SEAT(+1), G(ONLINE_OK 'tickets'), G(CHANGED), INSERT booking (confirmed,
 * ticket), spend (SKIP LOCKED pick) + assertSpent, answers.
 */
export async function bookWithHeldTicket(
  db: Db,
  input: ClientClaimBase & { actorUserId: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ bookingId: string; ticketId: string }>> {
  const planned: { bookingId?: string } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const notes = cleanNotes(input.customerNotes);
    const [target] = await Promise.all([
      readEventTarget(db, input.professionalId, input.occurrenceId),
      assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
    ]);
    assertOnline(target, "tickets", input.seenStartsAt, now);
    const answers = buildAnswers(await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: target.eventId }), input.answers, {
      enforceRequired: true,
    });
    if (!answers.ok) throw new PlanError("invalid", answers.message);
    const answerRows = answers.rows;
    const bookingId = nanoid();
    planned.bookingId = bookingId;

    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const { professionalId, occurrenceId, customerId } = input;
      return [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [occurrenceId]),
        R(c, occurrenceId),
        SEAT(c, occurrenceId, 1),
        G(db, onlineOk(c, { professionalId, occurrenceId, mode: "tickets" }), "unavailable"),
        G(db, unchangedStart(occurrenceId, input.seenStartsAt), "changed"),
        insertBooking(c, {
          id: bookingId,
          professionalId,
          occurrenceId,
          customerId,
          status: "confirmed",
          coverage: "ticket",
          createdBy: "client",
          customerNotes: notes,
        }),
        spendTicket(c, {
          bookingId,
          customerId,
          occurrenceId,
          by: { actor: "client", userId: input.actorUserId },
        }),
        assertSpent(c, bookingId),
        ...insertAnswers(c, { professionalId, bookingId, rows: answerRows }),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  if (!planned.bookingId) throw new Error("bookWithHeldTicket: the plan produced no booking");
  const bookingId = planned.bookingId;
  return {
    ok: true,
    effects: confirmedEffects(bookingId, input.occurrenceId, true),
    bookingId,
    ticketId: await bookedTicketOf(db, bookingId),
  };
}

/**
 * E3: an event séance in `free` mode. Batch: lock occurrence, R, SEAT(+1),
 * G(ONLINE_OK 'free'), G(CHANGED), INSERT booking (confirmed, free), answers.
 * One live registration per séance and e-mail: booking_active_uq.
 */
export async function registerFree(
  db: Db,
  input: ClientClaimBase,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ bookingId: string }>> {
  const planned: { bookingId?: string } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const notes = cleanNotes(input.customerNotes);
    const [target] = await Promise.all([
      readEventTarget(db, input.professionalId, input.occurrenceId),
      assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
    ]);
    assertOnline(target, "free", input.seenStartsAt, now);
    const answers = buildAnswers(await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: target.eventId }), input.answers, {
      enforceRequired: true,
    });
    if (!answers.ok) throw new PlanError("invalid", answers.message);
    const answerRows = answers.rows;
    const bookingId = nanoid();
    planned.bookingId = bookingId;

    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const { professionalId, occurrenceId, customerId } = input;
      return [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [occurrenceId]),
        R(c, occurrenceId),
        SEAT(c, occurrenceId, 1),
        G(db, onlineOk(c, { professionalId, occurrenceId, mode: "free" }), "unavailable"),
        G(db, unchangedStart(occurrenceId, input.seenStartsAt), "changed"),
        insertBooking(c, {
          id: bookingId,
          professionalId,
          occurrenceId,
          customerId,
          status: "confirmed",
          coverage: "free",
          createdBy: "client",
          customerNotes: notes,
        }),
        ...insertAnswers(c, { professionalId, bookingId, rows: answerRows }),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  if (!planned.bookingId) throw new Error("registerFree: the plan produced no booking");
  const bookingId = planned.bookingId;
  return { ok: true, effects: confirmedEffects(bookingId, input.occurrenceId, true), bookingId };
}

/** Coverage of a pro registration: guest on any mode, else the event's mode (`none` needs « Invité »). */
function proCoverage(asGuest: boolean, mode: BookingMode): BookingCoverage {
  if (asGuest) return "guest";
  if (mode === "tickets") return "ticket";
  if (mode === "free") return "free";
  throw new PlanError("not_eligible");
}

/** registerPro guard: a scheduled séance of this pro whose mode still matches the coverage. */
function proRegistrationOk(a: { professionalId: string; occurrenceId: string; coverage: BookingCoverage }): SQL {
  return sql`exists (select 1 from occurrence o join event e on e.id = o.event_id
    where o.id = ${a.occurrenceId} and o.professional_id = ${a.professionalId} and o.status = 'scheduled'
      and (${a.coverage}::text = 'guest'
           or (${a.coverage}::text = 'ticket' and e.booking_mode = 'tickets')
           or (${a.coverage}::text = 'free' and e.booking_mode = 'free')))`;
}

/**
 * E2 pro « Inscrire » on an EVENT séance: past, private, outside notice and
 * horizon or under a time off are allowed; a cancelled séance is not. The pro
 * may force a ticket (still eligible) and add « +1 place » (extraSeat).
 * Required questions are not enforced. Effects: the client's confirmation and
 * the Google sync, never « nouvelle inscription » to the pro.
 */
export async function registerPro(
  db: Db,
  input: {
    professionalId: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    occurrenceId: string;
    customerId: string;
    asGuest: boolean;
    ticketId?: string | null;
    extraSeat?: boolean;
    answers?: AnswerInput[];
  },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ bookingId: string; coverage: BookingCoverage; ticketId: string | null }>> {
  const planned: { bookingId?: string; coverage?: BookingCoverage } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [target] = await Promise.all([
      readEventTarget(db, input.professionalId, input.occurrenceId),
      assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
    ]);
    if (target.status !== "scheduled") throw new PlanError("unavailable");
    const coverage = proCoverage(input.asGuest, target.bookingMode);
    const forced = input.ticketId ?? null;
    if (forced !== null) {
      if (coverage !== "ticket") throw new PlanError("invalid");
      const usable = await usableTickets(db, { customerId: input.customerId, occurrenceId: input.occurrenceId });
      if (!usable.some((t) => t.id === forced)) throw new PlanError("not_eligible");
    }
    const answers = buildAnswers(await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: target.eventId }), input.answers, {
      enforceRequired: false,
    });
    if (!answers.ok) throw new PlanError("invalid", answers.message);
    const answerRows = answers.rows;
    const bookingId = nanoid();
    planned.bookingId = bookingId;
    planned.coverage = coverage;

    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const { professionalId, occurrenceId, customerId } = input;
      const queries: Query[] = [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [occurrenceId]),
        R(c, occurrenceId),
        SEAT(c, occurrenceId, 1, { extraSeat: input.extraSeat ?? false }),
        G(db, proRegistrationOk({ professionalId, occurrenceId, coverage }), "unavailable"),
        insertBooking(c, {
          id: bookingId,
          professionalId,
          occurrenceId,
          customerId,
          status: "confirmed",
          coverage,
          createdBy: "pro",
          customerNotes: null,
        }),
      ];
      if (coverage === "ticket") {
        queries.push(
          spendTicket(c, {
            bookingId,
            customerId,
            occurrenceId,
            ticketId: forced,
            by: { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId },
          }),
          assertSpent(c, bookingId),
        );
      }
      queries.push(...insertAnswers(c, { professionalId, bookingId, rows: answerRows }));
      return queries;
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const { bookingId, coverage } = planned;
  if (!bookingId || !coverage) throw new Error("registerPro: the plan produced no booking");
  return {
    ok: true,
    effects: confirmedEffects(bookingId, input.occurrenceId, false),
    bookingId,
    coverage,
    ticketId: coverage === "ticket" ? await bookedTicketOf(db, bookingId) : null,
  };
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, eq, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { setAvailabilityRules, setTicketTypeArchived } from "@/lib/booking/catalog";
import { bookRdv, type BookRdvInput } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { fulfillPurchase } from "@/lib/booking/fulfillment";
import { instantAt } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { occurrence, timeOff } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import type { TicketTypeInput } from "@/lib/tickets";
import { addMinutes, dbTest, inParallel, type DbHarness } from "@/test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeOffer,
  makeType,
  ticketsOf,
} from "@/test/fixtures";

type Outcome = { ok: true } | { ok: false; code: string };
const codeOf = (r: Outcome): string => (r.ok ? "ok" : r.code);

/** Global hours 08:00–20:00 every day; an RDV type; `at(minute)` = that minute on the day in 3 days (Paris). */
async function rdvSetup(h: DbHarness, typeOver: Partial<TicketTypeInput> = {}) {
  const hours = await setAvailabilityRules(h.db, {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId: null,
    rules: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((dayOfWeek) => ({ dayOfWeek, startMinute: 480, endMinute: 1200 })),
  });
  assert.ok(hours.ok, JSON.stringify(hours));
  const typeId = await makeType(h.db, h.pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60, ...typeOver });
  const day = dayIn(h.pro, 3);
  const at = (minute: number) => instantAt(day, minute, h.pro.tz);
  return { typeId, day, at };
}

function rdv(h: DbHarness, typeId: string, startsAt: Date, customerId: string, mode: "held_ticket" | "free"): BookRdvInput {
  return { professionalId: h.pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, startsAt, customerId, actorUserId: null, mode };
}

async function scheduledRdvs(h: DbHarness) {
  return h.db
    .select()
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(and(eq(occurrence.professionalId, h.pro.proId), isNull(occurrence.eventId), eq(occurrence.status, "scheduled")));
}

dbTest("E4 held ticket: creates the RDV, confirms it and spends the ticket", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(h.db, h.pro, customerId, s.typeId, 1);

  const res = await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(600), customerId, "held_ticket"));
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.equal(res.purchaseId, null);
  assert.equal(res.checkoutExpiresAt, null);
  assert.equal(res.amountCents, null);
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [
    { type: "email.booking_confirmed", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "email.pro_new_booking", bookingId: res.bookingId },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: res.occurrenceId },
  ]);
  const o = await getOccurrence(h.db, res.occurrenceId);
  assert.equal(o.eventId, null);
  assert.equal(o.ticketTypeId, s.typeId);
  assert.equal(o.seriesDate, null);
  assert.equal(o.capacity, 1);
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 1);
  assert.equal(o.status, "scheduled");
  assert.equal(o.startsAt.getTime(), s.at(600).getTime());
  assert.equal(o.endsAt.getTime(), s.at(660).getTime());
  assert.equal(o.blockStart.getTime(), s.at(600).getTime());
  assert.equal(o.blockEnd.getTime(), s.at(660).getTime());
  const b = await getBooking(h.db, res.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "confirmed");
  assert.equal(b.coverage, "ticket");
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(h.db, customerId);
  assert.equal(t.id, ticketId);
  assert.equal(t.status, "booked");
  assert.equal(t.bookingId, res.bookingId);

  // No ticket left: nothing is created.
  const other = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(780), other, "held_ticket"))), "no_ticket");
  assert.equal((await scheduledRdvs(h)).length, 1);
});

dbTest("T6a: two clients on the same slot, one wins", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h, { rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 15 });
  const [a, b] = await Promise.all([makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)]);
  const settled = await inParallel([
    () => bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(600), a, "free")),
    () => bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(600), b, "free")),
  ]);
  const codes = settled.map((r) => (r.status === "fulfilled" ? codeOf(r.value) : `threw: ${String(r.reason)}`)).sort();
  assert.deepEqual(codes, ["ok", "slot_taken"]);
  assert.equal((await scheduledRdvs(h)).length, 1);
});

dbTest("T6b: an RDV over an event séance is slot_taken; back-to-back is fine", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h, { rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 30 });
  // makeEvent default: once, in 3 days, 10:00–11:00 (Paris), private séances block too.
  await makeEvent(h.db, h.pro, { isPrivate: true, bookingMode: "none" });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(630), customerId, "free"))), "slot_taken");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(660), customerId, "free"))), "ok");
});

dbTest("E4 buffers widen the block: neighbours inside the buffer are slot_taken", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h, { rdvFree: true, rdvBufferBeforeMin: 15, rdvBufferAfterMin: 15 });
  const [a, b, c] = await Promise.all([makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)]);
  const first = await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(600), a, "free"));
  assert.ok(first.ok, JSON.stringify(first));
  const o = await getOccurrence(h.db, first.occurrenceId);
  assert.equal(o.blockStart.getTime(), s.at(585).getTime());
  assert.equal(o.blockEnd.getTime(), s.at(675).getTime());
  // 11:00 → block 10:45–12:15 overlaps 09:45–11:15 (EXCLUDE).
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(660), b, "free"))), "slot_taken");
  // 11:30 → block 11:15–12:45 only touches it.
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, s.typeId, s.at(690), c, "free"))), "ok");
});

dbTest("E4 free type, mode checks, hours, archived type and time off", async (h) => {
  const free = await rdvSetup(h, { rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 15 });
  const paid = await makeType(h.db, h.pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);

  const ok = await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, free.typeId, free.at(540), customerId, "free"));
  assert.ok(ok.ok, JSON.stringify(ok));
  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, ok.bookingId)).coverage, "free");

  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, free.typeId, free.at(600), customerId, "held_ticket"))), "unavailable");
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, paid, free.at(600), customerId, "free"))), "unavailable");
  // 20:00 is outside the 08:00–20:00 hours (the 15 min would end at 20:15).
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, free.typeId, free.at(1200), customerId, "free"))), "unavailable");

  await h.db.insert(timeOff).values({
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    startAt: free.at(720),
    endAt: free.at(780),
    allDay: false,
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, free.typeId, free.at(735), customerId, "free"))), "slot_taken");

  const archived = await setTicketTypeArchived(h.db, { professionalId: h.pro.proId, ticketTypeId: free.typeId, archived: true });
  assert.ok(archived.ok);
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, rdv(h, free.typeId, free.at(900), customerId, "free"))), "unavailable");
});

dbTest("E4 buy: a pending RDV, then paid like T14", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, s.typeId, {
    quantity: 5,
    priceCents: 22500,
    validityCount: 6,
    validityUnit: "month",
  });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro);
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await bookRdv(
    h.db,
    { professionalId: h.pro.proId, ticketTypeId: s.typeId, startsAt: s.at(600), customerId, actorUserId: null, mode: "buy", offerId },
    now,
  );
  assert.ok(res.ok, JSON.stringify(res));
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, []);
  assert.equal(res.amountCents, 22500);
  assert.equal(res.checkoutExpiresAt?.getTime(), addMinutes(now, 31).getTime());
  assert.ok(res.purchaseId);
  const pending = await getBooking(h.db, res.bookingId);
  assert.equal(pending.status, "pending");
  assert.equal(pending.purchaseId, res.purchaseId);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(h.db, res.occurrenceId)).seatsTaken, 1);

  const paid = await fulfillPurchase(h.db, res.purchaseId, {
    sessionId: `cs_test_${nanoid()}`,
    paymentIntentId: `pi_test_${nanoid()}`,
    livemode: false,
  });
  assert.equal(paid.outcome, "fulfilled");
  assert.equal(paid.bookingConfirmed, true);
  const tickets = await ticketsOf(h.db, customerId);
  assert.equal(tickets.length, 5);
  assert.equal(tickets.filter((t) => t.status === "booked" && t.bookingId === res.bookingId).length, 1);

  // An offer of another type cannot buy this RDV.
  const otherType = await makeType(h.db, h.pro);
  const foreignOffer = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, otherType);
  const foreign = await bookRdv(h.db, {
    professionalId: h.pro.proId,
    ticketTypeId: s.typeId,
    startsAt: s.at(780),
    customerId,
    actorUserId: null,
    mode: "buy",
    offerId: foreignOffer,
  });
  assert.equal(codeOf(foreign), "unavailable");
});

dbTest("E4 an elapsed RDV hold frees the slot for the next client", async (h) => {
  const s = await rdvSetup(h);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(h.db, h.pro, s.typeId);
  const [a, b] = await Promise.all([makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro), makeCustomer(h.db, h.pro)]);
  const now = new Date();
  const buy = (customerId: string) =>
    ({ professionalId: h.pro.proId, ticketTypeId: s.typeId, startsAt: s.at(600), customerId, actorUserId: null, mode: "buy", offerId }) as const;

  const first = await bookRdv(h.db, buy(a), now);
  assert.ok(first.ok, JSON.stringify(first));
  assert.equal(codeOf(await bookRdv(h.db, buy(b), addMinutes(now, 10))), "slot_taken");

  const second = await bookRdv(h.db, buy(b), addMinutes(now, 46));
  assert.ok(second.ok, JSON.stringify(second));
  const old = await getOccurrence(h.db, first.occurrenceId);
  assert.equal(old.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(old.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(h.db, first.bookingId)).status, "expired");
  assert.equal((await scheduledRdvs(h)).length, 1);
});
```

```ts

/* ───────────────────────── E4: RDV libre ───────────────────────── */

export type BookRdvInput = {
  professionalId: string;
  ticketTypeId: string;
  /** a slot start the P5 action took from the RDV slot loader */
  startsAt: Date;
  customerId: string;
  answers?: AnswerInput[];
  customerNotes?: string | null;
  actorUserId: string | null;
} & ({ mode: "held_ticket" } | { mode: "free" } | { mode: "buy"; offerId: string });

export type BookRdvData = {
  occurrenceId: string;
  bookingId: string;
  purchaseId: string | null;
  checkoutExpiresAt: Date | null;
  amountCents: number | null;
};

/** An RDV type of this pro with its duration, buffers and effective window (type overrides, else the pro's). */
async function readRdvType(db: Db, professionalId: string, ticketTypeId: string) {
  const [row] = await db
    .select({
      rdvEnabled: ticketType.rdvEnabled,
      rdvFree: ticketType.rdvFree,
      durationMin: ticketType.rdvDurationMin,
      bufferBeforeMin: ticketType.rdvBufferBeforeMin,
      bufferAfterMin: ticketType.rdvBufferAfterMin,
      archivedAt: ticketType.archivedAt,
      typeHorizonDays: ticketType.rdvBookingHorizonDays,
      typeNoticeMinutes: ticketType.rdvMinNoticeMinutes,
      typeCancelHours: ticketType.rdvCancelDeadlineHours,
      tz: professional.timezone,
      bookingHorizonDays: professional.bookingHorizonDays,
      minNoticeMinutes: professional.minNoticeMinutes,
      cancelDeadlineHours: professional.cancelDeadlineHours,
    })
    .from(ticketType)
    .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, ticketType.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(ticketType.id, ticketTypeId), eq(ticketType.professionalId, professionalId)));
  if (!row) throw new PlanError("not_found");
  return {
    rdvEnabled: row.rdvEnabled,
    rdvFree: row.rdvFree,
    durationMin: row.durationMin,
    bufferBeforeMin: row.bufferBeforeMin,
    bufferAfterMin: row.bufferAfterMin,
    archived: row.archivedAt !== null,
    tz: row.tz,
    window: effectiveWindow(
      {
        bookingHorizonDays: row.bookingHorizonDays,
        minNoticeMinutes: row.minNoticeMinutes,
        cancelDeadlineHours: row.cancelDeadlineHours,
      },
      {
        bookingHorizonDays: row.typeHorizonDays,
        minNoticeMinutes: row.typeNoticeMinutes,
        cancelDeadlineHours: row.typeCancelHours,
      },
    ),
  };
}

/** The type's own weekly hours when it has any, else the global ones (spec § 6.4 availability_rule). */
async function rdvRules(db: Db, professionalId: string, ticketTypeId: string) {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      ticketTypeId: availabilityRule.ticketTypeId,
      dayOfWeek: availabilityRule.dayOfWeek,
      startMinute: availabilityRule.startMinute,
      endMinute: availabilityRule.endMinute,
    })
    .from(availabilityRule)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(availabilityRule.professionalId, professionalId),
        or(eq(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId, ticketTypeId), isNull(availabilityRule.ticketTypeId)),
      ),
    );
  const own = rows.filter((r) => r.ticketTypeId === ticketTypeId);
  return own.length > 0 ? own : rows.filter((r) => r.ticketTypeId === null);
}

/** E4 type guard: still RDV-enabled, not archived, same free/paid nature. Asserted 'unavailable'. */
function rdvTypeOk(a: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string; isFree: boolean }): SQL {
  return sql`exists (select 1 from ticket_type tt
    where tt.id = ${a.ticketTypeId} and tt.professional_id = ${a.professionalId}
      and tt.rdv_enabled and tt.archived_at is null and tt.rdv_free = ${a.isFree}::boolean)`;
}

/**
 * E4 slot guard: no scheduled event séance (private, `none` and empty ones
 * included) overlaps the block, no time off overlaps it, notice and local-date
 * horizon (type overrides, else the pro's). Asserted 'slot_taken'.
 */
function rdvSlotFree(
  c: BatchCtx,
  a: { professionalId: string; ticketTypeId: string; startsAt: Date; blockStart: Date; blockEnd: Date },
): SQL {
  return sql`not exists (select 1 from occurrence o
      where o.professional_id = ${a.professionalId} and o.event_id is not null and o.status = 'scheduled'
        and tstzrange(o.block_start, o.block_end) && tstzrange(${ts(a.blockStart)}, ${ts(a.blockEnd)}))
    and not exists (select 1 from time_off t
      where t.professional_id = ${a.professionalId} and t.start_at < ${ts(a.blockEnd)} and t.end_at > ${ts(a.blockStart)})
    and exists (select 1 from ticket_type tt join professional p on p.id = tt.professional_id
      where tt.id = ${a.ticketTypeId}
        and ${ts(a.startsAt)} >= ${ts(c.now)} + make_interval(mins => coalesce(tt.rdv_min_notice_minutes, p.min_notice_minutes))
        and (${ts(a.startsAt)} at time zone p.timezone)::date
            <= (${ts(c.now)} at time zone p.timezone)::date + coalesce(tt.rdv_booking_horizon_days, p.booking_horizon_days))`;
}

/**
 * E4. Plan: type rdv_enabled, not archived, free iff mode free; start
 * bookable (notice, horizon) and inside the weekly hours (fitsAvailability:
 * the type's rules, else the global ones); « buy »: offer of this type on
 * sale whose validity covers the start; answers (type questions, required
 * enforced). The slot-grid membership (computeSlots) is the P5 action's job.
 * Batch: lock professional → the overlapping scheduled RDVs (block range, id
 * asc) [→ customer for « buy »]; expire their elapsed holds and release the
 * seats in aggregate (an RDV at 0 is cancelled and leaves the EXCLUDE);
 * INSERT the RDV occurrence (capacity 1, seats_taken 1, block ± buffers:
 * occurrence_rdv_excl → slot_taken); G(type); G(slot); then held ticket
 * (booking confirmed + spend), free (booking confirmed, free) or buy (offer
 * unchanged, hold quota, purchase + pending booking). Answers.
 * Effects: held/free: confirmation, pro notice, Google; buy: none.
 */
export async function bookRdv(
  db: Db,
  input: BookRdvInput,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: RaceOpts = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<BookRdvData>> {
  const planned: { data?: BookRdvData } = {};
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const notes = cleanNotes(input.customerNotes);
      const [type] = await Promise.all([
        readRdvType(db, input.professionalId, input.ticketTypeId),
        assertCustomer(db, input.professionalId, input.customerId),
      ]);
      const isFree = input.mode === "free";
      if (!type.rdvEnabled || type.archived || type.rdvFree !== isFree || type.durationMin === null) {
        throw new PlanError("unavailable");
      }
      const startsAt = input.startsAt;
      const endsAt = new Date(startsAt.getTime() + type.durationMin * 60_000);
      const blockStart = new Date(startsAt.getTime() - type.bufferBeforeMin * 60_000);
      const blockEnd = new Date(endsAt.getTime() + type.bufferAfterMin * 60_000);
      if (!onlineBookability({ startsAt, now, tz: type.tz, window: type.window }).ok) {
        throw new PlanError("unavailable");
      }
      const [rules, questions, offer] = await Promise.all([
        rdvRules(db, input.professionalId, input.ticketTypeId),
        loadQuestions(db, { ticketTypeId: input.ticketTypeId }),
        input.mode === "buy" ? readOffer(db, input.professionalId, input.offerId) : Promise.resolve(null),
      ]);
      if (!fitsAvailability(rules, startsAt, endsAt, type.tz)) throw new PlanError("unavailable");
      let buy: OfferRow | null = null;
      if (input.mode === "buy") {
        if (!offer) throw new PlanError("not_found");
        if (!offer.onSale || offer.ticketTypeId !== input.ticketTypeId) throw new PlanError("unavailable");
        if (!offerCovers(now, validityOf(offer), type.tz, startsAt)) throw new PlanError("offer_too_short");
        buy = offer;
      }
      const answers = buildAnswers(questions, input.answers, { enforceRequired: true });
      if (!answers.ok) throw new PlanError("invalid", answers.message);
      const answerRows = answers.rows;

      const occurrenceId = nanoid();
      const bookingId = nanoid();
      const purchaseId = buy ? nanoid() : null;
      const checkoutExpiresAt = buy ? checkoutExpiry(now, "buybook") : null;
      planned.data = {
        occurrenceId,
        bookingId,
        purchaseId,
        checkoutExpiresAt,
        amountCents: buy ? buy.priceCents : null,
      };

      return (aid) => {
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
        const { professionalId, ticketTypeId, customerId } = input;
        const overlappingRdvs = sql`select id from occurrence
          where professional_id = ${professionalId} and event_id is null and status = 'scheduled'
            and tstzrange(block_start, block_end) && tstzrange(${ts(blockStart)}, ${ts(blockEnd)})`;
        const queries: Query[] = [
          lockRows(db, "professional", [professionalId]),
          db.execute(sql`${overlappingRdvs} order by id for update`),
        ];
        if (buy) queries.push(lockRows(db, "customer", [customerId]));
        queries.push(
          expireElapsedHolds(c, overlappingRdvs),
          releaseEndedSeats(c),
          db.insert(occurrence).values({
            id: occurrenceId,
            professionalId,
            eventId: null,
            ticketTypeId,
            seriesDate: null,
            startsAt,
            endsAt,
            blockStart,
            blockEnd,
            capacity: 1,
            seatsTaken: 1,
            status: "scheduled",
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          }),
          G(db, rdvTypeOk({ professionalId, ticketTypeId, isFree }), "unavailable"),
          G(db, rdvSlotFree(c, { professionalId, ticketTypeId, startsAt, blockStart, blockEnd }), "slot_taken"),
        );
        const base = { id: bookingId, professionalId, occurrenceId, customerId, createdBy: "client" as const, customerNotes: notes };
        if (input.mode === "held_ticket") {
          queries.push(
            insertBooking(c, { ...base, status: "confirmed", coverage: "ticket" }),
            spendTicket(c, {
              bookingId,
              customerId,
              occurrenceId,
              by: { actor: "client", userId: input.actorUserId },
            }),
            assertSpent(c, bookingId),
          );
        } else if (input.mode === "free") {
          queries.push(insertBooking(c, { ...base, status: "confirmed", coverage: "free" }));
        } else if (buy && purchaseId && checkoutExpiresAt) {
          queries.push(
            G(db, offerUnchanged(buy, professionalId, { kind: "rdv", ticketTypeId }), "stale"),
            assertHoldQuota(c, customerId),
            insertPurchase(c, { id: purchaseId, professionalId, customerId, offer: buy, checkoutExpiresAt }),
            insertBooking(c, {
              ...base,
              status: "pending",
              coverage: "ticket",
              purchaseId,
              holdExpiresAt: holdExpiry(now),
            }),
          );
        }
        queries.push(...insertAnswers(c, { professionalId, bookingId, rows: answerRows }));
        return queries;
      };
    },
    { afterPlan: opts.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  if (!planned.data) throw new Error("bookRdv: the plan produced no data");
  const data = planned.data;
  const effects = input.mode === "buy" ? [] : confirmedEffects(data.bookingId, data.occurrenceId, true);
  return { ok: true, effects, ...data };
}
```

<!-- G5 -->

## Contract deviations (G5)

Each item below either fills a gap the contract leaves open or overrides a contract line that would break a spec rule. Everything else follows the contract to the letter.

1. **Extra exports from `src/lib/booking/cancel.ts` (additive).** `expireSessionEffects(ended)` and `occurrenceCancelledEffects(db, aid, occurrenceIds)` are exported because `series.ts` (Tasks 27, 28) reuses them. No contract name or signature changes. A failed `tryAtomic` result becomes an `EngineFail` through Task 16's `failOf(res)` (`duplicate` → `stale`) in `cancel.ts`, `series.ts` and `wallet.ts`; G5 adds no second helper for that.
2. **E6c staleness guard: both sides are sorted in SQL.** The contract compares `array_agg(o.id order by o.id)` with a JS-sorted `textArray(seen)`. JS sorts by UTF-16 code unit. Postgres sorts by the database collation, which can ignore case at the first level and skip `-` and `_`. Two equal sets of nanoids could therefore compare unequal. The guard compares `array_agg(id order by id)` with `(select array_agg(x order by x) from unnest($seen::text[]) as x)`. The semantics are the same (set equality), and the result no longer depends on the collation.
3. **End of the old segment (`editSeriesFrom` split, `deleteSeriesFrom`).** The guard sets `until_date = min(d − 1, lastSeriesDate(oldRule))`, and leaves the rule columns untouched when the old rule already stops before d. Spec E7 lot 1 says `until_date = d − 1`, and the two agree whenever the old rule still produces a date ≥ d. They differ in two cases:
   - Rewriting a `count` segment as `until d − 1` without the `min` would let `ensureHorizon` materialise dates past its N-th séance (phantoms).
   - A `once` segment cannot take `end_mode = 'until'` (`event_once_chk` → 23514).
4. **In-place `editSeriesFrom` keeps the planned-row-count guard** after the lock: `count(rows of $e with series_date ≥ d) = planned`. The contract skips step 4 in place. Without this guard, rows that `ensureHorizon` inserts between the plan and the batch would survive as old-rule phantom séances (the T8 class of bug).
5. **`generated_until`** of the edited segment is written by the step-2 INSERT (split) or the step-1 UPDATE (in place), not by a separate step-8 UPDATE. The value is the same, with one statement fewer.
6. **`moveAttendee`.** It does these things on top of the contract:
   - A `ticket` booking may only move to a target event in `tickets` mode, as `ELIGIBLE` requires.
   - When `force` is false, the batch re-asserts the expiry (`ticket_expires_before`), so a target retimed after the plan is still caught.
   - `R` runs on both séances before the two SEATs.
   - An RDV source is refused with `invalid`. Spec E7 says « Un RDV se déplace par annuler et réinscrire ».
7. **Task 25 tests for « after a pro change / move » at 23 h.** They stamp `occurrence.changed_at` and `booking.moved_at` with a raw UPDATE, because `updateOccurrence` (Task 27) and `moveAttendee` (Task 29) come later. Tasks 27 and 29 each add the same check through the real function.

**Clarifications (no contract change):**
- **Error codes where the contract is silent:**
  - E6a: an unknown booking id gives `not_found`; a booking that already ended gives `stale`.
  - `updateOccurrence`: an RDV, unenroll ids that are not on the séance, or an empty patch give `invalid`. A séance cancelled or moved since the read gives `changed`. `full` cannot occur there, because `GREATEST` clamps the capacity.
  - `editEventInPlace` on an archived event gives `not_found`.
  - The never-booked `DELETE` of `deleteSeriesFrom` is version-guarded (`stale`).
- **Google sync from `editEventInPlace`.** It also emits `google.sync_occurrence` for future séances with active bookings when the title, description or location changes. This follows spec E7: « Puis resynchronisation Google ».
- **`deletionImpact.pendingPayments`** counts pending purchases whose checkout is still open or being processed. It does not count stale ones that are waiting for the nightly expiry.
- **`ensureAllHorizons`** logs a failing pro and moves on to the next one. The report shape has no per-pro error list.
- **`createCustomer` / `upsertCustomer`** belong to Task 19 (G3). G5 only consumes them: `upsertCustomer` in T11, and `makeCustomer` everywhere.

```ts
// src/lib/db/atomic.ts (Task 14)
export type Db = NeonHttpDatabase<typeof schema>;
export type Query = BatchItem<"pg">;
export type BatchCtx = { db: Db; aid: string; now: Date };
export type ActorRef = { actor: Actor; userId: string | null };
export type TryAtomicResult =
  | ({ ok: true } & AtomicOk)
  | { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode; message?: string }
  | { ok: false; code: "duplicate" };
export class PlanError extends Error { constructor(readonly code: EngineErrorCode, readonly detail?: string) }
export function tryAtomic(db: Db, plan: () => Promise<BuildBatch>, opts?: { ctx?: "write" | "delete"; replanOnStale?: boolean; afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> }): Promise<TryAtomicResult>;
export function G(db: Db, pred: SQL, code: AssertCode): Query;
export function lockRows(db: Db, table: LockTable, ids: readonly string[]): Query;
export function SEAT(c: BatchCtx, occurrenceId: string, delta: -1 | 0 | 1, opts?: { extraSeat?: boolean }): Query;
export function RET(c: BatchCtx, by: ActorRef): Query;
export function ts(d: Date): SQL;
export function textArray(values: readonly string[]): SQL;
// src/lib/booking/types.ts (Task 16)
export type Effect; export type EngineFail; export type EngineResult<T extends object = object>;
export function syncEffects(occurrenceIds: readonly string[]): Effect[];
export function fail(code: EngineErrorCode, message?: string): EngineFail;
export function failOf(res: { ok: false; code: EngineErrorCode | "duplicate"; message?: string }): EngineFail;
// src/lib/db/schema.ts (Task 4): booking, occurrence, ticketPurchase. src/lib/tickets.ts (Task 1): BOUNDS.
```

```ts
export function cancelOccurrencesQuery(c: BatchCtx, args: { professionalId: string; occurrenceIds: readonly string[] }): Query;
```

Create `src/lib/booking/cancel.db.test.ts` (Write tool, whole file):

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { addDaysMs, addMinutes, dbTest, inParallel, type DbHarness } from "../../test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeOffer,
  makeType,
  movementsOf,
  ticketsOf,
} from "../../test/fixtures";
import { instantAt } from "../civil-date";
import { ts } from "../db/atomic";
import { cancelBookingsByPro, cancelByClient, cancelOccurrence, restoreOccurrence } from "./cancel";
import { setAvailabilityRules } from "./catalog";
import { attachCheckoutSession, bookRdv, bookWithHeldTicket, registerPro, startBuyAndBook } from "./claims";

type Outcome = { ok: boolean; code?: string };

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

const label = (s: PromiseSettledResult<Outcome>): string =>
  s.status === "rejected" ? `threw: ${String(s.reason)}` : s.value.ok ? "ok" : String(s.value.code);

dbTest("T3: client and pro cancel the same booking at once: one wins, exactly one return", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  const booked = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
    actorUserId: null,
  });
  must(booked, "book");

  const settled = await inParallel<Outcome>([
    () => cancelByClient(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId, bookingId: booked.bookingId, actorUserId: null }),
    () =>
      cancelBookingsByPro(db, {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        actorUserId: pro.userId,
        occurrenceId: occ.id,
        bookingIds: [booked.bookingId],
      }),
  ]);
  const outcomes = settled.map(label);
  assert.equal(outcomes.filter((o) => o === "ok").length, 1, outcomes.join(", "));
  const loser = outcomes.find((o) => o !== "ok");
  assert.ok(loser === "too_late_or_done" || loser === "stale", outcomes.join(", "));

  const moves = await movementsOf(db, ticketId);
  assert.equal(moves.filter((m) => m.kind === "return").length, 1);
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, customerId);
  assert.equal(t.status, "available");
  assert.equal(t.bookingId, null);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(db, booked.bookingId)).status, "cancelled");
});

dbTest("T7: cancelling a séance during 8 parallel claims keeps I5 and returns every ticket", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], capacity: 12 });
  const customers: { customerId: string; ticketId: string }[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
    const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
    customers.push({ customerId, ticketId });
  }
  const claim = (customerId: string) => () =>
    bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: occ.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt, actorUserId: null });

  const first = inParallel<Outcome>(customers.slice(0, 4).map((c) => claim(c.customerId)));
  const cancelled = cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: occ.id });
  const second = inParallel<Outcome>(customers.slice(4).map((c) => claim(c.customerId)));
  const [a, cancelRes, b] = await Promise.all([first, cancelled, second]);
  must(cancelRes, "cancelOccurrence");

  const claims = [...a, ...b].map(label);
  for (const o of claims) assert.ok(o === "ok" || o === "unavailable", claims.join(", "));
  const okClaims = claims.filter((o) => o === "ok").length;
  // every claim committed before the cancellation was ended by it, none after it got in
  assert.equal(cancelRes.bookingsCancelled, okClaims);
  assert.equal(cancelRes.ticketsReturned, okClaims);

  const o = await getOccurrence(db, occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 0);
  for (const c of customers) {
    const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, c.customerId);
    assert.equal(t.status, "available", `ticket of ${c.customerId}`);
    const kinds = (await movementsOf(db, c.ticketId)).map((m) => m.kind);
    assert.equal(kinds.filter((k) => k === "spend").length, kinds.filter((k) => k === "return").length);
  }
});

async function bookedSeance(h: DbHarness) {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  const booked = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
    actorUserId: null,
  });
  must(booked, "book");
  return { typeId, occ, customerId, ticketId, bookingId: booked.bookingId };
}

dbTest("E5: 25 h before the start the client cancels and gets the ticket back", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  const res = await cancelByClient(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId: s.customerId, bookingId: s.bookingId, actorUserId: null },
    addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, -25 * 60),
  );
  must(res, "cancel");
  assert.equal(res.ticketReturned, true);
  assert.equal(res.occurrenceId, s.occ.id);
  assert.deepEqual(
    res.effects.map((e) => e.type),
    ["email.booking_cancelled", "email.pro_booking_cancelled", "google.sync_occurrence"],
  );
  const b = await getBooking(db, s.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(b.endedBy, "client");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
});

dbTest("E5: 23 h before the start (deadline 24 h) is too late", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  const res = await cancelByClient(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId: s.customerId, bookingId: s.bookingId, actorUserId: null },
    addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, -23 * 60),
  );
  assert.equal(res.ok ? "ok" : res.code, "too_late_or_done");
  assert.equal((await getBooking(db, s.bookingId)).status, "confirmed");
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("E5: 23 h before the start is allowed after the pro changed the séance", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  const b = await getBooking(db, s.bookingId);
  // what updateOccurrence (Task 27) stamps when the pro moves the séance
  await db.execute(sql`update occurrence set changed_at = ${ts(addMinutes(b.createdAt, 1))} where id = ${s.occ.id}`);
  const res = await cancelByClient(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId: s.customerId, bookingId: s.bookingId, actorUserId: null },
    addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, -23 * 60),
  );
  must(res, "cancel after a change");
});

dbTest("E5: 23 h before the start is allowed after the pro moved the client", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  // what moveAttendee (Task 29) stamps
  await db.execute(sql`update booking set moved_at = ${ts(new Date())} where id = ${s.bookingId}`);
  const res = await cancelByClient(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId: s.customerId, bookingId: s.bookingId, actorUserId: null },
    addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, -23 * 60),
  );
  must(res, "cancel after a move");
});

dbTest("E5: never once the séance started, never twice, never someone else's booking", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  const input = { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId: s.customerId, bookingId: s.bookingId, actorUserId: null };
  const started = await cancelByClient(db, input, addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, 1));
  assert.equal(started.ok ? "ok" : started.code, "too_late_or_done");
  const other = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const notMine = await cancelByClient(db, { ...input, customerId: other });
  assert.equal(notMine.ok ? "ok" : notMine.code, "not_found");
  must(await cancelByClient(db, input), "first cancel");
  const twice = await cancelByClient(db, input);
  assert.equal(twice.ok ? "ok" : twice.code, "too_late_or_done");
});

dbTest("E5 on an RDV libre: the RDV occurrence is cancelled and leaves the EXCLUDE", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 });
  const allWeek = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((dayOfWeek) => ({ dayOfWeek, startMinute: 0, endMinute: 1440 }));
  must(await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: null, rules: allWeek }), "rules");
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const startsAt = instantAt(dayIn(pro, 3), 600, pro.tz);
  const rdv = await bookRdv(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, startsAt, customerId, actorUserId: null, mode: "free" });
  must(rdv, "bookRdv");

  const res = await cancelByClient(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, customerId, bookingId: rdv.bookingId, actorUserId: null });
  must(res, "cancel");
  assert.equal(res.ticketReturned, false);
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, rdv.occurrenceId);
  assert.equal(o.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 0);
  // the slot is free again
  const again = await bookRdv(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, startsAt, customerId, actorUserId: null, mode: "free" });
  must(again, "rebook the same slot");
});

dbTest("E6a: the pro cancels a confirmed and a pending booking; the open checkout is expired", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(db, pro, typeId);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const c1 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, c1, typeId, 1);
  const confirmed = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId: c1,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
    actorUserId: null,
  });
  must(confirmed, "held ticket");
  const c2 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const pending = await startBuyAndBook(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    customerId: c2,
    seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt,
    offerId,
  });
  must(pending, "buy + book");
  const sessionId = `cs_test_${pending.purchaseId}`;
  must(
    await attachCheckoutSession(db, { purchaseId: pending.purchaseId, checkoutSessionId: sessionId, checkoutUrl: "https://checkout.example.test/s" }),
    "attach",
  );

  const res = await cancelBookingsByPro(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    bookingIds: [confirmed.bookingId, pending.bookingId],
    reason: "Cours déplacé",
  });
  must(res, "cancelBookingsByPro");
  assert.equal(res.cancelled, 2);
  assert.equal(res.ticketsReturned, 1);
  assert.ok(res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.booking_cancelled" && e.bookingId === pending.bookingId && e.by === "pro"));
  assert.ok(
    res.effects.some(
      (e) => e.type === "stripe.expire_session" && e.purchaseId === pending.purchaseId && e.checkoutSessionId === sessionId,
    ),
  );
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
  const b = await getBooking(db, confirmed.bookingId);
  assert.equal(b.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(b.endedBy, "pro");
  assert.equal(b.cancelReason, "Cours déplacé");

  const again = await cancelBookingsByPro(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: occ.id,
    bookingIds: [confirmed.bookingId],
  });
  assert.equal(again.ok ? "ok" : again.code, "stale");
});

dbTest("E6a: allowed on a past séance (the pro fixes a mistake), the ticket comes back", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [past],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, -5, now) } }, addDaysMs(now, -10));
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  const reg = await registerPro(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: past.id,
    customerId,
    asGuest: false,
  });
  must(reg, "registerPro on a past séance");
  const res = await cancelBookingsByPro(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: past.id,
    bookingIds: [reg.bookingId],
  });
  must(res, "cancel");
  assert.equal(res.ticketsReturned, 1);
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, customerId);
  assert.equal(t.id, ticketId);
  assert.equal(t.status, "available");
});

dbTest("E6b: cancelling a séance ends every booking, returns the tickets, and is idempotent", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const bookingIds: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
    await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
    const r = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: occ.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt, actorUserId: null });
    must(r, "book");
    bookingIds.push(r.bookingId);
  }
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: occ.id }, now);
  must(res, "cancelOccurrence");
  assert.equal(res.bookingsCancelled, 2);
  assert.equal(res.ticketsReturned, 2);
  const mail = res.effects.find((e) => e.type === "email.occurrence_cancelled");
  assert.ok(mail && mail.type === "email.occurrence_cancelled");
  assert.deepEqual([...mail.bookingIds].sort(), [...bookingIds].sort());
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.status, "cancelled");
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal(o.cancelledAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());

  const again = await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: occ.id });
  must(again, "second cancelOccurrence");
  assert.equal(again.bookingsCancelled, 0);
  assert.equal(again.ticketsReturned, 0);

  const missing = await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: "nope" });
  assert.equal(missing.ok ? "ok" : missing.code, "not_found");
});

dbTest("Rétablir: a cancelled future séance comes back empty; never after its start", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await bookedSeance(h);
  must(await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id }), "cancel");
  const now = new Date();
  const res = await restoreOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id }, now);
  must(res, "restore");
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, []);
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.status, "scheduled");
  assert.equal(o.cancelledAt, null);
  assert.equal(o.changedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(db, s.bookingId)).status, "cancelled");
  must(await restoreOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id }), "restore is idempotent");

  must(await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id }), "cancel again");
  const late = await restoreOccurrence(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id }, addMinutes(s.occ.startsAt, 1));
  assert.equal(late.ok ? "ok" : late.code, "not_found");
});
```
