Run: `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --env-file-if-exists=.env.test --test --test-concurrency=1 src/lib/booking/cancel.db.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL. The file does not load (`Error: Cannot find module './cancel'`), and node:test reports `# fail 1`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `cancel.ts`**

Create `src/lib/booking/cancel.ts` (Write tool, whole file):

```ts
/**
 * Cancellations (spec § 11 E5, E6a, E6b, rétablir, E6c). Every write is one
 * tryAtomic batch: lock first, guarded UPDATE + G, SEAT once per occurrence,
 * RET once per action. Effects are descriptors run after commit (P3/P4).
 */
import { and, eq, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  RET,
  SEAT,
  lockRows,
  textArray,
  ts,
  tryAtomic,
  type ActorRef,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
  type Query,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, occurrence, ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { BOUNDS } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { fail, failOf, syncEffects, type Effect, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/* ───────────────────────── Shared helpers ───────────────────────── */

/** `return` movements written by one action. */
async function countReturned(db: Db, aid: string): Promise<number> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ n: number }>(
    sql`select count(*)::int as n from ticket_movement where action_id = ${aid} and kind = 'return'`,
  );
  return Number(res.rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
}

/* ───────────────────────── Statement builders ───────────────────────── */

/** UPDATE booking → cancelled (ended_at, ended_by, ended_action_id = aid, cancel_reason) for the scope, pending|confirmed, this pro. */
export function endBookings(
  c: BatchCtx,
  args: {
    professionalId: string;
    scope: { occurrenceIds: readonly string[] } | { occurrenceId: string; bookingIds: readonly string[] };
    by: ActorRef;
    reason?: string | null;
  },
): Query {
  const scope =
    "occurrenceIds" in args.scope
      ? sql`occurrence_id = any(${textArray(args.scope.occurrenceIds)})`
      : sql`occurrence_id = ${args.scope.occurrenceId} and id = any(${textArray(args.scope.bookingIds)})`;
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update booking set status = 'cancelled', ended_at = ${ts(c.now)}, ended_by = ${args.by.actor}::actor,
      ended_action_id = ${c.aid}, cancel_reason = ${args.reason ?? null}, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    where professional_id = ${args.professionalId} and status in ('pending', 'confirmed') and ${scope}`);
}

/** UPDATE occurrence → cancelled, cancelled_at = now, seats_taken = 0 WHERE id = any AND pro AND scheduled. */
export function cancelOccurrencesQuery(
  c: BatchCtx,
  args: { professionalId: string; occurrenceIds: readonly string[] },
): Query {
  return c.db.execute(sql`
    update occurrence set status = 'cancelled', cancelled_at = ${ts(c.now)}, seats_taken = 0, updated_at = ${ts(c.now)}
    where id = any(${textArray(args.occurrenceIds)}) and professional_id = ${args.professionalId} and status = 'scheduled'`);
}

/** Final read after commit: bookings ended by `aid`, with their purchase state (for effects). */
export async function readEndedByAction(
  db: Db,
  aid: string,
): Promise<
  { bookingId: string; occurrenceId: string; purchaseId: string | null; purchasePending: boolean; checkoutSessionId: string | null }[]
> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      bookingId: booking.id,
      occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId,
      purchaseId: booking.purchaseId,
      purchaseStatus: ticketPurchase.status,
      checkoutSessionId: ticketPurchase.checkoutSessionId,
    })
    .from(booking)
    .leftJoin(ticketPurchase, eq(ticketPurchase.id, booking.purchaseId))
    .where(eq(booking.endedActionId, aid))
    .orderBy(booking.id);
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    bookingId: r.bookingId,
    occurrenceId: r.occurrenceId,
    purchaseId: r.purchaseId,
    purchasePending: r.purchaseStatus === "pending",
    checkoutSessionId: r.checkoutSessionId,
  }));
}

/** stripe.expire_session for every ended booking whose purchase is still pending with a session. */
export function expireSessionEffects(ended: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof readEndedByAction>>): Effect[] {
  const effects: Effect[] = [];
  for (const b of ended) {
    if (b.purchaseId && b.purchasePending && b.checkoutSessionId) {
      effects.push({ type: "stripe.expire_session", purchaseId: b.purchaseId, checkoutSessionId: b.checkoutSessionId });
    }
  }
  return effects;
}

/**
 * E6b effects for séances cancelled by action `aid`: one « séance annulée »
 * e-mail per séance that had bookings, Stripe expiry for pending purchases,
 * a Google sync per séance. Shared with series.ts (orphans cancelled).
 */
export async function occurrenceCancelledEffects(
  db: Db,
  aid: string,
  occurrenceIds: readonly string[],
): Promise<{ effects: Effect[]; bookingsCancelled: number }> {
  const ended = await readEndedByAction(db, aid);
  const effects: Effect[] = [];
  for (const occurrenceId of occurrenceIds) {
    const bookingIds = ended.filter((b) => b.occurrenceId === occurrenceId).map((b) => b.bookingId);
    if (bookingIds.length > 0) effects.push({ type: "email.occurrence_cancelled", occurrenceId, bookingIds });
  }
  effects.push(...expireSessionEffects(ended), ...syncEffects(occurrenceIds));
  return { effects, bookingsCancelled: ended.length };
}

/* ───────────────────────── E5: client cancellation ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * E5 (verified session or guest link; the P4 action checks ownership).
 * Batch: lock occurrence; guarded UPDATE (deadline OR changed_at > created_at
 * OR moved_at); G(marked by aid, 'too_late_or_done'); SEAT(o, 0); RET(client).
 */
export async function cancelByClient(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; customerId: string; bookingId: string; actorUserId: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ occurrenceId: string; ticketReturned: boolean }>> {
  let occurrenceId = "";
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [b] = await db
      .select({ id: booking.id, occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId })
      .from(booking)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(booking.id, input.bookingId),
          eq(booking.professionalId, input.professionalId),
          eq(booking.customerId, input.customerId),
        ),
      );
    if (!b) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    occurrenceId = b.occurrenceId;
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      return [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [b.occurrenceId]),
        db.execute(sql`
          update booking b set status = 'cancelled', ended_at = ${ts(now)}, ended_by = 'client',
            ended_action_id = ${aid}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where b.id = ${b.id} and b.customer_id = ${input.customerId} and b.occurrence_id = ${b.occurrenceId}
            and b.status = 'confirmed'
            and exists (
              select 1 from occurrence o
              join professional p on p.id = o.professional_id
              left join event e on e.id = o.event_id
              left join ticket_type tt on tt.id = o.ticket_type_id
              where o.id = b.occurrence_id and o.starts_at > ${ts(now)}
                and (o.starts_at - make_interval(hours => coalesce(e.cancel_deadline_hours, tt.rdv_cancel_deadline_hours, p.cancel_deadline_hours)) > ${ts(now)}
                     or o.changed_at > b.created_at
                     or b.moved_at is not null))`),
        G(db, sql`exists (select 1 from booking where id = ${b.id} and ended_action_id = ${aid})`, "too_late_or_done"),
        SEAT(c, b.occurrenceId, 0),
        RET(c, { actor: "client", userId: input.actorUserId }),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const returned = await countReturned(db, res.aid);
  return {
    ok: true,
    effects: [
      { type: "email.booking_cancelled", bookingId: input.bookingId, by: "client" },
      { type: "email.pro_booking_cancelled", bookingId: input.bookingId },
      ...syncEffects([occurrenceId]),
    ],
    occurrenceId,
    ticketReturned: returned > 0,
  };
}

/* ───────────────────────── E6a: pro cancels bookings ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * E6a: one or several bookings of ONE occurrence, any time (past too, to fix
 * a mistake). Batch: lock occurrence; end them; G(all marked, 'stale');
 * SEAT(o, 0); RET(pro).
 */
export async function cancelBookingsByPro(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; occurrenceId: string; bookingIds: string[]; reason?: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ cancelled: number; ticketsReturned: number }>> {
  const ids = [...new Set(input.bookingIds)];
  const reason = input.reason?.trim() || null;
  if (ids.length === 0 || ids.length > BOUNDS.capacity.max) return fail("invalid");
  if (reason !== null && reason.length > BOUNDS.note) return fail("invalid");

  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [o] = await db
      .select({ id: occurrence.id })
      .from(occurrence)
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, input.occurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!o) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    const found = await db
      .select({ id: booking.id, status: booking.status })
      .from(booking)
      .where(and(eq(booking.occurrenceId, o.id), eq(booking.professionalId, input.professionalId), inArray(booking.id, ids)));
    if (found.length !== ids.length) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    if (found.some((b) => b.status !== "pending" && b.status !== "confirmed")) throw new PlanError("stale");
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      return [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o.id]),
        endBookings(c, {
          professionalId: input.professionalId,
          scope: { occurrenceId: o.id, bookingIds: ids },
          by: { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId },
          reason,
        }),
        G(
          db,
          sql`(select count(*)::int from booking where ended_action_id = ${aid} and id = any(${textArray(ids)})) = ${ids.length}::int`,
          "stale",
        ),
        SEAT(c, o.id, 0),
        RET(c, { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId }),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const ended = await readEndedByAction(db, res.aid);
  const effects: Effect[] = [
    ...ended.map((b): Effect => ({ type: "email.booking_cancelled", bookingId: b.bookingId, by: "pro" })),
    ...expireSessionEffects(ended),
    ...syncEffects([input.occurrenceId]),
  ];
  return { ok: true, effects, cancelled: ended.length, ticketsReturned: await countReturned(db, res.aid) };
}

/* ───────────────────────── E6b: pro cancels a séance ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * E6b, idempotent: the guarded UPDATE locks the occurrence (→ cancelled,
 * seats 0); G(cancelled, 'not_found'); end every pending|confirmed booking;
 * RET(pro). An already cancelled séance is a success with nothing to do.
 */
export async function cancelOccurrence(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; occurrenceId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ bookingsCancelled: number; ticketsReturned: number }>> {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [o] = await db
      .select({ id: occurrence.id })
      .from(occurrence)
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, input.occurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!o) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const by: ActorRef = { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId };
      return [
        cancelOccurrencesQuery(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, occurrenceIds: [o.id] }),
        G(
          db,
          sql`(select status from occurrence where id = ${o.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}) = 'cancelled'`,
          "not_found",
        ),
        endBookings(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, scope: { occurrenceIds: [o.id] }, by }),
        RET(c, by),
      ];
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const { effects, bookingsCancelled } = await occurrenceCancelledEffects(db, res.aid, [input.occurrenceId]);
  return { ok: true, effects, bookingsCancelled, ticketsReturned: await countReturned(db, res.aid) };
}

/* ───────────────────────── Rétablir ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * Future EVENT séance only (never an RDV): → scheduled, cancelled_at NULL,
 * changed_at = now. The roster stays empty; nothing goes to Google before the
 * first booking. Idempotent on a scheduled séance.
 */
export async function restoreOccurrence(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; occurrenceId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult> {
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [o] = await db
      .select({ id: occurrence.id })
      .from(occurrence)
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, input.occurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!o) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    return () => [
      db.execute(sql`
        update occurrence set status = 'scheduled', cancelled_at = null, changed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
        where id = ${o.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and status = 'cancelled'
          and event_id is not null and starts_at > ${ts(now)}`),
      G(db, sql`(select status from occurrence where id = ${o.id}) = 'scheduled'`, "not_found"),
    ];
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return { ok: true, effects: [] };
}
```

```ts
import { dbTest, inParallel } from "../../test/db";
import { dayIn, getBooking, getOccurrence, giveTickets, makeCustomer, makeEvent, makeType, ticketsOf } from "../../test/fixtures";

type Outcome = { ok: boolean; code?: string };

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

const label = (s: PromiseSettledResult<Outcome>): string =>
  s.status === "rejected" ? `threw: ${String(s.reason)}` : s.value.ok ? "ok" : String(s.value.code);

async function timeOffCount(db: Db, proId: string): Promise<number> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ n: number }>(sql`select count(*)::int as n from time_off where professional_id = ${proId}`);
  return Number(res.rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
}

dbTest("T6c: an RDV and a time off on the same slot are serialised by the professional lock", async ({ db, pro }) => {

  const allWeek = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((dayOfWeek) => ({ dayOfWeek, startMinute: 0, endMinute: 1440 }));
  must(await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: null, rules: allWeek }), "rules");
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const day = dayIn(pro, 3);
  const startsAt = instantAt(day, 600, pro.tz);

  const [rdv, off] = await inParallel<Outcome>([
    () => bookRdv(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: typeId, startsAt, customerId, actorUserId: null, mode: "free" }),
    () =>
      createTimeOffWithConflicts(db, {

        actorUserId: pro.userId,
        startAt: instantAt(day, 0, pro.tz),

        reason: null,
        seenOccurrenceIds: [],
        cancelBooked: false,
      }),

  assert.ok(pair === "slot_taken / ok" || pair === "ok / stale", pair);
  assert.equal(await timeOffCount(db, pro.proId), label(off) === "ok" ? 1 : 0);
});
```

```ts
/* ───────────────────────── E6c: time off ───────────────────────── */

export type TimeOffConflict = { occurrenceId: string; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; title: string; activeBookings: number };

/** Block range of a scheduled occurrence overlaps [startAt, endAt). */
function overlapsRange(startAt: Date, endAt: Date) {
  return sql`tstzrange(${occurrence.blockStart}, ${occurrence.blockEnd}) && tstzrange(${ts(startAt)}, ${ts(endAt)})`;
}

/** E6c preview: scheduled occurrences (séances and RDV) overlapping the range that have seats taken, id asc. */
export async function previewTimeOffConflicts(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; startAt: Date; endAt: Date },
): Promise<TimeOffConflict[]> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      occurrenceId: occurrence.id,
      startsAt: occurrence.startsAt,
      endsAt: occurrence.endsAt,
      eventTitle: event.title,
      typeName: ticketType.name,
      activeBookings: sql<number>`(select count(*)::int from booking b where b.occurrence_id = ${occurrence.id} and b.status in ('pending', 'confirmed'))`.mapWith(
        Number,
      ),
    })
    .from(occurrence)
    .leftJoin(event, eq(event.id, occurrence.eventId))
    .leftJoin(ticketType, eq(ticketType.id, occurrence.ticketTypeId))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId),
        eq(occurrence.status, "scheduled"),
        sql`${occurrence.seatsTaken} > 0`,
        overlapsRange(input.startAt, input.endAt),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(occurrence.id);
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    occurrenceId: r.occurrenceId,
    startsAt: r.startsAt,
    endsAt: r.endsAt,
    title: r.eventTitle ?? r.typeName ?? "",
    activeBookings: r.activeBookings,
  }));
}

/**
 * E6c. Batch: lock professional; lock every scheduled occurrence overlapping
 * the range (empty ones too, id asc); INSERT time_off; G(set of overlapping
 * scheduled occurrences with seats taken = seen set, 'stale'); cancelBooked →
 * E6b on that set. Returns `stale` to the UI (new preview), never re-plans.
 */
export async function createTimeOffWithConflicts(
  db: Db,
  input: {
    professionalId: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    startAt: Date;
    endAt: Date;
    allDay: boolean;
    reason: string | null;
    seenOccurrenceIds: string[];
    cancelBooked: boolean;
  },
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<{ timeOffId: string; cancelledOccurrences: number }>> {
  const reason = input.reason?.trim() || null;
  const seen = [...new Set(input.seenOccurrenceIds)];
  if (!(input.endAt.getTime() > input.startAt.getTime())) {
    return fail("invalid", "La fin de l'indisponibilité doit être après son début.");
  }
  if (reason !== null && reason.length > BOUNDS.note) return fail("invalid");
  const timeOffId = nanoid();
  const toCancel = input.cancelBooked ? seen : [];

  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const by: ActorRef = { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId };
      const overlap = sql`professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and status = 'scheduled'
        and tstzrange(block_start, block_end) && tstzrange(${ts(input.startAt)}, ${ts(input.endAt)})`;
      const queries: Query[] = [
        lockRows(db, "professional", [input.professionalId]),
        db.execute(sql`select id from occurrence where ${overlap} order by id for update`),
        db.insert(timeOff).values({
          id: timeOffId,
          professionalId: input.professionalId,
          startAt: input.startAt,
          endAt: input.endAt,
          allDay: input.allDay,
          reason,
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        }),
        // Both sides sorted by the database collation: an order-free set comparison.
        G(
          db,
          sql`(select coalesce(array_agg(id order by id), '{}'::text[]) from occurrence where ${overlap} and seats_taken > 0)
            = (select coalesce(array_agg(x order by x), '{}'::text[]) from unnest(${textArray(seen)}) as x)`,
          "stale",
        ),
      ];
      if (toCancel.length > 0) {
        queries.push(
          cancelOccurrencesQuery(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, occurrenceIds: toCancel }),
          G(
            db,
            sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(toCancel)}) and status = 'cancelled') = ${toCancel.length}::int`,
            "stale",
          ),
          endBookings(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, scope: { occurrenceIds: toCancel }, by }),
          RET(c, by),
        );
      }
      return queries;
    },
    { afterPlan: opts.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const effects = toCancel.length > 0 ? (await occurrenceCancelledEffects(db, res.aid, toCancel)).effects : [];
  return { ok: true, effects, timeOffId, cancelledOccurrences: toCancel.length };
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { addMinutes, dbTest, type DbHarness } from "../../test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeType,
  occurrencesOf,
  ticketsOf,
} from "../../test/fixtures";
import { instantAt } from "../civil-date";
import { event, eventTicketType } from "../db/schema";
import { cancelByClient } from "./cancel";
import { bookWithHeldTicket } from "./claims";
import { bookingsExpiringBefore, editEventInPlace, updateOccurrence } from "./series";

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

/** A séance 3 days ahead at 10:00 with `n` clients booked with a ticket each. */
async function seanceWithBookings({ db, pro }: DbHarness, n: number, ticketExpiresAt: Date | null = null) {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const { eventId, occurrences } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId] });
  const occ = occurrences[0];
  const bookingIds: string[] = [];
  const customerIds: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
    await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1, { expiresAt: ticketExpiresAt });
    const b = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: occ.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt, actorUserId: null });
    must(b, "book");
    bookingIds.push(b.bookingId);
    customerIds.push(customerId);
  }
  return { typeId, eventId, occ, bookingIds, customerIds };
}

dbTest("E7 cette séance: moving it detaches the time, stamps changed_at and notifies the attendees", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await seanceWithBookings(h, 1);
  const now = new Date();
  const newStart = instantAt(dayIn(pro, 4), 660, pro.tz);
  const res = await updateOccurrence(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id, seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt, patch: { startsAt: newStart, durationMin: 90 } },
    now,
  );
  must(res, "move");
  assert.equal(res.timeChanged, true);
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [
    { type: "email.occurrence_changed", occurrenceId: s.occ.id, bookingIds: s.bookingIds },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: s.occ.id },
  ]);
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.startsAt.getTime(), newStart.getTime());
  assert.equal(o.endsAt.getTime(), newStart.getTime() + 90 * 60_000);
  assert.equal(o.blockStart.getTime(), o.startsAt.getTime());
  assert.equal(o.blockEnd.getTime(), o.endsAt.getTime());
  assert.equal(o.timeDetached, true);
  assert.equal(o.changedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(o.seriesDate, s.occ.seriesDate);

  const stale = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: { capacity: 3 },
  });
  assert.equal(stale.ok ? "ok" : stale.code, "changed");
});

dbTest("E7 cette séance: a capacity below the seats taken is clamped by GREATEST", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await seanceWithBookings(h, 3);
  const res = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: { capacity: 1 },
  });
  must(res, "capacity");
  assert.equal(res.timeChanged, false);
  assert.ok(!res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.occurrence_changed"));
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.capacity, 3);
  assert.equal(o.capacityOverride, true);
  assert.equal(o.timeDetached, false);
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 3);
});

dbTest("E7 cette séance: a location override e-mails the attendees, without detaching the time", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await seanceWithBookings(h, 1);
  const res = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: { locationOverride: "  Salle B  " },
  });
  must(res, "location");
  assert.ok(res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.occurrence_changed" && e.occurrenceId === s.occ.id));
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.locationOverride, "Salle B");
  assert.equal(o.timeDetached, false);
  assert.equal(o.changedAt, null);
});

dbTest("E7 cette séance: moving past a ticket's validity lists it, and « Désinscrire » returns the ticket", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const expiresAt = instantAt(dayIn(pro, 4), 0, pro.tz);
  const s = await seanceWithBookings(h, 1, expiresAt);
  const newStart = instantAt(dayIn(pro, 5), 600, pro.tz);
  const expiring = await bookingsExpiringBefore(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: s.occ.id, newStartsAt: newStart });
  assert.deepEqual(
    expiring.map((e) => [e.bookingId, e.expiresAt.getTime()]),
    [[s.bookingIds[0], expiresAt.getTime()]],
  );
  assert.ok(expiring[0].customerName.length > 0);

  const res = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: { startsAt: newStart },
    unenrollBookingIds: s.bookingIds,
  });
  must(res, "move + unenroll");
  assert.ok(res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.booking_cancelled" && e.bookingId === s.bookingIds[0] && e.by === "pro"));
  assert.ok(!res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.occurrence_changed"));
  const o = await getOccurrence(db, s.occ.id);
  assert.equal(o.seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal((await getBooking(db, s.bookingIds[0])).status, "cancelled");
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, s.customerIds[0]);
  assert.equal(t.status, "available");
});

dbTest("E7 cette séance: an empty patch is invalid, an unknown séance is not_found", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const s = await seanceWithBookings(h, 0);
  const empty = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: s.occ.id,
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: {},
  });
  assert.equal(empty.ok ? "ok" : empty.code, "invalid");
  const missing = await updateOccurrence(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: "nope",
    seenStartsAt: s.occ.startsAt,
    patch: { capacity: 4 },
  });
  assert.equal(missing.ok ? "ok" : missing.code, "not_found");
});

dbTest("E7 éditeur: capacity propagates to future séances without capacity_override; version guards", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const { eventId, occurrences } = await makeEvent(db, pro, {
    ticketTypeIds: [typeId],
    capacity: 12,
    rule: {
      startDate: dayIn(pro, 2),
      startMinute: 600,
      freq: "daily",
      interval: 1,
      weekdays: null,
      monthlyBy: null,
      monthWeek: null,
      endMode: "count",
      untilDate: null,
      occurrenceCount: 3,
    },
  });
  assert.equal(occurrences.length, 3);
  const [first, second, third] = occurrences;
  must(
    await updateOccurrence(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      actorUserId: pro.userId,
      occurrenceId: second.id,
      seenStartsAt: second.startsAt,
      patch: { capacity: 5 },
    }),
    "override one séance",
  );

  const res = await editEventInPlace(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    eventId,
    seenVersion: 1,
    patch: { capacity: 20, title: "Cours du soir" },
  });
  must(res, "edit in place");
  assert.equal(res.version, 2);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, first.id)).capacity, 20);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, second.id)).capacity, 5);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, third.id)).capacity, 20);
  const [ev] = await db.select().from(event).where(eq(event.id, eventId));
  assert.equal(ev.capacity, 20);
  assert.equal(ev.title, "Cours du soir");
  assert.equal(ev.version, 2);

  const stale = await editEventInPlace(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    eventId,
    seenVersion: 1,
    patch: { capacity: 8 },
  });
  assert.equal(stale.ok ? "ok" : stale.code, "stale");
  assert.equal((await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).length, 3);
});

dbTest("E7 éditeur: links, questions and location are replaced in place; the location change is e-mailed", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement part 1 of `series.ts`**

Create `src/lib/booking/series.ts` (Write tool, whole file):

```ts
/**
 * E7 (spec § 11): « cette séance », the event editor (in place), « cette
 * séance et les suivantes » and « supprimer à partir du ». One tryAtomic
 * batch per action; series edits are version-guarded and re-plan once on stale.
 */
import { and, asc, eq, gt, inArray, sql, type SQL } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";
import { z } from "zod";

import {
  G,
  PlanError,
  RET,
  SEAT,
  lockRows,
  textArray,
  ts,
  tryAtomic,
  type ActorRef,
  type BatchCtx,
  type Db,
  type Query,
} from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { booking, customer, event, occurrence, question, ticket } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import {
  BOOKING_MODES,
  BOUNDS,
  hexColorSchema,
  idSchema,
  questionInputSchema,
  windowOverridesSchema,
  type BookingMode,
  type QuestionInput,
  type WindowOverrides,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

import { endBookings, expireSessionEffects, readEndedByAction } from "./cancel";
import { fail, failOf, syncEffects, type Effect, type EngineResult } from "./types";

/* ───────────────────────── Shared helpers ───────────────────────── */

const durationOk = (min: number) =>
  Number.isInteger(min) &&
  min >= BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.min &&
  min <= BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.max &&
  min % BOUNDS.eventDurationMin.step === 0;

/** Active (pending + confirmed) bookings of these occurrences, grouped by occurrence. */
async function activeBookingsByOccurrence(db: Db, occurrenceIds: readonly string[]): Promise<Map<string, string[]>> {
  const byOcc = new Map<string, string[]>();
  if (occurrenceIds.length === 0) return byOcc;
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: booking.id, occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId })
    .from(booking)
    .where(and(inArray(booking.occurrenceId, [...occurrenceIds]), inArray(booking.status, ["pending", "confirmed"])))
    .orderBy(booking.id);
  for (const r of rows) byOcc.set(r.occurrenceId, [...(byOcc.get(r.occurrenceId) ?? []), r.id]);
  return byOcc;
}

/** Questions as rows to insert (choice keeps its options; other types store none). */
function questionValues(q: z.infer<typeof questionInputSchema>) {
  return { label: q.label, type: q.type, options: q.type === "choice" ? q.options : null, required: q.required };
}

/* ───────────────────────── « Cette séance » ───────────────────────── */

export type OccurrencePatch = { startsAt?: Date; durationMin?: number; capacity?: number; locationOverride?: string | null };

/**
 * « Cette séance » (event séances only; an RDV is moved by cancel + rebook).
 * Batch: lock; G(scheduled ∧ event séance ∧ starts_at = seen, 'changed');
 * optional E6a on unenrollBookingIds (end, G, SEAT(o, 0)); UPDATE occurrence
 * (time_detached + changed_at when date/time/duration change; capacity =
 * GREATEST(cap, seats_taken) + capacity_override; location_override); G(the
 * row has the planned times, 'changed'); RET(pro) when bookings ended.
 */
export async function updateOccurrence(
  db: Db,
  input: {
    professionalId: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    occurrenceId: string;
    seenStartsAt: Date;
    patch: OccurrencePatch;
    unenrollBookingIds?: string[];
  },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ timeChanged: boolean }>> {
  const { patch } = input;
  const unenroll = [...new Set(input.unenrollBookingIds ?? [])];
  if (patch.durationMin !== undefined && !durationOk(patch.durationMin)) return fail("invalid", "Durée invalide.");
  if (
    patch.capacity !== undefined &&
    (!Number.isInteger(patch.capacity) || patch.capacity < BOUNDS.capacity.min || patch.capacity > BOUNDS.capacity.max)
  ) {
    return fail("invalid", "Capacité invalide.");
  }
  if (patch.startsAt !== undefined && Number.isNaN(patch.startsAt.getTime())) return fail("invalid");
  const location = patch.locationOverride === undefined ? undefined : patch.locationOverride?.trim() || null;
  if (location && location.length > BOUNDS.location) return fail("invalid", "Lieu trop long.");
  const hasPatch =
    patch.startsAt !== undefined || patch.durationMin !== undefined || patch.capacity !== undefined || location !== undefined;
  if (!hasPatch && unenroll.length === 0) return fail("invalid", "Rien à modifier.");

  const outcome = { timeChanged: false, locationChanged: false };
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [o] = await db
      .select({
        id: occurrence.id,
        eventId: occurrence.eventId,
        status: occurrence.status,
        startsAt: occurrence.startsAt,
        endsAt: occurrence.endsAt,
        locationOverride: occurrence.locationOverride,
        eventLocation: event.location,
      })
      .from(occurrence)
      .leftJoin(event, eq(event.id, occurrence.eventId))
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, input.occurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!o) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    if (o.eventId === null) {
      throw new PlanError("invalid", "Un rendez-vous se déplace en l'annulant puis en le réservant à nouveau.");
    }
    if (o.status !== "scheduled" || o.startsAt.getTime() !== input.seenStartsAt.getTime()) throw new PlanError("changed");
    if (unenroll.length > 0) {
      const found = await db
        .select({ id: booking.id, status: booking.status })
        .from(booking)
        .where(and(inArray(booking.id, unenroll), eq(booking.occurrenceId, o.id)));
      if (found.length !== unenroll.length) throw new PlanError("invalid");
      if (found.some((b) => b.status !== "pending" && b.status !== "confirmed")) throw new PlanError("changed");
    }

    const newStarts = patch.startsAt ?? o.startsAt;
    const durationMs = patch.durationMin !== undefined ? patch.durationMin * 60_000 : o.endsAt.getTime() - o.startsAt.getTime();
    const newEnds = new Date(newStarts.getTime() + durationMs);
    const timeChanged = newStarts.getTime() !== o.startsAt.getTime() || newEnds.getTime() !== o.endsAt.getTime();
    outcome.timeChanged = timeChanged;
    const oldPlace = o.locationOverride ?? o.eventLocation;
    const newPlace = (location === undefined ? o.locationOverride : location) ?? o.eventLocation;
    outcome.locationChanged = oldPlace !== newPlace;

    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const by: ActorRef = { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId };
      const sets: SQL[] = [sql`updated_at = ${ts(now)}`];
      if (timeChanged) {
        sets.push(
          sql`starts_at = ${ts(newStarts)}`,
          sql`ends_at = ${ts(newEnds)}`,
          sql`block_start = ${ts(newStarts)}`,
          sql`block_end = ${ts(newEnds)}`,
          sql`time_detached = true`,
          sql`changed_at = ${ts(now)}`,
        );
      }
      if (patch.capacity !== undefined) {
        sets.push(sql`capacity = greatest(${patch.capacity}::int, seats_taken)`, sql`capacity_override = true`);
      }
      if (location !== undefined) sets.push(sql`location_override = ${location}`);

      const queries: Query[] = [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [o.id]),
        G(
          db,
          sql`exists (select 1 from occurrence where id = ${o.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}
            and status = 'scheduled' and event_id is not null and starts_at = ${ts(input.seenStartsAt)})`,
          "changed",
        ),
      ];
      if (unenroll.length > 0) {
        queries.push(
          endBookings(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, scope: { occurrenceId: o.id, bookingIds: unenroll }, by }),
          G(
            db,
            sql`(select count(*)::int from booking where ended_action_id = ${aid} and id = any(${textArray(unenroll)})) = ${unenroll.length}::int`,
            "changed",
          ),
          SEAT(c, o.id, 0),
        );
      }
      queries.push(
        db.execute(sql`update occurrence set ${sql.join(sets, sql`, `)}
          where id = ${o.id} and status = 'scheduled' and event_id is not null`),
        G(
          db,
          sql`exists (select 1 from occurrence where id = ${o.id} and status = 'scheduled'
            and starts_at = ${ts(newStarts)} and ends_at = ${ts(newEnds)})`,
          "changed",
        ),
      );
      if (unenroll.length > 0) queries.push(RET(c, by));
      return queries;
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);

  const effects: Effect[] = [];
  if (unenroll.length > 0) {
    const ended = await readEndedByAction(db, res.aid);
    for (const b of ended) effects.push({ type: "email.booking_cancelled", bookingId: b.bookingId, by: "pro" });
    effects.push(...expireSessionEffects(ended));
  }
  if (outcome.timeChanged || outcome.locationChanged) {
    const bookingIds = (await activeBookingsByOccurrence(db, [input.occurrenceId])).get(input.occurrenceId) ?? [];
    if (bookingIds.length > 0) effects.push({ type: "email.occurrence_changed", occurrenceId: input.occurrenceId, bookingIds });
  }
  effects.push(...syncEffects([input.occurrenceId]));
  return { ok: true, effects, timeChanged: outcome.timeChanged };
}

/** Move preview: confirmed ticket bookings whose ticket is no longer valid at newStartsAt (expires_at ≤ newStartsAt). */
export async function bookingsExpiringBefore(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; occurrenceId: string; newStartsAt: Date },
): Promise<{ bookingId: string; customerName: string; expiresAt: Date }[]> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ bookingId: booking.id, customerName: customer.name, expiresAt: ticket.expiresAt })
    .from(booking)
    .innerJoin(customer, eq(customer.id, booking.customerId))
    .innerJoin(ticket, eq(ticket.bookingId, booking.id))
    .where(
      and(
        eq(booking.occurrenceId, input.occurrenceId),
        eq(booking.professionalId, input.professionalId),
        eq(booking.status, "confirmed"),
        sql`${ticket.expiresAt} is not null and ${ticket.expiresAt} <= ${ts(input.newStartsAt)}`,
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(asc(customer.name), asc(booking.id));
  return rows.flatMap((r) => (r.expiresAt ? [{ bookingId: r.bookingId, customerName: r.customerName, expiresAt: r.expiresAt }] : []));
}

/* ───────────────────────── Event editor (in place) ───────────────────────── */

export type EventInPlacePatch = {
  title?: string;
  description?: string | null;
  location?: string | null;
  color?: string;
  isPrivate?: boolean;
  bookingMode?: BookingMode;
  ticketTypeIds?: string[];
  questions?: QuestionInput[];
  overrides?: WindowOverrides;
  capacity?: number;
};

const nullableText = (max: number) =>
  z
    .string()
    .trim()
    .max(max)
    .nullable()
    .transform((v) => (v ? v : null));

const inPlaceSchema = z.object({
  title: z.string().trim().min(1).max(BOUNDS.title).optional(),
  description: nullableText(BOUNDS.description).optional(),
  location: nullableText(BOUNDS.location).optional(),
  color: hexColorSchema.optional(),
  isPrivate: z.boolean().optional(),
  bookingMode: z.enum(BOOKING_MODES).optional(),
  ticketTypeIds: z.array(idSchema).max(50).optional(),
  questions: z.array(questionInputSchema).max(BOUNDS.questions).optional(),
  overrides: windowOverridesSchema.optional(),
  capacity: z.number().int().min(BOUNDS.capacity.min).max(BOUNDS.capacity.max).optional(),
});

/**
 * Event editor, in place on ONE segment: UPDATE event … version + 1,
 * last_action_id = aid WHERE id AND version = seen; G(last_action_id = aid,
 * 'stale'); links, questions and capacity propagation (future scheduled
 * séances without capacity_override: GREATEST(cap, seats_taken)) in the same batch.
 */
export async function editEventInPlace(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; eventId: string; seenVersion: number; patch: EventInPlacePatch },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ version: number }>> {
  const parsed = inPlaceSchema.safeParse(input.patch);
  if (!parsed.success) return fail("invalid", parsed.error.issues[0]?.message);
  const p = parsed.data;
  const typeIds = p.ticketTypeIds === undefined ? undefined : [...new Set(p.ticketTypeIds)];

  const outcome = { locationChanged: false, describedChanged: false };
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [ev] = await db
      .select({ id: event.id, location: event.location, title: event.title, description: event.description, archivedAt: event.archivedAt })
      .from(event)
      .where(and(eq(event.id, input.eventId), eq(event.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!ev || ev.archivedAt) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    outcome.locationChanged = p.location !== undefined && p.location !== ev.location;
    outcome.describedChanged =
      outcome.locationChanged ||
      (p.title !== undefined && p.title !== ev.title) ||
      (p.description !== undefined && p.description !== ev.description);

    const existing = p.questions
      ? await db.select({ id: question.id }).from(question).where(eq(question.eventId, ev.id))
      : [];
    const existingIds = new Set(existing.map((q) => q.id));
    const questions = (p.questions ?? []).map((q, position) => {
      if (q.id !== undefined && !existingIds.has(q.id)) throw new PlanError("invalid", "Question inconnue.");
      return { ...q, position, newId: q.id ?? nanoid() };
    });
    const keptIds = questions.filter((q) => q.id !== undefined).map((q) => q.newId);

    return (aid) => {
      const sets: SQL[] = [
        sql`version = version + 1`,
        sql`last_action_id = ${aid}`,
        sql`updated_at = ${ts(now)}`,
      ];
      if (p.title !== undefined) sets.push(sql`title = ${p.title}`);
      if (p.description !== undefined) sets.push(sql`description = ${p.description}`);
      if (p.location !== undefined) sets.push(sql`location = ${p.location}`);
      if (p.color !== undefined) sets.push(sql`color = ${p.color}`);
      if (p.isPrivate !== undefined) sets.push(sql`is_private = ${p.isPrivate}`);
      if (p.bookingMode !== undefined) sets.push(sql`booking_mode = ${p.bookingMode}::booking_mode`);
      if (p.capacity !== undefined) sets.push(sql`capacity = ${p.capacity}::int`);
      if (p.overrides !== undefined) {
        sets.push(
          sql`booking_horizon_days = ${p.overrides.bookingHorizonDays}::int`,
          sql`min_notice_minutes = ${p.overrides.minNoticeMinutes}::int`,
          sql`cancel_deadline_hours = ${p.overrides.cancelDeadlineHours}::int`,
        );
      }
      const queries: Query[] = [
        db.execute(sql`update event set ${sql.join(sets, sql`, `)}
          where id = ${ev.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and version = ${input.seenVersion}::int
            and archived_at is null`),
        G(db, sql`(select last_action_id from event where id = ${ev.id}) = ${aid}`, "stale"),
      ];
      if (typeIds !== undefined) {
        queries.push(db.execute(sql`delete from event_ticket_type where event_id = ${ev.id}`));
        if (typeIds.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              insert into event_ticket_type (event_id, ticket_type_id, professional_id, created_at)
              select ${ev.id}, id, ${input.professionalId}, ${ts(now)} from ticket_type
              where id = any(${textArray(typeIds)}) and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}`),
          );
        }
        queries.push(
          G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from event_ticket_type where event_id = ${ev.id}) = ${typeIds.length}::int`, "invalid"),
        );
      }
      if (p.questions !== undefined) {
        queries.push(
          db.execute(sql`delete from question where event_id = ${ev.id} and not (id = any(${textArray(keptIds)}))`),
        );
        for (const q of questions.filter((x) => x.id !== undefined)) {
          const v = questionValues(q);
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              update question set label = ${v.label}, type = ${v.type}::question_type,
                options = ${v.options ? textArray(v.options) : null}, required = ${v.required},
                position = ${q.position}::int, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
              where id = ${q.newId} and event_id = ${ev.id}`),
          );
        }
        const fresh = questions.filter((x) => x.id === undefined);
        if (fresh.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.insert(question).values(
              fresh.map((q) => ({
                id: q.newId,
                professionalId: input.professionalId,
                eventId: ev.id,
                ...questionValues(q),
                position: q.position,
                createdAt: now,
                updatedAt: now,
              })),
            ),
          );
        }
        queries.push(
          G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from question where event_id = ${ev.id}) = ${questions.length}::int`, "stale"),
        );
      }
      if (p.capacity !== undefined) {
        const propagated = sql`event_id = ${ev.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and status = 'scheduled'
          and starts_at > ${ts(now)} and not capacity_override`;
        queries.push(
          // Lock first, id asc (event → occurrence): a fan-out UPDATE alone would lock in scan order
          // and could deadlock with sweepHolds, E6c or moveAttendee.
          db.execute(sql`select id from occurrence where ${propagated} order by id for update`),
          db.execute(sql`
            update occurrence set capacity = greatest(${p.capacity}::int, seats_taken), updated_at = ${ts(now)}
            where ${propagated}`),
        );
      }
      return queries;
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);

  const effects: Effect[] = [];
  if (outcome.describedChanged) {
    const future = await db
      .select({ id: occurrence.id, locationOverride: occurrence.locationOverride })
      .from(occurrence)
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.eventId, input.eventId), eq(occurrence.status, "scheduled"), gt(occurrence.startsAt, now)));
    const byOcc = await activeBookingsByOccurrence(
      db,
      future.map((o) => o.id),
    );
    if (outcome.locationChanged) {
      for (const o of future) {
        const bookingIds = byOcc.get(o.id) ?? [];
        if (o.locationOverride === null && bookingIds.length > 0) {
          effects.push({ type: "email.occurrence_changed", occurrenceId: o.id, bookingIds });
        }
      }
    }
    effects.push(...syncEffects([...byOcc.keys()]));
  }
  return { ok: true, effects, version: input.seenVersion + 1 };
}
```

```ts
export type SeriesEditInput = { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; eventId: string; seenVersion: number; fromDate: DateKey; rule: RecurrenceRuleInput; durationMin: number; orphans: "keep" | "cancel" };
export function previewSeriesEdit(db: Db, input: Omit<SeriesEditInput, "actorUserId" | "seenVersion" | "orphans">, now?: Date): Promise<EngineResult<{ summary: SeriesEditSummary; remainingCount: number | null; inPlace: boolean }>>;
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";

import { addDaysMs, dbTest, type DbHarness } from "../../test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeType,
  occurrencesOf,
  ticketsOf,
} from "../../test/fixtures";
import { addDays, dayOfWeek } from "../civil-date";
import type { Db } from "../db/atomic";
import { event } from "../db/schema";
import { expandDates } from "../recurrence";
import type { RecurrenceRuleInput } from "../tickets";
import { registerPro } from "./claims";
import { ensureHorizon, horizonEnd } from "./occurrences";
import { deleteSeriesFrom, editSeriesFrom, previewSeriesEdit } from "./series";

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

const weekly = (startDate: string, weekdays: number[], startMinute = 600): RecurrenceRuleInput => ({
  startDate,
  startMinute,
  freq: "weekly",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays,
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "never",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: null,
});

async function getEvent(db: Db, id: string) {
  const [ev] = await db.select().from(event).where(eq(event.id, id));
  return ev;
}

/**
 * A Mon/Wed/Fri 10:00 series created 14 days ago (rows exist before d = tomorrow),
 * materialised up to (then) today + 400 = now + 386 days.
 */
async function pastSeries({ db, pro }: DbHarness, now: Date) {
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const { eventId } = await makeEvent(
    db,
    pro,
    { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], rule: weekly(dayIn(pro, -14, now), [1, 3, 5]) },
    addDaysMs(now, -14),
  );
  return { typeId, eventId, d: dayIn(pro, 1, now) };
}

const sorted = (xs: readonly string[]) => [...xs].sort();
const byStart = <T extends { startsAt: Date }>(rows: readonly T[]): T[] =>
  [...rows].sort((a, b) => a.startsAt.getTime() - b.startsAt.getTime());

dbTest(
  "T8a: a claim on a row planned for delete makes the split stale; the re-plan keeps it as an isolated séance",
  async (h) => {
    const { db, pro } = h;
    const now = new Date();
    const { eventId, d } = await pastSeries(h, now);
    const friday = (await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).find((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") >= d && dayOfWeek(o.seriesDate ?? "") === 5);
    assert.ok(friday, "a Friday séance after d");
    const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
    let bookingId = "";
    const newRule = weekly(d, [1, 3]);

    const res = await editSeriesFrom(
      db,
      {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        actorUserId: pro.userId,
        eventId,
        seenVersion: 1,
        fromDate: d,
        rule: newRule,
        durationMin: 60,
        orphans: "keep",
      },
      now,
      {
        afterPlan: async () => {
          const r = await registerPro(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: friday.id, customerId, asGuest: true });
          must(r, "claim inside afterPlan");
          bookingId = r.bookingId;
        },
      },
    );
    must(res, "split after one re-plan");
    assert.notEqual(res.eventId, eventId);
    assert.equal(res.summary.orphans, 1);

    const b = await getBooking(db, bookingId);
    assert.equal(b.status, "confirmed");
    const isolated = await getOccurrence(db, friday.id);
    assert.equal(isolated.eventId, res.eventId);
    assert.equal(isolated.timeDetached, true);
    assert.equal(isolated.status, "scheduled");

    const old = await getEvent(db, eventId);
    assert.equal(old.endMode, "until");
    assert.equal(old.untilDate, addDays(d, -1));
    assert.equal(old.version, 2);
    const fresh = await getEvent(db, res.eventId);
    assert.equal(fresh.seriesRootId, old.seriesRootId ?? old.id);

    // no phantom séance: the old segment stops before d, the new one = new-rule dates ∪ the isolated séance
    assert.ok((await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).every((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") < d));
    const expected = expandDates({ ...newRule, durationMin: 60 }, d, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
    assert.deepEqual(
      sorted((await occurrencesOf(db, res.eventId)).map((o) => o.seriesDate ?? "")),
      sorted([...expected, friday.seriesDate ?? ""]),
    );
  },
);

dbTest("T8b: ensureHorizon rows inserted after the split's plan make it stale; the re-plan absorbs them", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const now = new Date();
  const { eventId, d } = await pastSeries(h, now);
  const newRule = weekly(d, [2, 4], 720);
  const res = await editSeriesFrom(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, eventId, seenVersion: 1, fromDate: d, rule: newRule, durationMin: 60, orphans: "keep" },
    now,
    {
      afterPlan: async () => {
        const hz = await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now);
        must(hz, "ensureHorizon inside afterPlan");
        assert.ok(hz.inserted > 0, "the series was 14 days behind");
      },
    },
  );
  must(res, "split");
  assert.ok((await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).every((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") < d));
  const expected = expandDates({ ...newRule, durationMin: 60 }, d, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
  assert.deepEqual(sorted((await occurrencesOf(db, res.eventId)).map((o) => o.seriesDate ?? "")), sorted(expected));
  const again = await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now);
  must(again, "ensureHorizon after");
  assert.equal(again.inserted, 0);
});

dbTest("T8b: a split committed after ensureHorizon's plan leaves no phantom séance", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const now = new Date();
  const { eventId, d } = await pastSeries(h, now);
  const newRule = weekly(d, [2, 4], 720);
  let newEventId = "";
  const hz = await ensureHorizon(db, pro.proId, now, {
    afterPlan: async () => {
      const r = await editSeriesFrom(
        db,
        { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, eventId, seenVersion: 1, fromDate: d, rule: newRule, durationMin: 60, orphans: "keep" },
        now,
      );
      must(r, "split inside afterPlan");
      newEventId = r.eventId;
    },
  });
  must(hz, "ensureHorizon");
  assert.ok((await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).every((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") < d));
  const expected = expandDates({ ...newRule, durationMin: 60 }, d, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
  assert.deepEqual(sorted((await occurrencesOf(db, newEventId)).map((o) => o.seriesDate ?? "")), sorted(expected));
});

dbTest("E7 suivantes: without rows before d the edit happens in place (same event, version + 1)", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeId = await makeType(db, pro);
  const start = dayIn(pro, 2, now);
  const { eventId, occurrences } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], rule: weekly(start, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]) }, now);
  assert.ok(occurrences.length > 300);
  const preview = await previewSeriesEdit(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, eventId, fromDate: start, rule: weekly(start, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], 720), durationMin: 60 },
    now,
  );
  must(preview, "preview");
  assert.equal(preview.inPlace, true);
  assert.equal(preview.summary.modified, occurrences.length);

  const res = await editSeriesFrom(
    db,
    {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      actorUserId: pro.userId,
      eventId,
      seenVersion: 1,
      fromDate: start,
      rule: weekly(start, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], 720),
      durationMin: 60,
      orphans: "keep",
    },
    now,
  );
  must(res, "in place");
  assert.equal(res.eventId, eventId);
  const ev = await getEvent(db, eventId);
  assert.equal(ev.version, 2);
  assert.equal(ev.startMinute, 720);
  const rows = byStart(await occurrencesOf(db, eventId));
  assert.equal(rows.length, occurrences.length);
  const first = rows[0];
  assert.equal(first.startsAt.getTime() - occurrences[0].startsAt.getTime(), 2 * 3_600_000);
  assert.equal(first.changedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  const segments = await db.select({ id: event.id }).from(event).where(eq(event.seriesRootId, ev.seriesRootId ?? eventId));
  assert.equal(segments.length, 1);

  const stale = await editSeriesFrom(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, eventId, seenVersion: 1, fromDate: start, rule: weekly(start, [1]), durationMin: 60, orphans: "keep" },
    now,
  );
  assert.equal(stale.ok ? "ok" : stale.code, "stale");
});

dbTest("E7 supprimer à partir du: booked séances are cancelled, empty ones deleted, the series ends at d − 1", async (h) => {
  const { db, pro } = h;
  const now = new Date();
  const { typeId, eventId, d } = await pastSeries(h, now);
  const rowsAfter = byStart(await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).filter((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") >= d);
  const target = rowsAfter[0];
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeId, 1);
  const reg = await registerPro(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, occurrenceId: target.id, customerId, asGuest: false });
  must(reg, "registerPro");

  const res = await deleteSeriesFrom(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, eventId, seenVersion: 1, fromDate: d }, now);
  must(res, "delete from d");
  assert.equal(res.deletedEvent, false);
  assert.equal(res.cancelledOccurrences, 1);
  assert.equal(res.deletedOccurrences, rowsAfter.length - 1);
  assert.ok(res.effects.some((e) => e.type === "email.occurrence_cancelled" && e.occurrenceId === target.id));
  const left = (await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).filter((o) => (o.seriesDate ?? "") >= d);
  assert.deepEqual(
    left.map((o) => [o.id, o.status]),
    [[target.id, "cancelled"]],
  );
  const ev = await getEvent(db, eventId);
  assert.equal(ev.untilDate, addDays(d, -1));
  assert.equal(ev.archivedAt, null);
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, customerId);
  assert.equal(t.status, "available");
});

  const { eventId, occurrences } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeId], rule: weekly(start, [0, 3]) }, now);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const reg = await registerPro(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    occurrenceId: occurrences[0].id,
    customerId,
    asGuest: true,
  });
  must(reg, "registerPro");
  const res = await deleteSeriesFrom(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, eventId, seenVersion: 1, fromDate: start }, now);
  must(res, "delete from start");
  const ev = await getEvent(db, eventId);
  assert.equal(ev.archivedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(ev.endMode, "never");
  const rows = await occurrencesOf(db, eventId);
  assert.deepEqual(
    rows.map((o) => [o.id, o.status]),
    [[occurrences[0].id, "cancelled"]],
  );
});
```

```ts
/* ───────────────────── « Cette séance et les suivantes » ───────────────────── */

/** « Séance modifiée » e-mails + Google sync for the occurrences that still have active bookings. */
function changedEffects(byOcc: Map<string, string[]>): Effect[] {
  const effects: Effect[] = [];
  for (const [occurrenceId, bookingIds] of byOcc) {
    effects.push({ type: "email.occurrence_changed", occurrenceId, bookingIds });
  }
  return [...effects, ...syncEffects([...byOcc.keys()])];
}

export type SeriesEditInput = {
  professionalId: string;
  actorUserId: string;
  eventId: string;
  seenVersion: number;
  fromDate: DateKey;
  rule: RecurrenceRuleInput;
  durationMin: number;
  orphans: "keep" | "cancel";
};

type SeriesContext = {
  tz: string;
  ev: EventRow;
  oldRule: RecurrenceRule;
  /** rows with series_date ≥ d, as the planner wants them */
  rows: SeriesRow[];
  /** false = no row before d: the edit is applied in place */
  split: boolean;
  /** rows of the segment (all dates): used by delete */
  totalRows: number;
};

/**
 * Plan reads shared by preview, edit and delete: the pro's tz, the segment,
 * its rows ≥ d (with activity) and whether rows exist before d. Throws
 * PlanError: not_found (unknown or archived event), invalid (d before local
 * today or a row ≥ d already started).
 */
async function loadSeriesContext(
  db: Db,
  args: { professionalId: string; eventId: string; fromDate: DateKey },
  now: Date,
): Promise<SeriesContext> {
  const [pro] = await db.select({ tz: professional.timezone }).from(professional).where(eq(professional.id, args.professionalId));
  if (!pro) throw new PlanError("not_found");
  const [ev] = await db
    .select()
    .from(event)
    .where(and(eq(event.id, args.eventId), eq(event.professionalId, args.professionalId)));
  if (!ev || ev.archivedAt) throw new PlanError("not_found");
  if (!dateKeySchema.safeParse(args.fromDate).success) throw new PlanError("invalid");
  if (args.fromDate < todayKey(now, pro.tz)) {
    throw new PlanError("invalid", "La modification doit partir d'aujourd'hui ou d'une date future.");
  }

  const rows = await db
    .select({
      id: occurrence.id,
      seriesDate: occurrence.seriesDate,
      startsAt: occurrence.startsAt,
      endsAt: occurrence.endsAt,
      status: occurrence.status,
      timeDetached: occurrence.timeDetached,
      activeBookings: sql<number>`(select count(*)::int from booking b where b.occurrence_id = ${occurrence.id} and b.status in ('pending', 'confirmed'))`.mapWith(
        Number,
      ),
      ticketBookings: sql<number>`(select count(*)::int from booking b where b.occurrence_id = ${occurrence.id} and b.status = 'confirmed' and b.coverage = 'ticket')`.mapWith(
        Number,
      ),
      hasHistory: sql<boolean>`exists (select 1 from booking b where b.occurrence_id = ${occurrence.id})`,
    })
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(and(eq(occurrence.eventId, ev.id), gte(occurrence.seriesDate, args.fromDate)))
    .orderBy(asc(occurrence.seriesDate));
  if (rows.some((r) => r.startsAt.getTime() <= now.getTime())) {
    throw new PlanError("invalid", "Une séance concernée a déjà commencé.");
  }
  const [before] = await db
    .select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int`.mapWith(Number) })
    .from(occurrence)
    .where(and(eq(occurrence.eventId, ev.id), lt(occurrence.seriesDate, args.fromDate)));
  const before_ = before?.n ?? 0;

  return {
    tz: pro.tz,
    ev,
    oldRule: ruleOf(ev),
    rows: rows.map((r) => ({ ...r, seriesDate: String(r.seriesDate), hasHistory: r.hasHistory === true })),
    split: before_ > 0,
    totalRows: before_ + rows.length,
  };
}

/** Validates the new rule and runs the pure planner. */
function planEdit(
  ctx: SeriesContext,
  args: { fromDate: DateKey; rule: RecurrenceRuleInput; durationMin: number },
  now: Date,
): { plan: SeriesEditPlan; newRule: RecurrenceRule } {
  const parsed = recurrenceRuleSchema.safeParse(args.rule);
  if (!parsed.success) throw new PlanError("invalid", parsed.error.issues[0]?.message);
  if (!durationOk(args.durationMin)) throw new PlanError("invalid", "Durée invalide.");
  if (parsed.data.startDate < args.fromDate) {
    throw new PlanError("invalid", "La nouvelle règle doit commencer à la date choisie ou après.");
  }
  const newRule: RecurrenceRule = { ...parsed.data, durationMin: args.durationMin };
  const plan = planSeriesEdit({
    tz: ctx.tz,
    fromDate: args.fromDate,
    horizonEnd: horizonEnd(now, ctx.tz),
    oldRule: ctx.oldRule,
    newRule,
    rows: ctx.rows,
  });
  if (plan.retime.some((r) => r.startsAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) || plan.insert.some((r) => r.startsAt.getTime() <= now.getTime())) {
    throw new PlanError("invalid", "Une séance de la nouvelle règle serait déjà passée.");
  }
  return { plan, newRule };
}

/** Rule columns of `event` for a new rule (SET list). */
function ruleSets(r: RecurrenceRule): SQL[] {
  return [
    sql`start_date = ${r.startDate}::date`,
    sql`start_minute = ${r.startMinute}::int`,
    sql`duration_min = ${r.durationMin}::int`,
    sql`freq = ${r.freq}::recurrence_freq`,
    sql`"interval" = ${r.interval}::smallint`,
    sql`weekdays = ${r.weekdays ? sql`${sql.param(r.weekdays)}::smallint[]` : null}`,
    sql`monthly_by = ${r.monthlyBy}::monthly_by`,
    sql`month_week = ${r.monthWeek}::smallint`,
    sql`end_mode = ${r.endMode}::recurrence_end`,
    sql`until_date = ${r.untilDate}::date`,
    sql`occurrence_count = ${r.occurrenceCount}::int`,
  ];
}

/**
 * The end of the old segment at d: until = min(d − 1, its last date), unless
 * the old rule already stops before d (then its rule columns stay as they are).
 */
function endOldSegmentSets(oldRule: RecurrenceRule, fromDate: DateKey): SQL[] {
  const last = lastSeriesDate(oldRule);
  if (last !== null && last < fromDate) return [];
  const until = last === null ? addDays(fromDate, -1) : minKey(addDays(fromDate, -1), last);
  return [sql`end_mode = 'until'`, sql`until_date = ${until}::date`, sql`occurrence_count = null`];
}

/** Same reads as editSeriesFrom, no write: the preview the editor shows. */
export async function previewSeriesEdit(
  db: Db,
  input: Omit<SeriesEditInput, "actorUserId" | "seenVersion" | "orphans">,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ summary: SeriesEditSummary; remainingCount: number | null; inPlace: boolean }>> {
  try {
    const ctx = await loadSeriesContext(db, input, now);
    const { plan } = planEdit(ctx, input, now);
    return { ok: true, effects: [], summary: plan.summary, remainingCount: plan.remainingCount, inPlace: !ctx.split };
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof PlanError) return fail(err.code, err.detail);
    throw err;
  }
}

/**
 * « Cette séance et les suivantes » (spec § 11 E7, contract § 5.20 batch
 * order). Split when rows exist before d, else in place on the same event.
 * Re-plans once on stale (a concurrent claim, ensureHorizon rows, a version bump).
 */
export async function editSeriesFrom(
  db: Db,
  input: SeriesEditInput,
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<{ eventId: string; summary: SeriesEditSummary }>> {
  const committed: { plan: SeriesEditPlan | null; targetId: string } = { plan: null, targetId: "" };
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const ctx = await loadSeriesContext(db, input, now);
      const { plan, newRule } = planEdit(ctx, input, now);
      const { ev } = ctx;
      const targetId = ctx.split ? nanoid() : ev.id;
      const oldQuestions = ctx.split
        ? await db.select().from(question).where(eq(question.eventId, ev.id)).orderBy(asc(question.position))
        : [];
      const drafts: OccurrenceDraft[] = plan.insert.map((d) => ({ id: nanoid(), ...d }));
      committed.plan = plan;
      committed.targetId = targetId;

      return (aid) => {
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
        const by: ActorRef = { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId };
        const segmentRows = sql`event_id = ${ev.id} and series_date >= ${input.fromDate}::date`;
        const queries: Query[] = [];

        // 1. version guard; the old segment ends at d − 1 (split) or takes the new rule (in place)
        const sets: SQL[] = [sql`version = version + 1`, sql`last_action_id = ${aid}`, sql`updated_at = ${ts(now)}`];
        if (ctx.split) sets.push(...endOldSegmentSets(ctx.oldRule, input.fromDate));
        else sets.push(...ruleSets(newRule), sql`generated_until = ${plan.generatedUntil}::date`);
        queries.push(
          db.execute(sql`update event set ${sql.join(sets, sql`, `)}
            where id = ${ev.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and version = ${input.seenVersion}::int
              and archived_at is null`),
          G(db, sql`(select last_action_id from event where id = ${ev.id}) = ${aid}`, "stale"),
        );

        // 2. the new segment, its links and its questions (split only)
        if (ctx.split) {
          queries.push(
            db.insert(event).values({
              id: targetId,
              professionalId: input.professionalId,
              title: ev.title,
              description: ev.description,
              location: ev.location,
              color: ev.color,
              bookingMode: ev.bookingMode,
              isPrivate: ev.isPrivate,
              capacity: ev.capacity,
              durationMin: newRule.durationMin,
              startDate: newRule.startDate,
              startMinute: newRule.startMinute,
              freq: newRule.freq,
              interval: newRule.interval,
              weekdays: newRule.weekdays,
              monthlyBy: newRule.monthlyBy,
              monthWeek: newRule.monthWeek,
              endMode: newRule.endMode,
              untilDate: newRule.untilDate,
              occurrenceCount: newRule.occurrenceCount,
              bookingHorizonDays: ev.bookingHorizonDays,
              minNoticeMinutes: ev.minNoticeMinutes,
              cancelDeadlineHours: ev.cancelDeadlineHours,
              generatedUntil: plan.generatedUntil,
              seriesRootId: ev.seriesRootId ?? ev.id,
              version: 1,
              lastActionId: aid,
              createdAt: now,
              updatedAt: now,
            }),
            db.execute(sql`
              insert into event_ticket_type (event_id, ticket_type_id, professional_id, created_at)
              select ${targetId}, ticket_type_id, professional_id, ${ts(now)} from event_ticket_type where event_id = ${ev.id}`),
          );
          if (oldQuestions.length > 0) {
            queries.push(
              db.insert(question).values(
                oldQuestions.map((q) => ({
                  id: nanoid(),
                  professionalId: input.professionalId,
                  eventId: targetId,
                  label: q.label,
                  type: q.type,
                  options: q.options,
                  required: q.required,
                  position: q.position,
                  createdAt: now,
                  updatedAt: now,
                })),
              ),
            );
          }
        }

        // 3. lock the rows ≥ d; 4. reparent them (split), then the planned-row-count guard
        queries.push(db.execute(sql`select id from occurrence where ${segmentRows} order by id for update`));
        if (ctx.split) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`update occurrence set event_id = ${targetId}, updated_at = ${ts(now)} where ${segmentRows}`),
            G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where event_id = ${targetId}) = ${ctx.rows.length}::int`, "stale"),
          );
        } else {
          queries.push(
            G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where ${segmentRows}) = ${ctx.rows.length}::int`, "stale"),
          );
        }

        // 5. retime
        if (plan.retime.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              update occurrence o set starts_at = v.s, ends_at = v.e, block_start = v.s, block_end = v.e,
                changed_at = case when o.starts_at <> v.s or o.ends_at <> v.e then ${ts(now)} else o.changed_at end,
                capacity = case when o.capacity_override then o.capacity else greatest(${ev.capacity}::int, o.seats_taken) end,
                updated_at = ${ts(now)}
              from unnest(${textArray(plan.retime.map((r) => r.id))}, ${tsArray(plan.retime.map((r) => r.startsAt))},
                ${tsArray(plan.retime.map((r) => r.endsAt))}) as v(id, s, e)
              where o.id = v.id and o.event_id = ${targetId}`),
          );
        }

        // 6. delete the never-booked rows whose date disappears
        if (plan.delete.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              delete from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(plan.delete)}) and seats_taken = 0
                and not exists (select 1 from booking b where b.occurrence_id = occurrence.id)`),
            G(db, sql`not exists (select 1 from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(plan.delete)}))`, "stale"),
          );
        }

        // 7. empty rows with history → cancelled; booked orphans → isolated, or E6b
        if (plan.cancelEmpty.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              update occurrence set status = 'cancelled', cancelled_at = coalesce(cancelled_at, ${ts(now)}), updated_at = ${ts(now)}
              where id = any(${textArray(plan.cancelEmpty)}) and seats_taken = 0`),
            G(
              db,
              sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(plan.cancelEmpty)}) and status = 'cancelled') = ${plan.cancelEmpty.length}::int`,
              "stale",
            ),
          );
        }
        if (plan.orphanBooked.length > 0 && input.orphans === "keep") {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`update occurrence set time_detached = true, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
              where id = any(${textArray(plan.orphanBooked)})`),
          );
        }
        if (plan.orphanBooked.length > 0 && input.orphans === "cancel") {
          queries.push(
            cancelOccurrencesQuery(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, occurrenceIds: plan.orphanBooked }),
            G(
              db,
              sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(plan.orphanBooked)}) and status = 'cancelled') = ${plan.orphanBooked.length}::int`,
              "stale",
            ),
            endBookings(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, scope: { occurrenceIds: plan.orphanBooked }, by }),
          );
        }

        // 8. new dates (generated_until was set with the segment in step 1 or 2)
        if (drafts.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            insertOccurrences(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, eventId: targetId, capacity: ev.capacity, drafts }),
          );
        }

        // 9. tickets of cancelled orphans come back
        if (plan.orphanBooked.length > 0 && input.orphans === "cancel") queries.push(RET(c, by));
        return queries;
      };
    },
    { replanOnStale: true, afterPlan: opts.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const plan = committed.plan;
  if (!plan) throw new Error("editSeriesFrom: no committed plan");

  const changedIds = plan.retime.filter((r) => r.timeChanged).map((r) => r.id);
  const effects = changedEffects(await activeBookingsByOccurrence(db, changedIds));
  if (plan.orphanBooked.length > 0 && input.orphans === "cancel") {
    effects.push(...(await occurrenceCancelledEffects(db, res.aid, plan.orphanBooked)).effects);
  }
  return { ok: true, effects, eventId: committed.targetId, summary: plan.summary };
}

/* ───────────────────────── « Supprimer à partir du » ───────────────────────── */

/**
 * Never-booked event: DELETE event (cascade; a 23503 from a booking taken
 * meanwhile → stale → re-plan). Else: version-guarded end (until = d − 1, or
 * archived_at when d ≤ start_date), lock rows ≥ d, G(row count), delete the
 * rows without history, cancel (E6b) the scheduled ones with history, RET.
 */
export async function deleteSeriesFrom(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; eventId: string; seenVersion: number; fromDate: DateKey },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ deletedEvent: boolean; cancelledOccurrences: number; deletedOccurrences: number }>> {
  const outcome = { deletedEvent: false, toCancel: [] as string[], deleted: 0 };
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      const ctx = await loadSeriesContext(db, input, now);
      const { ev } = ctx;
      const booked = await db.execute<{ yes: boolean }>(
        sql`select exists (select 1 from booking b join occurrence o on o.id = b.occurrence_id where o.event_id = ${ev.id}) as yes`,
      );

      if (booked.rows[0]?.yes !== true) {
        Object.assign(outcome, { deletedEvent: true, toCancel: [], deleted: ctx.totalRows });
        return () => [
          db.execute(sql`delete from event where id = ${ev.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}
            and version = ${input.seenVersion}::int`),
          G(db, sql`not exists (select 1 from event where id = ${ev.id})`, "stale"),
        ];
      }

      const del = ctx.rows.filter((r) => !r.hasHistory).map((r) => r.id);
      const toCancel = ctx.rows.filter((r) => r.hasHistory && r.status === "scheduled").map((r) => r.id);
      Object.assign(outcome, { deletedEvent: false, toCancel, deleted: del.length });
      return (aid) => {
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
        const by: ActorRef = { actor: "pro", userId: input.actorUserId };
        const segmentRows = sql`event_id = ${ev.id} and series_date >= ${input.fromDate}::date`;
        const end = input.fromDate <= ev.startDate ? [sql`archived_at = ${ts(now)}`] : endOldSegmentSets(ctx.oldRule, input.fromDate);
        const queries: Query[] = [
          db.execute(sql`update event set ${sql.join(
            [sql`version = version + 1`, sql`last_action_id = ${aid}`, sql`updated_at = ${ts(now)}`, ...end],
            sql`, `,
          )} where id = ${ev.id} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and version = ${input.seenVersion}::int`),
          G(db, sql`(select last_action_id from event where id = ${ev.id}) = ${aid}`, "stale"),
          db.execute(sql`select id from occurrence where ${segmentRows} order by id for update`),
          G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where ${segmentRows}) = ${ctx.rows.length}::int`, "stale"),
        ];
        if (del.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            db.execute(sql`
              delete from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(del)}) and seats_taken = 0
                and not exists (select 1 from booking b where b.occurrence_id = occurrence.id)`),
            G(db, sql`not exists (select 1 from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(del)}))`, "stale"),
          );
        }
        if (toCancel.length > 0) {
          queries.push(
            cancelOccurrencesQuery(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, occurrenceIds: toCancel }),
            G(
              db,
              sql`(select count(*)::int from occurrence where id = any(${textArray(toCancel)}) and status = 'cancelled') = ${toCancel.length}::int`,
              "stale",
            ),
            endBookings(c, { professionalId: input.professionalId, scope: { occurrenceIds: toCancel }, by }),
            RET(c, by),
          );
        }
        return queries;
      };
    },
    { replanOnStale: true },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  const effects =
    outcome.toCancel.length > 0 ? (await occurrenceCancelledEffects(db, res.aid, outcome.toCancel)).effects : [];
  return {
    ok: true,
    effects,
    deletedEvent: outcome.deletedEvent,
    cancelledOccurrences: outcome.toCancel.length,
    deletedOccurrences: outcome.deleted,
  };
}
```

```ts
export type TicketSelection = { ticketIds: string[] } | { ticketTypeId: string; quantity: number };
export function convertTickets(db: Db, input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection; toTicketTypeId: string }, now?: Date, opts?: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> }): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>>;
export function closeTickets(db: Db, input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection; reason: TicketCloseReason; note?: string | null }, now?: Date): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>>;
export function pointTickets(db: Db, input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection; note?: string | null }, now?: Date): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>>;
export function unpointTickets(db: Db, input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection }, now?: Date): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>>;
export function moveAttendee(db: Db, input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; bookingId: string; targetOccurrenceId: string; force: boolean }, now?: Date): Promise<EngineResult<{ fromOccurrenceId: string }>>;
export function loadTypeFacts(db: Db, professionalId: string, now?: Date): Promise<TypeFacts[]>;
```
