```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { addMinutes, dbTest, inParallel } from "../../test/db";
import {
  dayIn,
  getBooking,
  getOccurrence,
  giveTickets,
  makeCustomer,
  makeEvent,
  makeOffer,
  makeType,
  movementsOf,
  ticketsOf,
} from "../../test/fixtures";
import { instantAt } from "../civil-date";
import { cancelByClient } from "./cancel";
import { setTicketTypeArchived } from "./catalog";
import { bookWithHeldTicket } from "./claims";
import { fulfillPurchase, startPurchase } from "./fulfillment";
import {
  closeTickets,
  convertTickets,
  loadTypeFacts,
  moveAttendee,
  pointTickets,
  unpointTickets,
} from "./wallet";

type Outcome = { ok: boolean; code?: string };

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

const label = (s: PromiseSettledResult<Outcome>): string =>
  s.status === "rejected" ? `threw: ${String(s.reason)}` : s.value.ok ? "ok" : String(s.value.code);

dbTest("T9a: convert and spend race for the last ticket: exactly one takes effect", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const typeB = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [occ],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA] });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1);

  const settled = await inParallel<Outcome>([
    () =>
      convertTickets(db, {
        professionalId: pro.proId,
        actorUserId: pro.userId,
        customerId,
        selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
        toTicketTypeId: typeB,
      }),
    () => bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: occ.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: occ.startsAt, actorUserId: null }),
  ]);
  const [conv, spend] = settled.map(label);
  assert.ok((conv === "ok" && spend === "no_ticket") || (conv === "no_ticket" && spend === "ok"), `${conv} / ${spend}`);
  const kinds = (await movementsOf(db, ticketId)).map((m) => m.kind);
  assert.equal(kinds.filter((k) => k === "convert" || k === "spend").length, 1, kinds.join());
  const [t] = await ticketsOf(db, customerId);
  if (conv === "ok") {
    assert.equal(t.ticketTypeId, typeB);
    assert.equal(t.status, "available");
    assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, occ.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
  } else {
    assert.equal(t.ticketTypeId, typeA);
    assert.equal(t.status, "booked");
  }
});

dbTest("T9b: two identical converts at once: one converts, the other finds no ticket", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const typeB = await makeType(db, pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const [ticketId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1);
  const convert = () =>
    convertTickets(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      actorUserId: pro.userId,
      customerId,
      selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
      toTicketTypeId: typeB,
    });
  const outcomes = (await inParallel<Outcome>([convert, convert])).map(label).sort();
  assert.deepEqual(outcomes, ["no_ticket", "ok"]);
  const moves = await movementsOf(db, ticketId);
  assert.equal(moves.filter((m) => m.kind === "convert").length, 1);
  const res = await db.execute<{ from_ticket_type_id: string; ticket_type_id: string }>(
    sql`select from_ticket_type_id, ticket_type_id from ticket_movement where ticket_id = ${ticketId} and kind = 'convert'`,
  );
  assert.deepEqual(res.rows[0], { from_ticket_type_id: typeA, ticket_type_id: typeB });
});

dbTest("T9c: a paid ticket cannot be « retiré »; it can be closed with a reason", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const offerId = await makeOffer(db, pro, typeA);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const purchase = await startPurchase(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, offerId, customerId });
  must(purchase, "startPurchase");
  const paid = await fulfillPurchase(db, purchase.purchaseId, { sessionId: null, paymentIntentId: null, livemode: false });
  assert.equal(paid.outcome, "fulfilled");
  const [paidId] = paid.ticketIds;
  const [offeredId] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1);

  const retirePaid = await closeTickets(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    customerId,
    selection: { ticketIds: [paidId] },
    reason: "retire",
  });
  assert.equal(retirePaid.ok ? "ok" : retirePaid.code, "retire_paid");

  const retireByType = await closeTickets(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    customerId,
    selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
    reason: "retire",
  });
  must(retireByType, "retire by type");
  assert.deepEqual(retireByType.ticketIds, [offeredId]);
  const none = await closeTickets(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    customerId,
    selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
    reason: "retire",
  });
  assert.equal(none.ok ? "ok" : none.code, "no_ticket");

  const noNote = await closeTickets(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    customerId,
    selection: { ticketIds: [paidId] },
    reason: "autre",
  });
  assert.equal(noNote.ok ? "ok" : noNote.code, "invalid");
  must(
    await closeTickets(db, {
      professionalId: pro.proId,
      actorUserId: pro.userId,
      customerId,
      selection: { ticketIds: [paidId] },
      reason: "rembourse",
      note: "Remboursé en espèces",
    }),
    "close paid",
  );
  const closed = (await ticketsOf(db, customerId)).find((t) => t.id === paidId);
  assert.equal(closed?.status, "closed");
  assert.equal(closed?.closedReason, "rembourse");
  assert.equal(closed?.closedNote, "Remboursé en espèces");
  const kinds = (await movementsOf(db, paidId)).map((m) => `${m.kind}:${m.toStatus}`);
  assert.deepEqual(kinds, ["purchase:available", "close:closed"]);
});

dbTest("Pointer / annuler le pointage: available ↔ used, an expired ticket goes back to expired", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const now = new Date();
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1);
  const [shortLived] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: addMinutes(now, 60) });

  const pointed = await pointTickets(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, customerId, selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 }, note: "Cours rattrapé au parc" },
    now,
  );
  must(pointed, "point");
  assert.deepEqual(pointed.ticketIds, [shortLived], "the ticket expiring first is used first");
  const reason = await db.execute<{ reason: string | null }>(
    sql`select reason from ticket_movement where ticket_id = ${shortLived} and kind = 'pointage'`,
  );
  assert.equal(reason.rows[0]?.reason, "Cours rattrapé au parc");

  const later = addMinutes(now, 120);
  const unpointed = await unpointTickets(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, customerId, selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 } },
    later,
  );
  must(unpointed, "unpoint");
  const t = (await ticketsOf(db, customerId)).find((x) => x.id === shortLived);
  assert.equal(t?.status, "expired");
  const last = (await movementsOf(db, shortLived)).at(-1);
  assert.equal(last?.kind, "unpoint");
  assert.equal(last?.toStatus, "expired");

  const nothing = await unpointTickets(db, {
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    actorUserId: pro.userId,
    customerId,
    selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
  });
  assert.equal(nothing.ok ? "ok" : nothing.code, "no_ticket");
});

dbTest("Déplacer un inscrit: eligibility, expiry (force) and seats on both séances", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const typeB = await makeType(db, pro);
  const day3 = dayIn(pro, 3);
  const day5 = dayIn(pro, 5);
  const {
    occurrences: [src],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: day3 } });
  const {
    occurrences: [sameType],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: day5 } });
  const {
    occurrences: [otherType],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeB], rule: { startDate: day5, startMinute: 840 } });
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: instantAt(dayIn(pro, 4), 0, pro.tz) });
  const b = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: src.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: src.startsAt, actorUserId: null });
  must(b, "book");
  const move = (targetOccurrenceId: string, force: boolean) =>
    moveAttendee(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, bookingId: b.bookingId, targetOccurrenceId, force });

  const wrongType = await move(otherType.id, true);
  assert.equal(wrongType.ok ? "ok" : wrongType.code, "not_eligible");
  const expires = await move(sameType.id, false);
  assert.equal(expires.ok ? "ok" : expires.code, "ticket_expires_before");

  const now = new Date();
  const res = await moveAttendee(
    db,
    { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, bookingId: b.bookingId, targetOccurrenceId: sameType.id, force: true },
    now,
  );
  must(res, "forced move");
  assert.equal(res.fromOccurrenceId, src.id);
  assert.deepEqual(res.effects, [
    { type: "email.attendee_moved", bookingId: b.bookingId, fromOccurrenceId: src.id },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: src.id },
    { type: "google.sync_occurrence", occurrenceId: sameType.id },
  ]);
  const moved = await getBooking(db, b.bookingId);
  assert.equal(moved.occurrenceId, sameType.id);
  assert.equal(moved.movedAt?.getTime(), now.getTime());
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, src.id)).seatsTaken, 0);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, sameType.id)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("Déplacer un inscrit: a full target is `full`, a target already booked by the client is `already_booked`", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const {
    occurrences: [src],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, 3) } });
  const {
    occurrences: [full],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], capacity: 1, rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, 4) } });
  const {
    occurrences: [twice],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, 5) } });
  const c1 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const c2 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, c1, typeA, 2);
  await giveTickets(db, pro, c2, typeA, 1);
  const book = async (customerId: string, o: { id: string; startsAt: Date }) => {
    const r = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, occurrenceId: o.id, customerId, seenStartsAt: o.startsAt, actorUserId: null });
    must(r, "book");
    return r.bookingId;
  };
  const b1 = await book(c1, src);
  await book(c1, twice);
  await book(c2, full);

  const toFull = await moveAttendee(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, bookingId: b1, targetOccurrenceId: full.id, force: false });
  assert.equal(toFull.ok ? "ok" : toFull.code, "full");
  const toTwice = await moveAttendee(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, actorUserId: pro.userId, bookingId: b1, targetOccurrenceId: twice.id, force: false });
  assert.equal(toTwice.ok ? "ok" : toTwice.code, "already_booked");
  assert.equal((await getBooking(db, b1)).occurrenceId, src.id);
  assert.equal((await getOccurrence(db, src.id)).seatsTaken, 1);
});

dbTest("loadTypeFacts: archived, RDV, linked to an upcoming séance, orphan", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const linked = await makeType(db, pro);
  const rdv = await makeType(db, pro, { rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 });
  const orphan = await makeType(db, pro);
  const archived = await makeType(db, pro);
  await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [linked, archived] });
  must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: pro.proId, ticketTypeId: archived, archived: true }), "archive");

  const facts = new Map((await loadTypeFacts(db, pro.proId)).map((f) => [f.ticketTypeId, f]));
  assert.deepEqual(facts.get(linked), { ticketTypeId: linked, archived: false, rdvEnabled: false, linkedToUpcomingEvent: true });
  assert.deepEqual(facts.get(rdv), { ticketTypeId: rdv, archived: false, rdvEnabled: true, linkedToUpcomingEvent: false });
  assert.deepEqual(facts.get(orphan), { ticketTypeId: orphan, archived: false, rdvEnabled: false, linkedToUpcomingEvent: false });
  assert.equal(facts.get(archived)?.archived, true);
});

});
```

```ts
/* ───────────────────── Task 29: ticket actions + move (E8) ───────────────────── */

/** Which tickets an action targets: explicit ids, or N of a type picked in JS. */
export type TicketSelection = { ticketIds: string[] } | { ticketTypeId: string; quantity: number };

type E8Ticket = {
  id: string;
  ticketTypeId: string;
  status: TicketStatus;
  expiresAt: Date | null;
  purchaseId: string | null;
  createdAt: Date;
  statusChangedAt: Date;
};

type E8Spec = {
  professionalId: string;
  customerId: string;
  selection: TicketSelection;
  now: Date;
  /** JS mirror of the statement's expected state */
  qualifies: (t: E8Ticket) => boolean;
  order: (tickets: E8Ticket[]) => E8Ticket[];
  /** ONE statement: WITH u AS (UPDATE ticket … RETURNING …) INSERT INTO ticket_movement … SELECT … FROM u */
  statement: (c: BatchCtx, picked: E8Ticket[]) => Query;
  /** runs at the start of every plan (re-plan included) */
  check?: () => Promise<void>;
  extraGuards?: (c: BatchCtx) => Query[];
  afterPlan?: () => Promise<void>;
};

const e8Valid = (t: E8Ticket, at: Date) => t.expiresAt === null || t.expiresAt.getTime() > at.getTime();

/** zod-free validation of a selection (1..BOUNDS.walletQuantity tickets). */
function e8SelectionOk(selection: TicketSelection): boolean {
  if ("ticketIds" in selection) {
    const n = new Set(selection.ticketIds).size;
    return n >= 1 && n <= BOUNDS.walletQuantity && n === selection.ticketIds.length;
  }
  return Number.isInteger(selection.quantity) && selection.quantity >= 1 && selection.quantity <= BOUNDS.walletQuantity;
}

/**
 * The E8 pattern (spec § 11 E8): pick ids in JS (plan), one transition +
 * movement statement, G(movements of aid = N, 'stale'); re-plans once on
 * stale; fewer than N qualifying tickets → no_ticket.
 */
async function e8Run(db: Db, spec: E8Spec): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  if (!e8SelectionOk(spec.selection)) return fail("invalid");
  const picked: { ids: string[] } = { ids: [] };
  const res = await tryAtomic(
    db,
    async () => {
      if (spec.check) await spec.check();
      const [cust] = await db
        .select({ id: customer.id })
        .from(customer)
        .where(and(eq(customer.id, spec.customerId), eq(customer.professionalId, spec.professionalId)));
      if (!cust) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      const sel = spec.selection;
      const rows: E8Ticket[] = await db
        .select({
          id: ticket.id,
          ticketTypeId: ticket.ticketTypeId,
          status: ticket.status,
          expiresAt: ticket.expiresAt,
          purchaseId: ticket.purchaseId,
          createdAt: ticket.createdAt,
          statusChangedAt: ticket.statusChangedAt,
        })
        .from(ticket)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(ticket.professionalId, spec.professionalId),
            eq(ticket.customerId, spec.customerId),
            "ticketIds" in sel ? inArray(ticket.id, sel.ticketIds) : eq(ticket.ticketTypeId, sel.ticketTypeId),
          ),
        );
      if ("ticketIds" in sel && rows.length !== sel.ticketIds.length) throw new PlanError("not_found");
      const want = "ticketIds" in sel ? sel.ticketIds.length : sel.quantity;
      const chosen = spec.order(rows.filter(spec.qualifies)).slice(0, want);
      if (chosen.length < want) throw new PlanError("no_ticket");
      picked.ids = chosen.map((t) => t.id);
      return (aid) => {
        const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now: spec.now };
        return [
          spec.statement(c, chosen),
          G(db, sql`(select count(*)::int from ticket_movement where action_id = ${aid}) = ${want}::int`, "stale"),
          ...(spec.extraGuards ? spec.extraGuards(c) : []),
        ];
      };
    },
    { replanOnStale: true, afterPlan: spec.afterPlan },
  );
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return { ok: true, effects: [], ticketIds: picked.ids };
}

/** Columns every E8 movement shares, from the CTE `u` (id, professional_id, customer_id, purchase_id). */
function e8Movement(
  c: BatchCtx,
  actorUserId: string,
  cols: { kind: MovementKind; from: SQL; to: SQL; type: SQL; fromType: SQL; reason: string | null },
): SQL {
  return sql`
    insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status, to_status,
      ticket_type_id, from_ticket_type_id, purchase_id, actor, actor_user_id, reason, created_at)
    select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${c.aid}, ${cols.kind}::movement_kind, ${cols.from}, ${cols.to},
      ${cols.type}, ${cols.fromType}, purchase_id, 'pro'::actor, ${actorUserId}, ${cols.reason}, ${ts(c.now)}
    from u`;
}

/**
 * Convertir A → B (keeps purchase_id and expires_at): available tickets valid
 * at now, `convert` movements with from_ticket_type_id; G(N); G(B active).
 */
export async function convertTickets(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection; toTicketTypeId: string },
  now: Date = new Date(),
  opts: { afterPlan?: () => Promise<void> } = {},
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  const sel = input.selection;
  if (!("ticketIds" in sel) && sel.ticketTypeId === input.toTicketTypeId) {
    return fail("invalid", "Choisissez un autre type de ticket.");
  }
  return e8Run(db, {
    professionalId: input.professionalId,
    customerId: input.customerId,
    selection: sel,
    now,
    afterPlan: opts.afterPlan,
    async check() {
      const [to] = await db
        .select({ id: ticketType.id })
        .from(ticketType)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(ticketType.id, input.toTicketTypeId),
            eq(ticketType.professionalId, input.professionalId),
            isNull(ticketType.archivedAt),
          ),
        );
      if (!to) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    },
    qualifies: (t) => t.status === "available" && e8Valid(t, now) && t.ticketTypeId !== input.toTicketTypeId,
    order: consumptionOrder,
    statement: (c, picked) =>
      db.execute(sql`
        with u as (
          update ticket t set ticket_type_id = ${input.toTicketTypeId}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          from unnest(${textArray(picked.map((t) => t.id))}, ${textArray(picked.map((t) => t.ticketTypeId))}) as f(id, from_type)
          where t.id = f.id and t.professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and t.customer_id = ${input.customerId}
            and t.ticket_type_id = f.from_type and t.status = 'available'
            and (t.expires_at is null or t.expires_at > ${ts(now)})
          returning t.id, t.professional_id, t.customer_id, t.purchase_id, f.from_type
        )
        ${e8Movement(c, input.actorUserId, {
          kind: "convert",
          from: sql`'available'::ticket_status`,
          to: sql`'available'::ticket_status`,
          type: sql`${input.toTicketTypeId}`,
          fromType: sql`from_type`,
          reason: null,
        })}`),
    extraGuards: () => [
      G(
        db,
        sql`exists (select 1 from ticket_type where id = ${input.toTicketTypeId} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and archived_at is null)`,
        "stale",
      ),
    ],
  });
}

/**
 * Clore (rembourse | compense | autre + note) or Retirer (reason retire):
 * available → closed. A type selection picks only offered tickets for retire;
 * an explicit paid id with retire hits ticket_retire_paid_chk → retire_paid.
 */
export async function closeTickets(
  db: Db,
  input: {
    professionalId: string;
    actorUserId: string;
    customerId: string;
    selection: TicketSelection;
    reason: TicketCloseReason;
    note?: string | null;
  },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  const note = input.note?.trim() || null;
  if (note !== null && note.length > BOUNDS.note) return fail("invalid");
  if (input.reason === "autre" && note === null) return fail("invalid", "Précisez le motif.");
  const byType = !("ticketIds" in input.selection);
  const offeredOnly = input.reason === "retire" && byType;
  return e8Run(db, {
    professionalId: input.professionalId,
    customerId: input.customerId,
    selection: input.selection,
    now,
    qualifies: (t) => t.status === "available" && (!offeredOnly || t.purchaseId === null),
    order: consumptionOrder,
    statement: (c, picked) =>
      db.execute(sql`
        with u as (
          update ticket set status = 'closed', closed_reason = ${input.reason}::ticket_close_reason, closed_note = ${note},
            status_changed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and customer_id = ${input.customerId}
            and id = any(${textArray(picked.map((t) => t.id))}) and status = 'available'
            ${offeredOnly ? sql`and purchase_id is null` : sql``}
          returning id, professional_id, customer_id, purchase_id, ticket_type_id
        )
        ${e8Movement(c, input.actorUserId, {
          kind: "close",
          from: sql`'available'::ticket_status`,
          to: sql`'closed'::ticket_status`,
          type: sql`ticket_type_id`,
          fromType: sql`null`,
          reason: note,
        })}`),
  });
}

/** Pointer: available (valid at now) → used, `pointage` movement (the note goes to reason). */
export async function pointTickets(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection; note?: string | null },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  const note = input.note?.trim() || null;
  if (note !== null && note.length > BOUNDS.note) return fail("invalid");
  return e8Run(db, {
    professionalId: input.professionalId,
    customerId: input.customerId,
    selection: input.selection,
    now,
    qualifies: (t) => t.status === "available" && e8Valid(t, now),
    order: consumptionOrder,
    statement: (c, picked) =>
      db.execute(sql`
        with u as (
          update ticket set status = 'used', status_changed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and customer_id = ${input.customerId}
            and id = any(${textArray(picked.map((t) => t.id))}) and status = 'available'
            and (expires_at is null or expires_at > ${ts(now)})
          returning id, professional_id, customer_id, purchase_id, ticket_type_id
        )
        ${e8Movement(c, input.actorUserId, {
          kind: "pointage",
          from: sql`'available'::ticket_status`,
          to: sql`'used'::ticket_status`,
          type: sql`ticket_type_id`,
          fromType: sql`null`,
          reason: note,
        })}`),
  });
}

/**
 * Annuler le pointage: used → available, or expired when past expires_at
 * (like RET); `unpoint` movement. A type selection takes the most recently
 * pointed tickets first.
 */
export async function unpointTickets(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; customerId: string; selection: TicketSelection },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ ticketIds: string[] }>> {
  return e8Run(db, {
    professionalId: input.professionalId,
    customerId: input.customerId,
    selection: input.selection,
    now,
    qualifies: (t) => t.status === "used",
    order: (tickets) =>
      [...tickets].sort((a, b) => b.statusChangedAt.getTime() - a.statusChangedAt.getTime() || (a.id < b.id ? 1 : a.id > b.id ? -1 : 0)),
    statement: (c, picked) =>
      db.execute(sql`
        with u as (
          update ticket set
            status = case when expires_at is not null and expires_at <= ${ts(now)} then 'expired'::ticket_status else 'available'::ticket_status end,
            status_changed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and customer_id = ${input.customerId}
            and id = any(${textArray(picked.map((t) => t.id))}) and status = 'used'
          returning id, professional_id, customer_id, purchase_id, ticket_type_id, status
        )
        ${e8Movement(c, input.actorUserId, {
          kind: "unpoint",
          from: sql`'used'::ticket_status`,
          to: sql`status`,
          type: sql`ticket_type_id`,
          fromType: sql`null`,
          reason: null,
        })}`),
  });
}

/**
 * Déplacer un inscrit (confirmed booking) to another EVENT séance: lock both
 * occurrences (id asc); R both; SEAT(src, −1), SEAT(tgt, +1); move with
 * moved_at; G(moved, 'stale'); G(target scheduled ∧ event séance ∧ (coverage ≠
 * ticket ∨ its ticket's type accepted in tickets mode), 'not_eligible');
 * without force, G(ticket valid at the target start, 'ticket_expires_before').
 */
export async function moveAttendee(
  db: Db,
  input: { professionalId: string; actorUserId: string; bookingId: string; targetOccurrenceId: string; force: boolean },
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<EngineResult<{ fromOccurrenceId: string }>> {
  const from = { id: "" };
  const res = await tryAtomic(db, async () => {
    const [b] = await db
      .select({ id: booking.id, occurrenceId: booking.occurrenceId, status: booking.status, coverage: booking.coverage, srcEventId: occurrence.eventId })
      .from(booking)
      .innerJoin(occurrence, eq(occurrence.id, booking.occurrenceId))
      .where(and(eq(booking.id, input.bookingId), eq(booking.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!b) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    if (b.status !== "confirmed") throw new PlanError("stale");
    if (b.srcEventId === null) throw new PlanError("invalid", "Un rendez-vous ne se déplace pas : annulez-le puis réservez à nouveau.");
    if (b.occurrenceId === input.targetOccurrenceId) throw new PlanError("invalid");
    const [tgt] = await db
      .select({ id: occurrence.id, eventId: occurrence.eventId, status: occurrence.status, startsAt: occurrence.startsAt, bookingMode: event.bookingMode })
      .from(occurrence)
      .leftJoin(event, eq(event.id, occurrence.eventId))
      .where(and(eq(occurrence.id, input.targetOccurrenceId), eq(occurrence.professionalId, input.professionalId)));
    if (!tgt) throw new PlanError("not_found");
    if (tgt.eventId === null || tgt.status !== "scheduled") throw new PlanError("not_eligible");
    if (b.coverage === "ticket") {
      const [t] = await db
        .select({ ticketTypeId: ticket.ticketTypeId, expiresAt: ticket.expiresAt })
        .from(ticket)
        .where(eq(ticket.bookingId, b.id));
      if (!t) throw new PlanError("stale");
      const [link] = await db
        .select({ eventId: eventTicketType.eventId })
        .from(eventTicketType)
        .where(and(eq(eventTicketType.eventId, tgt.eventId), eq(eventTicketType.ticketTypeId, t.ticketTypeId)));
      if (!link || tgt.bookingMode !== "tickets") throw new PlanError("not_eligible");
      if (!input.force && t.expiresAt !== null && t.expiresAt.getTime() <= tgt.startsAt.getTime()) {
        throw new PlanError("ticket_expires_before");
      }
    }
    from.id = b.occurrenceId;
    return (aid) => {
      const c: BatchCtx = { db, aid, now };
      const queries: Query[] = [
        lockRows(db, "occurrence", [b.occurrenceId, tgt.id]),
        R(c, b.occurrenceId),
        R(c, tgt.id),
        SEAT(c, b.occurrenceId, -1),
        SEAT(c, tgt.id, 1),
        db.execute(sql`
          update booking set occurrence_id = ${tgt.id}, moved_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
          where id = ${b.id} and occurrence_id = ${b.occurrenceId} and professional_id = ${input.professionalId}
            and status = 'confirmed'`),
        G(db, sql`(select occurrence_id from booking where id = ${b.id} and status = 'confirmed') = ${tgt.id}`, "stale"),
        G(
          db,
          sql`exists (
            select 1 from booking bk
            join occurrence o on o.id = ${tgt.id}
            join event e on e.id = o.event_id
            where bk.id = ${b.id} and o.professional_id = ${input.professionalId} and o.status = 'scheduled'
              and (bk.coverage <> 'ticket' or (e.booking_mode = 'tickets' and exists (
                select 1 from ticket t
                join event_ticket_type l on l.event_id = o.event_id and l.ticket_type_id = t.ticket_type_id
                where t.booking_id = bk.id))))`,
          "not_eligible",
        ),
      ];
      if (!input.force) {
        queries.push(
          G(
            db,
            sql`not exists (select 1 from ticket t join occurrence o on o.id = ${tgt.id}
              where t.booking_id = ${b.id} and t.expires_at is not null and t.expires_at <= o.starts_at)`,
            "ticket_expires_before",
          ),
        );
      }
      return queries;
    };
  });
  if (!res.ok) return failOf(res);
  return {
    ok: true,
    effects: [
      { type: "email.attendee_moved", bookingId: input.bookingId, fromOccurrenceId: from.id },
      ...syncEffects([from.id, input.targetOccurrenceId]),
    ],
    fromOccurrenceId: from.id,
  };
}

/** One query per pro: the facts classifyTypes (src/lib/wallet-rules.ts) needs. */
export async function loadTypeFacts(db: Db, professionalId: string, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<TypeFacts[]> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
      archivedAt: ticketType.archivedAt,
      rdvEnabled: ticketType.rdvEnabled,
      linked: sql<boolean>`exists (
        select 1 from event_ticket_type l
        join event e on e.id = l.event_id
        where l.ticket_type_id = ${ticketType.id} and e.booking_mode = 'tickets' and e.archived_at is null
          and exists (select 1 from occurrence o where o.event_id = e.id and o.status = 'scheduled' and o.starts_at > ${ts(now)}))`,
    })
    .from(ticketType)
    .where(eq(ticketType.professionalId, professionalId))
    .orderBy(asc(ticketType.position), asc(ticketType.createdAt), asc(ticketType.id));
  return rows.map((r) => ({
    ticketTypeId: r.ticketTypeId,
    archived: r.archivedAt !== null,
    rdvEnabled: r.rdvEnabled,
    linkedToUpcomingEvent: r.linked === true,
  }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing nightly tests**

Create `src/lib/booking/nightly.db.test.ts` (Write tool, whole file). `runNightly` is global, so this test needs the `tickets-test` branch to hold only the current test's pro, which is what `dbTest` guarantees when the suite runs with `--test-concurrency=1`:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { addDaysMs, addMinutes, dbTest } from "../../test/db";
import { dayIn, giveTickets, makeCustomer, makeEvent, makeType, movementsOf, occurrencesOf, ticketsOf } from "../../test/fixtures";
import { ts } from "../db/atomic";
import { event } from "../db/schema";
import { HEALTH_KEYS, readHealth } from "../throttle";
import type { RecurrenceRuleInput } from "../tickets";
import { archiveFinishedEvents, ensureAllHorizons, expireTickets, purgeThrottle, runNightly } from "./nightly";
import { horizonEnd } from "./occurrences";

const weekly = (startDate: string, extra: Partial<RecurrenceRuleInput> = {}): RecurrenceRuleInput => ({
  startDate,
  startMinute: 600,
  freq: "weekly",
  interval: 1,
  weekdays: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
  monthlyBy: null,
  monthWeek: null,
  endMode: "never",
  untilDate: null,
  occurrenceCount: null,
  ...extra,
});

dbTest("E12 expireTickets: available tickets past expires_at become expired with one `expire` movement", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const customerId = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const now = new Date();
  const [soon] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: addMinutes(now, 1) });
  const [forever] = await giveTickets(db, pro, customerId, typeA, 1);

  const res = await expireTickets(db, addMinutes(now, 2));
  assert.ok(res.expired >= 1);
  const byId = new Map((await ticketsOf(db, customerId)).map((t) => [t.id, t]));
  assert.equal(byId.get(soon)?.status, "expired");
  assert.equal(byId.get(forever)?.status, "available");
  const last = (await movementsOf(db, soon)).at(-1);
  assert.equal(last?.kind, "expire");
  assert.equal(last?.fromStatus, "available");
  assert.equal(last?.toStatus, "expired");

  await expireTickets(db, addMinutes(now, 3));
  assert.equal((await movementsOf(db, soon)).filter((m) => m.kind === "expire").length, 1);
});

dbTest("E12 archiveFinishedEvents: finished once/until series are archived; never and upcoming ones are not", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const past = addDaysMs(now, -20);
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const { eventId: once } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, -18, now) } }, past);
  const { eventId: until } = await makeEvent(
    db,
    pro,
    { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: weekly(dayIn(pro, -18, now), { endMode: "until", untilDate: dayIn(pro, -5, now) }) },
    past,
  );
  const { eventId: endless } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: weekly(dayIn(pro, -18, now)) }, past);
  const { eventId: upcoming } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA] }, now);

  const res = await archiveFinishedEvents(db, now);
  assert.ok(res.archived >= 2);
  const archivedAt = async (id: string) => (await db.select().from(event).where(eq(event.id, id)))[0]?.archivedAt?.getTime() ?? null;
  assert.equal(await archivedAt(once), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(await archivedAt(until), now.getTime());
  assert.equal(await archivedAt(endless), null);
  assert.equal(await archivedAt(upcoming), null);
});

dbTest("E12 ensureAllHorizons: a series created 10 days ago is brought to today + 400", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  const { eventId } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: weekly(dayIn(pro, -10, now)) }, addDaysMs(now, -10));
  const res = await ensureAllHorizons(db, now);
  assert.ok(res.pros >= 1);
  assert.ok(res.inserted >= 10);
  const last = (await occurrencesOf(db, eventId)).map((o) => o.seriesDate ?? "").sort().at(-1);
  assert.equal(last, horizonEnd(now, pro.tz));
});

dbTest("E12 purgeThrottle: rows older than 2 days go; recent rows and health counters stay", async ({ db }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const tag = nanoid(8);
  const keys = { old: `t:test:${tag}:old`, recent: `t:test:${tag}:recent`, health: `health:test-${tag}` };
  await db.execute(sql`
    insert into throttle (key, window_start, hits) values
      (${keys.old}, ${ts(addDaysMs(now, -3))}, 1),
      (${keys.recent}, ${ts(addDaysMs(now, -1))}, 1),
      (${keys.health}, ${ts(addDaysMs(now, -10))}, 7)`);
  try {
    const res = await purgeThrottle(db, now);
    assert.ok(res.purged >= 1);
    const left = await db.execute<{ key: string }>(
      sql`select key from throttle where key in (${keys.old}, ${keys.recent}, ${keys.health}) order by key`,
    );
    assert.deepEqual(
      left.rows.map((r) => r.key),
      [keys.health, keys.recent].sort(),
    );
  } finally {
    await db.execute(sql`delete from throttle where key in (${keys.old}, ${keys.recent}, ${keys.health})`);
  }
});

dbTest("E12 runNightly: every step runs, invariants hold, the count is stored for the health card", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro);
  await makeEvent(db, pro, { ticketTypeIds: [typeA] });
  const now = new Date();
  const report = await runNightly(db, now);
  assert.deepEqual(report.errors, []);
  assert.equal(report.invariantViolations, 0);
  const health = await readHealth(db);
  assert.equal(health[HEALTH_KEYS.invariantViolations]?.value, 0);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing T11 test**

Create `src/lib/booking/delete-user.db.test.ts` (Write tool, whole file). It populates every domain table and every status of one pro through the engine, checks the E13 reads, deletes the user with the `deleteUser` statement, and checks that nothing is left:

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { addMinutes, createTestPro, dbTest, deleteTestUser, testEmail } from "../../test/db";
import { dayIn, giveTickets, makeCustomer, makeEvent, makeOffer, makeType, occurrencesOf } from "../../test/fixtures";
import { instantAt } from "../civil-date";
import type { Db } from "../db/atomic";
import { deletionImpact, pendingCheckoutSessions } from "./account";
import { loadQuestions } from "./answers";
import { cancelByClient, cancelOccurrence, createTimeOffWithConflicts } from "./cancel";
import { setAvailabilityRules, setTicketTypeArchived } from "./catalog";
import { attachCheckoutSession, bookRdv, bookWithHeldTicket, registerFree, registerPro, startBuyAndBook } from "./claims";
import { fulfillPurchase, startPurchase } from "./fulfillment";
import { expireTickets } from "./nightly";
import { createEvent } from "./occurrences";
import { releasePurchase } from "./release";
import { closeTickets, convertTickets, pointTickets, unpointTickets, upsertCustomer } from "./wallet";

function must<T extends { ok: boolean }>(res: T, what: string): asserts res is Extract<T, { ok: true }> {
  assert.ok(res.ok, `${what}: ${JSON.stringify(res)}`);
}

/** Every domain table: each has its own professional_id → professional ON DELETE CASCADE (R2). */
const DOMAIN_TABLES = [
  "ticket_type",
  "ticket_offer",
  "availability_rule",
  "event",
  "event_ticket_type",
  "question",
  "occurrence",
  "customer",
  "ticket_purchase",
  "booking",
  "booking_answer",
  "ticket",
  "ticket_movement",
  "time_off",
] as const;

async function rowCounts(db: Db, proId: string): Promise<Record<string, number>> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const table of DOMAIN_TABLES) {
    const res = await db.execute<{ n: number }>(
      sql`select count(*)::int as n from ${sql.identifier(table)} where professional_id = ${proId}`,
    );
    out[table] = Number(res.rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
  }
  return out;
}

async function distinct(db: Db, table: string, column: string, proId: string): Promise<string[]> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ v: string }>(
    sql`select distinct ${sql.identifier(column)}::text as v from ${sql.identifier(table)} where professional_id = ${proId} order by 1`,
  );
  return res.rows.map((r) => r.v).sort();
}

dbTest("T11: deleteUser on a fully populated pro removes the whole domain; other pros keep their client, unlinked", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  const p = pro.proId;
  const actor = pro.userId;
  const allWeek = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((dayOfWeek) => ({ dayOfWeek, startMinute: 0, endMinute: 1440 }));

  // ── catalog: types (one archived later, two RDV), offers, hours (global + per type), questions
  const typeA = await makeType(db, pro, { name: "Cours collectif" });
  const typeB = await makeType(db, pro, { name: "Stage d'été" });
  const rdvPaid = await makeType(db, pro, { name: "Cours particulier", rdvEnabled: true, rdvDurationMin: 60 });
  const rdvFree = await makeType(db, pro, { name: "Appel découverte", rdvEnabled: true, rdvFree: true, rdvDurationMin: 15 });
  const offerA = await makeOffer(db, pro, typeA, { quantity: 4, priceCents: 8500, validityCount: 3, validityUnit: "month" });
  await makeOffer(db, pro, rdvPaid, { priceCents: 5000 });
  must(await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId: p, ticketTypeId: null, rules: allWeek }), "global hours");
  must(await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId: p, ticketTypeId: rdvFree, rules: allWeek }), "type hours");

  // ── events: tickets mode with a question (4 daily séances from day 2), free mode, private
  const created = await createEvent(db, {
    professionalId: p,
    event: {
      title: "Altinha débutants",
      ticketTypeIds: [typeA, typeB],
      questions: [{ label: "Avez-vous déjà pratiqué ?", type: "boolean", required: true }],
      rule: {
        startDate: dayIn(pro, 2, now),
        startMinute: 600,
        freq: "daily",
        interval: 1,
        weekdays: null,
        monthlyBy: null,
        monthWeek: null,
        endMode: "count",
        untilDate: null,
        occurrenceCount: 4,
      },
    },
  });
  must(created, "createEvent");
  const [s1, s2, s3, s4] = (await occurrencesOf(db, created.eventId)).sort((a, b) => a.startsAt.getTime() - b.startsAt.getTime());
  const [questionRow] = await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: created.eventId });
  const {
    occurrences: [freeSeance],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { bookingMode: "free", rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, 3, now), startMinute: 1080 } });
  const {
    occurrences: [privateSeance],
  } = await makeEvent(db, pro, { isPrivate: true, ticketTypeIds: [typeA], rule: { startDate: dayIn(pro, 3, now), startMinute: 1140 } });

  // ── c1: a paid carnet, then booked (with an answer), cancelled, pointed, unpointed, converted, closed, an RDV
  const c1 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const shop = await startPurchase(db, { professionalId: p, offerId: offerA, customerId: c1 });
  must(shop, "startPurchase");
  const paid = await fulfillPurchase(db, shop.purchaseId, { sessionId: null, paymentIntentId: null, livemode: false });
  assert.equal(paid.outcome, "fulfilled");
  must(
    await bookWithHeldTicket(db, {
      professionalId: p,
      occurrenceId: s1.id,
      customerId: c1,
      seenStartsAt: s1.startsAt,
      actorUserId: null,
      answers: [{ questionId: questionRow.id, value: "Oui" }],
    }),
    "book s1",
  );
  const onS2 = await bookWithHeldTicket(db, {
    professionalId: p,
    occurrenceId: s2.id,
    customerId: c1,
    seenStartsAt: s2.startsAt,
    actorUserId: null,
    answers: [{ questionId: questionRow.id, value: "Non" }],
  });
  must(onS2, "book s2");
  must(await cancelByClient(db, { professionalId: p, customerId: c1, bookingId: onS2.bookingId, actorUserId: null }), "cancel s2");
  must(await pointTickets(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, customerId: c1, selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 2 } }), "point");
  must(await unpointTickets(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, customerId: c1, selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 } }), "unpoint");
  must(
    await convertTickets(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, customerId: c1, selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 }, toTicketTypeId: typeB }),
    "convert",
  );
  must(
    await closeTickets(db, {
      professionalId: p,
      actorUserId: actor,
      customerId: c1,
      selection: { ticketTypeId: typeA, quantity: 1 },
      reason: "rembourse",
      note: "Remboursé en espèces",
    }),
    "close",
  );
  await giveTickets(db, pro, c1, rdvPaid, 1);
  must(
    await bookRdv(db, {
      professionalId: p,
      ticketTypeId: rdvPaid,
      startsAt: instantAt(dayIn(pro, 3, now), 840, pro.tz),
      customerId: c1,
      actorUserId: null,
      mode: "held_ticket",
    }),
    "RDV with a ticket",
  );

  // ── c2: buy + book still pending with an open checkout
  const c2 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const pending = await startBuyAndBook(db, {
    professionalId: p,
    occurrenceId: s3.id,
    customerId: c2,
    seenStartsAt: s3.startsAt,
    offerId: offerA,
    answers: [{ questionId: questionRow.id, value: "Oui" }],
  });
  must(pending, "buy + book (pending)");
  const sessionId = `cs_test_${pending.purchaseId}`;
  must(
    await attachCheckoutSession(db, { purchaseId: pending.purchaseId, checkoutSessionId: sessionId, checkoutUrl: "https://checkout.example.test/s" }),
    "attach",
  );

  // ── c3: buy + book released (booking and purchase expired)
  const c3 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  const released = await startBuyAndBook(db, {
    professionalId: p,
    occurrenceId: s3.id,
    customerId: c3,
    seenStartsAt: s3.startsAt,
    offerId: offerA,
    answers: [{ questionId: questionRow.id, value: "Non" }],
  });
  must(released, "buy + book (released)");
  must(await releasePurchase(db, released.purchaseId), "release");

  // ── c4: free registration, guest on a private séance, free RDV, an expired offered ticket, a cancelled séance
  const c4 = await makeCustomer(db, pro);
  must(await registerFree(db, { professionalId: p, occurrenceId: freeSeance.id, customerId: c4, seenStartsAt: freeSeance.startsAt }), "free");
  must(await registerPro(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, occurrenceId: privateSeance.id, customerId: c4, asGuest: true }), "guest");
  must(
    await bookRdv(db, {
      professionalId: p,
      ticketTypeId: rdvFree,
      startsAt: instantAt(dayIn(pro, 3, now), 960, pro.tz),
      customerId: c4,
      actorUserId: null,
      mode: "free",
    }),
    "free RDV",
  );
  // Fresh clock: offerTickets refuses an expiry that is not after its own now, and the test has run for a while.
  const t0 = new Date();
  await giveTickets(db, pro, c4, typeA, 1, { expiresAt: addMinutes(t0, 1), now: t0 });
  await expireTickets(db, addMinutes(t0, 2));
  must(await registerPro(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, occurrenceId: s4.id, customerId: c4, asGuest: true }), "guest on s4");
  must(await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId: p, actorUserId: actor, occurrenceId: s4.id }), "cancel s4");

  // ── a time off with no séance, an archived type
  must(
    await createTimeOffWithConflicts(db, {
      professionalId: p,
      actorUserId: actor,
      startAt: instantAt(dayIn(pro, 20, now), 0, pro.tz),
      endAt: instantAt(dayIn(pro, 21, now), 0, pro.tz),
      allDay: true,
      reason: "Vacances",
      seenOccurrenceIds: [],
      cancelBooked: false,
    }),
    "time off",
  );
  must(await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId: p, ticketTypeId: typeB, archived: true }), "archive B");

  // ── the pro (A) is also a client of another pro (B)
  const other = await createTestPro(db);
  try {
    const { customerId: elsewhere } = await upsertCustomer(db, {
      professionalId: other.proId,
      email: testEmail("pro-a"),
      name: "Pro A",
      userId: pro.userId,
    });

    // fully populated: every table, every status
    const before = await rowCounts(db, p);
    for (const table of DOMAIN_TABLES) assert.ok(before[table] > 0, `${table} is empty before the delete`);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "booking", "status", p), ["cancelled", "confirmed", "expired", "pending"]);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "booking", "coverage", p), ["free", "guest", "ticket"]);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "ticket", "status", p), ["available", "booked", "closed", "expired", "used"]);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "ticket_purchase", "status", p), ["expired", "paid", "pending"]);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "occurrence", "status", p), ["cancelled", "scheduled"]);
    assert.deepEqual(await distinct(db, "ticket_movement", "kind", p), [
      "close",
      "convert",
      "expire",
      "offer",
      "pointage",
      "purchase",
      "return",
      "spend",
      "unpoint",
    ]);

    // E13 reads, before the delete
    assert.deepEqual(await pendingCheckoutSessions(db, p), [{ purchaseId: pending.purchaseId, checkoutSessionId: sessionId }]);
    assert.deepEqual(await deletionImpact(db, p, now), { ticketsInCirculation: 1, pendingPayments: 1 });

    // the deleteUser path: one DELETE FROM "user"
    await deleteTestUser(db, pro.userId);
    const after = await rowCounts(db, p);
    for (const table of DOMAIN_TABLES) assert.equal(after[table], 0, `${table} still has rows`);
    const link = await db.execute<{ user_id: string | null }>(sql`select user_id from customer where id = ${elsewhere}`);
    assert.equal(link.rows[0]?.user_id, null);
  } finally {
    await deleteTestUser(db, other.userId);
  }
}, { timeout: 300_000 });
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `nightly.ts`**

Create `src/lib/booking/nightly.ts` (Write tool, whole file):

```ts
/**
 * E12 (spec § 11): the nightly job's steps. /api/cron/daily (P4) calls
 * runNightly. Correctness never depends on it: R(o) releases holds before
 * every claim and usability never waits for `expire`.
 */
import { and, asc, eq, isNull, ne, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { nanoid } from "nanoid";

import { todayKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { textArray, ts, type Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { event, professional } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { lastSeriesDate } from "@/lib/recurrence";
import { HEALTH_KEYS, setHealth } from "@/lib/throttle";
import { THROTTLE_PURGE_DAYS } from "@/lib/tickets";

import { checkInvariants } from "./invariants";
import { ensureHorizon, ruleOf } from "./occurrences";
import { expireStalePurchases, sweepHolds } from "./release";

/** ensureHorizon calls per pro and night (each inserts at most MATERIALIZE_MAX_ROWS rows). */
const HORIZON_CALLS_PER_PRO = 10;

/** Step 3, ONE statement: available tickets with expires_at ≤ now → expired, one `expire` movement each (actor system). */
export async function expireTickets(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ expired: number }> {
  const aid = nanoid();
  const res = await db.execute(sql`
    with u as (
      update ticket set status = 'expired', status_changed_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
      where status = 'available' and expires_at is not null and expires_at <= ${ts(now)}
      returning id, professional_id, customer_id, ticket_type_id, purchase_id
    )
    insert into ticket_movement (professional_id, customer_id, ticket_id, action_id, kind, from_status, to_status,
      ticket_type_id, purchase_id, actor, created_at)
    select professional_id, customer_id, id, ${aid}, 'expire'::movement_kind, 'available'::ticket_status,
      'expired'::ticket_status, ticket_type_id, purchase_id, 'system'::actor, ${ts(now)}
    from u`);
  return { expired: res.rowCount ?? 0 };
}

/** Step 4: ensureHorizon for every pro with a non-archived event, looping while incomplete (≤ HORIZON_CALLS_PER_PRO calls). */
export async function ensureAllHorizons(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ pros: number; inserted: number }> {
  const pros = await db
    .selectDistinct({ id: event.professionalId })
    .from(event)
    .where(isNull(event.archivedAt))
    .orderBy(asc(event.professionalId));
  let inserted = 0;
  for (const { id } of pros) {
    for (let call = 0; call < HORIZON_CALLS_PER_PRO; call++) {
      const res = await ensureHorizon(db, id, now);
      if (!res.ok) {
        console.error(`[nightly] ensureHorizon failed for pro ${id}: ${res.code}`);
        break;
      }
      inserted += res.inserted;
      if (res.complete) break;
    }
  }
  return { pros: pros.length, inserted };
}

/**
 * Step 5: non-archived events in until/count mode (once included) whose last
 * rule date is before local today and with no future scheduled séance →
 * archived_at = now. `never` events are archived only by « supprimer ».
 */
export async function archiveFinishedEvents(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ archived: number }> {
  const candidates = await db
    .select({ ev: event, tz: professional.timezone })
    .from(event)
    .innerJoin(professional, eq(professional.id, event.professionalId))
    .where(and(isNull(event.archivedAt), ne(event.endMode, "never")));
  const finished = candidates
    .filter(({ ev, tz }) => {
      const last = lastSeriesDate(ruleOf(ev));
      return last !== null && last < todayKey(now, tz);
    })
    .map(({ ev }) => ev.id);
  if (finished.length === 0) return { archived: 0 };
  const res = await db.execute(sql`
    update event set archived_at = ${ts(now)}, updated_at = ${ts(now)}
    where id = any(${textArray(finished)}) and archived_at is null
      and not exists (select 1 from occurrence o where o.event_id = event.id and o.status = 'scheduled' and o.starts_at > ${ts(now)})`);
  return { archived: res.rowCount ?? 0 };
}

/** Step 6: throttle rows older than THROTTLE_PURGE_DAYS; `health:*` counters are kept. */
export async function purgeThrottle(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<{ purged: number }> {
  const res = await db.execute(sql`
    delete from throttle
    where window_start < ${ts(now)} - make_interval(days => ${THROTTLE_PURGE_DAYS}::int) and key not like 'health:%'`);
  return { purged: res.rowCount ?? 0 };
}

export type NightlyReport = {
  expiredBookings: number;
  expiredPurchases: number;
  expiredTickets: number;
  horizon: { pros: number; inserted: number };
  archivedEvents: number;
  purgedThrottle: number;
  invariantViolations: number;
  /** "step: message" for each step that threw (the next steps still run) */
  errors: string[];
};

/** Steps 1–7 in order, each isolated; step 7 stores the violation count in `health:invariant_violations`. */
export async function runNightly(db: Db, now: Date = new Date()): Promise<NightlyReport> {
  const report: NightlyReport = {
    expiredBookings: 0,
    expiredPurchases: 0,
    expiredTickets: 0,
    horizon: { pros: 0, inserted: 0 },
    archivedEvents: 0,
    purgedThrottle: 0,
    invariantViolations: 0,
    errors: [],
  };
  const step = async (name: string, run: () => Promise<void>) => {
    try {
      await run();
    } catch (err) {
      report.errors.push(`${name}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
    }
  };
  await step("sweepHolds", async () => {
    report.expiredBookings = (await sweepHolds(db, now)).expiredBookings;
  });
  await step("expireStalePurchases", async () => {
    report.expiredPurchases = (await expireStalePurchases(db, now)).expired;
  });
  await step("expireTickets", async () => {
    report.expiredTickets = (await expireTickets(db, now)).expired;
  });
  await step("ensureAllHorizons", async () => {
    report.horizon = await ensureAllHorizons(db, now);
  });
  await step("archiveFinishedEvents", async () => {
    report.archivedEvents = (await archiveFinishedEvents(db, now)).archived;
  });
  await step("purgeThrottle", async () => {
    report.purgedThrottle = (await purgeThrottle(db, now)).purged;
  });
  await step("checkInvariants", async () => {
    const violations = await checkInvariants(db);
    report.invariantViolations = violations.length;
    for (const v of violations.slice(0, 20)) console.error(`[nightly] ${v.invariant} ${v.rowId}: ${v.detail}`);
    await setHealth(db, HEALTH_KEYS.invariantViolations, violations.length, now);
  });
  return report;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `account.ts`**

Create `src/lib/booking/account.ts` (Write tool, whole file):

```ts
/** E13 (spec § 11): what deleteUser (P3, src/server/admin.ts) reads before `DELETE FROM "user"`. */
import { and, asc, eq, isNotNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { ts, type Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { ticketPurchase } from "@/lib/db/schema";

/** Pending purchases of the pro with a Checkout session to expire (best effort, before the delete). */
export async function pendingCheckoutSessions(
  db: Db,
  professionalId: string,
): Promise<{ purchaseId: string; checkoutSessionId: string }[]> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ purchaseId: ticketPurchase.id, checkoutSessionId: ticketPurchase.checkoutSessionId })
    .from(ticketPurchase)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(ticketPurchase.professionalId, professionalId),
        eq(ticketPurchase.status, "pending"),
        isNotNull(ticketPurchase.checkoutSessionId),
      ),
    )
    .orderBy(asc(ticketPurchase.id));
  return rows.flatMap((r) => (r.checkoutSessionId ? [{ purchaseId: r.purchaseId, checkoutSessionId: r.checkoutSessionId }] : []));
}

/**
 * delete-user-card impact: usable tickets in circulation (available, not
 * expired at now) and payments still in progress (pending with an open
 * checkout, or an asynchronous payment being processed).
 */
export async function deletionImpact(
  db: Db,
  professionalId: string,
  now: Date = new Date(),
): Promise<{ ticketsInCirculation: number; pendingPayments: number }> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ tickets: number; payments: number }>(sql`
    select
      (select count(*)::int from ticket where professional_id = ${professionalId} and status = 'available'
        and (expires_at is null or expires_at > ${ts(now)})) as tickets,
      (select count(*)::int from ticket_purchase where professional_id = ${professionalId} and status = 'pending'
        and (checkout_expires_at > ${ts(now)} or processing_at is not null)) as payments`);
  const row = res.rows[0];
  return { ticketsInCirculation: Number(row?.tickets ?? 0), pendingPayments: Number(row?.payments ?? 0) };
}
```

<!-- G6 -->

## Contract deviations (G6)

1. **`src/test/auth.ts`: `TestAuth.auth` uses a different type.** The contract types it as `ReturnType<typeof betterAuth>`. I checked this with tsc against the repo's `node_modules`: assigning `betterAuth(buildAuthOptions(...))` to `Auth<BetterAuthOptions>` fails with TS2322, because the plugin-typed `api` (admin `role` and `banned`) is not assignable. The module therefore exports `TestAuthInstance = ReturnType<typeof buildInstance>` and uses that type for `TestAuth.auth`. The other declarations (`TEST_BASE_URL`, `makeTestAuth`, `BrowserCall`, `browser`, `deleteUsersByEmail`) are unchanged.
2. **`lastCode(email)` skips mails that carry no code.** It returns the code of the last mail to `email` whose subject starts with 6 digits. Example: in T12 the password-disabled mail comes after the code and has no code, so it is skipped.
3. **`browser()` reads a non-JSON body as `null`.** The spike helper threw instead. A disabled path answers the text `Not Found` with status 404, and the extra 404 test calls those paths.
4. **Task 34: there are 6 offers, not 7.** Spec § 15.4 and contract § 5.24 list 3 collectif offers, 1 essai, 2 particulier and 0 découverte. `content.db.test.ts` asserts 6.
5. **`scripts/content/apply-catalog.ts` has three more exports.** These are additions and change nothing that already exists:
   - `expectOk(label, result)`: the seed uses it to stop with a clear error on any engine failure.
   - `bootstrapCatalog(db, slug, now)` and `BootstrapOutcome`: the bootstrap guard as a function, so `content.db.test.ts` can test the refusals.
   - `scripts/bootstrap-content.ts` is now a thin CLI wrapper around `bootstrapCatalog`.
6. **`src/lib/auth.db.test.ts` has three more tests beyond T12, T13 and T15:**
   - `session.create.after` links the `customer` rows of a verified sign-in;
   - the closed emailOTP, core link-verification and admin endpoints answer 404 over HTTP;
   - a `forget-password` code request mails nothing and stores no code.
7. **`src/lib/auth-options.ts` is the contract's verified code, with comments added and three hardening changes.** The comments carry over the explanations from the old `auth.ts`. The changes (verified against better-auth 1.6.19 in `node_modules`):
   - **`CORE_DISABLED_PATHS` = `/send-verification-email` and `/verify-email`, added to `AUTH_DISABLED_PATHS`.** With `overrideDefaultEmailVerification`, the plugin injects `emailVerification.sendVerificationEmail` (`plugins/email-otp/index.mjs:24-35`). The core `POST /send-verification-email` then mails a « code de confirmation » to any existing unverified address, with no session (`api/routes/email-verification.mjs:89-116`). That path is not `/email-otp/send-verification-otp`, so `hooks.before` never caps it. Only Better Auth's per-IP rule applies (3 per 60 s, `rate-limiter/index.mjs:379-383`), so rotating IPs could mail one address without limit. No link is ever mailed, so `/verify-email` is dead too, and nothing in the app calls either endpoint. Sign-up is unaffected, because core sign-up calls the sender in-process (`sign-up.mjs:241-245`).
   - **`hooks.before` answers `{ success: true }` to `type: "forget-password"`** before the cap and before any code is generated. `/email-otp/send-verification-otp` accepts that type (`routes.mjs:16-21`), but reset by code is closed (`OTP_DISABLED_PATHS`), so the mail would be a misleading code that cannot be used.
   - **`sendVerificationOTP` mails only `sign-in` and `email-verification` codes**, which is the spec § 12 mapping. Any other type sends nothing. This is defence in depth for in-process calls.
8. **How I read the seed steps (no deviation):**
   - "3 past séances (now = start − 2 d)": the first 3 débutants séances starting on or after P+3, so the P+1 purchase comes first.
   - "registerPro … past séance (P+7)": the first débutants séance with `series_date ≥ P+7`, registered at its start + 2 h.
   - The seed deletes the session that sign-up creates automatically, and resets `last_login_at` to null.
9. **`bootstrapCatalog` ignores `time_off` (contract § 5.13 / § 8 say `hasDomainData`).** It refuses only when the pro already has a `ticket_type`, an `event` or a `customer` row. It does this through a private `hasCatalogOrClients` in `apply-catalog.ts`, and `hasDomainData` (Task 17) is unchanged. `time_off` is kept from v1 (spec § 6.1), and migrations 0010 and 0011 wipe every other v1 domain table. With the time-off check, the owner's real page in production would be refused (`has_data`) if it ever recorded an unavailability, and that page is the only target of bootstrap (spec § 15.4, P6). The catalog never conflicts with a time off: empty séances that overlap one are hidden, and online claims check it. The content test proves that a page holding only a time off is accepted, and that bootstrap still creates no time off.
10. **Script output starts with dotenv's line.** `scripts/load-env.ts` loads dotenv 17.4.2, which prints `◇ injected env (N) from .env.local // tip: …` to stdout on every run (`quiet` defaults to false). In every seed and bootstrap run, `→ <host>` is therefore the second line, and it is a record, not a gate. The seed's real gate is its own `SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS` guard, which throws before any write. `bootstrap-content.ts` has no allowlist (P6 runs it on production), so Task 35 Step 7 chains the Step 2 host check in front of it with `&&`. That check passes `quiet: true` to dotenv and exits 1 when the host is not allowed.

```ts
import { escapeHtml, layout } from "@/lib/email-layout";

/**
 * Sign-in code (emailOTP type "sign-in"). The code leads the subject so a
 * mail preview shows it, and the DB tests read it with /^(\d{6})\b/.
 */
export function loginCodeEmail(args: { code: string }): { subject: string; html: string } {
  const code = escapeHtml(args.code);
  return {
    subject: `${args.code} est votre code de connexion Skedul`,
    html: layout("Votre code de connexion", `
    <p>Voici votre code pour vous connecter à Skedul :</p>
    <p style="margin:24px 0;font-size:32px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:8px;color:#18181b">${code}</p>
    <p>Il est valable 5 minutes.</p>
    <p style="color:#71717a;font-size:13px">Si vous n'avez pas demandé ce code, ignorez cet email : personne ne peut se connecter sans lui.</p>
  `),
  };
}
```

```ts
import { escapeHtml, layout } from "@/lib/email-layout";

/**
 * Address confirmation code (emailOTP type "email-verification"): sent on a
 * password sign-up and by « Confirmez votre adresse ». A code, never a link:
 * a mail scanner that pre-opens links must not be able to confirm an address.

export function verifyEmailCodeEmail(args: { code: string }): { subject: string; html: string } {

    <p style="color:#71717a;font-size:13px">Si vous n'avez pas créé de compte Skedul, ignorez cet email.</p>

}
```

```ts
import { escapeHtml, layout } from "@/lib/email-layout";

/** Sent by Better Auth's `sendResetPassword`; `url` is its one-hour token link. */
export function passwordResetEmail(args: { name: string; url: string }) {
  const firstName = escapeHtml(args.name.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "");
  return layout("Choisissez un nouveau mot de passe", `
    <p>Bonjour${firstName ? ` ${firstName}` : ""},</p>
    <p>Vous avez demandé à réinitialiser le mot de passe de votre compte Skedul. Ce lien est valable 1 heure et ne sert qu'une fois.</p>
    <p style="margin:24px 0"><a href="${escapeHtml(args.url)}" style="display:inline-block;background:#6366f1;color:#fff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;padding:12px 20px;border-radius:10px">Choisir un nouveau mot de passe</a></p>
    <p style="color:#71717a;font-size:13px">Si vous n'êtes pas à l'origine de cette demande, ignorez cet email : votre mot de passe actuel reste valable.</p>
  `);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Rewrite `src/lib/email.ts`**

Read the file first, then replace its whole content with the version below. Three things change:
- the `email-layout` import goes, since it would otherwise be unused;
- the « Templates » section goes;
- a re-export is added, so `auth.ts` keeps compiling until Task 32.

```ts
import "server-only";
import { Resend } from "resend";

import { env } from "@/lib/env";

export { passwordResetEmail } from "@/lib/emails/password-reset";

const resend = env.RESEND_ENABLED ? new Resend(env.RESEND_API_KEY!) : null;

interface SendArgs {
  to: string;
  subject: string;
  html: string;
}

type SendResult =
  | { id: string; mocked: boolean; failed?: false }
  | { id: null; mocked: false; failed: true };

/**
 * Sends an email via Resend, or logs to the console in mock mode.
 *
 * Best effort: a failure is logged and returned, never thrown. Emails are sent
 * after a booking or payment is already saved, so a Resend outage or a rejected
 * sender must not turn a successful booking into an error for the customer.
 */
export async function sendEmail({ to, subject, html }: SendArgs): Promise<SendResult> {
  if (!resend) {
    console.info(`\n📧 [email:mock] → ${to}\n   subject: ${subject}\n`);
    return { id: "mock", mocked: true };
  }
  try {
    const { data, error } = await resend.emails.send({
      from: env.EMAIL_FROM,
      to,
      subject,
      html,
    });
    if (error) {
      console.error(`[email] Resend rejected "${subject}":`, error);
      return { id: null, mocked: false, failed: true };
    }
    return { id: data?.id ?? "unknown", mocked: false };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`[email] Resend request failed for "${subject}":`, err);
    return { id: null, mocked: false, failed: true };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `src/lib/auth-options.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Better Auth options WITHOUT server-only / env / next: src/lib/auth.ts wraps
 * them with nextCookies(); the seed and the DB tests build their own instance
 * with a `sendEmail` that captures codes (spec § 12).
 */
import type { BetterAuthOptions } from "better-auth";
import { drizzleAdapter } from "better-auth/adapters/drizzle";
import { createAuthMiddleware } from "better-auth/api";
import { emailOTP } from "better-auth/plugins";
import { and, eq, isNull, ne } from "drizzle-orm";

import { ADMIN_HTTP_ENDPOINTS, adminPlugin } from "@/lib/auth-admin";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import * as schema from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { loginCodeEmail } from "@/lib/emails/login-code";
import { passwordDisabledEmail } from "@/lib/emails/password-disabled";
import { passwordResetEmail } from "@/lib/emails/password-reset";
import { verifyEmailCodeEmail } from "@/lib/emails/verify-email-code";
import { consumeThrottle, otpThrottleKey } from "@/lib/throttle";
import { OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT } from "@/lib/tickets";

export type AuthMail = { to: string; subject: string; html: string };

export type AuthDeps = {
  db: Db;
  baseURL: string;
  secret: string;
  /** Never throws (src/lib/email.ts); tests capture instead of sending. */
  sendEmail: (mail: AuthMail) => Promise<unknown>;
  google?: { clientId: string; clientSecret: string } | null;
  /** Test clock for the per-e-mail code cap; defaults to the real time. */
  now?: () => Date;
  /** Mock e-mail mode: also print the password-reset link. */
  logResetLink?: boolean;
};

export const OTP_SEND_PATH = "/email-otp/send-verification-otp";
export const OTP_SIGN_IN_PATH = "/sign-in/email-otp";
export const OTP_VERIFY_EMAIL_PATH = "/email-otp/verify-email";
/** The only emailOTP endpoints served over HTTP. */
export const OTP_HTTP_PATHS = [OTP_SEND_PATH, OTP_SIGN_IN_PATH, OTP_VERIFY_EMAIL_PATH] as const;

/**
 * Encrypted storage + "reuse": a resend within the 5 minutes sends the SAME
 * code, so the per-e-mail cap below never invalidates a code already sent.
 * With overrideDefaultEmailVerification the plugin's own sendVerificationOnSignUp
 * is inert: the sign-up code comes from core emailVerification.sendOnSignUp.
 */
export const OTP_PLUGIN_OPTIONS = {
  otpLength: 6,
  expiresIn: 300,
  allowedAttempts: 3,
  storeOTP: "encrypted",
  resendStrategy: "reuse",
  overrideDefaultEmailVerification: true,
} as const;

/** Every other emailOTP endpoint (reset, change e-mail, check…), derived from the plugin. */
export const OTP_DISABLED_PATHS: string[] = Object.values(
  emailOTP({ ...OTP_PLUGIN_OPTIONS, async sendVerificationOTP() {} }).endpoints,
)
  .map((endpoint) => (endpoint as { path?: string }).path)
  .filter((path): path is string => typeof path === "string")
  .filter((path) => !(OTP_HTTP_PATHS as readonly string[]).includes(path));

/**
 * Core link-verification endpoints. With overrideDefaultEmailVerification no
 * link is ever mailed, and /send-verification-email would send address codes
 * outside the per-address cap of hooks.before (only 3 per 60 s per IP).
 * Sign-up still sends its code: core sign-up calls the sender in-process.
 */
export const CORE_DISABLED_PATHS: string[] = ["/send-verification-email", "/verify-email"];

/** Closed over HTTP only; in-process `auth.api.*` calls keep working. */
export const AUTH_DISABLED_PATHS: string[] = [...ADMIN_HTTP_ENDPOINTS, ...OTP_DISABLED_PATHS, ...CORE_DISABLED_PATHS];

/**
 * Explicitly enabled so dev and tests take the production path. Stored in the
 * `rate_limit` table (per instance memory is useless on serverless); the 600 s
 * global window lets Better Auth's purge respect the custom windows.
 */
export const AUTH_RATE_LIMIT = {
  enabled: true,
  storage: "database",
  window: 600,
  max: 6000,
  customRules: {
    [OTP_SEND_PATH]: { window: 600, max: 5 },
    [OTP_SIGN_IN_PATH]: { window: 600, max: 10 },
    [OTP_VERIFY_EMAIL_PATH]: { window: 600, max: 10 },
  },
} as const;

export function buildAuthOptions(deps: AuthDeps) {
  const { db } = deps;
  const now = deps.now ?? (() => new Date());

  const otpPlugin = emailOTP({
    ...OTP_PLUGIN_OPTIONS,
    async sendVerificationOTP({ email, otp, type }) {
      // Only these two codes are ever mailed: reset and change-email by code are closed.
      if (type !== "sign-in" && type !== "email-verification") return;
      const mail = type === "sign-in" ? loginCodeEmail({ code: otp }) : verifyEmailCodeEmail({ code: otp });
      await deps.sendEmail({ to: email, ...mail });
    },
  });

  return {
    appName: "Skedul",
    baseURL: deps.baseURL,
    secret: deps.secret,
    database: drizzleAdapter(db, {
      provider: "pg",
      schema: {
        user: schema.user,
        session: schema.session,
        account: schema.account,
        verification: schema.verification,
        rateLimit: schema.rateLimit,
      },
    }),
    emailAndPassword: {
      enabled: true,
      autoSignIn: true,
      minPasswordLength: 8,
      // "Mot de passe oublié" → /forgot-password. The link is single-use and
      // expires after one hour. Better Auth answers the same way whether or not
      // the email has an account; sendEmail never throws, so a send failure
      // cannot reveal which addresses exist either.
      resetPasswordTokenExpiresIn: 60 * 60,
      sendResetPassword: async ({ user, url }) => {
        await deps.sendEmail({
          to: user.email,
          subject: "Réinitialisez votre mot de passe Skedul",
          html: passwordResetEmail({ name: user.name, url }),
        });
        if (deps.logResetLink) console.info(`   reset link: ${url}\n`);
      },
      // A reset may come from someone who had the old password: end every session.
      revokeSessionsOnPasswordReset: true,
      // Using the emailed link proves the user owns the address.
      onPasswordReset: async ({ user }) => {
        try {
          await db.update(schema.user).set({ emailVerified: true }).where(eq(schema.user.id, user.id));
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("[auth] could not mark the email verified after reset", err);
        }
      },
    },
    // A password sign-up gets a CODE (the plugin overrides the default link).
    emailVerification: { sendOnSignUp: true },
    socialProviders: deps.google
      ? {
          google: {
            clientId: deps.google.clientId,
            clientSecret: deps.google.clientSecret,
            // Force Google to return a refresh_token even on re-consent.
            accessType: "offline",
            prompt: "select_account consent",
          },
        }
      : undefined,
    user: {
      additionalFields: {
        role: { type: "string", required: false, defaultValue: "client", input: false },
      },
    },
    session: { cookieCache: { enabled: true, maxAge: 5 * 60 } },
    rateLimit: AUTH_RATE_LIMIT,
    hooks: {
      // Per-address cap (5 codes an hour). Past it, answer like a send BEFORE
      // the endpoint runs: no new code is generated, the live one stays valid.
      before: createAuthMiddleware(async (ctx) => {
        if (ctx.path !== OTP_SEND_PATH) return;
        const body = ctx.body as { email?: unknown; type?: unknown } | undefined;
        // Reset by code is closed (OTP_DISABLED_PATHS): never mail a forget-password code.
        if (body?.type === "forget-password") return ctx.json({ success: true });
        const raw = body?.email;
        if (typeof raw !== "string") return;
        const allowed = await consumeThrottle(db, otpThrottleKey(raw), OTP_EMAIL_LIMIT, now());
        if (!allowed) return ctx.json({ success: true });
      }),
    },
    databaseHooks: {
      session: {
        create: {
          // "Dernière connexion" (real sign-ins only, not impersonation), then
          // best-effort link of this verified address's customer rows, all pros.
          after: async (session) => {
            if (session.impersonatedBy) return;
            try {
              await db.update(schema.user).set({ lastLoginAt: new Date() }).where(eq(schema.user.id, session.userId));
              const [u] = await db
                .select({ email: schema.user.email, emailVerified: schema.user.emailVerified })
                .from(schema.user)
                .where(eq(schema.user.id, session.userId));
              if (u?.emailVerified) {
                await db
                  .update(schema.customer)
                  .set({ userId: session.userId })
                  .where(and(eq(schema.customer.email, u.email.toLowerCase()), isNull(schema.customer.userId)));
              }
            } catch (err) {
              console.error("[auth] session hook failed", err);
            }
          },
        },
      },
      user: {
        update: {
          // Pre-account takeover fix (spec § 12): the first sign-in CODE on an
          // unverified password account proves the address; the password was
          // set by whoever signed up, so it is deleted, with every other
          // session. Over HTTP this runs after the endpoint, so newSession is set.
          after: async (user, ctx) => {
            if (ctx?.path !== OTP_SIGN_IN_PATH || !user.emailVerified) return;
            try {
              const credential = and(eq(schema.account.userId, user.id), eq(schema.account.providerId, "credential"));
              const found = await db.select({ id: schema.account.id }).from(schema.account).where(credential);
              if (found.length === 0) return;
              const keep = ctx.context.newSession?.session.id ?? null;
              await db.delete(schema.account).where(credential);
              await db
                .delete(schema.session)
                .where(keep ? and(eq(schema.session.userId, user.id), ne(schema.session.id, keep)) : eq(schema.session.userId, user.id));
              await deps.sendEmail({ to: user.email, ...passwordDisabledEmail({ forgotPasswordUrl: `${deps.baseURL}/forgot-password` }) });
            } catch (err) {
              console.error("[auth] pre-account takeover purge failed", err);
            }
          },
        },
      },
    },
    disabledPaths: AUTH_DISABLED_PATHS,
    plugins: [adminPlugin, otpPlugin],
  } satisfies BetterAuthOptions;
}
```
