- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `src/lib/auth.db.test.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Auth DB tests (spec § 14: T12, T13, T15) through auth.handler, like HTTP.
 * Not dbTest: these users are created by Better Auth itself; each test uses
 * unique addresses and random IPs (rate-limit and throttle rows persist) and
 * deletes its users in a finally.
 */
import test from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";

import { browser, deleteUsersByEmail, makeTestAuth, TEST_BASE_URL } from "../test/auth";
import { createTestPro, DB_SKIP, deleteTestUser, getTestDb, testEmail } from "../test/db";
import type { Db } from "./db/atomic";
import * as schema from "./db/schema";

const OPTS = { skip: DB_SKIP, timeout: 120_000 };
const CODE_RE = /^(\d{6})\b/;
const PASSWORD_DISABLED = "Votre mot de passe Skedul a été désactivé";

async function userByEmail(db: Db, email: string) {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.user).where(eq(schema.user.email, email));
  assert.ok(row, `no user for ${email}`);
  return row;
}

async function credentialCount(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await db
    .select({ id: schema.account.id })
    .from(schema.account)
    .where(and(eq(schema.account.userId, userId), eq(schema.account.providerId, "credential")));
  return rows.length;
}

async function sessionCount(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<number> {
  const rows = await db.select({ id: schema.session.id }).from(schema.session).where(eq(schema.session.userId, userId));
  return rows.length;
}

function sessionEmail(json: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | undefined {
  return (json?.user as { email?: string } | undefined)?.email;
}

test("T12 a sign-in code on an unverified password account deletes the password and the other sessions", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const t = makeTestAuth(db);
  const email = testEmail("t12");
  try {
    const attacker = browser(t);
    const signUp = await attacker("/sign-up/email", { email, password: "password123", name: "Intrus" });
    assert.equal(signUp.status, 200);
    const user = await userByEmail(db, email);
    assert.equal(user.emailVerified, false);
    assert.equal(await credentialCount(db, user.id), 1);

    const victim = browser(t);
    assert.equal((await victim("/email-otp/send-verification-otp", { email, type: "sign-in" })).status, 200);
    const codeMail = t.mails[t.mails.length - 1];
    assert.equal(codeMail.to, email);
    assert.ok(codeMail.subject.endsWith("est votre code de connexion Skedul"), codeMail.subject);
    const otp = t.lastCode(email);
    assert.ok(otp, "no sign-in code captured");
    assert.equal((await victim("/sign-in/email-otp", { email, otp })).status, 200);

    assert.equal((await userByEmail(db, email)).emailVerified, true);
    assert.equal(await credentialCount(db, user.id), 0, "the password set at sign-up must be gone");
    assert.equal(await sessionCount(db, user.id), 1, "only the session created by the code survives");

    // Cached read FIRST: a no-cache read expires the jar's cookies. The 5-min
    // cookie cache still shows the attacker, which is why every client-data
    // read uses disableCookieCache (spec § 12).
    assert.notEqual((await attacker("/get-session")).json, null);
    assert.equal((await attacker("/get-session?disableCookieCache=true")).json, null);
    assert.equal(sessionEmail((await victim("/get-session?disableCookieCache=true")).json), email);

    const disabled = t.mails.filter((m) => m.to === email && m.subject === PASSWORD_DISABLED);
    assert.equal(disabled.length, 1);
    assert.ok(disabled[0].html.includes(`href="${TEST_BASE_URL}/forgot-password"`));
  } finally {
    await deleteUsersByEmail(db, [email]);
  }
});

test("T13 the 6th code request of the hour sends nothing and the first code still signs in", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const t = makeTestAuth(db);
  const email = testEmail("t13");
  try {
    // A new browser (new IP) per request: only the per-address cap can bite.
    for (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
      const res = await browser(t)("/email-otp/send-verification-otp", { email, type: "sign-in" });
      assert.equal(res.status, 200, `request ${i}`);
      assert.deepEqual(res.json, { success: true }, `request ${i}`);
    }
    const sent = t.mails.filter((m) => m.to === email);
    assert.equal(sent.length, 5, "the 6th request must not send an e-mail");
    const codes = sent.map((m) => CODE_RE.exec(m.subject)?.[1]);
    assert.ok(codes[0], "no code in the first e-mail");
    assert.deepEqual(new Set(codes), new Set([codes[0]]), "a resend within 5 minutes reuses the first code");

    const signIn = await browser(t)("/sign-in/email-otp", { email, otp: codes[0] });
    assert.equal(signIn.status, 200);
    const user = await userByEmail(db, email);
    assert.equal(user.emailVerified, true);
    assert.equal(user.role, "client");
  } finally {
    await deleteUsersByEmail(db, [email]);
  }
});

test("T15 a signed-in unverified user confirms the address by code and keeps the password", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const t = makeTestAuth(db);
  const email = testEmail("t15");
  try {
    const call = browser(t);
    assert.equal((await call("/sign-up/email", { email, password: "password123", name: "Client" })).status, 200);
    const signUpMails = t.mails.filter((m) => m.to === email);
    assert.equal(signUpMails.length, 1, "a password sign-up sends exactly one code");
    assert.ok(signUpMails[0].subject.endsWith("est votre code de confirmation Skedul"), signUpMails[0].subject);
    assert.ok(!signUpMails[0].html.includes("/verify-email?token="), "a code, never a link");

    const otp = t.lastCode(email);
    assert.ok(otp, "no confirmation code captured");
    assert.equal((await call("/email-otp/verify-email", { email, otp })).status, 200);

    const user = await userByEmail(db, email);
    assert.equal(user.emailVerified, true);
    assert.equal(await credentialCount(db, user.id), 1, "confirming the address keeps the password");
    assert.equal(sessionEmail((await call("/get-session?disableCookieCache=true")).json), email);
    assert.ok(!t.mails.some((m) => m.subject === PASSWORD_DISABLED));
  } finally {
    await deleteUsersByEmail(db, [email]);
  }
});

test("a verified sign-in links the customer rows of that address", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const t = makeTestAuth(db);
  const pro = await createTestPro(db);
  const email = testEmail("link");
  try {
    const [row] = await db
      .insert(schema.customer)
      .values({ professionalId: pro.proId, email, name: "Client lié" })
      .returning({ id: schema.customer.id });
    const call = browser(t);
    assert.equal((await call("/email-otp/send-verification-otp", { email, type: "sign-in" })).status, 200);
    assert.equal((await call("/sign-in/email-otp", { email, otp: t.lastCode(email) })).status, 200);
    const user = await userByEmail(db, email);
    const [customer] = await db
      .select({ userId: schema.customer.userId })
      .from(schema.customer)
      .where(eq(schema.customer.id, row.id));
    assert.equal(customer.userId, user.id);
  } finally {
    await deleteUsersByEmail(db, [email]);
    await deleteTestUser(db, pro.userId);
  }
});

test("closed endpoints answer 404 over HTTP", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const call = browser(makeTestAuth(db));
  const email = testEmail("closed");
  assert.equal((await call("/email-otp/check-verification-otp", { email, type: "sign-in", otp: "000000" })).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call("/email-otp/reset-password", { email, otp: "000000", password: "password123" })).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call("/forget-password/email-otp", { email })).status, 404);
  // Core link verification: would mail address codes outside the per-address cap.
  assert.equal((await call("/send-verification-email", { email })).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call("/verify-email?token=x")).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call("/admin/list-users")).status, 404);
});

test("a forget-password code request mails nothing and stores no code", OPTS, async () => {
  const db = await getTestDb();
  const t = makeTestAuth(db);
  const email = testEmail("forgot");
  try {
    const call = browser(t);
    assert.equal((await call("/sign-up/email", { email, password: "password123", name: "Client" })).status, 200);
    assert.equal(t.mails.filter((m) => m.to === email).length, 1, "the sign-up code only");

    const res = await call("/email-otp/send-verification-otp", { email, type: "forget-password" });
    assert.equal(res.status, 200);
    assert.deepEqual(res.json, { success: true });
    assert.equal(t.mails.filter((m) => m.to === email).length, 1, "no mail for a forget-password code");
    const stored = await db
      .select({ id: schema.verification.id })
      .from(schema.verification)
      .where(eq(schema.verification.identifier, `forget-password-otp-${email}`));
    assert.equal(stored.length, 0, "the hook answers before any code is generated");
  } finally {
    await deleteUsersByEmail(db, [email]);
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `src/test/auth.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Better Auth test instance + cookie-jar browser for the auth DB tests (T12,
 * T13, T15). Calls go through `auth.handler`, like HTTP: the deferred
 * database hooks then run after the endpoint with `ctx.context.newSession`
 * set, exactly as in production (spec § 12, spike S8). Never named *.test.ts.
 */
import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { buildAuthOptions, type AuthDeps, type AuthMail } from "../lib/auth-options";
import { textArray, type Db } from "../lib/db/atomic";
import { testIp } from "./db";

/** http (not https): cookies carry no __Secure- prefix; nothing listens there. */
export const TEST_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:3000";

/** Codes lead the subject (src/lib/emails/login-code.ts, verify-email-code.ts). */
const CODE_RE = /^(\d{6})\b/;

function buildInstance(db: Db, sendEmail: AuthDeps["sendEmail"]) {
  return betterAuth(
    buildAuthOptions({
      db,
      baseURL: TEST_BASE_URL,
      secret: process.env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET ?? "test-secret-0123456789abcdefghijklmnop",
      sendEmail,
    }),
  );
}

/** The instance type with its plugins (a bare ReturnType<typeof betterAuth> rejects it). */
export type TestAuthInstance = ReturnType<typeof buildInstance>;

export type TestAuth = {
  auth: TestAuthInstance;
  /** every e-mail the instance sent, in order */
  mails: AuthMail[];
  /** the 6-digit code of the last code e-mail sent to `email` */
  lastCode(email: string): string | undefined;
};

export function makeTestAuth(db: Db): TestAuth {
  const mails: AuthMail[] = [];
  const auth = buildInstance(db, async (mail) => {
    mails.push(mail);
  });
  return {
    auth,
    mails,
    lastCode(email) {
      const to = email.toLowerCase();
      for (let i = mails.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const code = CODE_RE.exec(mails[i].subject)?.[1];
        if (code && mails[i].to.toLowerCase() === to) return code;
      }
      return undefined;
    },
  };
}

export type BrowserCall = (
  path: string,
  body?: unknown,
) => Promise<{ status: number; json: Record<string, unknown> | null }>;

/** A body that is not a JSON object (404 "Not Found", `null`) reads as null. */
function parseJson(text: string): Record<string, unknown> | null {
  if (!text) return null;
  try {
    const value = JSON.parse(text) as unknown;
    return value !== null && typeof value === "object" ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * One cookie jar = one browser. Origin = TEST_BASE_URL (passes the CSRF
 * origin check); x-forwarded-for = `ip`, a fresh random one by default, so
 * every browser gets its own Better Auth rate-limit buckets. GET when `body`
 * is undefined, else a JSON POST.
 */
export function browser(t: TestAuth, ip: string = testIp()): BrowserCall {
  const jar = new Map<string, string>();
  return async (path, body) => {
    const headers = new Headers({ origin: TEST_BASE_URL, "x-forwarded-for": ip });
    if (body !== undefined) headers.set("content-type", "application/json");
    if (jar.size) headers.set("cookie", [...jar].map(([k, v]) => `${k}=${v}`).join("; "));
    const res = await t.auth.handler(
      new Request(`${TEST_BASE_URL}/api/auth${path}`, {
        method: body === undefined ? "GET" : "POST",
        headers,
        body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
      }),
    );
    for (const line of res.headers.getSetCookie()) {
      const [pair, ...attrs] = line.split(";");
      const i = pair.indexOf("=");
      const name = pair.slice(0, i).trim();
      const value = pair.slice(i + 1).trim();
      if (!value || attrs.some((a) => /^\s*max-age=0\s*$/i.test(a))) jar.delete(name);
      else jar.set(name, value);
    }
    return { status: res.status, json: parseJson(await res.text()) };
  };
}

/** The auth tests create users outside dbTest: one DELETE FROM "user" (sessions and accounts cascade). */
export async function deleteUsersByEmail(db: Db, emails: readonly string[]): Promise<void> {
  if (emails.length === 0) return;
  await db.execute(sql`delete from "user" where email = any(${textArray(emails.map((e) => e.toLowerCase()))})`);
}
```

```ts
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { and, asc, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { altinhaCatalog } from "../../../scripts/content/altinha";
import { applyCatalog, bootstrapCatalog } from "../../../scripts/content/apply-catalog";
import { dbTest } from "../../test/db";
import { dayOfWeek, diffDays, instantAt, todayKey } from "../civil-date";
import type { Db } from "../db/atomic";
import * as schema from "../db/schema";
import { hasDomainData } from "./catalog";

async function countRows(db: Db, table: string, professionalId: string): Promise<number> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ n: number }>(
    sql`select count(*)::int as n from ${sql.identifier(table)} where professional_id = ${professionalId}`,
  );
  return Number(res.rows[0]?.n ?? 0);
}

async function seances(db: Db, eventId: string) {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(schema.occurrence)
    .where(eq(schema.occurrence.eventId, eventId))
    .orderBy(asc(schema.occurrence.startsAt));
}

dbTest("applyCatalog writes the Altinha catalog through the engine", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  assert.equal(await hasDomainData(db, pro.proId), false);
  const applied = await applyCatalog(db, pro.proId, altinhaCatalog(todayKey(now, pro.tz)), now);

  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(applied.typeIds).sort(), ["collectif", "decouverte", "essai", "particulier"]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(applied.eventIds).sort(), ["atelier", "competition", "debutants", "intermediaires", "roda"]);
  assert.deepEqual(
    Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(applied.offerIds).map(([key, ids]) => [key, ids.length])),
    { collectif: 3, essai: 1, particulier: 2, decouverte: 0 },
  );
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "ticket_type", pro.proId), 4);
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "ticket_offer", pro.proId), 6);
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "event", pro.proId), 5);
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "event_ticket_type", pro.proId), 5);
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "question", pro.proId), 2);
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "availability_rule", pro.proId), 17);

  const [settings] = await db
    .select({
      horizon: schema.professional.bookingHorizonDays,
      notice: schema.professional.minNoticeMinutes,
      cancel: schema.professional.cancelDeadlineHours,
    })
    .from(schema.professional)
    .where(eq(schema.professional.id, pro.proId));
  assert.deepEqual(settings, { horizon: 60, notice: 60, cancel: 24 });

  const globalRules = await db
    .select()
    .from(schema.availabilityRule)
    .where(and(eq(schema.availabilityRule.professionalId, pro.proId), isNull(schema.availabilityRule.ticketTypeId)));
  assert.equal(globalRules.length, 12);

  const [decouverte] = await db
    .select()
    .from(schema.ticketType)
    .where(eq(schema.ticketType.id, applied.typeIds.decouverte));
  assert.equal(decouverte.rdvEnabled, true);
  assert.equal(decouverte.rdvFree, true);
  assert.equal(decouverte.rdvDurationMin, 15);

  const debutants = await seances(db, applied.eventIds.debutants);
  assert.ok(debutants.length > 100, String(debutants.length));
  for (const o of debutants) {
    assert.ok([1, 3].includes(dayOfWeek(o.seriesDate!)), o.seriesDate!);
    assert.equal(o.startsAt.getTime(), instantAt(o.seriesDate!, 1140, pro.tz).getTime());
    assert.equal(o.capacity, 12);
  }

  const roda = await seances(db, applied.eventIds.roda);
  assert.ok(roda.length > 20, String(roda.length));
  for (let i = 0; i < roda.length; i++) {
    assert.equal(dayOfWeek(roda[i].seriesDate!), 6);
    if (i > 0) assert.equal(diffDays(roda[i - 1].seriesDate!, roda[i].seriesDate!), 14);
  }

  const atelier = await seances(db, applied.eventIds.atelier);
  assert.ok(atelier.length >= 12, String(atelier.length));
  for (const o of atelier) {
    assert.equal(dayOfWeek(o.seriesDate!), 0);
    assert.ok(Number(o.seriesDate!.slice(8)) <= 7, o.seriesDate!);
  }

  const [competition] = await db.select().from(schema.event).where(eq(schema.event.id, applied.eventIds.competition));
  assert.equal(competition.isPrivate, true);
  const [rodaEvent] = await db.select().from(schema.event).where(eq(schema.event.id, applied.eventIds.roda));
  assert.equal(rodaEvent.bookingMode, "free");

  assert.equal(await hasDomainData(db, pro.proId), true);
});

dbTest("bootstrapCatalog accepts a v1 time off, refuses an unknown page and a page that has data", async ({ db, pro }) => {
  const now = new Date();
  assert.deepEqual(await bootstrapCatalog(db, `${pro.slug}-inconnu`, now), { ok: false, reason: "unknown_pro" });

  // A time off kept from v1 is not catalog data: the owner's real page may have one (P6).
  await db.insert(schema.timeOff).values({
    professionalId: pro.proId,
    startAt: new Date("2026-01-05T00:00:00Z"),
    endAt: new Date("2026-01-06T00:00:00Z"),
    allDay: true,
    reason: "Vacances",
  });

  const first = await bootstrapCatalog(db, pro.slug, now);
  assert.equal(first.ok, true);
  assert.deepEqual(await bootstrapCatalog(db, pro.slug, now), { ok: false, reason: "has_data" });
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "ticket_type", pro.proId), 4, "the refused run wrote nothing");
  assert.equal(await countRows(db, "time_off", pro.proId), 1, "bootstrap creates no time off");

  for (const table of ["customer", "ticket_purchase", "ticket", "booking"]) {
    assert.equal(await countRows(db, table, pro.proId), 0, table);
  }
});
```

```ts
/**
 * The Altinha Paris catalog (spec § 15.4): ticket types and offers, events,
 * RDV hours and booking settings. Shared by the seed (demo account) and
 * scripts/bootstrap-content.ts (the owner's real account). Data only: it is
 * written through the engine by ./apply-catalog.ts.
 */
import { addDays, dayOfWeek, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import type {
  AvailabilityRuleInput,
  BookingMode,
  BookingSettings,
  OfferInput,
  QuestionInput,
  RecurrenceRuleInput,
  TicketTypeInput,
} from "@/lib/tickets";

export type CatalogType = {
  key: "collectif" | "essai" | "particulier" | "decouverte";
  type: TicketTypeInput;
  offers: Omit<OfferInput, "ticketTypeId">[];
  /** per-type RDV hours (replace the global ones for this type) */
  availability?: AvailabilityRuleInput[];
  /** RDV questions */
  questions?: QuestionInput[];
};

export type CatalogEvent = {
  key: "debutants" | "intermediaires" | "roda" | "atelier" | "competition";
  title: string;
  description: string;
  location: string;
  color: string;
  bookingMode: BookingMode;
  isPrivate: boolean;
  capacity: number;
  durationMin: number;
  rule: RecurrenceRuleInput;
  ticketTypeKeys: CatalogType["key"][];
  questions?: QuestionInput[];
};

export type CatalogContent = {
  settings: BookingSettings;
  /** global RDV hours */
  availability: AvailabilityRuleInput[];
  types: CatalogType[];
  events: CatalogEvent[];
};

/** The demo pro's profile, copied from the v1 seed (bootstrap keeps the real pro's profile). */
export const ALTINHA_PROFILE = {
  slug: "altinha-paris",
  businessName: "Altinha Paris",
  headline: "Aulas de altinha em Paris, com a energia do Rio ☀️",
  bio: "A gente começou a dar aulas pra compartilhar a altinha e fazer esse esporte crescer aqui em Paris. Nascemos e crescemos no Rio, então trazemos essa energia pra cá. As aulas duram 1h, em grupos pequenos organizados por nível, pra todo mundo evoluir no seu ritmo. De segunda a sábado, entre Nation e Tuileries. 🤍",
  brandColor: "#d97706",
  themePreset: "amber",
  headerLayout: "vitrine",
  avatarKind: "generated",
  avatarStyle: "fun-emoji",
  avatarSeed: "altinha-paris",
  timezone: "Europe/Paris",
  currency: "eur",
};

/** Fixed demo client accounts (also deleted by `seed --wipe`). */
export const SEED_CLIENT_EMAILS = ["beatriz@example.com"] as const;

/** Rules for every listed day, `start`–`end` minutes after local midnight. */
function hours(days: readonly number[], ranges: readonly [number, number][]): AvailabilityRuleInput[] {
  return days.flatMap((day) => ranges.map(([startMinute, endMinute]) => ({ dayOfWeek: day, startMinute, endMinute })));
}

/** First date ≥ `from` falling on `weekday` (0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday). */
function nextWeekday(from: DateKey, weekday: number): DateKey {
  return addDays(from, (weekday - dayOfWeek(from) + 7) % 7);
}

function weekly(startDate: DateKey, startMinute: number, weekdays: number[], interval = 1): RecurrenceRuleInput {
  return {
    startDate,
    startMinute,
    freq: "weekly",
    interval,
    weekdays,
    monthlyBy: null,
    monthWeek: null,
    endMode: "never",
    untilDate: null,
    occurrenceCount: null,
  };
}

/**
 * Spec § 15.4. Every event starts on `startDate` (local), never ends, and
 * produces its first séance on the first matching date ≥ startDate.
 */
export function altinhaCatalog(startDate: DateKey): CatalogContent {
  return {
    settings: { bookingHorizonDays: 60, minNoticeMinutes: 60, cancelDeadlineHours: 24 },
    // Mon–Sat, 10:00–13:00 and 17:30–20:30
    availability: hours([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], [
      [600, 780],
      [1050, 1230],
    ]),
    types: [
      {
        key: "collectif",

          color: "#16a34a",
        },
        offers: [
          { quantity: 1, priceCents: 2500 },
          { quantity: 4, priceCents: 8500, validityCount: 3, validityUnit: "month" },
          { quantity: 10, priceCents: 19000, validityCount: 6, validityUnit: "month" },
        ],
      },
      {
        key: "essai",
        type: {
          name: "Cours d'essai",
          description: "Votre premier cours pour découvrir l'altinha, sans engagement.",
          color: "#f59e0b",
        },
        offers: [{ quantity: 1, priceCents: 1500 }],
      },
      {
        key: "particulier",
        type: {
          name: "Cours particulier",
          description: "Un cours rien que pour vous, au rythme de vos objectifs.",

    ],
    events: [
      {
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `scripts/content/apply-catalog.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * Writes a CatalogContent through the engine (settings, RDV hours, types,
 * offers, RDV questions, events with their links, questions and séances).
 * Used by scripts/seed.ts and scripts/bootstrap-content.ts. Sends no e-mail.
 */
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import {
  createOffer,
  createTicketType,
  setAvailabilityRules,
  setTypeQuestions,
  updateBookingSettings,
} from "@/lib/booking/catalog";
import { createEvent } from "@/lib/booking/occurrences";
import type { EngineFail, EngineResult } from "@/lib/booking/types";
import { todayKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import type { Db } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { professional } from "@/lib/db/schema";

import { altinhaCatalog, type CatalogContent } from "./altinha";

export type AppliedCatalog = {
  typeIds: Record<string, string>;
  offerIds: Record<string, string[]>;
  eventIds: Record<string, string>;
};

/** Scripts treat any engine failure as fatal: the result, narrowed to ok, or a throw naming the step. */
export function expectOk<R extends EngineResult<object>>(label: string, result: R): Exclude<R, EngineFail> {
  const checked = result as EngineResult<object>;
  if (!checked.ok) {
    throw new Error(`${label}: ${checked.code}${checked.message ? ` (${checked.message})` : ""}`);
  }
  return result as Exclude<R, EngineFail>;
}

function idOf(ids: Record<string, string>, key: string): string {
  const id = ids[key];
  if (!id) throw new Error(`Unknown ticket type key "${key}" in the catalog`);
  return id;
}

/** Throws on the first failure (scripts only: there is no partial-write recovery). */
export async function applyCatalog(
  db: Db,
  professionalId: string,
  content: CatalogContent,
  now: Date,
): Promise<AppliedCatalog> {
  expectOk("settings", await updateBookingSettings(db, { professionalId, settings: content.settings }, now));
  expectOk(
    "global hours",
    await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId, ticketTypeId: null, rules: content.availability }, now),
  );

  const typeIds: Record<string, string> = {};
  const offerIds: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  for (const entry of content.types) {
    const { ticketTypeId } = expectOk(
      `type ${entry.key}`,
      await createTicketType(db, { professionalId, type: entry.type }, now),
    );
    typeIds[entry.key] = ticketTypeId;
    offerIds[entry.key] = [];
    for (const offer of entry.offers) {
      const { offerId } = expectOk(
        `offer ${entry.key} ×${offer.quantity}`,
        await createOffer(db, { professionalId, offer: { ...offer, ticketTypeId } }, now),
      );
      offerIds[entry.key].push(offerId);
    }
    if (entry.availability) {
      expectOk(
        `hours ${entry.key}`,
        await setAvailabilityRules(db, { professionalId, ticketTypeId, rules: entry.availability }, now),
      );
    }
    if (entry.questions) {
      expectOk(
        `questions ${entry.key}`,
        await setTypeQuestions(db, { professionalId, ticketTypeId, questions: entry.questions }, now),
      );
    }
  }

  const eventIds: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const entry of content.events) {
    const { eventId } = expectOk(
      `event ${entry.key}`,
      await createEvent(
        db,
        {
          professionalId,
          event: {
            title: entry.title,
            description: entry.description,
            location: entry.location,
            color: entry.color,
            bookingMode: entry.bookingMode,
            isPrivate: entry.isPrivate,
            capacity: entry.capacity,
            durationMin: entry.durationMin,
            rule: entry.rule,
            ticketTypeIds: entry.ticketTypeKeys.map((key) => idOf(typeIds, key)),
            questions: entry.questions ?? [],
          },
        },
        now,
      ),
    );
    eventIds[entry.key] = eventId;
  }

  return { typeIds, offerIds, eventIds };
}

export type BootstrapOutcome =
  | { ok: true; professionalId: string; applied: AppliedCatalog }
  | { ok: false; reason: "unknown_pro" | "has_data" };

/**
 * Catalog or clients already present. Unlike hasDomainData (catalog.ts), a
 * time off does not count: time_off is kept from v1 (spec § 6.1), never
 * conflicts with the catalog, and the owner's real page may have some (P6).
 */
async function hasCatalogOrClients(db: Db, professionalId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const res = await db.execute<{ found: boolean }>(sql`select (
    exists (select 1 from ticket_type where professional_id = ${professionalId})
    or exists (select 1 from event where professional_id = ${professionalId})
    or exists (select 1 from customer where professional_id = ${professionalId})
  ) as found`);
  return res.rows[0]?.found === true;
}

/**
 * `bootstrap-content --pro <slug>` (spec § 15.4): the Altinha catalog only, on
 * an existing pro WITHOUT ticket types, events or customers (a v1 time off is fine).
 * No customer, purchase, ticket, booking or time off; no e-mail.
 */
export async function bootstrapCatalog(db: Db, slug: string, now: Date): Promise<BootstrapOutcome> {
  const [pro] = await db
    .select({ id: professional.id, timezone: professional.timezone })
    .from(professional)
    .where(eq(professional.slug, slug));
  if (!pro) return { ok: false, reason: "unknown_pro" };
  if (await hasCatalogOrClients(db, pro.id)) return { ok: false, reason: "has_data" };
  const applied = await applyCatalog(db, pro.id, altinhaCatalog(todayKey(now, pro.timezone)), now);
  return { ok: true, professionalId: pro.id, applied };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `scripts/bootstrap-content.ts`**

```ts
import "./load-env";

import { db } from "@/lib/db";

import { bootstrapCatalog } from "./content/apply-catalog";

/**
 * Creates the Altinha catalog ONLY (types, offers, events with their links,
 * questions and séances, RDV hours, booking settings) on an existing pro
 * page that has no domain data yet (spec § 15.4). No customer, purchase,
 * ticket, booking or time off; no e-mail.
 *
 *   ./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/bootstrap-content.ts --pro <slug>
 *
 * Production (P6): `vercel env pull --environment=production .env.prod.local`,
 * then set DATABASE_URL for this one command (dotenv never overrides a variable
 * that is already set). The target host is printed, after dotenv's
 * « injected env » line, before anything is read or written.
 */
async function main() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const at = args.indexOf("--pro");
  const slug = at >= 0 ? args[at + 1]?.trim() : undefined;
  if (!slug || slug.startsWith("--")) {
    console.error("Usage : tsx scripts/bootstrap-content.ts --pro <slug>");
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const url = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
  if (!url) throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not set");
  console.info(`→ ${new URL(url).host}`);

  const outcome = await bootstrapCatalog(db, slug, new Date());
  if (!outcome.ok) {
    console.error(
      outcome.reason === "unknown_pro"
        ? `✗ Aucune page pro « ${slug} ».`
        : `✗ « ${slug} » a déjà des types, des événements ou des clients : rien n'a été écrit.`,
    );
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const { typeIds, offerIds, eventIds } = outcome.applied;
  const offers = Object.values(offerIds).reduce((n, ids) => n + ids.length, 0);
  console.info(
    `✓ ${slug} : ${Object.keys(typeIds).length} types, ${offers} offres, ${Object.keys(eventIds).length} événements créés.`,
  );
}

main()
  .then(() => process.exit(0))
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `scripts/seed.ts`**

Read the placeholder, then replace its whole content:

```ts
import "./load-env";

import { betterAuth } from "better-auth";
import { and, asc, eq, gt, lt, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

import { buildAuthOptions } from "@/lib/auth-options";
import { loadQuestions } from "@/lib/booking/answers";
import { cancelOccurrence, createTimeOffWithConflicts } from "@/lib/booking/cancel";
import { createTicketType, setTicketTypeArchived } from "@/lib/booking/catalog";
import { bookRdv, bookWithHeldTicket, registerFree, registerPro, startBuyAndBook } from "@/lib/booking/claims";
import { fulfillPurchase, startPurchase } from "@/lib/booking/fulfillment";
import { checkInvariants } from "@/lib/booking/invariants";
import { expireTickets } from "@/lib/booking/nightly";
import { ensureHorizon } from "@/lib/booking/occurrences";
import {
  closeTickets,
  convertTickets,
  createCustomer,
  offerTickets,
  pointTickets,
  upsertCustomer,
} from "@/lib/booking/wallet";
import { addDays, dayOfWeek, instantAt, todayKey, type DateKey } from "@/lib/civil-date";
import { db } from "@/lib/db";
import { textArray } from "@/lib/db/atomic";
import { occurrence, professional, session, user } from "@/lib/db/schema";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

import { ALTINHA_PROFILE, SEED_CLIENT_EMAILS, altinhaCatalog } from "./content/altinha";
import { applyCatalog, expectOk, type AppliedCatalog } from "./content/apply-catalog";

/**
 * Seed v2 (spec § 15.4): the Altinha Paris demo, written ONLY through the
 * engine with an injected clock, so the history (past séances, an expired
 * carnet, a cancelled séance) is real data that passes checkInvariants.
 *
 *   ./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/seed.ts --wipe
 *
 * Guards: never with APP_ENV=production; only on a DATABASE_URL host listed
 * in SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS; --wipe deletes the demo users only (their pages
 * cascade), never a TRUNCATE; without --wipe it refuses if they exist.
 */

const PASSWORD = "password123";
const TZ = ALTINHA_PROFILE.timezone;
const DEMO_USERS = sql`email like '%@skedul.app' or email like '%@skedul.test' or email = any(${textArray(SEED_CLIENT_EMAILS)})`;

/** T = the real now; every other instant of the story is local wall-clock time. */
const T = new Date();
const TODAY = todayKey(T, TZ);
const at = (days: number, minute: number): Date => instantAt(addDays(TODAY, days), minute, TZ);
const plusMinutes = (d: Date, minutes: number): Date => new Date(d.getTime() + minutes * 60_000);
/** P: the pro opened the page 8 weeks ago, at 09:00. */
const P = at(-56, 540);

type Seance = typeof occurrence.$inferSelect;

async function guard(wipe: boolean): Promise<void> {
  const host = new URL(env.DATABASE_URL).host;
  console.info(`→ ${host}`);
  if (env.APP_ENV === "production") throw new Error("Refus : APP_ENV=production.");
  const allowed = (process.env.SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS ?? "")
    .split(",")
    .map((h) => h.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (!allowed.includes(host)) throw new Error(`Refus : ${host} n'est pas dans SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS.`);
  if (wipe) {
    const res = await db.execute(sql`delete from "user" where ${DEMO_USERS}`);
    console.info(`🧹 ${res.rowCount ?? 0} compte(s) de démo supprimé(s), avec leurs pages.`);
    return;
  }
  const res = await db.execute<{ n: number }>(sql`select count(*)::int as n from "user" where ${DEMO_USERS}`);
  if (Number(res.rows[0]?.n ?? 0) > 0) {
    throw new Error("Des comptes de démo existent déjà : relancez avec --wipe pour les remplacer.");
  }
}

const seedAuth = betterAuth(
  buildAuthOptions({ db, baseURL: env.APP_URL, secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET, sendEmail: async () => {} }),
);

/**
 * A password account with a confirmed address and its role. No e-mail leaves
 * (sendEmail is a no-op); the session auto-created by the sign-up and its
 * « Dernière connexion » stamp are removed: nobody has signed in yet.
 */
async function createUser(name: string, email: string, role: "admin" | "pro" | "client"): Promise<string> {
  const res = await seedAuth.api.signUpEmail({ body: { name, email, password: PASSWORD } });
  await db.update(user).set({ emailVerified: true, role, lastLoginAt: null }).where(eq(user.id, res.user.id));
  await db.delete(session).where(eq(session.userId, res.user.id));
  return res.user.id;
}

async function seancesOf(eventId: string): Promise<Seance[]> {
  return db.select().from(occurrence).where(eq(occurrence.eventId, eventId)).orderBy(asc(occurrence.startsAt));
}

function firstFrom(rows: readonly Seance[], from: Date, label: string): Seance {
  const row = rows.find((o) => o.status === "scheduled" && o.startsAt.getTime() >= from.getTime());
  if (!row) throw new Error(`Aucune séance ${label} après le ${from.toISOString()}`);
  return row;
}

/** First local date ≥ today + `days` whose weekday is listed. */
function dayFrom(days: number, weekdays: readonly number[]): DateKey {
  let key = addDays(TODAY, days);
  while (!weekdays.includes(dayOfWeek(key))) key = addDays(key, 1);
  return key;
}

/** First local date ≥ today + `days` without any séance holding a seat (the time off must cancel nothing). */
async function dayWithoutBookings(professionalId: string, days: number): Promise<DateKey> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
    const key = addDays(TODAY, days + i);
    const start = instantAt(key, 0, TZ);
    const end = instantAt(addDays(key, 1), 0, TZ);
    const [row] = await db
      .select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
      .from(occurrence)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(occurrence.professionalId, professionalId),
          eq(occurrence.status, "scheduled"),
          gt(occurrence.seatsTaken, 0),
          lt(occurrence.blockStart, end),
          gt(occurrence.blockEnd, start),
        ),
      );
    if (Number(row?.n ?? 0) === 0) return key;
  }
  throw new Error("Aucun jour libre pour l'indisponibilité");
}

function offerOf(catalog: AppliedCatalog, key: string, index: number): string {
  const id = catalog.offerIds[key]?.[index];
  if (!id) throw new Error(`Offre ${key}[${index}] introuvable`);
  return id;
}

async function main() {
  const wipe = process.argv.includes("--wipe");
  await guard(wipe);
  console.info("🌱 Seed Skedul v2 (tickets et événements)…");

  /* ── 1. Accounts ─────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  await createUser("Admin Skedul", "admin@skedul.test", "admin");
  const proUserId = await createUser("Altinha Paris", "pro@skedul.app", "pro");
  const beatrizUserId = await createUser("Beatriz Almeida", "beatriz@example.com", "client");

  /* ── 2. Pro page and catalog, as of 8 weeks ago ──────────────── */
  const [pro] = await db
    .insert(professional)
    .values({ userId: proUserId, ...ALTINHA_PROFILE })
    .returning({ id: professional.id });
  const professionalId = pro.id;
  const catalog = await applyCatalog(db, professionalId, altinhaCatalog(todayKey(P, TZ)), P);
  const typeIds = catalog.typeIds;
  const [debutantsQuestion] = await loadQuestions(db, { eventId: catalog.eventIds.debutants });
  const [objectiveQuestion] = await loadQuestions(db, { ticketTypeId: typeIds.particulier });
  if (!debutantsQuestion || !objectiveQuestion) throw new Error("Questions du catalogue introuvables");
  const debutants = await seancesOf(catalog.eventIds.debutants);
  const debutantsUpcoming = firstFrom(debutants, at(1, 0), "débutants");

  /** E9 + E10 (mock payment): the tickets of one paid purchase. */
  async function buy(customerId: string, offerId: string, paidAt: Date): Promise<string[]> {
    const started = expectOk("achat", await startPurchase(db, { professionalId, offerId, customerId }, paidAt));
    const paid = await fulfillPurchase(
      db,
      started.purchaseId,
      { sessionId: null, paymentIntentId: null, livemode: false },
      plusMinutes(paidAt, 2),
    );
    if (paid.outcome !== "fulfilled") throw new Error(`Paiement ${started.purchaseId} : ${paid.outcome}`);
    return paid.ticketIds;
  }

  /* ── 3. Customers ────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  const { customerId: beatriz } = await upsertCustomer(
    db,
    {
      professionalId,
      email: "beatriz@example.com",
      name: "Beatriz Almeida",
      phone: "+33 6 11 22 33 44",
      userId: beatrizUserId,
    },
    P,
  );
  const newCustomer = async (name: string, email: string, now: Date): Promise<string> =>
    expectOk(`client ${email}`, await createCustomer(db, { professionalId, name, email }, now)).customerId;
  const rafael = await newCustomer("Rafael Souza", "rafael@example.com", at(-120, 590));
  const thomas = await newCustomer("Thomas Leroy", "thomas@example.com", at(-53, 590));
  const lea = await newCustomer("Léa Martin", "lea@example.com", at(-51, 590));
  const marina = await newCustomer("Marina Costa", "marina@example.com", T);

  /* ── 4. Beatriz: carnet of 10, 3 past classes, pointed, converted, RDV, cancelled séance ── */
  await buy(beatriz, offerOf(catalog, "collectif", 2), at(-55, 600));
  const beatrizPast = debutants.filter((o) => o.startsAt >= at(-53, 0) && o.startsAt < T).slice(0, 3);
  if (beatrizPast.length < 3) throw new Error("Pas assez de séances débutants passées");
  for (const [i, o] of beatrizPast.entries()) {
    expectOk(
      "Beatriz, séance passée",
      await bookWithHeldTicket(
        db,
        {
          professionalId,
          occurrenceId: o.id,
          customerId: beatriz,
          seenStartsAt: o.startsAt,
          actorUserId: beatrizUserId,
          answers: [{ questionId: debutantsQuestion.id, value: "Oui" }],
          customerNotes: i === 0 ? "Bora! Tô amando as aulas 🙌" : null,
        },
        plusMinutes(o.startsAt, -2 * 24 * 60),
      ),
    );
  }
  expectOk(
    "Beatriz, pointage",
    await pointTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: beatriz,
        selection: { ticketTypeId: typeIds.collectif, quantity: 1 },
        note: "Cours rattrapé au parc",
      },
      at(-36, 600),
    ),
  );
  expectOk(
    "Beatriz, conversion",
    await convertTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: beatriz,
        selection: { ticketTypeId: typeIds.collectif, quantity: 1 },
        toTicketTypeId: typeIds.particulier,
      },
      at(-26, 600),
    ),
  );
  expectOk(
    "Beatriz, cours particulier",
    await bookRdv(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        ticketTypeId: typeIds.particulier,
        startsAt: instantAt(dayFrom(5, [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]), 660, TZ),
        customerId: beatriz,
        answers: [{ questionId: objectiveQuestion.id, value: "Tenir 50 touches sans faire tomber la balle" }],
        actorUserId: beatrizUserId,
        mode: "held_ticket",
      },
      T,
    ),
  );
  const intermediaires = firstFrom(await seancesOf(catalog.eventIds.intermediaires), at(1, 0), "intermédiaires");
  expectOk(
    "Beatriz, intermédiaires",
    await bookWithHeldTicket(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        occurrenceId: intermediaires.id,
        customerId: beatriz,
        seenStartsAt: intermediaires.startsAt,
        actorUserId: beatrizUserId,
      },
      T,
    ),
  );
  expectOk(
    "séance annulée",
    await cancelOccurrence(db, { professionalId, actorUserId: proUserId, occurrenceId: intermediaires.id }, plusMinutes(T, 5)),
  );

  /* ── 5. Rafael: a carnet of 4 bought 4 months ago, expired unused ── */
  await buy(rafael, offerOf(catalog, "collectif", 1), at(-120, 600));

  /* ── 6. Thomas: a trial class, booked on the next débutants séance ── */
  await buy(thomas, offerOf(catalog, "essai", 0), at(-53, 600));
  expectOk(
    "Thomas, débutants",
    await bookWithHeldTicket(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        occurrenceId: debutantsUpcoming.id,
        customerId: thomas,
        seenStartsAt: debutantsUpcoming.startsAt,
        actorUserId: null,
        customerNotes: "Jamais joué, mais super curieux d'essayer !",
      },
      T,
    ),
  );

  /* ── 7. Marina: buy + book (T14 shape), one ticket closed, a free discovery call ── */
  const bought = expectOk(
    "Marina, achat + réservation",
    await startBuyAndBook(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        occurrenceId: debutantsUpcoming.id,
        customerId: marina,
        seenStartsAt: debutantsUpcoming.startsAt,
        offerId: offerOf(catalog, "collectif", 1),
        answers: [{ questionId: debutantsQuestion.id, value: "Non" }],
      },
      T,
    ),
  );
  const paid = await fulfillPurchase(
    db,
    bought.purchaseId,
    { sessionId: null, paymentIntentId: null, livemode: false },
    plusMinutes(T, 2),
  );
  if (paid.outcome !== "fulfilled" || !paid.bookingConfirmed) {
    throw new Error(`Marina : paiement ${paid.outcome}, inscription confirmée = ${paid.bookingConfirmed}`);
  }
  expectOk(
    "Marina, clôture",
    await closeTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: marina,
        selection: { ticketTypeId: typeIds.collectif, quantity: 1 },
        reason: "rembourse",
        note: "Remboursé en espèces",
      },
      plusMinutes(T, 3),
    ),
  );
  expectOk(
    "Marina, appel découverte",
    await bookRdv(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        ticketTypeId: typeIds.decouverte,
        startsAt: instantAt(dayFrom(6, [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]), 720, TZ),
        customerId: marina,
        actorUserId: null,
        mode: "free",
      },
      T,
    ),
  );

  /* ── 8. Léa: 2 trial tickets offered, registered by the pro, free roda ── */
  expectOk(
    "Léa, tickets offerts",
    await offerTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: lea,
        ticketTypeId: typeIds.essai,
        quantity: 2,
        expiresAt: null,
        reason: "Bienvenue chez Altinha Paris",
        notify: false,
      },
      at(-51, 600),
    ),
  );
  const leaSeance = debutants.find((o) => o.seriesDate !== null && o.seriesDate >= addDays(TODAY, -49));
  if (!leaSeance || leaSeance.startsAt >= T) throw new Error("Aucune séance débutants passée pour Léa");
  expectOk(
    "Léa, inscrite par le pro",
    await registerPro(
      db,
      { professionalId, actorUserId: proUserId, occurrenceId: leaSeance.id, customerId: lea, asGuest: false },
      plusMinutes(leaSeance.startsAt, 120),
    ),
  );
  const roda = firstFrom(await seancesOf(catalog.eventIds.roda), at(1, 0), "roda");
  expectOk(
    "Léa, roda",
    await registerFree(db, { professionalId, occurrenceId: roda.id, customerId: lea, seenStartsAt: roda.startsAt }, T),
  );

  /* ── 9. Alerts: 🔴 tickets of an archived type, 🟠 a type linked to nothing ── */
  const summer = expectOk(
    "type Stage d'été",
    await createTicketType(
      db,
      { professionalId, type: { name: "Stage d'été 2026", description: "Le stage intensif de juillet.", color: "#ef4444" } },
      at(-40, 600),
    ),
  ).ticketTypeId;
  expectOk(
    "Rafael, tickets du stage",
    await offerTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: rafael,
        ticketTypeId: summer,
        quantity: 2,
        expiresAt: null,
        reason: "Report du stage d'été",
        notify: false,
      },
      at(-40, 610),
    ),
  );
  expectOk(
    "archivage du stage",
    await setTicketTypeArchived(db, { professionalId, ticketTypeId: summer, archived: true }, at(-2, 600)),
  );
  const outdoor = expectOk(
    "type Cours en plein air",
    await createTicketType(db, { professionalId, type: { name: "Cours en plein air", color: "#84cc16" } }, at(-10, 600)),
  ).ticketTypeId;
  expectOk(
    "Thomas, ticket plein air",
    await offerTickets(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        customerId: thomas,
        ticketTypeId: outdoor,
        quantity: 1,
        expiresAt: null,
        reason: "Geste commercial",
        notify: false,
      },
      at(-10, 610),
    ),
  );

  /* ── 10. A day off in 3 weeks, on a day nobody booked ── */
  const offDay = await dayWithoutBookings(professionalId, 21);
  expectOk(
    "indisponibilité",
    await createTimeOffWithConflicts(
      db,
      {
        professionalId,
        actorUserId: proUserId,
        startAt: instantAt(offDay, 0, TZ),
        endAt: instantAt(addDays(offDay, 1), 0, TZ),
        allDay: true,
        reason: "Vacances",
        seenOccurrenceIds: [],
        cancelBooked: false,
      },
      T,
    ),
  );

  /* ── 11. Today: nightly expiry, rolling horizon, invariants ── */
  const { expired } = await expireTickets(db, T);
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    if (expectOk("horizon", await ensureHorizon(db, professionalId, T)).complete) break;
  }
  const violations = await checkInvariants(db, { proId: professionalId });
  if (violations.length > 0) {
    throw new Error(
      `checkInvariants → ${violations.length}\n${violations.map((v) => `  ${v.invariant} ${v.rowId} ${v.detail}`).join("\n")}`,
    );
  }
  console.info(`✓ ${expired} ticket(s) expiré(s) ; checkInvariants → 0`);

  /* ── 12. Summary ── */
  console.info("\n✅ Seed terminé.\n");
  console.info(`Comptes de démonstration (mot de passe : ${PASSWORD}) :`);
  console.info("  • Admin  → admin@skedul.test");
  console.info("  • Pro    → pro@skedul.app");
  console.info("  • Client → beatriz@example.com");
  console.info(`\nPage publique : /${ALTINHA_PROFILE.slug}`);
  console.info("Back-office   : /dashboard\n");
}

main()
  .then(() => process.exit(0))
  .catch((err) => {
    console.error("❌ Seed échoué :", err instanceof Error ? err.message : err);
    if (err instanceof Error && err.cause) console.error(err.cause);
    process.exit(1);
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Typecheck and lint before touching the database**

```bash
./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit
./node_modules/.bin/eslint scripts/seed.ts
```
Expected: no output.

- [ ] **Step 5: Run the seed on tickets-dev**

```bash
./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/seed.ts --wipe; echo "exit $?"
```
Expected:
- dotenv first prints `◇ injected env (N) from .env.local // tip: …`.
- The next line is `→ <host>`, and it must equal the tickets-dev host saved in Task 2. This line is a record, not a gate. The gate is the seed's own guard: when the host is not in `SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS`, it throws before any delete or write (`❌ Seed échoué : Refus : <host> n'est pas dans SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS.`, `exit 1`), and Step 2 showed that only the tickets-dev host is allowed. If the line still shows another host, stop and fix `.env.local` (its `SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS` is wrong too) before running anything else.
- Then `🧹 N compte(s) de démo supprimé(s), avec leurs pages.` N is 3 on the first run, because tickets-dev inherits the June demo users from `development`.
- Then `🌱 Seed Skedul v2 (tickets et événements)…`.
- Then `✓ 4 ticket(s) expiré(s) ; checkInvariants → 0`. These are Rafael's carnet of 4.
- Then `✅ Seed terminé.`, the accounts, `Page publique : /altinha-paris`, and `exit 0`.

If a step fails, the error names it, for example `❌ Seed échoué : Beatriz, cours particulier: slot_taken`. Fix the engine, not the scenario.

- [ ] **Step 6: The guard refuses a second run without `--wipe`**

```bash
./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/seed.ts; echo "exit $?"
```
Expected: dotenv's `◇ injected env …` line, then `→ <tickets-dev host>`, then `❌ Seed échoué : Des comptes de démo existent déjà : relancez avec --wipe pour les remplacer.` and `exit 1`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Bootstrap refuses the seeded page (read-only)**

`bootstrap-content.ts` has no host allowlist, because P6 runs it on production. The Step 2 check therefore gates it here: with `&&`, the script runs only when the check exits 0.

```bash
node -e "require('dotenv').config({path:'.env.local',quiet:true});const h=new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL).host;const ok=(process.env.SEED_ALLOWED_HOSTS||'').split(',').map(s=>s.trim()).includes(h);console.log((ok?'allowed: ':'NOT allowed: ')+h);process.exit(ok?0:1)" && ./node_modules/.bin/tsx scripts/bootstrap-content.ts --pro altinha-paris; echo "exit $?"
```
Expected:
- `allowed: <tickets-dev host>`. If you get `NOT allowed: …` followed by `exit 1`, the script did not run: stop and redo Step 2.
- Then dotenv's `◇ injected env …` line, then `→ <tickets-dev host>`, then `✗ « altinha-paris » a déjà des types, des événements ou des clients : rien n'a été écrit.` and `exit 1`.
